论文列表

光学晶格上的矩阵全息

Matrix Holography on an Optical Lattice

Robin L\"owenberg, Julian Sonner · hep-th, cond-mat.quant-gas, physics.atom-ph, quant-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们提出一种基于原子系综晶格和时间平均Floquet动力学的模拟方案，以构造玻色型BFSS类似矩阵量子力学中典型的四体势。与需要深度电路的基于门电路的数字化实现不同，我们的方法在每个Floquet周期内通过一组固定的控制阶段生成目标模型，使得连续酉操作的数量相对于矩阵尺寸N保持不变。构造中的主要缩放成本由控制不断增长晶格所需的频率范围给出。我们证明该成本最多为O(N^3)，从而为大N提供多项式缩放路径，这有利于实验实现。此外，我们展示了耦合参数可以被调至超过O(1)，从而能够模拟全息相所需的强耦合物理。

English: We propose an analog quantum-simulation protocol based on a lattice of atomic ensembles and time-averaged Floquet dynamics to construct the four-body potentials that typically appear in bosonic BFSS-like matrix quantum mechanics. In contrast to gate-based digital implementations requiring deep circuits, our approach generates the target model through a fixed set of control stages per Floquet cycle, keeping the number of consecutive unitaries constant with respect to the matrix size $N$. The principal scaling cost in our construction is given by the frequency range required to control the growing lattice. We show that this cost grows at most as $\mathcal{O}(N^3)$, giving a polynomial scaling route to large$-N$, attractive to experimental implementation. Additionally, we demonstrate that the coupling parameter can be tuned beyond $\mathcal{O}(1)$, enabling the simulation of strong-coupling physics necessary for holographic phases.

一句话导读：提出一个基于光学晶格与Floquet驱动、可扩展至大N的玻色型BFSS矩阵模型模拟方案，可进入强耦合全息区域。

相关度：medium — 论文核心是使用冷原子/光学晶格平台进行矩阵模型的全息量子模拟，与用户标签中的“冷原子/双组分BEC/凝聚态实验启发”相关，但与其余标签的直接关联较弱。

冷原子/双组分BEC/凝聚态实验启发

全息导出的带自旋强耦合完美流体状态方程

Equation of State of a Strongly Coupled Perfect Fluid with Spin from Holography

Andres Anabalon, Horatiu Nastase · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们利用全息方法推导了2+1维有限自旋的强耦合相对论完美流体的状态方程。对偶引力描述由一个AdS4旋转黑洞提供，其霍金温度、角速度和贝肯斯坦-霍金熵决定了边界流体的热力学性质。我们得到了状态方程并分析了其局部热力学稳定性，发现存在一个临界旋转角速度，超过该值时流体将变得热力学不稳定，从而给出了温度为T、角速度为ω的强耦合夸克胶子等离子体的旋转约束：4πT/ω > 2.77239。值得注意的是，同一个引力解还包含与第二个边界关联的第二个黑洞。尽管第二个黑洞不旋转，其对偶流体却旋转并拥有一个独特的“奇异”状态方程。我们的结果为有限自旋强耦合物质提供了全息状态方程，并建立了黑洞旋转、内禀角动量与相对论流体热力学稳定性之间的直接联系。

English: We derive the equation of state of a strongly coupled relativistic perfect fluid with finite spin in $2+1$ dimensions using holography. The dual gravitational description is provided by a spinning black hole in $AdS_4$, whose Hawking temperature, angular velocity, and Bekenstein-Hawking entropy determine the thermodynamic properties of the boundary fluid. We obtain the equation of state and analyze its local thermodynamic stability, finding a critical rotation above which the fluid becomes thermodynamically unstable providing a bound for the rotation of a strongly coupled quark gluon plasma at temperature $T$ and angular velocity $\omega$ given by $\frac{4\pi T}{\omega}> 2.77239$. Remarkably, the same gravitational solution contains a second black hole associated with a second boundary. Although this black hole is non-spinning, its dual fluid rotates and possesses a distinct, ``exotic'' equation of state. Our results provide a holographic equation of state for strongly coupled matter with finite spin and establish a direct connection between black-hole rotation, intrinsic angular momentum, and the thermodynamic stability of relativistic fluids.

一句话导读：利用AdS4旋转黑洞对偶推导带自旋强耦合流体的状态方程，并得到热力学稳定性对旋转的约束。

相关度：low — 该论文主要研究全息框架下带自旋流体的状态方程和热力学稳定性，而非用户重点关注的凝聚态/超导/相变等支线，因此直接相关性较低。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

二色 ABJM 中的生与灭

Birth and Death in Two-color ABJM

Marco S. Bianchi · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们精确求解了有限秩 U(2)×U(2) ABJM 理论中 half-BPS 算符两点度量的前导量子形变。在自然且与耦合无关的 Schur 标架中，其树级归一化的两圈修正成为双行 Young 图链上的 Jacobi 算符。一个基态变换将其映射为可逆生灭过程，其平稳分布是限制在至多两行图上的 Plancherel 测度。其本征模为 Racah 多项式，大 n 极限趋近径向 Ornstein–Uhlenbeck 动力学，而等价的二自旋描述将 Jacobi 算符识别为受限 SU(2) Casimir 算符。

English: We solve exactly the leading quantum deformation of the two-point metric of half-BPS operators in $U(2)\times U(2)$ ABJM theory at finite rank. In the natural coupling-independent Schur frame, its tree-normalized two-loop correction is a Jacobi operator on the chain of two-row Young diagrams. A ground-state transform maps it to a reversible birth--death process whose stationary distribution is the Plancherel measure conditioned on diagrams with at most two rows. Its eigenmodes are Racah polynomials, its large-$n$ limit approaches radial Ornstein--Uhlenbeck dynamics, and an equivalent two-spin description identifies the Jacobi operator with a restricted $SU(2)$ Casimir.

一句话导读：精确求解有限秩二色 ABJM 中 half-BPS 算符两点度量的一圈量子形变，并将其化归为与 Racah 多项式及 SU(2) Casimir 相关的可逆生灭过程。

相关度：low — 论文主题是 ABJM/AdS4 中算符谱的精确量子形变，属于全息字典与算符代数研究；与用户标注的全息超流、相变、QNM、经典引力等具体关注方向没有直接重叠。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

边界引力子相干性的有限应力张量矩层析

Finite stress-tensor moment tomography of boundary-graviton coherences

Dyuman Bhattacharya, Robert B. Mann · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：2+1维的爱因斯坦引力是一种拓扑理论，不允许存在局域体引力子。然而，具有Brown-Henneaux边界条件的AdS3引力确实允许边界引力子的存在，它们被描述为Virasoro后代。生活在渐近边界上的观察者可以使用边界应力张量的有限阶矩作为这些态的探针。我们固定一个最高权模块，并假设Shapovalov形式非退化。我们还假设观察者探测的未知态具有已知的有限后代支撑，并且层间相干性是未知数据的一部分。我们发现，对于级别N≥2的一个后代，N阶应力矩层析（即N次应力矩插入）在信息上是不完备的，而N+1阶应力矩层析则是信息完备的。也就是说，在酉正则点集上，正交纯态可以具有相同的完整N阶记录。同样，对于精确的有限窗口结果，当级别直到L≥2时，L阶层析是不完备的，而L+1阶层析则恢复每一个密度矩阵块。相应的精确阈值在左右全理论中成立。在具有半经典体解释的重扇区中，这些阈值说明了有限支撑的边界引力子态何时首次变得可区分。

English: Einstein gravity in 2+1 dimensions is a topological theory that does not admit the existence of local bulk gravitons. However, $\mathrm{AdS}_3$ gravity with Brown-Henneaux boundary conditions does admit the existence of boundary gravitons, which are described by Virasoro descendants. An observer living on the asymptotic boundary can use finite order moments of the boundary stress tensor as a probe of these states. We fix one highest-weight module and assume that the Shapovalov forms are nondegenerate. We also assume that the unknown state the observer is probing has a known finite descendant support and that interlevel coherence is part of the unknown data. We find that, given a descendant at level $N\geq2$, stress moment tomography at order $N$ (meaning $N$ stress moment insertions) is informationally incomplete, while tomography with stress moments of order $N+1$ is informationally complete. This is to say that, on the unitary regular locus, orthogonal pure states can have the same complete order-$N$ record. Likewise, for exact finite-window result for levels through $L\geq2$, tomography of order $L$ is incomplete while tomography of order $L+1$ recovers every density-matrix block. Corresponding exact thresholds hold in the full left-right theory. In heavy sectors for which a semiclassical bulk interpretation exists, these thresholds say when finite-support boundary-graviton states first become distinguishable.

一句话导读：研究AdS3/CFT2中边界应力张量矩层析对边界引力子态的信息完备性，发现N阶矩不足以区分N级以上态，而N+1阶则信息完备。

相关度：low — 论文聚焦于AdS3/CFT2量子态层析，属于全息量子信息基础研究，与用户所列全息超流超导、经典引力、QNM等特定支线不直接相关。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

能听到黑洞奇点的形状吗？

Can one hear the shape of a black hole singularity?

Sean A. Hartnoll, Alexander Zhiboedov · hep-th, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们计算了具有内部近奇点Kasner时代的渐近AdS黑洞的大过振准正则频率ω_n。我们表明，在边界动量k=0处，色散∂ω_n/∂k^2的大n行为可以干净地提取Kasner指数。我们证明，正如BKL近奇点混沌中发生的Kasner跃迁在准正则频率中是“可听的”，并揭示出过振数n与到奇点的固有时之间的映射。我们的结果是通过WKB分析获得的，该分析将非解析指数（由近奇点时空决定）与非普适数据（如从边界到奇点的光学距离）分离开来。我们在几个数值模型中检验了我们的公式。

English: We compute the large overtone quasinormal frequencies $\omega_n$ of asymptotically AdS black holes with interior near-singularity Kasner epochs. We show that the Kasner exponents can be extracted cleanly from the large $n$ behaviour of the dispersion $\partial \omega_n/\partial k^2$ at $k=0$, where $k$ is a boundary momentum. We show that Kasner transitions, as occur in BKL near-singularity chaos, are audible in the quasinormal frequencies and reveal a map between overtone number $n$ and proper time to the singularity. Our results are obtained using a WKB analysis that isolates non-analytic exponents, determined by the near-singularity spacetime, from non-universal data such as the optical distance from the boundary to the singularity. We check our formulae in several numerical models.

一句话导读：通过WKB分析，证明从AdS黑洞准正则频率的大过振色散中可提取内部Kasner奇点指数，使奇点几何“可听”。

相关度：high — 论文聚焦大过振准正则频率及其对黑洞内部Kasner奇点的探测，直接对应“QNM与非线性演化”和“经典引力/广义相对论”标签；但它不涉及全息超导/多分量序参量等具体动态，所以相关性高但不在用户最主要方向上。

QNM与非线性演化经典引力/广义相对论

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

幺正量子共形 Bondi-Metzner-Sachs 场论

Unitary quantum conformal Bondi-Metzner-Sachs field theories

Daniel Grumiller, Iva Lovrekovic · hep-th, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们对二维场论施加幺正性约束，这些场论的对称性构成量子共形BMS代数，该代数结合了共形对称性与渐近平坦时空的渐近对称性。正性约束给中心荷限定范围 0<c<2.053。相关顶点代数的正性仅允许四个中心荷：c = 1, 3/2, 13/7, 2。因而只剩下四个幺正场论候选者。我们计算了它们的原初谱，并表明其与 BPS-like 及全息不等式兼容。

English: We impose unitarity constraints on two-dimensional field theories whose symmetries form the quantum conformal Bondi-Metzner-Sachs algebra, which combines conformal symmetries with asymptotic symmetries of asymptotically flat spacetimes. Positivity constraints put bounds on the central charge, 0<c<2.053. Positivity of the associated vertex algebra allows only four central charges, c = 1, 3/2, 13/7, 2. Thus, only four candidates for unitary field theories remain. We calculate their spectra of primaries and show that they are compatible with BPS-like and holographic inequalities.

一句话导读：通过幺正性与顶点代数正性限制量子共形BMS场论的中心荷，得到仅四个允许值。

相关度：low — 论文研究平直时空渐近对称性与全息约束，属于平直时空全息/BMS支线，与用户关注的全息超流、强关联、相变、经典引力等支线不匹配。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

超越大 N 极限的纠缠楔重建：基于扭缠 Petz 映射

Entanglement Wedge Reconstruction Beyond the Large N Limit via the Twirled Petz Map

Arash Alipour Shahmiri, Maryam Sharifian, Niloofar Vardian · hep-th, gr-qc, quant-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：纠缠楔重建提供了 AdS/CFT 中体局域性的最精确表述，将纠缠楔识别为可从边界子区域重建的最大体区域。虽然这一陈述在大 N 展开的首阶已通过模流和 Petz 映射重建被充分理解，但其在半经典区域之外的外推仍不清晰。在这项工作中，我们证明量子纠错为解决该问题提供了自然框架。在首阶，我们复现了已知的首阶重建公式，并利用扭缠 Petz 映射，获得了一种系统且显式的方法来纳入次领头 1/N 修正。在领头近似之外，我们得到了由次领头 Petz 核控制的显式双局域修正。我们的结果为超越大 N 的体重建建立了受控方法，并强化了量子纠错在全息中的作用。该框架为在完全量子引力场景中理解体重建提供了可控路径。

English: Entanglement wedge reconstruction provides the sharpest formulation of bulk locality in AdS/CFT, identifying the entanglement wedge as the largest bulk region reconstructible from a boundary subregion. While this statement is well understood at leading order in the large N expansion through both modular flow and Petz map reconstruction, its extension beyond the semiclassical regime has remained unclear. In this work, we show that quantum error correction provides the natural framework for addressing this problem. At leading order, we reproduce the known leading-order reconstruction formulas, and by employing the twirled Petz map, we obtain a systematic and explicit method for incorporating subleading 1/N corrections. Beyond the leading approximation, we have obtained an explicit bi-local correction controlled by a subleading Petz kernel. Our results establish a controlled approach to bulk reconstruction beyond large N and sharpening the role of quantum error correction in holography. This framework provides a controlled path toward understanding bulk reconstruction in fully quantum gravitational settings.

一句话导读：利用扭缠 Petz 映射系统地推广纠缠楔重建至大 N 极限之外，获得次领头双局域修正。

相关度：medium — 该工作深化全息体重建与量子纠错理解，但与用户重点标签（超流超导、相变、QNM等）无直接交集。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

平面波极限下 ABJM/BMN 指标匹配

ABJM/BMN Index Matching in the Plane-Wave Limit

Chi-Ming Chang, Kangning Liu · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们确立了在平面波极限中，对于每个固定正陈-西蒙斯水平 k，ABJM 理论的精化超对称指标与 BMN 矩阵模型指标之间的逐系数相等性。对于固定重数（从而固定的有限 ABJM 规范秩）的多膜族，当所有纵向动量趋于无穷且其成对差值固定时，该相等性成立。标量修饰的等通量 ABJM 磁单极对应 BMN 模糊球膜真空：重数标志重合膜数，而通量大小给出每个膜沿 M 理论圆的动量。在指标层面，相关的 Higgs 极点给出了 ABJM 平面波贡献的对角和乐积分，其磁单极-调和核与 BMN 矩阵-调和核在有限的 BMN 上自旋截断之上一致。在有限动量下，完整指标差分为非平面波余项与该截断之上的调和尾部之和。增大 k 将前者移至更高的逸度阶，而不改变存活的核或截断贡献。这给出了之前单膜指标关系的逐系数、多膜推广。

English: We establish coefficientwise equality between the refined supersymmetric indices of ABJM theory and the BMN matrix model in the plane-wave limit at every fixed positive Chern-Simons level k. For multi-membrane families with fixed multiplicities, and hence fixed finite ABJM gauge rank, the equality holds as all longitudinal momenta tend to infinity with their pairwise differences fixed. Scalar-dressed equal-flux ABJM monopoles correspond to BMN vacua of fuzzy-sphere membranes: multiplicities count coincident membranes, whereas flux magnitudes give the momentum per membrane along the M-theory circle. At the index level, the associated Higgs residues yield a diagonal-holonomy integral for the ABJM plane-wave contribution, whose monopole-harmonic kernel agrees with the BMN matrix-harmonic kernel up to the finite BMN upper-spin cutoff. At finite momentum, the full index difference splits into the non-plane-wave remainder and the harmonic tail above this cutoff. Increasing k shifts the former to higher fugacity grade without changing the surviving kernel or the cutoff contribution. This gives a coefficientwise, multi-membrane extension of the earlier single-membrane index relation.

一句话导读：本文证明了在平面波极限下 ABJM 理论与 BMN 矩阵模型的精化超对称指标逐系数相等，并将单膜关系推广到多膜情形。

相关度：low — 该论文关注 ABJM 与 BMN 矩阵模型的超对称指标匹配，属于弦论/AdS/CFT 的形式化研究，与用户重点标签（全息凝聚态、经典引力、相变、QNM等）不直接相关。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

Wilson圈、M2膜与弦

Wilson loops, M2-branes and strings

Alexia Nix · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们建立了一种普适方法，用于研究渐近AdS₄背景中探针M2膜和基本弦的半经典量子化。在场论一侧，它们的配分函数对偶于一类三维N≥2 Chern-Simons-物质理论中半BPS Wilson圈的真空期望值。利用这一普适方法，我们得到了Wilson圈半经典行为的新预言，并在可用时将我们的结果与通过超对称局域化获得的表达式相匹配。我们还表明，正确的全息系综选择意味着探针M2膜的半经典量子化可给出对偶Wilson圈关于规范群秩N的全阶微扰答案。本文是2025年12月17-19日在雅典国立卡波季斯特里安大学举行的“雅典理论物理研讨会：十周年”会议投稿。

English: We establish a universal approach for studying the semiclassical quantization of both probe M2-branes and fundamental strings in asymptotically AdS$_{4}$ backgrounds. On the field theory side, their partition functions are dual to the vacuum expectation value of half-BPS Wilson loops of a class of three-dimensional $\mathcal{N}\geq 2$ Chern-Simons-matter theories. Utilizing this universal approach we arrive at novel predictions for the semiclassical behaviour of the Wilson loop or upon availability match our results with the previously obtained expressions via supersymmetric localization. We also show that the correct choice of holographic ensemble suggests that the semiclassical quantization of probe M2-branes yields the full perturbative answer in the rank of the gauge group $N$ for the dual Wilson loop. This is a proceedings contribution to the ''Athens Workshop in Theoretical Physics: 10th Anniversary'', held at the National and Kapodistrian University of Athens on December 17-19 2025.

一句话导读：提出统一方法研究AdS₄中探针M2膜和基本弦的半经典量子化，及其对偶的Chern-Simons物质理论Wilson圈真空期望值。

相关度：low — 该论文涉及AdS₄/CFT₃中Wilson圈与探针M2膜量化，属于AdS/CFT基础字典，但与用户重点关注的凝聚态、QNM、相变等具体方向不直接相关。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

偶然ABJM算子作为黑洞

Fortuitous ABJM Operators as Black Holes

Connor Behan, Leonardo Pipolo de Gioia · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：给定一个固定圈阶的超共形规范理论，BPS态可以通过两种方式研究：枚举幂零超荷的上同调类，或对角化反常维度矩阵。第二种方式可以更深入地理解全息理论如何根据算子是对偶于无地平线几何还是黑洞来区分它们。为此，我们在U(N)_k × U(N)_{-k} ABJM理论中将对角化形式化为N的参数。这使得定义算子轨迹并关注那些“偶然”算子成为可能，其特征是仅在有限个N值处是BPS的。与数值上难以研究多个这类轨迹的N=4超杨-米尔斯相比，ABJM使我们能够访问16条这样的轨迹及其后裔。我们证明在大N下它们采取特别简单的形式，并与可积性预言一致。我们还表明，它们中的许多（如同黑洞）只能被某些类型的引力子所修饰，并评述了这一上同调练习对完整算子性质的影响。最后，我们展示了ABJM理论中到第一个非BPS态的能隙，其定性差异与N=4超杨-米尔斯理论的预期方式一致。

English: Given a superconformal gauge theory at a fixed loop order, BPS states can be studied in two ways --- by enumerating cohomology classes of a nilpotent supercharge or by diagonalizing the anomalous dimension matrix. The second one allows for a deeper look at how holographic theories distinguish between operators based on whether they are dual to horizonless geometries or black holes. To this end, we carry out the diagonalization in $\mathrm{U}(N)_k \times \mathrm{U}(N)_{-k}$ ABJM theory with $N$ as a formal parameter. This makes it possible to define trajectories of operators and focus on the fortuitous ones which are characterized by being BPS at only finitely many values of $N$. In contrast to $\mathcal{N} = 4$ Super Yang-Mills, where it has been numerically prohibitive to study more than one of these, ABJM gives us access to 16 such trajectories along with their descendants. We show that they take an especially simple form at large $N$ where they agree with the predictions of integrability. We also show that many of them, as with black holes, can only be dressed by certain types of gravitons and comment on how this exercise in cohomology also has implications for properties of the full operators. Finally, we show that the gap to the first non-BPS state in ABJM theory qualitatively differs from that of $\mathcal{N} = 4$ Super Yang-Mills in the expected way.

一句话导读：研究了ABJM理论中偶然BPS算子的轨迹，发现大N极限下与可积性一致，且许多算子像黑洞一样只能被特定引力子修饰。

相关度：high — 论文以ABJM超共形理论中的fortuitous算子为对象，确实涉及全息对偶中算子与黑洞的区分以及可积性，属于AdS/CFT字典和黑洞量子引力的核心问题。

有限温度全息超流体中多量子涡旋的劈裂动力学

Splitting Dynamics of Multiply Quantized Vortices in Holographic Superfluid of Finite Temperature

Shanquan Lan, Xin Li, Jiexiong Mo, Yu Tian, Yu-Kun Yan, Hongbao Zhang · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们结合准正规模的线性扰动分析与完全非线性实时数值模拟，研究了有限温度二维全息超流体中具有绕数n=5,6,7,8的多量子涡旋的劈裂动力学。揭示了三个新的物理现象。第一，随着n增加，不稳定模的数目不再严格遵循2n-3公式。对于n=8的涡旋，p=2(n-1)的不稳定模在整个温度范围内缺失，因此只存在2n-4个不稳定模。第二，主导不稳定模随温度升高发生的转变表现出新的特征。对于n≤6的涡旋，主导模按p=2,3,…,n的顺序依次改变，而对于n≥7发生跳跃式转变——例如，对于n=7，主导模在T=0.325Tc处从p=2跳至p=4，然后在T=0.359Tc处直接跳到p=7；对于n=8，在T=0.302Tc处直接从p=2跳到p=8。第三，高绕数涡旋的单一分裂图案可包含多个具有不同拓扑结构的子分裂图案，如n=8涡旋的l=4图案在低温、中温和高温下表现出的三个子图案。非线性模拟证实了线性稳定性分析的预言，并讨论了对冷原子实验的启示。

English: We study the splitting dynamics of multiply quantized vortices with winding numbers $n=5,6,7$ and $8$ in a two-dimensional holographic superfluid at finite temperature, by combining linear perturbation analysis of quasinormal modes with fully nonlinear real-time numerical simulations. Three new physical phenomena are revealed. First, the number of unstable modes no longer strictly follows the $2n-3$ formula as $n$ increases. For the vortex with $n=8$, the unstable mode with $p=2(n-1)$ is absent throughout the entire temperature range, so that only $2n-4$ unstable modes exist. Second, the transition of the dominant unstable mode with increasing temperature exhibits new characteristics. For vortices with $n\le 6$, the dominant mode changes sequentially as $p=2,3,\dots,n$, whereas for $n\ge 7$ jump-like transitions occur-for instance, for $n=7$ the dominant mode jumps from $p=2$ to $p=4$ at $T=0.325T_c$ and then directly to $p=7$ at $T=0.359T_c$, and for $n=8$ it jumps directly from $p=2$ to $p=8$ at $T=0.302T_c$. Third, a single splitting pattern of high-winding-number vortices can contain multiple sub-splitting patterns with distinct topological structures, as exemplified by the $l=4$ pattern of the $n=8$ vortex, which exhibits three sub-patterns at low, intermediate and high temperatures. The nonlinear simulations confirm the predictions of the linear stability analysis, and the implications of our results for cold-atom experiments are discussed.

一句话导读：通过线性分析与非线性模拟研究全息超流中多量子涡旋的劈裂动力学，发现不稳定模式数偏离2n-3公式、主导模式跳跃式转变及多重子分裂图景。

相关度：high — 论文直接研究全息超流体中的涡旋劈裂动力学，属于全息超流超导与涡旋/非线性演化主题，同时涉及QNM分析和冷原子实验启示。

ABJM理论中的M5膜瞬子

M5 Instantons in ABJM

Jieming Lin, Jesse van Muiden, Zihan Wang · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们研究了M理论中渐近AdS4 × S7/Zk几何（其对偶为ABJM理论）内保超对称M5膜瞬子的存在性。这些瞬子的超对称嵌入由M5膜的κ符号决定，使其局域化在渐近AdS4几何的等变不动点处，并缠绕(S3 × S3)/Zk。在量子层面，我们发现这些瞬子的单圈配分函数为零，这表明体配分函数应改由超引力模和M2膜瞬子展开，这与对偶ABJM理论S3配分函数的已知结果一致。

English: We study the existence of supersymmetric M5-brane instantons in asymptotically AdS$_4 \times S^7/\mathbf{Z}_k$ geometries of M-theory, dual to the ABJM theory. The supersymmetric embeddings of these instantons are determined by the M5-brane $\kappa$-symmetry, which forces them to localise to the equivariant fixed points in the asymptotically AdS$_4$ geometry, and wrap $(S^3\times S^3)/\mathbf{Z}_k $. At the quantum level we find that the one-loop partition function of these instantons vanishes, suggesting that the bulk partition function is instead to be expanded in terms of supergravity modes and M2-brane instantons, in line with known results for the $S^3$ partition function of the dual ABJM theory.

一句话导读：本文发现ABJM对偶中M5膜瞬子的单圈配分函数为零，体配分函数需由超引力模与M2瞬子展开。

量子引力启发的Alcubierre曲速驱动几何

Quantum-gravity-inspired Alcubierre warp-drive geometries

Kimet Jusufi, Francisco S. N. Lobo · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：弦论中的T对偶通过其与路径积分对偶的对应关系，赋予低能传播子一个零点长度 l0=2π√α'，从而软化了引力场在短距离的行为。受相应涂抹源构造正则黑洞的启发，我们将曲速轮廓与该源的互补累积质量分数对应起来，构建了一个T对偶启发的Alcubierre几何。对于有效单尺度选择 f(r_s)=1-r_s^3/(r_s^2+l^2)^{3/2}（其中 l^2=R^2+l_0^2），该度规在泡中心是C^2的，在其他地方光滑，且能量密度、其体积积分和York展开均可给出闭式表达式。特别地，在几何单位下 E=-(15π/1024)v_s^2 l，且 |ρ_E| 被一个正比于 v_s^2/l^2 的常数界限所约束。在这一单尺度族中，该模型因此消除了固定速度下轮廓收缩极限 R→0 的发散，因为 l 不能低于 l_0。不过，这不应与常规Alcubierre薄壁极限在固定宏观泡半径下独立壁厚趋于零的正则化相混淆。然而我们要强调，该构造是一种有效猜想，而非从弦论修正场方程推导而来：R是宏观轮廓尺度，选择 l^2=R^2+l_0^2 是一种插值，经典极限 l_0→0 仍然是光滑的厚壁轮廓，而非分布的Alcubierre阶跃。奇异物质仍然是必需的，且在没有明确重整化应力张量计算的情况下，既未声称半经典稳定性，也未声称修正的量子能量不等式。

English: String T-duality, through its correspondence with path-integral duality, endows the low-energy propagator with a zero-point length $l_0=2\pi\sqrt{\alpha'}$ that softens the short-distance behavior of gravitational fields. Motivated by the regular black-hole construction obtained from the corresponding smeared source, we formulate a T-duality-inspired Alcubierre geometry by identifying the warp profile with the complementary cumulative mass fraction of that source. For the effective one-scale choice $f(r_s)=1-r_s^3/(r_s^2+l^2)^{3/2}$, with $l^2=R^2+l_0^2$, the metric is $C^2$ at the bubble center and smooth elsewhere, while the energy density, its volume integral, and the York expansion are available in closed form. In particular, $E=-(15\pi/1024)v_s^2 l$ in geometric units and $|\rho_E|$ is bounded by a constant times $v_s^2/l^2$. Within this one-scale family, the model therefore removes the divergence associated with the profile-contraction limit $R\to 0$ at fixed velocity, because $l$ cannot fall below $l_0$. This should not be confused with a regularization of the conventional Alcubierre thin-wall limit at fixed macroscopic bubble radius, in which an independent wall thickness is taken to zero. We emphasize, however, that the construction is an effective ansatz rather than a derivation from string-corrected field equations: $R$ is a macroscopic profile scale, the choice $l^2=R^2+l_0^2$ is an interpolation, and the classical $l_0\to 0$ limit remains a smooth thick-walled profile rather than the distributional Alcubierre top hat. Exotic matter remains necessary, and neither semiclassical stability nor a modified quantum energy inequality is claimed without an explicit renormalized stress-tensor calculation.

一句话导读：受弦论T对偶启发的有效单尺度Alcubierre几何，通过零点长度避免了轮廓收缩极限的发散，但仍是需要奇异物质的有效模型。

相关度：medium — 论文以经典引力框架研究量子引力启发的曲速驱动几何，属于经典引力/广义相对论支线，但与全息或用户重点标签无直接关联。

相对论介质中的引力有效场论

Effective Field Theory of Gravity in Relativistic Media

Beka Modrekiladze · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们构建了相对论介质中引力的有效场论。将介质积分掉后，真空引力依然适用，但费曼规则得到更新：引力子传播子由环境应力-能量两点函数修饰，介质诱导的体引力子顶点来自高阶关联函数，广义世界线耦合编码在匹配的威尔逊系数中。不同介质（理想流体、无碰撞物质、相干标量场如波暗物质）并非不同理论，而是在相同图中插入不同关联函数。我们推导了相对论流体的介质内规则，得到完整的1PN爱因斯坦-因费尔德-霍夫曼势、1PN斯托克斯阻力、引力自能与推广的克里斯托杜洛记忆，其新张量结构记录了周围流动方向，将永久应变变为“天体物理风向标”。这些规则还激活了真空中被禁止的现象：声学极点将受对称性保护、非跑动的黑洞潮汐洛夫数变为共振且跑动的量，并在运动介质中打开h→hh通道，给出闭式衰变率及由局域流几何决定的双折射“引力不透明度”。我们评估了观测前景，从爱因斯坦望远镜与LISA可及的失相和潮汐共振，到原理性的记忆与不透明度签名。

English: We develop an effective field theory of gravity in relativistic media. Integrating out the medium leaves vacuum gravity with updated Feynman rules: a graviton propagator dressed by the stress-energy two-point function of the environment, medium-induced bulk graviton vertices from higher correlators, and generalized worldline couplings encoded in matched Wilson coefficients. The diagram topologies are unchanged from vacuum, so different media (perfect fluids, collisionless matter, coherent scalar fields such as wave dark matter) are not different theories but different correlators inserted into the same diagrams. We derive the in-medium rules for a relativistic fluid and obtain the full 1PN Einstein-Infeld-Hoffmann potential, the 1PN Stokes drag, the gravitational self-energy and a generalized Christodoulou memory whose new tensor structure records the direction of the surrounding flow, turning the permanent strain into an $\textit{astrophysical weathervane}$. The same rules activate phenomena forbidden in vacuum: the sound pole converts the symmetry-protected, non-running black-hole Love number into a resonant, running one, and opens the channel $h\to hh$ in a moving medium, yielding a closed-form decay rate and a birefringent $\textit{gravitational opacity}$ set by the local flow geometry. We assess observational prospects, from dephasing and tidal resonances within reach of the Einstein Telescope and LISA to proof-of-principle memory and opacity signatures.

一句话导读：将介质效应系统纳入引力有效场论，预言黑洞洛夫数的共振跑动、h→hh衰变、流动方向记忆等新现象，并给出引力波观测前景。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论支线，涉及引力波、黑洞潮汐响应和记忆效应等经典引力现象，但不涉及AdS/CFT对应或全息量子引力，故对理论全息分类的相关性为中等。

度量-仿射 Gauss–Bonnet 引力中的主符号阻碍

A principal-symbol obstruction in metric-affine Gauss--Bonnet gravity

Hamed Bouzari Nezhad · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：四维黎曼 Gauss–Bonnet 组合到具有独立仿射联络的几何存在不止一种延续。两种自然候选——度规缩并代表与双 epsilon 代表——在 Levi-Civita 极限下一致，我们问它们是否也给独立联络以相同的首阶微分结构。在标量耦合 f(φ) 到 Gauss–Bonnet 项的度量-仿射模型中，我们比较它们在 Levi-Civita 位形附近的二次两导数联络主型。对于度规缩并延续，主型在类时主余矢处的二十四维无钩无挠见证子空间上求值，其秩为 18、零化度为 6，且不定；因此同一余矢处的完全无挠型不定且秩至少为 18。在局部 f(φ)≠0 处，线性化联络方程的两导数主符号非零，这阻碍了激活方向上联络的纯代数局部消去。对于双 epsilon 延续，相应的二次两导数形式在联络关于该位形的任意扰动下恒为零，因此其主符号随之消失，该特定阻碍不存在，尽管其缺失本身并不能确立代数可消去性。因此，独立联络的首阶微分结构依赖于黎曼 Gauss–Bonnet 组合的仿射延续。

English: The four-dimensional Riemannian Gauss--Bonnet combination admits more than one continuation to a geometry with an independent affine connection. Two natural candidates, the metric-contracted representative and the double-epsilon representative, agree in their Levi-Civita limit, and we ask whether they also give the independent connection the same leading differential structure. In a metric-affine model with a scalar coupling $f(\phi)$ to the Gauss--Bonnet term, we compare their quadratic two-derivative connection principal forms about the Levi-Civita locus. For the metric-contracted continuation, the principal form, evaluated on a twenty-four-dimensional hook-free torsion-free witness subspace at a timelike principal covector, has rank $18$ and nullity $6$ and is indefinite; the full torsion-free form at the same covector is therefore indefinite with rank at least $18$. Locally where $f(\phi)\neq0$, the two-derivative principal symbol of the linearized connection equations is nonzero, which obstructs purely algebraic local elimination of the connection in the activated directions. For the double-epsilon continuation, the corresponding quadratic two-derivative form vanishes identically for an arbitrary perturbation of the connection about that locus, so its principal symbol vanishes with it, and that particular obstruction is absent, though its absence does not by itself establish algebraic eliminability. Thus the leading differential structure of the independent connection depends on the affine continuation of the Riemannian Gauss--Bonnet combination.

一句话导读：该论文研究度量-仿射 Gauss–Bonnet 引力中独立联络的动力学主符号，发现其行为依赖于 Gauss–Bonnet 项的仿射延续选取，其中一种延续存在主符号阻碍而另一种没有。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力和修改引力中的偏微分方程约束分析，与用户的经典引力支线相关，但不涉及全息或其他重点研究分支，故相关性为中。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

动态张力弦状刺猬产生的正则黑洞

Regular black holes from dynamical-tension string hedgehogs

Sebastian Bahamonde, Eduardo I. Guendelman · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：一团具有非零常张力径向弦的球对称云，其能量密度正比于 $r^{-2}$，因此会产生奇异的锥形几何，而非渐近平坦的正则黑洞。我们表明，在修正测度弦论表述中可以移除这一障碍。在受约束的 $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ 刺猬分支上，由复合体张力标量生成的连续径向世界片流形可约化为爱因斯坦引力与非线性函数 $K(Y)$ 及辅助三形式场的耦合。该构造直接以径向张力 $\mathcal{T}(r)$ 给出了与模型无关的条件。中心处曲率有限要求 $\mathcal{T}=\mathcal{O}(r^2)$，而零能量条件禁止更快的首阶幂。因此，满足零能量条件的正正则中心必然是 de Sitter 的。ADM 质量有限要求张力在无穷远处被屏蔽，而若非平凡正剖面以快于 $r^{-2}$ 的方式被屏蔽，则主能量条件无法全局成立。一个精确的三次剖面实现了这些性质，并给出一个渐近平坦的几何正则黑洞连续族，其相对于 Schwarzschild 的首个修正出现在 $r^{-4}$ 阶。

English: A spherical cloud of radial strings with non-zero constant tension has an energy density proportional to $r^{-2}$ and therefore produces a singular conical geometry rather than an asymptotically flat regular black hole. We show that this obstruction can be removed within the modified-measure formulation of strings. A continuum of radial worldsheets whose tensions are generated by a composite bulk tension scalar reduces, on a constrained $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ hedgehog branch, to Einstein gravity coupled to a nonlinear function $K(Y)$ and an auxiliary three-form. This construction gives model-independent conditions directly in terms of the radial tension $\mathcal{T}(r)$. Finite curvature at the centre requires $\mathcal{T}=\mathcal{O}(r^2)$, while the null energy condition forbids a faster leading power. Thus a positive regular centre satisfying the null energy condition is necessarily de Sitter. Finite ADM mass requires the tension to be screened at infinity, whereas the dominant energy condition cannot hold globally for a non-trivial positive profile screened faster than $r^{-2}$. An exact cubic profile realises these properties and gives a continuous family of asymptotically flat geometrically regular black holes whose first correction to Schwarzschild appears at order $r^{-4}$.

一句话导读：利用修正测度弦导出的动态张力径向弦云，构造满足零能量条件、中心为 de Sitter 的渐近平坦正则黑洞族。

相关度：medium — 论文主要处理经典引力中正则黑洞解的构造与能量条件约束，不属于全息或凝聚态主线；由于用户关注标签中包含经典引力/广义相对论，故具有中等关联度。

经典引力/广义相对论

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

强场区检验PPN的新窗口：S301的恒星自转

A New Window for Testing the PPN in the Strong-Field Regime: Stellar Rotation of S301

Mohsen Khodadi, Salvatore Capozziello · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：最近发现的S301恒星轨道周期为8.7年、偏心率e=0.982，其近心点仅为140 Rs——约为S2的近心点1200 Rs的九分之一——为强场引力检验提供了前所未有的实验室。我们证明S301的恒星自旋进动为PPN参数γ提供了一种新探针，其修正相对于广义相对论按(2γ_PPN+1)/3缩放。对于最大投影速度漂移为46.1 km/s的情况，下一代光谱仪有望将γ_PPN约束到约10%的精度。当与S301的轨道进动结合时——广义相对论预言每圈近心点进动约1.95°，比S2的约0.2°大约一个量级，约束(2β_PPN+2γ_PPN-1)/3——自旋测量打破了β-γ简并。对S2和S301预计观测的MCMC分析给出σ_β≈2.3×10^-4，而γ的精度受自旋进动测量限制为σ_γ≈0.1。虽然自旋进动提供了独立的一致性检验，但其真正意义在于在φ/c²~10^-4和v/c~0.08处探测引力——比太阳系检验强四个数量级，比当前S星约束强一个数量级。这种互补方法为在强场区检验PPN框架开辟了新窗口，并展示了结合银河系中心多个恒星探针的威力。

English: The recently discovered S301 star, with an orbital period of 8.7 years and eccentricity $e = 0.982$, reaches a pericenter of just $140\,R_{\mathrm{s}}$ --- approximately nine times closer than S2's $\sim 1200R_{\mathrm{s}}$ --- offering an unprecedented laboratory for testing strong-field gravity. We demonstrate that S301's stellar spin precession provides a novel probe of the PPN parameter $\gamma$, scaling as $(2\gamma_{\mathrm{PPN}} + 1)/3$ relative to general relativity (GR). For maximum projected velocity shifts of $46.1~\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}}$, next-generation spectrographs could constrain $\gamma_{\mathrm{PPN}}$ to $\sim 10\%$ precision. When combined with S301's orbital precession --- a substantial $\sim 1.95^\circ$ pericenter advance per orbit (GR prediction), approximately an order of magnitude larger than S2's $\sim 0.2^\circ$, constraining $(2\beta_{\mathrm{PPN}} + 2\gamma_{\mathrm{PPN}} - 1)/3$ --- the spin measurement breaks the $\beta$-$\gamma$ degeneracy. A MCMC analysis of projected S2 and S301 observations yields $\sigma_\beta \approx 2.3 \times 10^{-4}$, with the $\gamma$ precision limited by the spin precession measurement to $\sigma_\gamma \approx 0.1$. While the spin precession provides an independent consistency check, its true significance lies in probing gravity at $\phi/c^2 \sim 10^{-4}$ and $v/c \sim 0.08$ --- four orders of magnitude stronger than Solar System tests and an order of magnitude stronger than current S-star constraints. This complementary approach opens a new window for testing the PPN framework in the strong-field regime and demonstrates the power of combining multiple stellar probes at the Galactic Center.

一句话导读：利用银河系中心恒星S301的自转进动与轨道进动结合，有望打破PPN参数β-γ简并，在强场区检验广义相对论。

相关度：high — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论检验，直接对应用户关注的经典引力支线，与全息或量子引力无关；其强场PPN检验和恒星探针方法在该方向具有较高价值。

隆起形变准正则模的Darboux等谱约束

Darboux Isospectrality Constraints on Quasinormal Modes Deformed by Bumps

Zhen-Xiao Zhang, Chen Lan, Yan-Gang Miao · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：局域的Gaussian或Pöschl-Teller隆起通常被用来探测Schwarzschild准正则模的敏感性，具体做法是将其同时加到Regge-Wheeler势和Zerilli势上。我们证明，这种附加方案通常与连接两个宇称扇区的Darboux变换不相容。原因是，Darboux变换将宇称盲的隆起限制为一族带有不可避免的r^{-2}尾巴的隆起，从而排除了附加方案中任意振幅、中心或宽度的Gaussian或Pöschl-Teller profile。我们给出了Darboux容许的轴型与极型隆起对的完整分类，并提出两种自洽方案：Darboux生成元方案（保留Gaussian或Pöschl-Teller profile作为隆起对的生成元，而不是隆起本身），以及Riccati补全方案（把Gaussian或Pöschl-Teller profile看作隆起对之一，其伙伴由Riccati方程解出）。我们的频域计算证实，若缺少Darboux自洽方案，会产生有限且虚假的轴-极准正则模劈裂，而基模质心移动几乎不变。

English: A localized Gaussian or P\"oschl-Teller bump is widely used to probe the sensitivity of Schwarzschild quasinormal modes, where it is typically added to both the Regge-Wheeler and Zerilli potentials. We show that such an additive prescription is generically incompatible with the Darboux transformation connecting the two parity sectors. The reason is that such a transformation restricts a parity-blind bump to a fixed family of bumps with an unavoidable $r^{-2}$ tail, excluding any Gaussian or P\"oschl-Teller profile with arbitrary amplitude, center, or width in the additive prescription. We give the complete classification of Darboux-admissible axial and polar pairs of bumps and propose two consistent scenarios: Darboux generator prescription in which a Gaussian or P\"oschl-Teller profile is retained as a generator of the bump pair rather than as the bump itself, and Riccati completion prescription in which a Gaussian or P\"oschl-Teller profile is regarded as one of the bump pair and its partner will be solved from the Riccati equation. Our frequency-domain calculations confirm that a finite and spurious axial-polar splitting of quasinormal modes will be produced if the Darboux-consistent scenarios are absent, while the centroid shift of the fundamental mode is nearly unchanged.

一句话导读：研究表明，把局域Gaussian/Pöschl-Teller隆起直接加到Regge-Wheeler和Zerilli势上会破坏两宇称之间的Darboux等谱联系，只有采用Darboux生成元或Riccati补全方案才能避免虚假的轴-极QNM劈裂。

相关度：medium — 论文研究经典史瓦西黑洞扰动中Regge-Wheeler/Zerilli势的隆起形变与Darboux等谱性，属于准正则模相关方法学研究，不涉及全息/量子引力，但与本对话中的QNM研究支线相关。

克尔环下沉降中地平线模式的精确抵消

Exact Cancellation of Horizon Modes in Kerr Ringdown

Kei-ichiro Kubota, Yusuke Manita, Hayato Motohashi, Daiki Watarai · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：环下沉降引力波通过准正规模探测黑洞时空。近期研究在滤除QNM的波形中发现了振荡特征，并引发关于它们是否代表近地平线“直达波”的相互矛盾诠释。我们重新考察粒子坠入Kerr黑洞的过程，证明了支撑该诠释的鞍点近似在近地平线附近失效。精确分析显示，每个源诱导的地平线模极点都被无限阶Matsubara零点独立于轨道和黑洞参数地抵消。时域数值积分也确认了这一顺序屏蔽过程的前四个阶段。因此，在近地平线微扰框架内，源诱导的地平线模信号在晚期不会存活。

English: Ringdown gravitational waves probe black hole spacetimes via quasinormal modes (QNMs). Recent studies have reported oscillatory features in QNM-filtered waveforms, and have led to conflicting interpretations as to whether they represent a near-horizon "direct wave". Reexamining a particle plunging into a Kerr black hole, we show that the saddle-point approximation underlying the proposed interpretation fails near the horizon. Our exact analysis reveals that every source-induced horizon-mode pole is canceled by an infinite tower of Matsubara zeros, independently of orbital and black-hole parameters. The first four stages of this sequential screening are also confirmed by time-domain numerical integration. Thus, within the near-horizon perturbative framework, source-induced horizon-mode signals do not survive at late times.

一句话导读：精确证明Kerr环下沉降中源诱导的地平线模被无限阶Matsubara零点抵消，因而在近地平线微扰框架内晚期不存活。

相关度：medium — 论文聚焦经典广义相对论中的Kerr黑洞环沉与QNM行为，不属于全息/凝聚态主线；但直接契合用户标签中的“经典引力/广义相对论”与“QNM与非线性演化”，因此具有中等相关性。

受测试场扰动的 Kerr-Taub-NUT 黑洞的广义第二定律

The generalized second law for Kerr-Taub-NUT black holes perturbed by test field

Koray D\"uzta\c{s}, Ahmet Cem Erdo\u{g}an · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们计算了受玻色子与费米子测试场扰动的 Kerr-Taub-NUT 黑洞的面积和熵的变化。我们证明，使面积变化为零的测试场临界频率严格大于 Kerr-Taub-NUT 黑洞的超辐射极限。因此，相关范围内的玻色子场以及不表现超辐射散射的费米子场均能导致 Kerr-Taub-NUT 黑洞面积下降。然而，对于 Kerr-Taub-NUT 黑洞，熵并不与面积直接成正比。只有频率低于超辐射极限的费米子场才能引起熵下降。黑洞与环境同时损失熵，意味着对于不满足零能量条件（NEC）的费米子扰动，广义第二定律（GSL）会发生普遍违反。对于满足 NEC 的玻色子扰动，我们评估了极限情形：近极端黑洞受到频率略高于超辐射极限的标量场扰动。我们的分析表明，若将冯·诺依曼熵赋予测试场，黑洞熵的最小增加恰好补偿环境的熵损失。因此，对于满足 NEC 的扰动，GSL 仍然成立。

English: We evaluate the variations in the area and the entropy of Kerr-Taub-NUT black holes perturbed by bosonic and fermionic test fields. We demonstrate that the critical frequency for a test field at which the variation of the area vanishes is strictly larger than the superradiance limit for Kerr-Taub-NUT black holes. Therefore both bosonic fields in the relevant range and fermionic fields which do not exhibit superradiant scattering can induce a decrease in the area of a Kerr-Taub-NUT black hole. However, the entropy is not directly proportional to the area for Kerr-Taub-NUT black holes. Only fermionic fields with frequencies below the superradiance limit can induce an entropy decrease. The simultaneous loss of entropy by the black hole and the environment identifies a generic violation of the Generalized Second Law (GSL) for fermionic perturbations that fail to satisfy the Null Energy Condition (NEC). For bosonic perturbations that satisfy the NEC, we evaluate the limiting case, where a nearly extremal black hole is perturbed by a scalar field with frequency slightly above the superradiance limit. Our analysis reveals that the minimum increase in the black hole entropy compensates for the entropy loss of the environment, when one assigns Von Neumann entropy to the test field. The GSL remains valid for perturbations satisfying the NEC.

一句话导读：研究 Kerr-Taub-NUT 黑洞在玻色子/费米子测试场扰动下的面积与熵变化，发现费米子可违反广义第二定律，而满足零能量条件的玻色子仍保持 GSL。

相关度：medium — 论文通过 Kerr-Taub-NUT 背景上的测试场研究黑洞熵和广义第二定律，属于半经典黑洞热力学；不涉及 AdS/CFT 对偶或全息模型，主要是黑洞/引力与信息基本问题，与经典引力分支有交集。

与度量-仿射几何非最小耦合的标量场

Scalar field with nonminimal couplings to metric-affine geometry

Ilaria Andrei, Damianos Iosifidis, Laur J\"arv, Margus Saal · gr-qc, hep-ph, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们研究了在仿射曲率为线性的作用量中与度量-仿射引力非最小耦合的标量场，作用量包含所有独立的、关于挠率和非度规的二次宇称偶与宇称奇项，包括混合收缩。我们还包含标量场导数与四个挠率和非度规矢量之间的类 Nieh-Yan 导数耦合。我们推导了联络、度规和标量场方程；由于联络方程是代数的，我们在一般非退化分支上消去独立联络，得到一个等价的度规标量-张量理论，其中非黎曼相互作用被编码在有效动能力函数中。我们对若干扇区进行了分类，发现纯二次非度规和纯二次挠率扇区分别使爱因斯坦框架动能力函数相对于最简单的度量-仿射标量-张量模型保持不变，而它们同时出现、导数耦合以及一般混合挠率-非度规相互作用可以修改它。在导数耦合扇区，射影一致性要求耦合之间满足一个关系。接着我们考虑多项式耦合函数，研究由此得到的正则场重定义和爱因斯坦框架势。特别地，我们说明了导数耦合和混合挠率-非度规耦合如何在正则场空间中重塑二次和四次势，并表明在适当选择导数耦合和非最小耦合时，二次约当框架势可以在正则归一化后被映射为自然暴胀势。最后，我们单独施加宇宙学原理，确定二次耦合和标量场超动量的约化组合，并在最简扇区中得到相应的修正宇宙学方程。

English: We study a scalar field nonminimally coupled to metric-affine gravity within actions linear in the affine curvature and containing all independent parity-even and parity-odd terms quadratic in torsion and nonmetricity, including mixed contractions. We also include Nieh-Yan-like derivative couplings between the scalar-field derivative and the four torsion and nonmetricity vectors. We derive the connection, metric, and scalar-field equations and, since the connection equation is algebraic, eliminate the independent connection on the generic nondegenerate branch to obtain an equivalent metric scalar-tensor theory in which the non-Riemannian interactions are encoded in an effective kinetic function. We classify several sectors and find that the pure quadratic nonmetricity and pure quadratic torsion sectors separately leave the Einstein-frame kinetic function unchanged relative to the simplest metric-affine scalar-tensor model, whereas their simultaneous presence, the derivative couplings, and generic mixed torsion-nonmetricity interactions can modify it. In the derivative-coupling sector, projective consistency imposes a relation among the couplings. We then consider polynomial coupling functions and study the resulting canonical field redefinition and Einstein-frame potentials. In particular, we illustrate how derivative and mixed torsion--nonmetricity couplings reshape quadratic and quartic potentials in canonical-field space, and show that a quadratic Jordan-frame potential can be mapped, for a suitable choice of derivative and nonminimal couplings, into a natural-inflation potential after canonical normalization. Finally, we separately impose the cosmological principle, determine the reduced combinations of quadratic couplings and the scalar field hypermomentum, and obtain the corresponding modified cosmological equations in the simplest sectors.

一句话导读：研究标量场与度量-仿射引力（含挠率、非度规及导数耦合）的非最小耦合，消去联络得到等效标量-张量理论并分析其势与宇宙学修正。

相关度：medium — 该文属于经典引力/修正引力与宇宙学，匹配用户重点标签中的经典引力/广义相对论支线，但与全息超流、QNM、冷原子等具体方向无直接重叠。

来自度量-仿射框架的相互作用流体宇宙学

Interacting fluid cosmologies from the metric-affine framework

Fotios K. Anagnostopoulos, Damianos Iosifidis · astro-ph.CO, gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：度量-仿射引力提供了一种自然的几何框架，其中时空曲率、挠率和非度量性被视为独立的自由度，从而产生超越广义相对论的新宇宙学动力学。这类理论的一个普遍结果是，即使没有显式的唯象耦合，宇宙流体之间也会出现有效相互作用。有趣的是，这发生在标准粒子物理模型保持不变的条件下。本文研究了由度量-仿射引力产生的相互作用宇宙模型，并分析了它们对宇宙背景演化的影响。我们推导了支配物质、辐射和暗能量分量的修正连续性方程，强调了暗区能量交换的几何起源。我们讨论了这些相互作用如何修改膨胀历史，并可能有效模拟暗能量的演化行为。所得的宇宙学场景与最新的观测数据（Ia型超新星Pantheon+、宇宙计时器和重子声学振荡DESI DR2）进行了比较。此外，利用模型选择准则如赤池信息准则、贝叶斯信息准则和贝叶斯证据，我们将上述模型与标准ΛCDM模型进行了比较。我们发现，在所考虑的数据集下，所提出的框架表现略好于ΛCDM。我们的分析表明，度量-仿射诱导的相互作用是对宇宙描述的一个可行且具有理论动机的替代方案。

English: Metric-affine gravity provides a natural geometric framework in which spacetime curvature, torsion, and non-metricity are treated as independent degrees of freedom, leading to novel cosmological dynamics beyond General Relativity. A generic consequence of such theories is the emergence of effective interactions among the cosmological fluids, even in the absence of explicit phenomenological couplings. Interestingly, the above happens while the Standard Model of Particle physics remains unmodified. In this work, we investigate interacting cosmological models that arise from metric-affine gravity and analyze their implications for the background evolution of the Universe. We derive the modified continuity equations governing the matter, radiation, and dark-energy components, highlighting the geometric origin of energy exchange in the dark sector. We discuss how these interactions modify the expansion history and can effectively mimic evolving dark-energy behavior. The resulting cosmological scenarios are confronted with the most recent observational data from Type Ia Supernovae (Pantheon+), Cosmic Chronometers, and Baryon Acoustic Oscillations (DESI DR2). Moreover, using model selection criteria, e.g., Akaike Information Criterion, Bayesian Information Criterion and the Bayesian Evidence, we compare the aforementioned models against the standard $\Lambda$CDM model. We find that the proposed framework performs slightly better than $\Lambda CDM$, in the light of the data sets considered here. Our analysis demonstrates that metric-affine-induced interactions constitute a viable and theoretically motivated alternative for the description of the Universe.

一句话导读：该工作表明度量-仿射引力可自然产生流体间相互作用，并作为暗能量行为的几何起源，且在与观测数据对比中略优于ΛCDM。

相关度：low — 论文属于经典修正引力和宇宙学观测研究，与用户主要关注的全息、凝聚态类比或黑洞量子信息等主题联系较弱，但属于经典引力/广义相对论的宽泛范畴。

和乐修正黑洞周围中微子动力学中的量子几何特征

Quantum geometric signatures in neutrino dynamics around a holonomy black hole

A. A. Ara\'{u}jo Filho · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们研究了有效和乐修正史瓦西几何中的三味中微子现象。推导了直至二阶后闵可夫斯基阶的弱偏转角，获得了径向和非径向轨迹上的半经典相位，并通过包含放大因子、Fermat 相位和波包重叠构造了双像味概率。交叉路径相位获得对数级和乐修正，并可保留绝对中微子质量标度的信息。我们还通过路径可预测性、干涉可见度和 I-concurrence 之间的平衡，刻画了味模式关联和轨迹-味纠缠。此外，研究了有效中微子球外的中微子-反中微子湮灭，和乐效应通过径向固有体积测度进入积分功率。对于太阳质量透镜和 10 MeV 中微子，数值结果显示振荡条纹发生位移，并且电子、缪子和陶子道之间的分布出现几何依赖的重分配。电子中微子的轨迹-味 concurrence 达到约 0.23，熵接近 0.10 bit，同时在所选构型中对质量排序的敏感性较弱。相比之下，湮灭功率单调增加，相对于史瓦西时空达到约 39.3% 的增强（对特定构型，即 λ=2 且 M/R_ν=1/3）。此外，在固定源参数和 M/R_ν=1/3 时，假设相对于史瓦西的积分湮灭功率最大超额为 10%，可得到条件约束 a/(2M)≤0.2879 和 |λ|≤0.6358。

English: We investigate three flavor neutrino phenomena in an effective holonomy corrected Schwarzschild geometry. We derive the weak-deflection angle through second post-Minkowskian order, obtain the semiclassical phases along radial and nonradial trajectories, and formulate the two-image flavor probability by including magnifications, Fermat phases, and wave packet overlap. The cross path phase acquires a logarithmic holonomy contribution and can retain information about the absolute neutrino mass scale. We also characterize flavor mode correlations and trajectory-flavor entanglement through the balance among path predictability, interference visibility, and I-concurrence. Neutrino-antineutrino annihilation outside an effective neutrinosphere is examined, with holonomy entering the integrated power through the radial proper volume measure. For a solar mass lens and $10\,\mathrm{MeV}$ neutrinos, the numerical results show displaced oscillation fringes and a geometry dependent redistribution among the electron, muon, and tau channels. The electron-neutrino trajectory-flavor concurrence reaches approximately $0.23$, with an entropy near $0.10$ bit, while remaining weakly sensitive to the mass ordering in the selected configuration. By contrast, the annihilation power increases monotonically, reaching an increase of approximately $39.3\%$ relative to Schwarzschild spacetime (for a particular configuration, i.e., $\lambda=2$ and $M/R_{\nu}=1/3$). Furthermore, at fixed source parameters and $M/R_\nu=1/3$, an assumed maximum excess of $10\%$ in the integrated annihilation power relative to Schwarzschild yields the conditional bounds $a/(2M)\leq0.2879$ and $|\lambda|\leq0.6358$.

一句话导读：在有效和乐修正史瓦西时空中研究中微子弱引力透镜、味振荡、轨迹-味纠缠以及湮灭功率增强，并给出对模型参数的约束。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论支线，但与用户关注的全息、凝聚态、QCD 等主方向关联较弱；列为经典引力参考即可。

流体动力学吸引子

Hydrodynamic attractors

Tilman Enss, Michal P. Heller, Alexandre Serantes, Clemens Werthmann · nucl-th, cond-mat.quant-gas, hep-ph, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：吸引子是在选定可观测量中定义的有效低维结构，来自不同初始状态的轨迹会趋近它；流体动力学吸引子指其晚期演化由流体动力学本构关系控制。受核碰撞和超冷原子气体启发，本章区分吸引子化（对初始状态空间某些方向敏感性丧失）与流体动力学化（流体动力学本构关系开始适用）。主要利用共形、boost不变的Bjorken流，我们比较Müller-Israel-Stewart类理论、动理学理论、全息和经典Yang-Mills场。由非流体动力学模式衰减产生的正向吸引区别于可挑选唯一正则解的回拉吸引，以及可在微观弛豫前压制初始状态敏感性的膨胀驱动吸引，后者可有或没有后续流体阶段。发散梯度展开及其超级数补全、状态空间图像和绝热流体动力学化提供互补描述。对核碰撞的应用包括粒子产生、横能量与流、吸引背景的初始化与线性响应、喷注淬火以及由吸引子启发的流体动力学模型修正。对超冷Fermi气体，我们评述散射长度驱动下体通道吸引子的理论提议，并将其与近期接触和动量分布动力学的短时测量相区分。

English: Attractors are effective low-dimensional structures, defined in a chosen set of observables, that trajectories from different initial states approach; hydrodynamic attractors are those on which the late-time evolution is governed by hydrodynamic constitutive relations. Motivated by nuclear collisions and ultracold atomic gases, this chapter distinguishes attractorization (the loss of sensitivity to some directions in the space of initial states) from hydrodynamization (the onset of validity of hydrodynamic constitutive relations). Using mainly conformal, boost-invariant Bjorken flow, we compare M\"uller-Israel-Stewart-type theories, kinetic theory, holography, and classical Yang-Mills fields. Forward attraction, produced by the decay of non-hydrodynamic modes, is distinguished from pullback attraction, which can select a unique regular solution, and from expansion-driven attraction, which can suppress initial-state sensitivity before microscopic relaxation, with or without a subsequent fluid regime. Divergent gradient expansions and their transseries completions, the state-space picture, and adiabatic hydrodynamization provide complementary descriptions. Applications to nuclear collisions include particle production, transverse energy and flow, the initialization of and linear response around attracting backgrounds, jet quenching, and attractor-informed modifications of hydrodynamic models. For ultracold Fermi gases, we review theoretical proposals for bulk-channel attractors under scattering-length drives and distinguish them from recent measurements of short-time contact and momentum-distribution dynamics.

一句话导读：综述流体动力学吸引子的定义、机制（正向/回拉/膨胀驱动）及其在核碰撞和超冷Fermi气体中的应用。

相关度：medium — 该文涉及非平衡热化与全息比较，并讨论超冷Fermi气体，与用户关注的冷原子/凝聚态实验启发和非平衡演化部分相关，但不涉及全息超流、探针膜或涡旋等主线。

来自 Virasoro TQFT 的三维 N=1 超引力：引力配分函数与离时有序关联子

3D $\mathcal{N}=1$ supergravity from Virasoro TQFT: Gravitational partition function and Out-of-time-order correlator

Arpan Bhattacharyya, Saptaswa Ghosh, Poulami Nandi, Sounak Pal · hep-th, gr-qc, math-ph, math.MP, quant-ph · v3 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：本文利用超 Virasoro TQFT 计算了不同边界拓扑下（带孔球面和环面）N=1 超引力的配分函数。我们使用超 Liouville 理论的融合核与模核来计算项链道共形块，并通过证明超共形块（作为希尔伯特空间中的态）的内积成立来展示该形式体系。最后，我们使用超 Virasoro TQFT 的工具计算了环面拓扑下带有超共形初级插入作为物质的离时有序关联子（OTOC）。

English: In this paper, we compute the partition functions of $\mathcal{N}=1$ SUGRA for different boundary topologies, i.e. \textcolor{black}{punctured sphere} and torus, using super-Virasoro TQFT. We use fusion and modular kernels of the super-Liouville theory to compute the necklace-channel conformal block and showcase formalism by proving that the inner product holds for superconformal blocks, defined as states in the Hilbert space. Finally, we compute the out-of-time-order correlator for the torus topology with superconformal primary insertions as matter using the tools of super-Virasoro TQFT.

引力常数作为黑洞热力学中的守恒荷

Gravitational constant as a conserved charge in black hole thermodynamics

Wontae Kim, Mungon Nam · hep-th, gr-qc · v3 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：近期工作表明，通过引入标量-规范对，拉格朗日量中各项的耦合常数可以被提升为守恒荷。然而，引力常数扮演着性质上不同的角色：$G^{-1}$ 是爱因斯坦-希尔伯特部分的整体归一化因子，而非某一单独项的系数。在本文中，我们证明引力常数仍然可以在一个修正的四维爱因斯坦-希尔伯特理论中被实现为守恒荷。利用两对标量-规范对以及准局域离壳 Abbott-Deser-Tekin 形式体系，我们构造了与质量、宇宙学常数和引力常数相关的守恒荷。所得的荷分配以完全自洽的方式给出了推广的热力学第一定律和 Smarr 公式。因此，我们的结果将耦合常数的守恒荷诠释扩展到了引力耦合本身，在一个具体的四维爱因斯坦引力框架中得以实现。

English: Recent work has shown that couplings multiplying individual terms in a Lagrangian can be promoted to conserved charges by introducing scalar-gauge pairs. The gravitational constant, however, plays a qualitatively different role: $G^{-1}$ appears as an overall normalization of the Einstein-Hilbert sector rather than as the coefficient of a single term. In this paper, we show that the gravitational constant can nevertheless be realized as a conserved charge in a modified four-dimensional Einstein-Hilbert theory. Using two scalar-gauge pairs and the quasi-local off-shell Abbott-Deser-Tekin formalism, we construct the conserved charges associated with the mass, the cosmological constant, and the gravitational constant. The resulting charge assignment yields the extended thermodynamic first law and the Smarr formula in a fully consistent manner. Our result therefore extends the conserved-charge interpretation of couplings to the universal gravitational coupling itself in a concrete four-dimensional Einstein-gravity setting.

一句话导读：将引力常数本身实现为修正爱因斯坦-希尔伯特理论中的守恒荷，并导出扩展热力学第一定律和 Smarr 公式。

相关度：high — 论文核心是经典引力与黑洞热力学，与用户重点标签中的'经典引力/广义相对论'直接匹配。

自旋Mellin振幅

Spinning Mellin amplitudes

Zhongjie Huang, Yichao Tang · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们提出了一个定义，用于涉及洛伦兹群张量表示中任意自旋算符的共形关联函数的Mellin振幅。这些表示覆盖所有玻色子局域算符。我们的策略是对所有涉及极化矢量的标量积进行离散Mellin变换，使得每个极化矢量可以被解释为一个虚构标量算符的位置。我们确立了这些自旋Mellin振幅的一般极点结构和因子化性质。我们还提供了一种系统算法来推导具有任意自旋交换的因子化公式，得到了直到自旋4的新显式结果。为了说明我们形式主义的实用性，我们在四维N=2超共形场论中引导了3点和4点流关联子，它们对偶于AdS5×S3中的胶子散射振幅。结果与文献中6点和8点标量超胶子振幅的雪花道一致。

English: We propose a definition of Mellin amplitudes for conformal correlators involving arbitrary spinning operators in tensor representations of the Lorentz group. These representations cover all bosonic local operators. Our strategy is to perform discrete Mellin transforms on all scalar products involving polarization vectors, so that each polarization vector can be interpreted as the position of a fictitious scalar operator. We establish the general pole structures and factorization properties of these spinning Mellin amplitudes. We also provide a systematic algorithm to derive factorization formulas with arbitrary spinning exchanges, yielding new explicit results up to spin-4. To illustrate the practicality of our formalism, we bootstrap the 3- and 4-point current correlators in a 4d $\mathcal{N}=2$ superconformal field theory, which are dual to gluon scattering amplitudes in $\mathrm{AdS}_5 \times \mathrm{S}^3$. The results agree with the snowflake channel of 6- and 8-point scalar supergluon amplitudes in the literature.

一句话导读：提出一种适用于任意自旋玻色子算符共形关联函数的Mellin振幅定义，并给出其极点结构与因子化算法，在超共形场论/AdS胶子散射中验证。

相关度：low — 论文聚焦于AdS/CFT中自旋算符的Mellin振幅形式化，与用户关注的全息超导、涡旋、相变等主题无直接关联。

高自旋全息中的BPS相与偶然性

BPS phases and fortuity in higher spin holography

Seok Kim, Jehyun Lee, Siyul Lee, Hyunwoo Oh · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们研究了球面上U(N)_k × U(1)_{−k}矢量Chern-Simons理论在弱耦合λ=N/k≪1下的BPS态，该理论对偶于AdS_4高自旋引力。众所周知，高自旋流在λ≠0时具有反常。我们证明，这些非BPS高自旋粒子在低能下形成多粒子“BPS束缚”，并将其解释为一种原始形式的小黑洞态。我们还构造了N=2时一个新的重BPS算子。我们通过大N指数在Planck尺度‘温度’下研究该系统的BPS相。高温下的退禁闭鞍点仅存在于某一阈值之上，类似于BTZ黑洞。低温鞍点由新颖的双割(2-cut)特征值分布给出。它们的相变涉及诸如全纯反常和背景独立性等微妙问题，我们对此展开了研究。特别地，通过研究特征值瞬子，我们得到了临界温度的下界。

English: We study the BPS states of $U(N)_k\times U(1)_{-k}$ vector Chern-Simons theory on a sphere at weak coupling $\lambda=\frac{N}{k}\ll 1$, dual to an AdS$_4$ higher spin gravity. Higher spin currents are well known to be anomalous at $\lambda\neq 0$. We show that these non-BPS higher spin particles form multi-particle `BPS bounds' at low energy, and interpret them as a primordial form of small black hole states. We also construct a new heavy BPS operator at $N=2$. We study the BPS phases of this system from the large $N$ index at Planckian `temperatures'. The deconfined saddles at high temperature exist only above a threshold, similar to the BTZ black holes. The low temperature saddles are given by novel 2-cut eigenvalue distributions. Their phase transition involves subtle issues like the holomorphic anomaly and the background independence, whose studies we initiate. In particular, we obtain a lower bound on the critical temperature by studying the eigenvalue instantons.

一句话导读：研究了高自旋全息背景下矢量Chern-Simons理论中的BPS相与相变，将非BPS高自旋粒子的多粒子束缚态解释为小黑洞态的原始形式，并分析了高温鞍点的阈值行为及低温双割特征值分布。

相关度：low — 论文主题为高自旋全息与AdS4/CFT3中的BPS态和相结构，与给定重点研究支线（全息超导、经典引力、QNM等）无直接交集，只有一般性的全息相变概念相关。

全息界面耗散的普适性

Universality of Dissipation across Holographic Interfaces

Andreas Karch, Mianqi Wang · hep-th · v3 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：受近期自旋链研究结果的启发，我们研究了一个二维全息界面共形场论（ICFT）中的耗散与弛豫，其中界面一侧的自由度耦合到外部热库，而另一侧保持隔离。在体描述中，该设置通过将超对称Janus几何与BTZ黑洞区域粘合来实现，耦合通过双迹形变实现。我们通过求解系统与热库的双迹匹配条件来确定体内的拟正规模。关注大频率下的固定AdS₂后代通道，我们引入无量纲比值c_UV作为界面引起的耗散率抑制的投影度量。解析上我们发现，在这一极限下，c_UV独立于与热库的耦合细节。它是描述界面上耗散与弛豫的普适界面观测量，是早期格点模型中提出的c_relax之外的一个有力候选可观测量。

English: Motivated by recent results in spin chains we study dissipation and relaxation in a two-dimensional \ak{holographic} interface conformal field theory (ICFT) in which degrees of freedom on one side of the interface are coupled to an external bath, while the other side remains isolated. In the bulk description this setup is realized by gluing a supersymmetric Janus geometry to a BTZ black hole region, with the coupling implemented through a double-trace deformation. We determine the quasinormal modes in the bulk by solving the double-trace matching conditions of the system and bath. Focusing on the fixed AdS$_2$ descendant channel at large frequency, we introduce the dimensionless ratio $c_{UV}$ as a projected measure of interface-induced suppression of dissipation rate. Analytically we find that in this limit, $c_{UV}$ is independent of coupling details to the bath. It is a strong candidate for a universal interface observable characterizing dissipation and relaxation across the interface, in additional to the $c_{\rm relax}$ raised in earlier work for lattice models.

一句话导读：研究了全息界面CFT中耗散的普适性，发现高频率极限下界面耗散抑制因子不依赖于热库耦合细节。

相关度：medium — 论文涉及全息界面、双迹形变和拟正规模，属于全息QNM/耗散研究方向，但不是最直接的重点标签如全息超导或相变气泡。

杨-米尔斯理论与引力中的天球能量-能量关联

Celestial energy-energy correlation in Yang-Mills theory and gravity

HongYi Ruan, YiZhe Zheng, Hua Xing Zhu · hep-th, hep-ph · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们引入了天球能量-能量关联子(cEEC)，这是一种红外与共线安全可观测，使得四维散射的天球共形对称性变得显明。cEEC被定义为在boost本征态上测得的平均零能量算子的关联函数，形式为天球上虚构的二维共形场论中的四点函数。cEEC的一个重要特征是它能够平滑插值于微扰规范理论和引力的不同关键区域之间，例如共线极限、Sudakov极限和Regge极限。我们在N=4超杨-米尔斯理论、纯杨-米尔斯理论、爱因斯坦引力以及N=8超引力中计算了cEEC到首个非平凡阶。在N=8超引力中，cEEC由天球对称性和边界数据唯一确定，表明自举方法能够为这类可观测量产生闭式结果。

English: We introduce the celestial energy-energy correlator (cEEC), an infrared and collinear safe observable that makes the celestial conformal symmetry of four-dimensional scattering manifest. The cEEC is defined as a correlation function of average null energy operators measured on boost eigenstates and takes the form of a four-point function in a fictitious two-dimensional conformal field theory on the celestial sphere. An important feature of the cEEC is that it smoothly interpolates between different key regimes of perturbative gauge theory and gravity, such as the collinear limit, the Sudakov limit, and the Regge limit. We compute the cEEC to the first nontrivial order in $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills theory, pure Yang-Mills theory, Einstein gravity, and $\mathcal{N}=8$ supergravity. In $\mathcal{N}=8$ supergravity, the cEEC is uniquely determined by celestial symmetries and boundary data, demonstrating that bootstrap methods can yield closed-form results for this class of observables.

一句话导读：引入天球能量-能量关联子(cEEC)，在杨-米尔斯理论与引力中计算，并展示其在N=8超引力中由对称性唯一确定。

相关度：low — 论文属于天球全息与散射振幅方向，与用户重点关注的超流超导、QNM、经典引力等标签无直接交集。

Anti-de Sitter背景中黑洞弦的经典双重复制

Classical double copy of black strings in an Anti-de Sitter background

G. Alencar, C. R. Muniz, M. S. Oliveira · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们研究了Anti-de Sitter（AdS）背景中静态黑洞弦解的经典双重复制。通过将黑洞弦度规写成柱形AdS几何上的Kerr-Schild形式，我们构造了相应的单重拷贝和零重拷贝。单重拷贝描述了一个满足类Maxwell方程并由有效色电荷线源产生的规范场，而零重拷贝则由一个与AdS背景共形耦合的标量场给出。我们还将分析扩展到带电黑洞弦，识别了规范扇区中的相关修正。这些结果表明，经典双重复制一致地适用于弯曲时空中的延展引力物体。

English: We study the classical double copy for static black string solutions in an Anti--de Sitter (AdS) background. By casting the black string metric into Kerr--Schild form over a cylindrical AdS geometry, we construct the corresponding single and zeroth copies. The single copy describes a gauge field satisfying Maxwell-like equations and sourced by an effective line of color charge, while the zeroth copy is given by a scalar field conformally coupled to the AdS background. We also extend the analysis to charged black strings, identifying the associated modifications in the gauge sector. These results show that the classical double copy consistently applies to extended gravitational objects in curved spacetimes.

一句话导读：在AdS背景下研究黑洞弦的经典双重复制，构造单重与零重拷贝，证明其适用于弯曲时空的延展引力物体。

引力散射中守恒定律的证明：尾项与 peeling 的破缺

A proof of conservation laws in gravitational scattering: tails and breaking of peeling

Geoffrey Comp\`ere, S\'ebastien Robert · hep-th, gr-qc, math-ph, math.MP · v4 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们提出一类渐近平直时空的定义，该定义与零无穷远处和引力散射、入射与出射辐射以及与物质相互作用的已知性质相容。对于这类时空，我们证明了空间无穷远处的三个对径匹配条件：一个针对所谓的对偶质量展布（dual mass aspect），一个针对剪切的领头尾项，另一个则非平凡地将两个零无穷远处的时空 peeling 性质与空间无穷远处的领头尾项和质量展布联系起来。此外，我们将这些恒等式重新表述为定义在空间无穷远处边界双曲面上的守恒律与通量平衡律。

English: We propose a definition of asymptotically flat spacetimes that is consistent with both null infinities and compatible with known properties of gravitational scattering, incoming and outgoing radiation, and interactions with matter. For this class of spacetimes, we prove three antipodal matching conditions at spatial infinity: one for the so-called dual mass aspect, one for the leading tail of the shear, and one that non-trivially relates the peeling properties of the spacetime at both null infinities to the leading tail and mass aspect at spatial infinity. Furthermore, we reformulate these identities as conservation and flux balance laws defined on the boundary hyperboloid at spatial infinity.

一句话导读：提出与辐射和物质耦合相洽的渐近平直时空类，证明空间无穷远处三个对径匹配条件，并将它们化为守恒律/通量平衡律。

Wilson network expansion for four-point contact and exchange scalar Feynman diagrams in AdS$_2$

K. B. Alkalaev, V. S. Khiteev · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们推导了AdS传播子的新积分恒等式，并进一步发展了AdS费曼图的Wilson网络展开。特别地，我们证明了二维四点接触图和交换标量图可以展开为若干无穷级数，这些级数是具有流动共形权重的Wilson线网络算子的矩阵元。每个级数由与对应图的外部和中间边相关的特定multi-trace算子所刻画。在共形边界附近的展开结果重现了相应四点Witten图分解为共形块的已知形式。

English: We derive new integral identities for AdS propagators and further develop the Wilson network expansion for AdS Feynman diagrams. In particular, we demonstrate that four-point contact and exchange scalar diagrams in two dimensions can be expanded into several infinite series of matrix elements of Wilson line network operators with running conformal weights. Each series is characterized by specific multi-trace operators associated with the external and intermediate edges of the corresponding graphs. The resulting expansions near the conformal boundary reproduce the well-known decompositions of the corresponding four-point Witten diagrams into conformal blocks.

一句话导读：提出了AdS_2中Wilson网络展开方法，将四点标量费曼图表示为multi-trace相关Wilson线算符的无穷级数，其边界展开与共形块分解一致。

全息纠缠熵、Wilson环与神经网络

Holographic entanglement entropy, Wilson loops, and neural networks

Veselin G. Filev · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们将人工神经网络应用于全息逆问题，利用Ryu-Takayanagi面积泛函作为可微损失函数，从边界纠缠熵重构bulk几何。在AdS-Schwarzschild背景上验证，该方法恢复覆盖整个bulk的黑度因子，最大绝对误差低于3e-3，且在多个独立训练运行中可重复。对于Gubser-Rocha等有限密度背景，我们证明了等时条带纠缠熵仅确定空间度量。通过引入与时间度量耦合的全息Wilson环数据，我们解决了这个精确的一函数简并。我们提出了结合Bilson和Hashimoto公式的半解析反演，以及一种广义的三网络变分方法，最小化面积与Nambu-Goto作用之和。神经网络对两个度量函数的最大相对误差均低于0.2%，无需闭式导数关系，并可通过增加一个网络和损失项容纳额外的全息观测量。

English: We apply artificial neural networks to the holographic inverse problem, reconstructing bulk geometry from boundary entanglement entropy by using the Ryu--Takayanagi area functional as a differentiable loss. Validated on the AdS-Schwarzschild background, this approach recovers the blackening factor with maximum absolute error below $3\times10^{-3}$ across the entire bulk, reproducibly over independent training runs. For finite-density backgrounds like the Gubser--Rocha model, we demonstrate that equal-time strip entanglement entropy determines only the spatial metric. We resolve this exact one-function degeneracy by incorporating holographic Wilson loop data, which couples to the timelike metric. We present a semi-analytical inversion combining Bilson's and Hashimoto's formulas, alongside a general three-network variational method minimizing the combined area and Nambu--Goto actions. The neural network achieves maximum relative errors below $0.2\%$ for both metric functions without closed-form derivative relations, and accommodates additional holographic observables at the cost of one extra network and loss term.

一句话导读：利用神经网络从全息纠缠熵和Wilson环数据重构bulk时空几何。

基本正交-辛角落的四分之一指标

Quarter-indices for basic ortho-symplectic corners

Yasuyuki Hatsuda, Tadashi Okazaki · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们研究了4维 N=4 超杨-米尔斯理论中具有正交和辛规范群的角落构型的超对称四分之一指标。对于基本 Y 型结点，利用 Gustafson 型积分公式和 Higgsing 方法，我们获得了指标的精确闭式表达式。我们证明了对偶构型之间四分之一指标的相等性，为角落构型的 S 对偶性提供了证据。在特殊的 fugacity 极限下，这些指标可以解释为 BCD 型 W 代数的真空特征，以及作为角落顶点算子代数的李超代数 osp(1|2N) 的真空特征。

English: We study supersymmetric quarter-indices for corner configurations in 4d $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills theory with orthogonal and symplectic gauge groups. For the basic Y-junctions, we obtain exact closed-form expressions for the indices by making use of the Gustafson type integral formula and the Higgsing method. We demonstrate the equality of the quarter-indices between dual configurations, providing evidence for S-duality of the corner configurations. In the special fugacity limit, the indices admit an interpretation in terms of the vacuum characters of the W-algebras of type BCD, and the Lie superalgebra $\mathfrak{osp}(1|2N)$ as the corner vertex operator algebras.

一句话导读：推导了正交/辛规范群 N=4 SYM 角落构型的四分之一指标闭式表达式，验证了 S 对偶，并在特殊极限下联系到 W 代数和 osp(1|2N) 真空特征。

相关度：low — 摘要内容与用户重点标签没有直接匹配：研究的是4d N=4 SYM 中角落构型的超对称指标与S-对偶，不涉及全息超流超导、探针膜、相变、经典引力等支线。

Kerr-Newman-NUT-AdS$_4$时空的多毛热力学

Multihair thermodynamics of Kerr-Newman-NUT-AdS$_4$ spacetimes

Di Wu, Shuang-Qing Wu · hep-th, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：本文发展了一种Lorentzian Kerr-Newman-NUT-AdS$_4$时空的多毛热力学描述，其中旋转、电荷、AdS压力和极向锥形张力进入一个齐次的Christodoulou-Ruffini型平方质量公式。NUT贡献由旋转类次级毛$J_n=mn/K^2$和电荷类次级毛$N=n/\sqrt K$描述，两者依赖于物理解族的度规参数，但在扩展的热力学状态空间中独立变化。质量公式在单一焓表示中导出了第一定律和Smarr关系，并采用了面积熵和Hawking温度。Kerr-Newman-AdS、渐近平坦的Kerr-Newman-NUT和无旋转的RN-NUT-AdS极限检验了角归一化以及NUT和压力的贡献。我们还考虑了替代的NUT变量（包括共形对偶质量），并推导了热力学体积和锥形缺陷长度的相应变化。该描述适用于所选的齐次状态空间，不需要次级毛成为独立的渐近或Misner-弦表面荷。

English: In this paper, we develop a multihair thermodynamic description of Lorentzian Kerr-Newman-NUT-AdS$_4$ spacetime in which rotation, electric charge, AdS pressure and polar conical tensions enter a homogeneous Christodoulou-Ruffini-type squared-mass formula. The NUT contribution is described by a rotation-like secondary hair $J_n=mn/K^2$ and a charge-like secondary hair $N=n/\sqrt K$. Both depend on the metric parameters of the physical solution family but are varied independently in the enlarged thermodynamic state space. The mass formula yields the first law and Smarr relation in a single enthalpy representation, with the area entropy and Hawking temperature. The Kerr-Newman-AdS, asymptotically flat Kerr-Newman-NUT and nonrotating RN-NUT-AdS limits check the angular normalization and the NUT and pressure contributions. We also consider alternative NUT variables, including the conformal dual mass, and derive the corresponding changes in thermodynamic volume and conical-defect lengths. This description applies to the chosen homogeneous state space and does not require the secondary hairs to be independent asymptotic or Misner-string surface charges.

一句话导读：对Kerr-Newman-NUT-AdS时空建立了包含旋转、电荷、AdS压力和锥形张力的多毛热力学描述，得到齐次平方质量公式及第一定律和Smarr关系。

相关度：medium — 该论文属于经典黑洞热力学，与用户关注的经典引力/广义相对论相关，但与全息、凝聚态、相变等特定支线关联不紧密。

鞍点不相容性在重局域淬火中产生真实多熵

Saddle Incompatibility Generates Genuine Multientropy in Heavy Local Quenches

Kosei Fujiki, Kenya Tasuki · hep-th, cond-mat.stat-mech, quant-ph · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们研究 AdS3/CFT2 中重局域淬火下三部分真实多熵的时间演化。其围绕真空的线性响应在任意相邻区间上逐点相消。在有限反作用下，真空减除的真实多熵仅来自三价测地线网络所选绕数三元组与二分测地线独立选择的绕数之间的不相容间隙。重-轻真空块计算从边界视角得到了相同公式。时间依赖在运动学上固定，且不依赖于重算符维数。这些结果表明，真正的多体纠缠由全局鞍点跃迁控制，而非局域能量响应或准粒子传播。

English: We study the time evolution of tripartite genuine multientropy in heavy local quenches via AdS${}_3$/CFT${}_2$. Its linear response around the vacuum cancels pointwise for arbitrary adjacent intervals. At finite backreaction, the vacuum-subtracted genuine multientropy arises only from an incompatibility gap between the triplet of windings selected by the trivalent geodesic network and the windings selected independently by the bipartite geodesics. The heavy-light vacuum-block calculation reproduces the same formula from the boundary perspective. The time dependence is kinematically fixed and is independent of the heavy operator dimension. These results show that the genuine multipartite entanglement is controlled by global saddle transition rather than by local energy response or quasiparticle propagation.

一句话导读：该文通过 AdS3/CFT2 重局域淬火模型，证明真实多熵的产生源于三体与二体测地线所选鞍点绕数的不相容性，揭示多体纠缠受全局鞍点跃迁控制。

相关度：medium — 该文涉及全息纠缠度量与动力学淬火，属于广义全息量子信息方向，与用户重点支线无直接匹配，但可作为相关背景。

有效Lifshitz-Born-Infeld黑洞：来自广义协变性破缺

Effective Lifshitz-Born-Infeld black holes from general covariance breaking

D. C. Moreira, A. S. Lemos, F. A. Brito · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：在这项工作中，我们提出了一个带有Born-Infeld电动力学的有效Lifshitz黑洞解，并探讨了它的一些性质。我们讨论了通过破缺微分同胚不变性来获得该解的机制，研究了涌现的因果结构，并分析了相关热力学中的临界行为和局域稳定性。

English: In this work we present an effective Lifshitz black hole solution with Born-Infeld electrodynamics and explore some of its properties. We discuss the mechanism for capturing the solution, achieved through diffeomorphism invariance breaking, study the emergent causal structure, and analyze aspects of critical behavior and local stability in the associated thermodynamics.

五维带电荷且旋转的近水平几何

Charged and rotating near-horizon geometries in five dimensions

Alex Colling, Jun Liu · hep-th, gr-qc · v3 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们以闭合解析形式给出了五维爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦理论中新的带电荷且旋转的近水平几何。这些解可由电荷和两个独立角动量参数化。我们还将这些近水平几何推广到作用量中含任意耦合常数的附加陈-西蒙斯项的理论。新解具有与带电荷极端迈尔斯-佩里黑洞及旋转极端赖斯纳-诺德斯特伦-唐赫利尼黑洞预期相同的熵关系，但在真空极限下它们并不约化为迈尔斯-佩里视界。水平截面为球形并具有Sasakian结构。我们利用该结构证明了我们解的一个特征：在没有任何对称性假设的情况下，它们是共转电场为非零常数的极值旋转视界中最一般的解。我们进一步将该构造推广到更高维，证明任何Sasaki-Einstein流形都会生成一个双参数族的带电荷且旋转的视界。

English: We present new charged and rotating near-horizon geometries in five-dimensional Einstein-Maxwell theory in closed analytic form. The solutions can be parametrised by the charge and two independent angular momenta. We also generalise these near-horizon geometries to theories with an additional Chern-Simons term in the action multiplied by an arbitrary coupling constant. The new solutions have the same entropy relations as expected for charged versions of extremal Myers-Perry black holes and for rotating versions of extremal Reissner-Nordstr\"om-Tangherlini black holes, but they do not reduce to the Myers-Perry horizon in the vacuum limit. The horizon cross-sections are spherical and carry a Sasakian structure. We exploit this structure to prove a characterisation of our solutions: without any symmetry assumptions, they are the most general rotating extremal horizons for which the co-rotating electric field is a (non-zero) constant. We further extend this construction to higher dimensions, where we show that any Sasaki-Einstein manifold generates a two-parameter family of charged and rotating horizons.

一句话导读：在五维爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦理论中构造了新的带电荷且旋转的极端近水平几何，并推广至含陈-西蒙斯项及更高维的Sasaki-Einstein流形。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论中极端黑洞近水平几何的精确构造，与用户重点标签中的‘经典引力/广义相对论’直接相关，但未涉及全息或QNM等支线。

论被辐射包围的黑洞：I. 经典考量

On Black Holes Surrounded by Radiation: I. Classical Considerations

Marcos Riojas, Matthew J. Strassler · hep-th, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们考虑爱因斯坦方程中球对称静态解，描述一个被厚层径向压力为零的轨道运动无质量粒子包围的Schwarzschild黑洞。该轨道气体超致密且超相对论，可视为稳定Einstein簇的临界稳定极限。这些解被称为“hillingar黑洞”，将光子球扩展到任意深度的区域。我们将这些天体与其他气体包围的黑洞比较，并指出它们在经典层面上具有许多特殊性质；特别是，对于无穷远观测者而言，它们在外观上与普通黑洞在光学上不可区分。我们推测这些天体（或类似天体）是否可能存在于自然界，以及尽管它们与普通黑洞外观相似，是否仍可能被观测到。其热力学与稳定性在姊妹篇中研究。

English: We consider spherically symmetric static solutions to Einstein's equations describing a Schwarzschild black hole enveloped by a thick shell of orbiting massless particles with zero radial pressure. The orbiting gas is ultra-compact and ultra-relativistic, and can be viewed as the marginally stable limit of a stable Einstein cluster. These solutions, which we refer to as "hillingar black holes", extend the photon sphere into a region of arbitrary depth. We compare these objects to black holes surrounded by other gases and note they have numerous special properties at the classical level; in particular, they appear optically indistinguishable from ordinary black holes to observers at infinity. We speculate concerning the possibility that these objects (or others much like them) might exist in nature, and whether they might be observable despite their similar outward appearance to ordinary black holes. We examine their thermodynamics and stability in companion papers.

一句话导读：研究被射内质粒子壳包围的Schwarzschild黑洞的经典解及其与普通黑洞的观测相似性。

相关度：high — 论文直接研究经典引力/广义相对论中的黑洞周围物质解，与用户的“经典引力/广义相对论”重点标签完全匹配。

论被辐射包围的黑洞 II：热力学

On Black Holes Surrounded by Radiation II: Thermodynamics

Marcos Riojas, Matthew J. Strassler · hep-th, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：在姊妹论文中，我们考虑了一个质量为m的Schwarzschild黑洞被一层厚厚的无质量粒子“海洋”包裹，该海洋将黑洞的光子球延伸为有限深度的区域。我们在那里表明，这个ADM质量为M的“hillingar黑洞”在光学上模拟了相同质量的普通黑洞。这里我们发现在热力学上它也有模拟效果：热平衡的形式假设意味着该系统具有与质量为M的普通黑洞相同的温度和熵。我们通过多种方法仔细检验了这一结果；进一步的方法和亚稳定性的迹象由我们中的一位在姊妹论文中给出。在AdS空间中，这种模拟不成立，解具有更丰富的结构。虽然目前尚不清楚这些系统是否能作为更现实系统的模型，我们注意到与黑洞演化的可能联系。特别地，假设热平衡能够建立并维持，半径≥3M的空腔中的HBH可以蒸发，可能在有限小体积内引发信息疑难。

English: In a companion paper we considered a Schwarzschild black hole of mass $m$ enveloped by a thick "ocean" of massless particles that extends the black hole's photon sphere into a region of finite depth. There we showed that this "hillingar black hole", of ADM mass $M$, optically mimics an ordinary black hole of the same mass. Here we find it also mimics the black hole thermodynamically: the formal assumption of thermal equilibrium implies the system has the same temperature and entropy as an ordinary black hole of mass $M$. We check this result carefully using multiple methods; a further method and indications of metastability are given by one of us in a companion paper. In AdS space, the mimicry does not hold, and the solutions have a richer structure. While it is far from clear that these systems are models for more realistic ones, we note possible connections with black hole evolution. In particular, assuming thermal equilibrium can be established and maintained, an HBH in a cavity of radius $\geq 3M$ can evaporate, potentially posing the information puzzle in a small finite volume.

一句话导读：研究表明被辐射包裹的Schwarzschild黑洞（hillingar黑洞）在热力学性质上模拟了同质量普通黑洞，AdS中则不然。

相关度：low — 该论文主要研究经典引力中的黑洞热力学与辐射环境，不涉及全息原理或用户重点标签中的全息超导、多分量序参量等方向。

与黑洞一样冷：扩展光子球

As Cold as a Black Hole: Extended Photon Spheres

Marcos Riojas · hep-th, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：人们普遍认为，在渐近平坦时空中，自引力辐射无法与黑洞达到热平衡。我们描述了一个对标准不稳定性论证的边际稳定连续体例外。我们的主要观察是，光子球控制 Israel 结条件（IJCs）、Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff（TOV）方程以及有限半径黑洞热力学：IJCs 与 TOV 方程在零径向压强下等价，光子球处逆比热正比于 $-\Lambda$，在固定总质量下添加壳层当且仅当局部比热为正时会降低渐近 Hawking 温度。利用这些结果，我们展示了如何在不使用 Euclid 路径积分的情况下计算热力学熵。这里描述的例外来自与 M.J. Strassler 的合作工作，我们发现“hillingar 黑洞”（HBH）模仿质量为 $M$ 的普通 Schwarzschild 黑洞，共享其 Hawking 温度、光子环，并在平衡态下共享其粗粒化熵 $S=4\pi M^2$。在这里我们表明这些特征并非调节所致；它们唯一地源于力学与热力学的联合约束。我们找到了热力学模仿和扩展光子球形成的条件；对于自相似解，二者重合。一个单参数自相似系统族——除 HBH 外，其余都需要在其扩展光子球边缘存在无质量壁——同时满足这两个条件。该族包含“最刚星”和“冻结星”。

English: It is widely believed that self-gravitating radiation cannot reach thermal equilibrium with a black hole in asymptotically flat spacetime. We describe a marginally stable continuum exception to the standard instability argument. Our main observation is that the photon sphere controls the Israel junction conditions (IJCs), the Tolman-Oppenheimer-Volkoff (TOV) equation, and finite-radius black hole thermodynamics: the IJCs and TOV equation are equivalent at zero radial pressure, the inverse specific heat at the photon sphere is proportional to $-\Lambda$, and adding shells at fixed total mass \textit{lowers} the asymptotic Hawking temperature if and only if the local specific heat is positive. Using these results, we show how to compute thermodynamic entropies without the Euclidean path integral. The exception described here results from companion work with M.J. Strassler, where we found that a "hillingar black hole" (HBH) mimics an ordinary Schwarzschild black hole of mass $M$, sharing its Hawking temperature, photon ring, and, in equilibrium, its coarse-grained entropy $S = 4 \pi M^2$. Here, we show these features are not tuned; they follow uniquely from joint mechanical and thermodynamic constraints. Conditions for thermodynamic mimicry and for the formation of extended photon spheres are found; for self-similar solutions they coincide. A one-parameter family of self-similar systems -- all of which, excepting the HBH, require massless walls at the edges of their extended photon spheres -- satisfies both conditions. This family includes "stiffest stars" and "frozen stars".

一句话导读：研究自引力辐射与黑洞热平衡的经典引力例外，证明光子球控制 Israel 结条件、TOV 方程和有限半径黑洞热力学，并给出自相似“hillingar 黑洞”家族。

相关度：high — 论文主要研究经典广义相对论中的黑洞热力学与光子球结构，与用户重点标签“经典引力/广义相对论”直接匹配。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

SYK模型中的量子信息

Quantum Information in SYK Model

Chen-Te Ma, Jeff Murugan, Masaki Tezuka · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们从量子信息的角度研究SYK模型中的体-边界对应关系。SYK模型描述具有随机全对全相互作用的马约拉纳费米子系统，其无序平均通常对高斯系综进行，在N大且低能极限下容许用JT引力进行对偶描述。该框架为探索近AdS2中的全息和涌现时空提供了一个最小化设定。我们通过量子混沌和纠缠的诊断来探测全息原理。在早期阶段，SYK模型饱和了Lyapunov指数的普适界，表明与半经典黑洞动力学一致的最大混沌。在晚期阶段，其谱统计由随机矩阵理论支配，反映了强混沌量子系统的普适特征。这些动力学性质在边界量子混沌与体半经典引力之间建立了具体联系。与此同时，我们分析了量子纠缠和算子代数结构，以研究相关冯·诺依曼代数中的跃迁及其对涌现几何的意义。为探索这些现象的稳健性，我们考虑了通过修正物质耦合和替代随机分布对SYK模型的形变。我们的结果阐明了量子信息论结构如何编码体引力动力学，并为时空涌现机制提供了洞见。

English: We investigate the bulk-boundary correspondence in the SYK model from a quantum information perspective. The SYK model describes a system of Majorana fermions with random all-to-all interactions, whose disorder average-typically taken over a Gaussian ensemble-admits a dual description in terms of JT gravity in the large-$N$, low-energy limit. This framework provides a minimal setting for exploring holography and emergent spacetime in nearly AdS$_2$. We probe the holographic principle through diagnostics of quantum chaos and entanglement. In the early-time regime, the SYK model saturates the universal bound on the Lyapunov exponent, signaling maximal chaos consistent with semiclassical black hole dynamics. In the late-time regime, its spectral statistics are governed by random matrix theory, reflecting universal features of strongly chaotic quantum systems. These dynamical properties establish a concrete link between boundary quantum chaos and bulk semiclassical gravity. In parallel, we analyze quantum entanglement and the structure of operator algebras to investigate transitions in the associated von Neumann algebras and their implications for emergent geometry. To explore the robustness of these phenomena, we consider deformations of the SYK model through modified matter couplings and alternative random distributions. Our results clarify how quantum information-theoretic structures encode bulk gravitational dynamics and provide insight into the mechanism of spacetime emergence.

一句话导读：从量子信息角度研究SYK模型的体-边界对应，通过混沌和纠缠诊断揭示量子信息结构编码体引力动力学与时空涌现。

相关度：high — 摘要直接围绕SYK模型、JT引力和量子信息中的全息对应，属于用户主题分类中的JT/SYK/AdS2方向，并与纠缠、混沌等全息量子信息研究高度相关。

有限N热M理论中接触结构诱导的辛近半平坦SU(3)结构

Contact Structure-induced Symplectic Nearly Half-Flat $SU(3)$ Structure in Finite-$N$ Thermal ${\cal M}$-Theory

Aalok Misra · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：在arXiv:1306.4339和2004.07259中构建的热QCD类理论的M理论对偶，如arXiv:2211.13186所示，涉及一个闭G_2结构七维流形M_7，该流形支持接触3-结构(C3S)，且诱导出一个“横向”SU(3)结构。我们证明这一诱导结构属于辛近半平坦(S(nearly)HF)类：dω_Φ精确为零，而dΩ_+在红外“MQGP极限”下被抑制为O(e^{-N^{1/3}/O(1)})量级，使得W_Φ^{SU(3)}=W_2^-成为唯一非零的Chiossi–Salamon挠率类，从而完成了在arXiv:1507.02692、2004.07259、2211.13186中开启的MQGP对偶的G-结构分类。作为一个物理应用，将M_7上G_2结构正三形式的微分的11维Hodge对偶进行Kaluza–Klein约化，并通过G_2/S(nearly)HF挠率数据加以组织，得到一个径向“磁场”B_KK(r)，其范数平方精确分解为外部与内部因子。B_KK(r)满足一个在视界处具有正则奇点的Bessel型ODE；正规性和红外正则性选出J_2，而上述内部因子的调和性可从大N抑制恢复。B_KK通过非线性幂律与IIA型味D6-膜的磁场相联系，为有限温度弦理论中产生弱磁场提供了一种自上而下的全息机制。

English: The ${\cal M}$-theory dual of thermal QCD-like theories constructed in arXiv:1306.4339, 2004.07259 involves, as shown in arXiv:2211.13186, a closed $G_2$-structure seven-fold $\mathcal{M}_7$ supporting Contact 3-Structures (C3S) that induce a ``transverse'' $SU(3)$-structure. We prove that this induced structure belongs to the symplectic nearly half-flat (S(nearly)HF) class: $d\omega_\Phi=0$ exactly, while $d\Omega_+$ is suppressed as $\mathcal{O}(e^{-N^{1/3}/\mathcal{O}(1)})$ in the infrared "MQGP limit", leaving $W_\Phi^{SU(3)}=W_2^-$ as the sole non-vanishing Chiossi--Salamon torsion class and completing the $G$-structure classification of the MQGP dual initiated in arXiv:1507.02692,2004.07259, 2211.13186. As a physical application, Kaluza--Klein reduction of the eleven-dimensional Hodge dual of the differential of the $G_2$-structure positive three-form on $\mathcal{M}_7$, organised through the $G_2$/S(nearly)HF torsion data, yields a radial ``magnetic field'' $\mathcal{B}_{\rm KK}(r)$ whose squared norm factorises exactly into external and internal factors. $\mathcal{B}_{\rm KK}(r)$ satisfies a Bessel-type ODE with a regular singular point at the horizon; normalisability and infrared regularity select $J_2$, with harmonicity of the aforementioned internal factor recovered from the large-$N$ suppression. $\mathcal{B}_{\rm KK}$ is related to the magnetic field of the type~IIA flavour $D6$-branes via a non-linear power law, furnishing a top-down holographic mechanism for generating weak magnetic fields in finite-temperature string theory.

一句话导读：该文证明了热QCD类理论的M理论全息对偶中由接触3-结构诱导的SU(3)结构属于辛近半平坦类，并给出由G_2/SU(3)挠率导致的KK径向磁场及其与D6-膜磁场的幂律关系。

相关度：low — 该论文主要研究热QCD全息对偶中的G2/SU(3)几何结构与磁场产生，与用户关注的全息超流、涡旋、相变、探针膜等方向不直接重叠，故相关性较低。
