度规符号作为辅助序参量：因果可行性判据

Metric Signature as an Auxiliary Order Parameter: A Causal Viability Criterion

Miguel Bermudez · gr-qc, hep-th, math-ph, math.MP · v3 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们描述了一种分支化的度规引力表述，其中时空号差由一个实对称内部场 H 通过 g = e H e^T 编码。一个 O(4) 不变的代数势具有满足 H^2=1 的非简并极小值，每个惯量 (p,q) 对应一个真空轨道。在 H 非简并的连通区域内，Sylvester 定律允许变量变换将所有导数放入度规扇区。H 的剩余分量为辅助量，沿其 O(4) 轨道的平坦方向为规范方向。我们直接从耦合的 tetrad 和 H 方程推导出这一陈述。作为诊断例子，我们研究了四维中五个平坦号差类附近的 R + alpha R^2 引力。对于固定的普适耦合，只有洛伦兹分支具有标准引力子符号、非快子标量子和双曲初值问题。欧几里得与分裂号差分支不定义普通因果相。在存在持久观察者的条件下，这为因果可行性判据提供了一种人择解释。它并不是关于分支的动力学跃迁理论或概率测度：任何真实跃迁都必须遇到 det H = 0，在那里分支化描述失效。

English: We describe a branchwise formulation of metric gravity in which the spacetime signature is encoded by a real symmetric internal field H through g = e H e^T. An O(4)-invariant algebraic potential has non-degenerate minima satisfying H^2 = 1, with one vacuum orbit for each inertia (p,q). Within a connected region where H is non-degenerate, Sylvester's law permits a change of variables that places all derivatives in the metric sector. The remaining components of H are auxiliary, while the flat directions along its O(4) orbit are gauge. We derive this statement directly from the coupled tetrad and H equations. As a diagnostic example, we study R + alpha R^2 gravity around the five flat signature classes in four dimensions. For fixed universal couplings, only one Lorentzian branch has the standard graviton sign, a non-tachyonic scalaron, and a hyperbolic initial-value problem. Euclidean and split-signature branches do not define ordinary causal phases. Conditional on the existence of persistent observers, this supplies an anthropic interpretation of the causal viability criterion. It is not a dynamical theory of transitions or a probability measure on the branches: any real transition must encounter det H = 0, where the branchwise description fails.

一句话导读：提出度规符号作为辅助序参量的分支化引力，表明在 R+αR^2 中仅洛伦兹分支满足因果可行条件。

相关度：medium — 本文研究经典引力中的度规符号与因果结构，属于经典引力/广义相对论支线，不含全息或量子引力内容。

德西特时空中线性化引力波的一致截断

Consistent Truncation of Linearized gravitational waves in de Sitter space-time

Ghanashyam Date, Harsh · gr-qc, hep-th · v3 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：利用来自有界源的引力波观测，将远离源的观测波形与源附近的局部动力学和环境联系起来是重要的一步。为了分离和识别源的不同特征，辐射场和源分布的多极展开都至关重要。在实际层面，这些展开通常在有限的多极阶处截断，因此会出现一致性问题。这在文献[CHK]中已被指出，表现为在某种渐近形式中出现不允许的log(r)项，并提出了一种与源守恒一致的特定四极截断。文献[NKD80]中错误地指出该特解不满足广义谐和规范条件，这可能是log(r)项出现的原因。本工作通过显式地对共形时间积分规范条件和源守恒方程来分析一致性问题，从而给出了一致截断的通用程序。该程序在共形坐标下的广义谐和规范和Bondi坐标下的Bondi规范中得到了验证。

English: An important step in using observations of gravitational waves from bounded sources is to relate the observed waveforms far away from a source to the local dynamics and environment of the source. To isolate and identify different features of the source, multipole expansion of both the radiation field and the source distribution are crucial. At a practical level, these expansions are truncated at some finite multipole order and a consistency issue arises. This was flagged in \cite{CHK} as the emergence of disallowed $\log(r)$ terms in certain asymptotic forms and a particular quadrupolar truncation was proposed which was consistent with the source conservation. In \cite{NKD80}, it was noted (incorrectly) that the particular solution does not satisfy the generalized harmonic gauge condition and this may be the source of appearance of the log(r) terms. In this work the consistency issue is analyzed by explicitly integrating the gauge conditions and the source conservation equations over the conformal time. This gives a general procedure for a consistent truncation. The procedure is verified in generalized harmonic gauge in conformal chart and in the Bondi gauge in Bondi chart.

一句话导读：系统研究德西特时空中线性化引力波多极展开的一致截断问题，通过积分规范条件和源守恒方程建立通用程序。

相关度：medium — 论文研究德西特时空中的经典引力波与规范截断问题，属于广义相对论范畴；虽涉及德西特时空，但未涉及全息对应或量子引力。

用中微子振荡检验修正 teleparallel 引力

Testing Modified Teleparallel Gravity by Neutrino Oscillations

H. Yazdani Ahmadabadi, H. Mohseni Sadjadi · hep-ph, gr-qc, hep-th · v3 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：在修正的 teleparallel 引力框架下，我们研究中微子味转换。我们展示了时空挠率的存在如何同时修改中微子味振荡和物质增强的 Mikheyev-Smirnov-Wolfenstein (MSW) 共振。利用太阳中微子实验数据，我们给出分析，以对修正的 teleparallel 模型参数及中微子-挠率耦合施加新的边界。数值结果表明，由此产生的中微子存活概率修正可解释当前和未来观测装置中的潜在观测差异，而无需引入新物理学或非标准相互作用。

English: In the framework of modified teleparallel gravity, we study neutrino flavor conversion. We show how the presence of spacetime torsion modifies both neutrino flavor oscillation and the matter-enhanced Mikheyev-Smirnov-Wolfenstein (MSW) resonance. Using data from solar neutrino experiments, we perform an analysis to place new bounds on the modified teleparallel model parameters and neutrino-torsion coupling. Our numerical results indicate that the resulting modifications to the neutrino survival probability may explain potential observational discrepancies in current and future observatories without invoking new physics or non-standard interactions.

一句话导读：利用太阳中微子振荡数据检验修正 teleparallel 引力中的时空挠率效应，并对模型参数施加新约束。

相关度：medium — 论文研究修正 teleparallel 引力中的挠率对中微子振荡的影响，属于经典修改引力支线而非全息/量子引力；与用户标注的‘经典引力/广义相对论’相匹配，但与其余全息相关重点标签无直接关联。

Starobinsky暴胀在最小双场标量-张量完备化中的稳健性

Robustness of Starobinsky inflation in a minimal two-field scalar-tensor completion

Boris Latosh · gr-qc, hep-th · v5 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们研究了在引入由标量-张量引力单圈有效作用启发的极简标量场扩展后，Starobinsky暴胀是否保持稳健。我们数值求解了由约化慢滚作用导出的四维背景系统，发现当额外标量至少与标量子（scalaron）一样重时，精确的Starobinsky分支在采样的慢滚区域内是有限时间吸引子。我们导出了张量和标量模式的二次作用，发现动力学部分健康，不存在标量或张量梯度不稳定性。在测试的导数耦合系数范围内，传播速度与光速不可区分。这些稳定性测试使用了完整的约束约化二次算符（|β|≤1），而给出的曲率谱则是通过约化标量演化获得的。针对β的直接完整谱比较、β=0处单独的完整-约化回归，以及对正则归一化系数差异的独立约束，将省略的导数耦合修正（包括数值和CPU-GPU容差）限制在验证完整界限以下，低于与物理结论相关的10^-3精度。在整个参数扫描范围内，曲率谱与Starobinsky模型保持极其接近。虽然熵种子模可贡献大约四分之一的最终曲率功率，但移除绝热-熵混合仅使总谱变化百万分之几，揭示了在异常稳健的可观测预测背后存在非平凡的多场动力学。

English: We investigate whether the Starobinsky inflation remains robust after including a minimal scalar extension motivated by the one-loop effective action of scalar-tensor gravity. We numerically solve the four-dimensional background system derived from the reduced slow-roll action and discover that the exact Starobinsky branch is a finite-time attractor in the sampled slow-roll domain when the additional scalar is at least as heavy as the scalaron. We derive quadratic actions for tensor and scalar modes and find a healthy kinetic sector with no scalar or tensor gradient instabilities. The propagation speeds are indistinguishable from the speed of light over the tested range of the derivative-coupling coefficient. These stability tests use the complete constraint-reduced quadratic operator over $|\beta|\leq1$, whereas the quoted production curvature spectra are obtained from the reduced scalar evolution. A direct complete-spectrum comparison in $\beta$, a separate complete--reduced regression at $\beta=0$, and independent bounds on the canonically normalised coefficient differences constrain the omitted derivative-coupling correction, including the numerical and CPU--GPU allowances, to at most $\csname VALIDATION_COMPLETE_BOUND\endcsname$, below the $10^{-3}$ accuracy relevant to the physical conclusion. The curvature spectra remain extraordinarily close to the Starobinsky model across the entire parameter scan. Although the entropy-seeded mode can provide approximately one quarter of the final curvature power, removing the adiabatic--entropy mixing changes the total spectrum by only a few parts in a million, revealing nontrivial multifield dynamics behind an exceptionally robust observable prediction.

一句话导读：该工作检验了带额外标量的Starobinsky暴胀模型的稳健性，发现精确Starobinsky分支是吸引子且观测谱几乎不变。

相关度：medium — 论文聚焦经典引力中的暴胀宇宙学，不涉及全息，但与经典引力/广义相对论的主题相关。

辐射纪元中微子导致的原初黑洞质量增长

Primordial Black Hole mass growth from neutrinos during the radiation era

Ma\"el Gonin · astro-ph.CO, hep-th · v3 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们提出原初黑洞通过中微子吸收进行质量演化的新图像。利用半经典方法并更仔细地考察早期等离子体动力学，我们重新研究了嵌入热浴中、质量范围从10^-3到10^9太阳质量的原初黑洞群体对辐射的热吸收。我们发现中等质量与超大质量原初黑洞存在显著质量增长；该效应会移动热历史预言的正负电子湮灭峰。取决于坍缩分数，中等质量范围附近还可能出现另一个显著峰。此外，由于原初黑洞从热浴中增长，暗物质中原初黑洞占比f_PBH也会改变。这些结果修正了此前关于原初黑洞质量演化的观点，并对暗物质原初黑洞观测具有意义。

English: We present a new picture of primordial black holes mass evolution through neutrino absorption. Using semi-classical approach and a closer look at the kinetic of the early plasma we revisit the thermal absorption of radiation by a population of primordial black holes ranging from $10^{-3}-10^9 M_\odot$ embedded in a thermal bath. We find significant mass growth of intermediate mass and supermassive PBHs, the effect shift the predicted $e^+e^-$ peak from thermal history. Depending on the value of the collapse fraction an additional peak around the intermediate mass range, might become significant. Moreover, because PBH grow from the thermal bath the fraction of DM in PBH $f_{\rm PBH}$ also change. These results revise the previous view on mass evolution of PBH and have implications for dark matter PBH observations.

一句话导读：原初黑洞可在辐射期通过中微子吸收显著增质，从而移动热历史e+e-峰并改变PBH暗物质占比。

相关度：low — 论文研究原初黑洞通过中微子吸收的质量增长及暗物质占比影响，属于宇宙学与经典黑洞演化，与用户列出的经典引力支线仅间接相关，不涉及全息或量子引力。

暗物质诱导致密星坍缩为黑洞的嬗变时间尺度

Transmutation Timescales for Dark Matter Induced Collapse of Compact Stars into Black Holes

H. A. Adarsha (MCNS, India), Chandrachur Chakraborty (MCNS, India), Sudip Bhattacharyya (TIFR, India) · astro-ph.HE, gr-qc, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：致密星捕获的超重非对称暗物质粒子可以热化、自引力并坍缩形成内寄生黑洞（EBH），其后续增长可能将宿主星转变为黑洞。年老毫秒脉冲星（MSPs）和白矮星（WDs）的持续存在因此对暗物质粒子质量 $m_\chi$ 和暗物质-核子散射截面 $\sigma_{n\chi}$ 施加了强有力的约束。我们通过求解EBH增长方程推导了嬗变时间尺度的解析表达式，一致地考虑了恒星物质的邦迪吸积、霍金蒸发以及稳态捕获状态下对EBH的持续暗物质供给。我们还在流体力学描述失效时引入了重子吸积中的量子效应，提供了EBH增长在粒子与流体区域统一的处理。采用对费米子和玻色子非对称暗物质物理透明的坍缩判据，我们计算了不同暗物质密度环境下代表性MSP和WD的EBH嬗变时间尺度。尽管物理成分与之前的研究大体相似，但本工作通过EBH增长的闭式解析处理和所采用的EBH形成时间尺度处方，推导出更新的约束，给出了更低的临界EBH质量以支持持续增长，并修正了嬗变时间尺度。要求MSP的嬗变时间超过约1 Gyr，WD的超过约10 Gyr，我们推导了在 $m_\chi \sim 10^{6}$--$10^{14}$ GeV 范围内对 $\sigma_{n\chi}$ 的修正约束，并表明初始质量小至约 $4\times10^{4}$ kg 的EBH可以进行持续增长，扩展了致密星所探测的暗物质参数空间区域。

English: Ultra-heavy asymmetric dark matter (DM) particles captured by compact stars can thermalize, self-gravitate, and collapse to form an endoparasitic black hole (EBH), whose subsequent growth may transmute the host star into a black hole. The continued existence of old millisecond pulsars (MSPs) and white dwarfs (WDs) thus places powerful constraints on the DM particle mass $m_\chi$ and the DM-nucleon scattering cross-section $\sigma_{\rm n\chi}$. We derive an analytical expression for the transmutation timescale by solving the EBH growth equation, consistently accounting for Bondi accretion of stellar matter, Hawking evaporation, and sustained DM feeding of the EBH in a steady-state capture regime. We also incorporate quantum effects in baryonic accretion when the hydrodynamic description breaks down, providing a unified treatment of EBH growth across both particle and fluid regimes. Adopting a physically transparent collapse criterion for fermionic and bosonic asymmetric DM, we compute EBH transmutation timescales for representative MSPs and WDs in environments with different DM densities. Although the physical ingredients are broadly similar to previous studies, this work derives updated constraints through a closed-form analytical treatment of EBH growth and an adopted prescription for the EBH formation timescale, yielding a lower critical EBH mass for sustained growth and revised transmutation timescales. Requiring the transmutation time to exceed $\sim 1$ Gyr for MSPs and $\sim 10$ Gyr for WDs, we derive revised constraints on $\sigma_{\rm n\chi}$ over $m_\chi \sim 10^{6}$--$10^{14}$ GeV, and show that EBHs with initial masses as small as $\sim 4\times10^{4}$ kg can undergo sustained growth, extending the region of DM parameter space probed by compact stars.

一句话导读：通过解析求解内寄生黑洞增长方程，推导暗物质诱导致密星嬗变为黑洞的时间尺度并更新对暗物质参数的约束。

相关度：low — 论文主要研究暗物质诱导恒星坍缩和黑洞形成的时间尺度，属于经典天体物理与引力现象，不涉及全息或量子引力理论。

Reissner-Nordström类比表面上的相干多径波响应

Coherent multipath wave response on Reissner-Nordstr\"{o}m analogue surface

Peng-Yu Chen, Sen Guo, Qing-Quan Jiang, Yu Liang, Kai Lin · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：为了揭示相对论致密天体的内禀度量如何控制宏观波现象，我们建立了一个理论框架，将Reissner-Nordström (RN)黑洞的电荷依赖空间几何映射到一个类比弯曲表面的相干响应。通过求解等距嵌入赤道切片上的精确空间测地线边值问题，我们提取了离散的多环路径长度谱，并通过有限路径表面惠更斯-菲涅耳构造将此几何骨干转化为物理波场。我们解析计算了任意阶缠绕轨迹及其高缠绕累积极限，证明了类比电荷扮演一个精确的物理拨盘，重构事件视界喉道并从根本上重组离散路径序列。此外，我们发现这一底层几何形变唯一地决定了宏观干涉，揭示稳态空间条纹、谱共振梳和瞬态时间回波阶梯是单一电荷控制路径谱的内在统一物理投影。这种从系统参数到响应的方法论为强场引力透镜与桌面变换光学之间建立了严格的理论桥梁，为未来多领域类比引力实验提供了高度可调的蓝图。

English: To uncover how the intrinsic metric of a relativistic compact object governs macroscopic wave phenomena, we establish a theoretical framework mapping the charge dependent spatial geometry of a Reissner-Nordstr\"{o}m (RN) black hole onto the coherent response of an analogue curved surface. By solving an exact spatial geodesic boundary value problem on an isometrically embedded equatorial slice, we extract the discrete multi-loop path-length spectrum and convert this geometric backbone into a physical wave field via a finite-path surface Huygens-Fresnel construction. We analytically compute the arbitrary order winding trajectories alongside their high winding accumulation limits, demonstrating that the analogue charge acts as a precise physical dial that reconfigures the event horizon throat and fundamentally reorganizes the discrete path sequence. Furthermore, we find that this underlying geometric deformation uniquely dictates the macroscopic interference, revealing that steady state spatial fringes, spectral resonance combs, and transient temporal echo ladders are intrinsically unified physical projections of a single charge controlled path spectrum. This systematic parameter to response methodology establishes a rigorous theoretical bridge between strong field gravitational lensing and tabletop transformation optics, providing a highly tunable blueprint for future multi domain analogue gravity experiments.

一句话导读：利用RN时空测地线谱与惠更斯-菲涅耳构造，建立了黑洞类比表面上多径波干涉的统一理论。

从紫外正则化场关联中得到的爱因斯坦张量

The Einstein Tensor from UV-regulated Field Correlations

Matheus H. Zambianco, Achim Kempf · gr-qc, quant-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：已知时空度规可以从量子场关联子中恢复。我们扩展了这一方案，证明了爱因斯坦张量可以直接用紫外正则化的场关联子来表示。相同的关联结构也直接决定里奇张量，而更高阶的同点导数可恢复联络和黎曼张量。我们进一步证明了重整化应力-能量张量可以在该框架内表述，直到标准的有限重整化自由度。这些结果为半经典爱因斯坦方程提供了基于关联的组织方式，并推进了用量子场关联子表述时空几何的纲领。

English: It is known that the spacetime metric can be recovered from quantum-field correlators. We extend this programme by showing that the Einstein tensor can be directly written in terms of UV-regulated field correlations. The same correlation structure also directly determines the Ricci tensor, while higher coincidence derivatives recover the connection and Riemann tensor. We further show that the renormalized stress-energy tensor can be formulated within this framework, up to the standard finite renormalization freedom. These results provide a correlation-based organization of the semiclassical Einstein equations and advance the programme of formulating spacetime geometry in terms of quantum field correlations.

一句话导读：该文展示了爱因斯坦张量可由紫外正则化场关联直接构造，为半经典爱因斯坦方程提供基于关联的重新组织。

用于引力波数据分析的紧凑时频表示

A compact time-frequency representation for gravitational-wave data analysis

Noah Pearson, Mesut \c{C}al{\i}\c{s}kan, Sophie Bini, Neil J. Cornish · gr-qc, astro-ph.IM · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：时频（小波）域分析由于在处理非平稳噪声方面的优势，正越来越多地用于引力波数据分析。常用的选择是 Wilson-Daubechies-Meyer（WDM）小波变换，其窗函数在频率上非常紧致，但在时间上更分散。本文考虑了一种由相移高斯函数之和构造的替代窗函数。该“高斯”窗在时频上更对称，因此也更紧致。我们研究了该窗函数的性质及其对引力波分析的影响。我们解析计算了该窗的时频方差乘积并进行了数值验证。在对称情形下，该构造达到 Heisenberg-Gabor 不确定极限的约 2.4% 以内。我们猜想该高斯窗达到了任何 WD 小波窗函数的最小时间-频率面积。

English: Time-frequency (wavelet) domain analyses are seeing greater use for gravitational-wave data analysis due to the advantages they have in handling non-stationary noise. A popular choice is the Wilson-Daubechies-Meyer (WDM) wavelet transform, which uses a window function that is very compact in frequency, but more spread out in time. In this work, we consider an alternative window function that is built from a sum of phase-shifted Gaussians. This "Gaussian" window is more symmetric in time-frequency, and consequently more compact. We examine the properties of this window function and the implications it has for gravitational-wave analyses. We calculate the window's time-frequency variance product analytically and verify it numerically. For the symmetric case, where the window has the same form in time and frequency, the construction comes within $\sim2.4\%$ of saturating the Heisenberg-Gabor uncertainty limit. We conjecture that this Gaussian window achieves the minimum time-frequency area of any WD wavelet window.

一句话导读：提出一种由相移高斯之和构造的时频小波窗，在约 2.4% 内达到 Heisenberg-Gabor 不确定极限，可用于引力波数据分析。

相关度：low — 本文属于引力波数据处理中的时频窗函数设计，不涉及全息理论、量子引力或经典时空动力学本身，与用户关注的理论全息支线和经典引力理论仅有间接关联。

空间引力波探测器数据中瞬态信号的自监督重建

Self-supervised reconstruction of transients in data from space-borne gravitational-wave detectors

Yuxiang Xu, Minghui Du, Bo Liang, Zihao Xiao, Tianyu Zhao, Peng Xu · gr-qc, astro-ph.GA, astro-ph.IM, hep-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：空间引力波探测器数据可能包含波形形态未知的瞬态信号。提取这些信号对于表征新源和减轻仪器异常很重要。然而，现有基于深度神经网络的方法依赖干净训练目标和波形类别特定的样本，这限制了在瞬态形态未预先指定时的适用性。本文开发了一种受 Noise2Noise 启发的自监督框架，从含噪观测中学习，无需干净训练目标，推断时也不需要瞬态特定的波形模板。在含噪大质量黑洞双星观测上训练的单一模型，可在源混淆的测试数据中高重叠地恢复大质量黑洞双星信号，并恢复仪器毛刺及其他引力波瞬态信号（如宇宙弦暴）的主要形态。除波形恢复外，当独立信息将候选瞬态识别为仪器信号时，其提取的波形可在无异常特定模板的情况下从数据中扣除。在模拟连续数据流中，该过程显著抑制了毛刺主导频段内的注入异常功率。这些结果支持使用自监督提取对未知形态的候选瞬态进行初始波形估计，从而支持后续表征，并在适当情况下对仪器异常进行条件扣除。

English: Space-based gravitational-wave (GW) data may contain transient signals whose waveform morphologies are not known in advance. Extracting these signals is important for characterizing new sources and mitigating instrumental anomalies. However, existing deep neural network (DNN)-based extraction approaches rely on clean training targets and waveform-class-specific examples, which may limit their applicability when the transient morphology is not specified in advance. This work develops a Noise2Noise (N2N)-inspired self-supervised framework that learns from noisy observations without clean training targets and requires no transient-specific waveform templates at inference. A single model trained on noisy massive black-hole binary (MBHB) observations provides high-overlap MBHB recovery and recovers the dominant morphologies of instrumental glitches and other GW transient signals such as cosmic string bursts in source-confused test data. Beyond waveform recovery, when independent information identifies a candidate transient as instrumental, its extracted waveform can be subtracted from the data without an anomaly-specific template. In a simulated continuous data stream, this procedure substantially suppresses the injected-anomaly power within the glitch-dominated frequency band. These results support the use of self-supervised extraction for initial waveform estimation of candidate transients with unknown morphologies, enabling subsequent characterization and, where appropriate, conditional subtraction of instrumental anomalies.

一句话导读：提出一种基于 Noise2Noise 的自监督框架，从未标注含噪引力波数据中重建未知形态瞬态信号，并可用于仪器假信号的提取与扣除。

相关度：medium — 论文处理空间引力波探测器数据中的瞬态波形提取与扣除，属于引力波观测与数据处理范畴，与经典引力/引力波研究直接相关，但不涉及爱因斯坦方程或时空理论本身。

浸没于外磁场中的量子 Oppenheimer–Snyder 黑洞的薄吸积盘与引力俘获截面

Thin accretion disk and gravitational capture cross sections of a quantum Oppenheimer--Snyder black hole immersed in an external magnetic field

Anuar Idrissov, Hernando Quevedo · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们研究了一个几何薄、光学厚的 Novikov–Thorne 吸积盘，以及一个浸没在外部渐近均匀磁场中的量子 Oppenheimer–Snyder 黑洞的引力俘获截面。外部的时空几何携带一个量子参数，我们讨论了其整个容许范围，该范围包含双视界黑洞、极值构型和无视界致密天体，并在光子球消失处结束。由于时空是静态的，Wald 势为纯轴向形式，因此磁场仅通过洛伦兹力作用于弱带电吸积物质，其所有效应由一个磁耦合参数控制。我们计算了带电圆轨道、最内层稳定圆轨道、辐射通量、有效温度、红移因子、微分和谱光度以及辐射效率，还计算了临界束缚轨道以及无质量、有质量和带电粒子的俘获截面。计算通过与精确 Schwarzschild 极限以及将热光度与效率联系起来的恒等式进行验证。量子参数对盘观测量影响很弱，而磁耦合显著提高效率、峰值通量和谱峰高度，并将峰移向更高频率。两个参数对吸收的影响符号相反。量子参数使所有俘获截面缩小，而磁耦合增大了有质量粒子的俘获截面但不改变光子截面，因此在测试场模型中阴影观测量仅由量子参数响应。对于带电粒子，均匀磁场限制其运动，没有粒子能从超过磁屏蔽半径的距离到达黑洞。

English: We study a geometrically thin, optically thick Novikov--Thorne accretion disk and the gravitational capture cross sections of a quantum Oppenheimer--Snyder black hole immersed in an external, asymptotically uniform magnetic field. The exterior geometry carries a single quantum parameter, and we work over its entire admissible range, which contains a two-horizon black hole, an extremal configuration and a horizonless compact object, and which ends where the photon sphere disappears. Since the spacetime is static, the Wald potential is purely axial, so the field acts on the disk only through the Lorentz force on weakly charged accreting matter and all of its effects are controlled by a single magnetic coupling. We compute the charged circular orbits, the innermost stable circular orbit, the radiative flux, the effective temperature, the redshift factor, the differential and spectral luminosity and the radiative efficiency, together with the marginally bound orbit and the capture cross sections of massless, massive and charged particles. The construction is checked against the exact Schwarzschild limit and against the identity that relates the bolometric luminosity to the efficiency. The quantum parameter changes the disk observables only weakly, whereas the magnetic coupling raises the efficiency, the peak flux and the height of the spectral peak by large factors and shifts that peak to higher frequency. The two parameters act with opposite signs on absorption. The quantum parameter shrinks every capture cross section, while the magnetic coupling enlarges the one for massive particles and leaves the photon cross section unchanged, so that within this test-field model shadow observables respond to the quantum parameter alone. For charged particles the uniform field confines the motion, and no particle reaches the hole from beyond a magnetic shielding radius.

一句话导读：研究量子 Oppenheimer–Snyder 黑洞在外部均匀磁场中的薄吸积盘与引力俘获截面，发现量子参数对盘观测量影响微弱而磁场耦合显著增强效率与光度。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典/半经典黑洞吸积盘、轨道与俘获截面的研究，不涉及全息或量子引力信息；与用户的“经典引力/广义相对论”支线有一定关联，但非全息或凝聚态/核物理主线。

连续引力波全天 FrequencyHough 搜索的替代神经网络后续筛选方法

Alternative neural-network follow-up for all-sky FrequencyHough in continuous gravitational-wave searches

Martina Di Cesare, Pia Astone, Rosario De Rosa, David Keitel, Cristiano Palomba, Marco Serra · gr-qc, astro-ph.IM · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：连续引力波是由旋转中子星或黑洞周围玻色子云发出的长寿命信号。虽然银河系预计拥有约 10^8 到 10^9 颗中子星，但目前通过电磁观测仅识别出约 10^3 颗。这一巨大的观测差距促使研究者基于替代信使开展搜索。特别是全天连续引力波搜索为探测电磁静默或未被发现的中子星提供了独特机会。在没有任何先验源信息的条件下探索广阔的参数空间，这些搜索可以探测银河系中大量隐藏的中子星种群。在本工作中，我们提出了一种将神经网络（NN）分类器纳入孤立中子星全天搜索策略的新方法。从地基探测器（如 LIGO 和 Virgo）运行的 FrequencyHough 流水线产生的候选体出发，所提方法在不增加计算成本的情况下提高了灵敏度，并且可以跨多个探测器并行化，以增强探测概率并减少误报。我们分析了第三次观测运行（O3）——2019 年 4 月 1 日至 2020 年 3 月 27 日的数据，并聚焦于频率范围 [129, 229] Hz 以及自转减慢区间 [-2.5×10^-9, 1.0×10^-9] Hz/s。模型在构造上与真实 O3 数据统计一致的噪声上训练，并在真实 O3 数据上测试。结果表明，即使在低应变下，模型也能稳健地区分信号与噪声，并且能成功识别训练自转减慢范围之外的硬件注入。

English: Continuous gravitational waves are long-lived signals emitted by spinning neutron stars (NSs) or boson clouds around black holes. While the Milky Way is expected to host $\mathrm{O}(10^{8}-10^{9})$ NSs, only $\mathrm{O}(10^{3})$ are currently identified through electromagnetic observations. This large observational gap motivates searches based on alternative messengers. In particular, all-sky searches for continuous gravitational waves (GWs) offer a unique opportunity to detect NSs that are electromagnetically silent or otherwise undiscovered. By exploring a broad region of parameter space without any a priori source information, these searches can probe the vast hidden NS population of our Galaxy. In this work, we present a novel way to include a Neural Network (NN) classifier into an all-sky search strategy for isolated NSs. Starting from candidates produced by the FrequencyHough pipeline running on data from ground-based detectors such as LIGO and Virgo, the proposed method improves sensitivity without increasing computational cost and can be parallelized across multiple detectors to enhance detection probability and reduce false alarms. We analyze data from the third observing run (O3) --- April 1, 2019 to March 27, 2020 --- and focus on the frequency range [129, 229] Hz and spin-down interval [$-2.5\cdot10^{-9}, 1.0\cdot10^{-9}$] Hz/s. The model is trained on noise constructed to be statistically consistent with real O3 data and tested on real O3 data. Results show robust performance in distinguishing signal from noise, even at low strain, and successful identification of hardware injections outside the training spin-down range.

一句话导读：本文提出在连续引力波全天搜索中利用神经网络分类器改进 FrequencyHough 候选体的甄别，在不增加计算成本的情况下提升灵敏度，并在 LIGO/Virgo O3 数据上得到验证。

相关度：medium — 论文聚焦于连续引力波搜索的数据分析方法，属于经典引力/广义相对论范畴；但主要贡献是信号处理和机器学习应用，不涉及新的引力理论或全息原理，因此对理论物理核心议题的相关性有限。

区分双黑洞旋近引力波波形中的透镜调制与进动调制

Distinguishing lensing and precessional modulation in binary black-hole inspiral waveforms

Tien N. Nguyen-Vo, Tamanjyot Singh, Benjamin McKallip, Michael Kesden, Lindsay King · gr-qc, astro-ph.CO · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：双黑洞（BBH）在旋近合并过程中发射引力波（GW）。这些引力波可能被沿视线方向的大尺度结构引力透镜化，从而可能产生同一源的多个像，其之间的固定时间延迟由透镜几何决定。随着BBH旋近，引力波频率增加，导致多个像之间依次发生相长和相消干涉。BBH还有可能与轨道角动量L不对齐的自旋S_i。在旋近过程中，这些不对齐自旋使L绕总角动量J进动，从而调制引力波辐射，类似于由不对齐喷流旋转进出视线而产生的脉冲星辐射。我们研究单个L形引力波探测器区分这两种调制来源的能力。我们发现，当像间时间延迟足够短，使得探测器灵敏度频带内引力波信号的持续时间中出现的干涉条纹少于三条时，进动调制可以模拟两个放大率相近像之间的透镜调制。由于中等红移处强引力透镜对引力波源罕见，而某些形成通道产生的BBH中不对齐自旋常见，因此排除进动调制对于识别真正透镜系统至关重要。

English: Binary black holes (BBHs) emit gravitational waves (GWs) as they inspiral towards merger. These GWs can be gravitationally lensed by large-scale structure along the line of sight, potentially creating multiple images of the same source with fixed time delays determined by the lensing geometry. As the BBHs inspiral, the GW frequency increases, leading to successive constructive and destructive interference between the multiple images. BBHs also have spins $\mathbf{S}_i$ that may be misaligned with their orbital angular momentum $\mathbf{L}$. As the BBHs inspiral, these misaligned spins cause $\mathbf{L}$ to precess about the total angular momentum $\mathbf{J}$, modulating the GW emission similar to pulsar emission resulting from a misaligned jet rotating in and out of the line of sight. We investigate the ability of a single L-shaped GW detector to distinguish between these two sources of modulation. We find that precessional modulation can mimic the lensing modulation between two images with comparable magnifications when the time delay between the images is short enough that fewer than three interference fringes occur during the time the GW signal spends in the sensitivity band of the detector. As strong lensing is rare for GW sources at moderate redshift while misaligned spins are common for BBHs produced in certain formation channels, ruling out precessional modulation is essential to identifying genuinely lensed systems.

一句话导读：研究如何区分双黑洞引力波中的透镜调制与自旋进动调制，指出在短时延情形下进动可模拟透镜。

相关度：low — 论文主要讨论经典引力波观测中的信号识别与波形模拟，不涉及全息对偶、量子引力或AdS/CFT等理论方向。

Bertotti-Robinson磁场中Kerr-Newman黑洞的阴影与薄盘图像

Shadows and Thin-Disk Images of Kerr-Newman Black Holes in a Bertotti-Robinson Magnetic Field

Xi Wan, Haiyu Wang, Zhenyu Zhang, Zelin Zhang, Bin Chen · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：本文研究了Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson (KN-BR)黑洞的光学性质。利用零测地线的可分离性，我们分析了不稳定的球面光子轨道并确定了光子壳层的径向范围。由于时空不是渐近平直的，我们构造了有限距离零角动量观测者屏幕上定义的临界曲线。随后，对从外部延伸到事件视界的几何薄且光学薄盘进行反向光线追踪，并检查所得的图像、强度轮廓、临界曲线面积和内阴影面积。我们发现，真正中性的Kerr-BR₀构型和特殊带电的Kerr-BR_s构型具有几乎相同的光学外观。值得注意的是，对于KN-BR黑洞，增大电荷会减小Kerr-Newman极限下的图像特征尺寸，但在本文考虑的磁化构型中却会增大图像尺寸。我们还发现，外部磁场强烈增大了视在图像尺度，而观测者倾角对内阴影面积的影响比临界曲线面积更显著。这些结果可为未来对这类奇异磁化黑洞的观测识别提供有用的理论参考。

English: In this paper, we investigate the optical properties of Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson (KN-BR) black holes. We use the separability of null geodesics to analyze unstable spherical photon orbits and determine the radial extent of the photon shell. Because the spacetime is not asymptotically flat, we construct the critical curve on the screen of a finite-distance zero-angular-momentum observer. We then perform backward ray tracing for a geometrically thin and optically thin disk that extends from the outer region to the event horizon, and examine the resulting images, intensity profiles, critical-curve areas, and inner-shadow areas. We find that the genuine neutral Kerr-BR$_0$ and specially charged Kerr-BR$_s$ configurations have nearly identical optical appearances. It is remarkable that for the KN-BR black holes increasing the electric charge reduces the characteristic image size in the Kerr-Newman limit but enlarges it in the magnetized configurations considered here. We also find that the external magnetic field strongly increases the apparent image scale, while the observer inclination affects the inner-shadow area more significantly than the critical-curve area. These results may provide useful theoretical insight for future observations aimed at identifying such exotic magnetized black holes.

一句话导读：研究KN-BR黑洞的阴影与薄盘图像，发现外部磁场强增大图像尺度，电荷效应在磁化与未磁化情形下相反。

小质量比极限下并合-振铃波形的剖析

Dissection of a merger-ringdown waveform in the small-mass-ratio limit

Patrick Bourg, Lorenzo K\"uchler, Benjamin Leather, Kevin Cunningham, Jack Lewis, Adam Pound, B\'eatrice Bonga, Marc Casals, Rodrigo Panosso Macedo · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：过去二十年的工作揭示了黑洞双星并合及随后振铃的丰富现象学，涉及准正则模式的织锦、非线性效应、尾部、瞬态以及包括引力记忆在内的长期效应。本文在小质量比极限下发展了一个分析非线性并合-振铃特征的框架，在该极限中各个效应可以被清晰隔离。针对近圆、非自旋黑洞双星的特殊情形，我们发现波形清晰地划分为并合前的长旋进阶段、持续约半个周期的并合阶段，以及由准正则模式主导的并合后振铃。我们证明二次准正则模式在可比到中等质量比的振铃阶段主导于线性泛音之上，并强调了引力波记忆的非线性效应，包括类似于视界吸收效应的三次波区现象。

English: Work over the past two decades has unveiled the rich phenomenology of black hole binary mergers and subsequent ringdowns, involving a tapestry of quasinormal modes (QNMs), nonlinearities, tails, transients, and secular effects including gravitational memory. Here we develop a framework for analyzing nonlinear merger-ringdown features in the small-mass-ratio limit, where individual effects can be cleanly isolated. Specializing to the case of a quasicircular, nonspinning black hole binary, we find the waveform sharply divides into a pre-merger extended inspiral phase, a merger phase lasting roughly half a cycle, and a post-merger ringdown dominated by QNMs. We show quadratic QNMs dominate over linear overtones in the ringdown phase for comparable-to-intermediate mass ratios, and we highlight nonlinear effects of gravitational-wave memory, including cubic wave-zone phenomena analogous to horizon absorption effects.

一句话导读：在小质量比极限下解剖黑洞并合-振铃波形，发现二次QNM在振铃中占主导并指出非线性引力记忆效应。

相关度：high — 论文直接属于经典引力/广义相对论研究，与用户重点标签中'经典引力/广义相对论'及'QNM与非线性演化'明确相关。

小凸起下全透射模式的（不）稳定性

The (in)stability on total transmission modes with small bumps

Xi-Ning Dong, Liang-Bi Wu, Yu-Sen Zhou, Zong-Kuan Guo · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：全透射模式（TTMs）是黑洞微扰方程的一类无反射解，与准正则模式（QNMs）密切相关，且可能对弱环境扰动表现出显著的敏感性。本工作通过在有效势中引入局域 Pöschl-Teller 凸起扰动，研究 Tangherlini 黑洞 TTM 的谱（不）稳定性，并采用改进的 Chebyshev-Lobatto 网格提高局域扰动的数值精度。对于 d=14、ℓ=2、s=2，纯虚 TTM 表现出相对较强的谱稳定性，而真正的复 TTM 即使在小扰动下也会发生显著迁移，这与先前伪谱分析揭示的谱稳定性一致。

English: Total transmission modes (TTMs) are a class of reflectionless solutions to black hole perturbation equations, closely related to quasinormal modes (QNMs), and can exhibit significant sensitivity to weak environmental perturbations. In this work, we investigate the spectrum (in)stability of TTMs of Tangherlini black holes by introducing a localized P\"{o}schl-Teller bump perturbation into the effective potential, and employ a modified Chebyshev-Lobatto grid to improve the numerical accuracy of the localized perturbation. For $d=14$, $\ell=2$, and $s=2$, the purely imaginary TTM exhibits relatively strong spectrum stability, whereas the genuine complex TTMs undergo significant migrations even for small perturbations, consistent with the spectrum stability revealed by previous pseudospectrum analyses.

一句话导读：在局域势垒扰动下，高维 Tangherlini 黑洞的复全透射模式谱不稳定，而纯虚模式相对稳定。

相关度：medium — 论文聚焦经典黑洞扰动中的 TTM/QNM 谱稳定性，属于黑洞物理与 QNM 研究，与全息/量子引力无直接关联；因其可联系引力波 ringdown 与伪谱分析，与用户关注中的 QNM 和经典引力方向相关。

QNM与非线性演化经典引力/广义相对论

爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-标量场理论中的双黑洞：从有效场论推导到第二后牛顿阶动力学

Binary Black Holes in Einstein-Maxwell-Dilaton Theory: Second Post-Newtonian Dynamics from Effective Field Theory

Pawan Kumar Gupta · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：致密双星并合产生的引力波探测为在强场区域检验广义相对论以及寻找其他引力理论的特征提供了强大机会。在这项工作中，我们考虑爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-标量场（EMd）理论，其中黑洞可以同时携带电荷和标量（标量场）荷。我们采用有效场论方法，结合度规的时间卡鲁扎-克莱因分解，以及非相对论引力场，推导了EMd理论中带电荷黑洞双星系统的保守两体拉格朗日量到第二后牛顿（PN）阶。我们的计算将先前已知的1PN阶保守动力学扩展到包含2PN阶的引力、电磁和标量场相互作用。我们在适当的爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦、标量-张量和广义相对论极限下验证了结果，并对静态2PN部分进行了独立的检验粒子极限检查。这些结果为发展更高精度波形模型以及利用引力波观测检验EMd理论提供了所需的保守动力学。

English: The detection of gravitational waves from compact binary coalescences provides a powerful opportunity to test general relativity in the strong-field regime and to search for signatures of alternative theories of gravity. In this work, we consider Einstein-Maxwell-Dilaton (EMd) theory, in which black holes can carry both electric and scalar (dilatonic) charges. We employ the effective field theory approach, together with a temporal Kaluza-Klein decomposition of the metric in terms of non-relativistic gravitational fields, to derive the conservative two-body Lagrangian for charged black-hole binaries in EMd theory through second post-Newtonian (PN) order. Our calculation extends the previously known conservative dynamics at 1PN order and includes the gravitational, electromagnetic, and dilaton interactions at 2PN order. We verify the result in the appropriate Einstein-Maxwell, scalar-tensor, and general relativistic limits, and perform an independent test-body-limit check of the static 2PN sector. These results provide the conservative dynamics needed for developing higher-accuracy waveform models and testing EMd theory with gravitational-wave observations.

一句话导读：利用有效场论方法推导了Einstein-Maxwell-Dilaton理论中双黑洞系统到第二后牛顿阶的保守动力学。

相关度：high — 论文直接研究经典引力/广义相对论中的双黑洞后牛顿动力学，与用户重点标签中的经典引力/广义相对论直接对应。

Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson时空的整体结构与稳定性

Global structure and stability of Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson spacetime

Yu-Sen Zhou, Liang-Bi Wu, Ming-Fei Ji, Wen-Tao Fu, Li-Ming Cao, Rong-Gen Cai · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson (KBR) 时空的 r=∞ 曲面并非真正的边界。我们构造其自然解析延拓，并证明一个 KBR 区域的 r=+∞ 与相邻区域的 r=-∞ 光滑连接。重复延拓得到由类虫洞桥连接的区域无限链，并暴露出相邻的环形奇点而无中间视界，从而违反弱宇宙监督猜想。随后我们在一个双宇宙散射区段上研究无质量标量场，以探测时空稳定性。对于轴对称扰动，我们解析证明了每个 ℓ 都存在纯虚增长型拟正规模，并将其起源追溯到违反时序的区域。在 (ℓ,m)=(2,2) 扇区，当转动足够大且磁场足够小时会出现不稳定分支。其边缘实模满足精确的视界通量平衡，支持一种黑洞炸弹诠释，即超辐射提取被双势垒中的俘获放大。同一谐振腔还支持一族弱阻尼模，并可能产生类似回波(echo)的响应。

English: The surface $r=\infty$ of the Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson spacetime is not a genuine boundary. We construct its natural analytic extension and show that $r=+\infty$ of one KBR region is smoothly connected to $r=-\infty$ of a neighboring one. Repeated continuation produces an infinite chain of regions connected by wormhole-like bridges and exposes the neighboring ring singularity without an intervening horizon, which violates the weak cosmic censorship conjecture. We then study a test massless scalar field on a two-universe scattering segment to probe the stability of the spacetime. For axisymmetric perturbations, we analytically establish purely imaginary growing quasinormal modes for every $\ell$ and trace their origin to the chronology-violating region. In the $(\ell,m)=(2,2)$ sector, unstable branches occur for sufficiently large rotation and sufficiently small magnetic field. Their marginal real modes obey an exact horizon-flux balance, supporting a black-hole-bomb interpretation in which superradiant extraction is amplified by trapping within the double-barrier potential. The same cavity also supports families of weakly damped modes and may produce echo-like responses.

一句话导读：对 Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson 时空的解析延拓与标量场扰动分析，发现环形奇点可因延拓而裸露、违反弱宇宙监督，并存在由超辐射与双势垒耦合导致的黑洞炸弹型拟正规不稳定模。

相关度：high — 该文属于用户关注的经典引力/广义相对论支线，且包含QNM与黑洞炸弹稳定性分析；未涉及全息/AdS-CFT。

经典引力/广义相对论QNM与非线性演化

NRSur7dq4v2：一种具有改进精度的多域进动替代模型

NRSur7dq4v2: A multi-domain precessing surrogate model with improved accuracy

Abhishek Ravishankar, Vijay Varma, Scott E. Field, Keefe Mitman, Nils Deppe, Leo C. Stein, Michael Boyle, Mark A. Scheel · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：数值相对论模拟为双黑洞并合提供了最精确的波形，但直接用于引力波数据分析成本过高。替代模型克服了这一成本，NRSur7dq4 因此常用于参数估计。然而，文献中有证据表明 NRSur7dq4 在波形的并合-铃宕部分的精度仍有改进空间，而该部分对高质量双黑洞事件的分析尤为重要。基于这些观察，我们构建了 NRSur7dq4v2，它是 NRSur7dq4 的多域扩展，其中重叠的时间子域允许对吸气阶段和并合-铃宕阶段进行更严格、独立的误差控制，之后再将它们平滑组合。为评估新模型在并合-铃宕区域的性能，我们通过准正规振荡拟合代理波形的铃宕部分，推断残余黑洞的质量和自旋。我们发现 NRSur7dq4v2 产生的残余质量和自旋估计显著改善，增益约为 NRSur7dq4 的 3 到 10 倍。与 NRSur7dq4 相比，NRSur7dq4v2 还包含高达 l=5 的模，某些次主导模的建模更精确，并引入了运行时模型复杂度功能，使用户能够直接控制评估成本与精度之间的权衡。最后，除模型开发外，我们还优化了 gwsurrogate 包，使进动替代模型的加速达到四倍。由此通过 gwsurrogate 调用的进动替代模型现在比 LALSimulation 中的对应模型略快。

English: Numerical relativity simulations provide the most accurate waveforms for binary black hole coalescences, but are prohibitively expensive for direct use in gravitational-wave data analysis. Surrogate models overcome this cost, and NRSur7dq4 is commonly used in parameter estimation for this reason. However, there is evidence in the literature that NRSur7dq4's accuracy could be improved in the merger-ringdown portion of the waveform, which is particularly important for the analysis of high-mass binary black hole events. Motivated by these observations, we construct NRSur7dq4v2, a multi-domain extension of NRSur7dq4 in which overlapping temporal subdomains allow tighter, independent error control over the inspiral and merger-ringdown portions of the waveform before they are smoothly combined. To assess the new model's performance in the merger-ringdown regime, we infer the mass and spin of the remnant black hole from quasi-normal fits to the ringdown portion of the surrogate waveform. We find that NRSur7dq4v2 produces significantly improved remnant mass and spin estimates, with gains of roughly factors of $3$ to $10$ over NRSur7dq4. Compared to NRSur7dq4, NRSur7dq4v2 also includes modes up to $\ell=5$, includes more accurate modeling of certain subdominant modes, and introduces a runtime model-complexity feature that gives users direct control over the tradeoff between evaluation cost and accuracy. Finally, alongside the model development, we have optimized the gwsurrogate package to achieve a fourfold speedup for precessing surrogates. The resulting precessing surrogates called through gwsurrogate are now slightly faster than their LALSimulation counterparts.

一句话导读：构建了多域代理模型 NRSur7dq4v2，改善双黑洞并合波形的并合-铃宕部分精度，并显著提升残余黑洞质量与自旋估计。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力与广义相对论下的数值相对论/引力波波形建模，与用户指定全息支线无关，但与经典引力重点标签相符。

现实各向异性中子星的非径向振荡：极向模

Nonradial oscillations of realistic anisotropic neutron stars: Polar modes

L. M. Becerra, Jos\'e F. Rodr\'iguez-Ruiz, E. A. Becerra-Vergara, F. D. Lora-Clavijo · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE, astro-ph.SR · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：在这项工作中，我们研究了具有各向异性压力的静态、球对称中子星在全广义相对论下的极向扰动，包括度规和流体的线性阶扰动。我们使用对径向矢量 k^α 扰动的一致处理来计算 f 模频率及对应的阻尼时间。特别地，其拉格朗日扰动 Δk^α 由度规扰动和流体拉格朗日位移决定，并被约束在 (ū, k̄) 平面内，其中 ū^α 是归一化的流体四速度。这一约束在扰动方程中引入了一个额外的动力学自由度。考虑三种状态方程以及 Horvat 和 Bowers-Liang 各向异性处方，我们发现 f 模频率随星体质量增加，范围从 1 到 3 kHz，而阻尼时间减小，范围从 0.5 到 1.25 秒。在切向压力超过径向压力的意义上增加各向异性通常降低振荡频率，而其对阻尼时间的影响取决于各向异性处方：对于 Horvat 模型，阻尼时间随各向异性增加而减小，但对于 Bowers-Liang 模型则随各向异性增加而增大。我们进一步发现 f 模频率的实部和虚部 Mω_R 和 Mω_I 与星体紧致度 C=M/R 之间的准普适关系，该关系对状态方程基本不敏感。这些关系的多项式拟合精度优于 10%，为通过未来的星震学观测约束中子星压力各向异性提供了一个简单的现象学框架。

English: In this work, we study the polar perturbations of static, spherically symmetric neutron stars with anisotropic pressure in full general relativity, including linear-order perturbations of both the metric and the fluid. We calculate the $f$-mode frequencies and the corresponding damping times using a consistent treatment of the perturbation of the radial vector $k^\alpha$. In particular, its Lagrangian perturbation $\Delta k^\alpha$ is determined by the metric perturbations and the fluid Lagrangian displacement and is constrained to the $(\tilde{u},\tilde{k})$ plane, where $\tilde{u}^\alpha$ is the normalized fluid four-velocity. This constraint introduces an additional dynamical degree of freedom into the perturbation equations. Considering three equations of state and the Horvat and Bowers-Liang prescriptions for pressure anisotropy, we find that the $f$-mode frequency increases with stellar mass, ranging from $1$ to $3$~kHz, while the damping time decreases, ranging from $0.5$ to $1.25$~s. Increasing anisotropy, in the sense of tangential pressure exceeding radial pressure, generally lowers the oscillation frequency, while its effect on the damping time depends on the anisotropy prescription: the damping time decreases with increasing anisotropy for the Horvat model but increases with increasing anisotropy for the Bowers-Liang model. We further find quasi-universal relations between the real and imaginary parts of the $f$-mode frequency, $M\omega_R$ and $M\omega_I$, and the stellar compactness $\mathcal{C}=M/R$, which are largely insensitive to the equation of state. Polynomial fits to these relations achieve an accuracy better than $10\%$, providing a simple phenomenological framework for constraining neutron-star pressure anisotropy through future asteroseismology observations.

一句话导读：在广义相对论框架下计算具有各向异性压力的静态球对称中子星的极向扰动，发现 f 模频率与阻尼时间对状态方程几乎无关的准普适关系。

相关度：high — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论下的中子星相对论振荡研究，与用户给出的'经典引力/广义相对论'重点支线直接相关。

动力学 Chern-Simons 引力中旋转黑洞的 Kerr 参考解析参数化

Kerr-referenced analytical parametrization of rotating black holes in dynamical Chern-Simons gravity

Sardor Murodov, Bahodir Ahmedov, Bekzod Rahmatov, Javlon Rayimbaev, Bobomurat Ahmedov · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：动力学 Chern-Simons 引力中的旋转黑洞非微扰解主要依赖数值求解，这限制其在强场中的应用。本文利用紧致径向坐标、低阶角向多极子和连分式，构造了接近广义相对论的数值分支的 Kerr 参考解析参数化。所得双参数族在验证过的弱耦合和中等自旋区域能精确重现数值度规。独立于标定集合的离格解证实该参数化在标定集之外仍保持精度。作者还通过赤道光环、临界碰撞参数以及不假设可分离性的完整二维零测地线光线追踪进一步评估该模型。所得阴影观测量与数值几何结果高度一致。因此，该参数化提供了无需依赖慢转动展开的实用解析度规，可用于测地线和强场计算。

English: Rotating black holes in dynamical Chern-Simons gravity are known nonperturbatively mainly through numerical solutions, which limits their direct use in strong-field applications. We construct a Kerr-referenced analytical parametrization of the near-general-relativistic numerical branch using a compact radial coordinate, low-order angular multipoles, and continued fractions. The resulting two-parameter family accurately reproduces the numerical metric throughout the validated weak-coupling and moderate-spin domain. Independent off-grid solutions confirm that the parametrization retains its accuracy away from the calibration set. We further assess the model through equatorial light rings, critical impact parameters, and full two-dimensional null-geodesic ray tracing without assuming separability. The resulting shadow observables closely match those obtained from the numerical geometries. This parametrization therefore provides a practical analytical metric for geodesic and strong-field calculations without relying on a slow-rotation expansion.

一句话导读：构造 dCS 引力旋转黑洞的 Kerr 参考解析参数化，并在测地线与阴影观测中准确复现数值结果。

相关度：high — 论文研究动力学 Chern-Simons 修正引力中旋转黑洞的解析参数化及测地线、阴影观测量，属于经典广义相对论/修正引力与黑洞强场物理范畴，不含全息或量子引力内容。

爱因斯坦方程特征初值问题的Picard迭代

Picard Iteration for the Characteristic Initial Value Problem in Einstein Equations

Shengrong Wu · gr-qc, cs.NA, math.NA, physics.comp-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们提出在双零规范下真空及爱因斯坦标量场方程的迭代算法，将非线性偏微分方程转化为常微分方程组。数值实现结合了特征约束求解、LGL谱元、无极点球面算子、Galerkin投影以及独立的一阶残差与一致性检验。

English: We present an iteration algorithm for vacuum and Einstein scalar-field equations in double-null gauge, which transform the non-linear PDE into systems of ODE. The numerical realization combines characteristic constraint solves, LGL spectral elements, pole-free spherical operators, Galerkin projection, and independent first-order residual and consistency checks.

一句话导读：提出一种针对爱因斯坦方程特征初值问题的Picard迭代数值方法，将非线性PDE化为ODE系统。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论中的数值相对论方法研究，与理论全息无直接关联，但与用户关注的经典引力/广义相对论支线相符。

带挠率毛的黑洞在三次Holst型庞加莱规范引力中：从奇异到正则几何

Black holes with torsion hair in cubic Holst-type Poincar\'e gauge gravity: from singular to regular geometries

Sebastian Bahamonde, Jorge Gigante Valcarcel, Matteo Magi · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：受庞加莱规范（PG）理论中的奇点定理启发，我们通过将曲率和挠率张量构造的三阶不变量引入引力作用量，研究了Holst二次模型的扩展。这类模型的特征是由一个赝标量模式控制的动力学结构，而挠率的其余不可约模式通过非线性相互作用贡献，这些相互作用可能对时空几何产生重要影响。特别是，与其他知名的PG理论模型一致，Birkhoff定理一般不成立，从而允许具有动力学挠率的新的精确静态球对称黑洞解。在对应于所涉挠率场的不可约模式和宇称分量的不同挠率扇区中，我们同时发现了奇异和正则构型。在奇异解中，我们获得了Kiselev类和Boulware-Deser类几何，以及具有不同代数和Lambert W度规修正的新几何。此外，我们找到了同时具有初级和次级挠率毛的正则黑洞，它们通过非线性挠率相互作用引起的因果收敛条件破坏而规避了奇点定理。因此，我们表明Holst型PG模型能够支持多种多样的黑洞几何，同时为规避PG理论中的奇点定理提供了明确机制。

English: Motivated by the singularity theorems of Poincar\'e Gauge (PG) theory, we investigate extensions of the Holst quadratic model by introducing cubic order invariants constructed from the curvature and torsion tensors into the gravitational action. Such models are characterised by a kinetic structure that is governed by a pseudoscalar mode, whereas the remaining irreducible modes of torsion contribute through nonlinear interactions that can have important implications for the space-time geometry. In particular, in line with other well-known models of PG theory, the Birkhoff theorem does not hold in general, allowing for new exact static and spherically symmetric black hole solutions with dynamical torsion. Across the different torsion sectors, corresponding to the irreducible modes and parity components of the torsion field involved in the analysis, we find both singular and regular configurations. Among the singular solutions, we obtain Kiselev-like and Boulware-Deser-like geometries, as well as new geometries with distinct algebraic and Lambert $W$ metric corrections. In addition, we find regular black holes with both primary and secondary torsion hair, which evade the singularity theorems through violations of the causal convergence conditions induced by the nonlinear torsion interactions. Therefore, we show that Holst-type PG models can support a rich variety of black hole geometries, while providing explicit mechanisms for evading the singularity theorems of PG theory.

一句话导读：研究三次Holst型庞加莱规范引力中带挠率毛的黑洞解，揭示了从奇异到正则的几何，并展示了如何突破奇点定理。

相关度：medium — 论文专注于经典引力理论中的规范引力和黑洞解，符合用户重点标签中的经典引力支线，但与全息/AdS等主线无关。

TARTARUS：一种用于弯曲时空光线追踪的高性能 Python 代码

TARTARUS: A High-Performance Python Code for Ray Tracing in Curved Spacetimes

David F. Bambague, Alexis Larra\~naga · gr-qc, astro-ph.IM · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：事件地平线望远镜（EHT）合作组近期对超大质量黑洞 M87* 和人马座 A* 的观测表明，需要稳健、高效且易获取的数值工具来模拟强弯曲时空中的光传播。将理论吸积模型与观测数据进行比较，在很大程度上依赖于光线追踪算法来计算光子从发射等离子体传播到远处观测者的轨迹。在本文中，我们提出了 TARTARUS（Tracer for Astrophysical Ray Trajectories Around Relativistic Ultra-compact Sources），这是一个高度模块化、可扩展的基于 Python 的框架，用于计算致密天体周围的零测地线。通过利用 Numba 对 Python 进行即时（JIT）编译，TARTARUS 弥合了 Python 的高级可访问性与 C++ 或 FORTRAN 等编译语言的执行速度之间的差距。该代码支持多种原生求解器（包括自适应嵌入 Runge-Kutta 对、Bulirsch-Stoer 外推器和 Verlet 格式）、用于处理结构相交（如事件视界和吸积盘）的事件检测机制，以及对解析和数值生成的背景度量的支持。我们通过一些测试展示了该代码的物理准确性和计算效率，包括哈密顿约束守恒的评估，以及绕 Kerr 黑洞的黑洞阴影和 Novikov-Thorne 薄吸积盘的光线追踪。

English: The recent observations of the supermassive black holes M87* and Sagittarius A* by the Event Horizon Telescope collaboration (EHT) have shown the need for robust, efficient, and accessible numerical tools to model light propagation in strongly curved spacetimes. Comparing theoretical accretion models with observational data relies heavily on ray-tracing algorithms that calculate the trajectories of photons traveling from a source plasma to a distant observer. In this paper, we present TARTARUS (Tracer for Astrophysical Ray Trajectories Around Relativistic Ultra-compact Sources), a new, highly modular, and extensible Python-based framework for computing null geodesics around compact objects. By leveraging Just-In-Time (JIT) compilation via Numba, TARTARUS bridges the gap between Python's high-level accessibility and the execution speed of compiled languages like C++ or FORTRAN. The code supports multiple native solvers (including adaptive embedded Runge-Kutta pairs, a Bulirsch-Stoer extrapolator, and a Verlet scheme), event-detection mechanics for handling structural intersections (e.g. event horizons and accretion disks), and support for both analytical and numerically generated background metrics. We demonstrate the code's physical accuracy and computational efficiency through some tests, including the evaluation of the Hamiltonian constraint conservation and ray-tracing of the shadow and the Novikov-Thorne thin accretion disks around a Kerr black hole.

一句话导读：提出一个高性能 Python 光线追踪框架，用于弯曲时空中的零测地线计算及 Kerr 黑洞阴影和薄吸积盘成像。

相关度：medium — 该论文主要关注经典广义相对论中的数值光线追踪与黑洞成像工具，是用户重点标签中经典引力/广义相对论支线的内容，但与全息原理或量子引力方向无直接关联。

非旋转准圆双星的二阶Teukolsky计算

Second-order Teukolsky calculations for nonspinning, quasicircular binaries

Benjamin Leather, Andrew Spiers, Adam Pound, Samuel D. Upton, Barry Wardell, Leanne Durkan, Niels Warburton · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：目前，唯一的二阶引力自力计算基于在Lorenz规范下直接求解微扰Einstein方程。该方法依赖于Einstein方程在Schwarzschild背景下的完全可分离性。本文提出了一种基于二阶Teukolsky方程的新方案。关键的是，该方法有望（相当直接地）推广到更具现实意义的Kerr背景。这里我们在绕Schwarzschild黑洞的准圆轨道这一最简设置中实现该方案。除了使用Teukolsky方程外，该方案还包含了相对于以往二阶自力计算的若干改进：紧致双曲切片、变换到Bondi-Sachs规范，以及谱方法与参数变分法的结合。我们还利用这些工具重新审视了二阶Lorenz规范计算中出现的红外发散，发现在Teukolsky情形下发散较弱，并在Bondi-Sachs规范中被完全消除。最后我们计算了渐近能量通量，并与之前的Lorenz规范计算结果进行了基准比较。

English: Currently, the only second-order gravitational self-force calculations have been based on directly solving the perturbative Einstein equations in the Lorenz gauge. That method relied on the complete separability of the Einstein equations in a Schwarzschild background. In this paper, we present a new scheme based on the second-order Teukolsky equation. Crucially, this method promises to extend (reasonably straightforwardly) to the more realistic case of a Kerr background. Here we implement the scheme in the simplest setting of quasicircular orbits around a Schwarzschild black hole. In addition to working with the Teukolsky equation, our scheme incorporates several other advances over previous second-order self-force calculations: compactified hyperboloidal slicing, transformation to a Bondi-Sachs gauge, and a combination of spectral and variation-of-parameters methods. We also use these tools to re-examine the infrared divergences that arise in second-order Lorenz-gauge calculations, showing they are less pronounced in the Teukolsky case and completely eliminated in the Bondi-Sachs gauge. We conclude by calculating the asymptotic energy fluxes and benchmarking them against previous Lorenz-gauge calculations.

一句话导读：提出基于二阶Teukolsky方程的引力自力计算新方案，有望推广到Kerr背景，并处理了红外发散问题。

相关度：low — 该论文专注于经典广义相对论中的二阶引力自力和Teukolsky方程，不涉及全息原理或量子引力，因此与理论全息研究的相关性较低。

利用引力波聚类打破双星形成机制简并

Breaking binary formation mechanism degeneracies with gravitational wave clustering

Nicola Bellomo, Michele Bosi, Sarah Libanore, Michele Liguori, Michela Mapelli, Federico Semenzato, Stefano Torniamenti · gr-qc, astro-ph.CO · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：得益于已探测到的近400个引力波事件，我们目前能够把握黑洞质量、自旋和距离分布的基本特征。然而，测量精度的快速提升并不一定对应着对引力波源的理论认知的深化，尤其是在当前理论模型的自由参数数量显著大于推断出的黑洞总体性质的背景下。本文展示了如何通过互补的数据分析策略，特别是研究引力波各向异性分布的统计性质，来逐步消减理论模型的空间。具体而言，我们展示了引力波聚类对每种双星形成机制的两个独特特征敏感：时延分布和双星寄主星系的性质。首先，我们考虑一个模型无关的场景，展示不同时延分布对引力波偏置的影响。然后，我们考虑一个现实场景，其中引力波事件来源于孤立双星演化或球状星团中的动力学过程，并研究引力波偏置如何对环境的具体性质具有独特敏感性。在两种场景中，我们都展示了星系与引力波星表之间的互相关能够区分具有不同时延或由特定形成渠道产生的不同双星子总体的模型。

English: Thanks to the almost 400 gravitational wave events detected, we are currently able to grasp the fundamental features of black hole mass, spin, and distance distributions. However, such a fast increase in the precision of the measurements does not necessarily correspond to a better theoretical understanding of gravitational wave sources, especially in current scenarios where the number of free parameters is significantly larger than the number of inferred properties of the black hole population. In this work, we showcase how the landscape of theoretical models can be chipped away by complementary data-analysis strategies, in particular by studying the statistical properties of gravitational wave anisotropic distribution. Specifically, we show how gravitational wave clustering is sensitive to two unique features of each binary formation mechanism: the time-delay distribution and the properties of the binary hosts. First, we consider a model-agnostic scenario and show the impact that different time-delay distributions have on the gravitational wave bias. Then, we consider a realistic scenario where gravitational wave events are sourced either by isolated binary evolution or dynamical processes in globular clusters, and study how the gravitational wave bias is unique sensitive to the specific properties of the environment. In both scenarios, we show how the cross-correlation between galaxy and gravitational wave catalogs is able to distinguish between models with different time delays or with different binary sub-populations originated in specific formation channels.

一句话导读：通过星系-引力波互相关探测引力波聚类，揭示双星形成机制中时延分布与寄主环境的关键特征。

相关度：medium — 论文聚焦引力波天体物理观测与统计方法，与全息理论无关，但属于经典引力/广义相对论框架下的引力波现象研究，符合经典引力支线。

“它正在离我们远去！”——黑洞视界与相对速度

"It's getting away from us!" - Black Hole Horizons and Relative Speed

Markus P\"ossel · physics.pop-ph, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：黑洞对公众和学生都具有相当大的吸引力，通常包含在普通天文学或现代物理的通识课程中。但教授黑洞基础知识面临相当大的挑战：任何严谨的描述都需要广义相对论（爱因斯坦的几何与引力理论）的概念和技术。而对该理论任何半严谨的介绍，包括所需的数学工具，都远超通识课程的水平。因此，在本科入门物理的叙述中，不可避免地借助类比、近似和简化模型来教授黑洞、引力波、引力透镜或宇宙学等主题。本文的目的是介绍一组用于教授黑洞的特定类比，所有这些类比都基于对黑洞附近观测者运动的建模，并依赖（相对）速度的概念来描述黑洞的性质。虽然我所要描述的大多数内容可以在现有文献中找到，但我不知道有任何文本试图在适合本科教学的水平上将这些内容整合成一个统一图景；这就是本文的目标。

English: Black holes hold considerable fascination for the general public and students alike, and are commonly included in general-science courses on astronomy or modern physics. But teaching about the basics of black holes poses a considerable challenge: Any rigorous description requires concepts and techniques from general relativity, Einstein's theory of geometry and gravitation. And any half-way rigorous introduction to that theory, including the required mathematical tools, is significantly beyond the level of general-science courses. Inevitably, accounts of relativistic physics at the introductory undergraduate level make use of analogies, approximations and simplified models to teach about topics like black holes, gravitational waves, gravitational lensing, or cosmology. The purpose of this article is to given an account of one particular set of analogies for teaching about black holes, all of which are based on modelling the motions of observers in the vicinity of the black hole and rely on the concept of (relative) speed to describe properties of the black hole. While most elements of what I am about to describe can be found in the existing literature, I am not aware of any text that attempts to pull them together into a unified picture at a suitable level of presentation for undergraduate-level teaching; that is the goal of the present text.

一句话导读：一篇面向本科通识教学的黑洞类比文章，利用相对速度来描述黑洞视界附近的观测者运动。

相关度：medium — 文章专注于经典引力与黑洞教学，属于用户关注的“经典引力/广义相对论”范畴，但并非原创研究，而是一种科普式综述。

层次三星系统中黑洞并合时间的精度

The accuracy of merger times in hierarchical black hole triples

Giulia Fumagalli, Alejandro Vigna-G\'omez · astro-ph.HE, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：轨道平均方程被广泛用于模拟致密双星在引力波驱动下的演化。其有效性依赖于辐射反作用时标远长于轨道周期，而对于高偏心系统这一假设可能不成立。本文通过比较轨道平均与非轨道平均方法，研究轨道平均对并合时间估计的影响。我们将分析应用于层次三星系统中两个不同天体物理动机下的黑洞双星族群，其中von Zeipel-Lidov-Kozai振荡可使内双星偏心率接近1。我们发现，在大范围偏心率下，轨道平均方程得到的并合时间估计仍非常准确，即使轨道时标超过辐射反作用时标。但当双星最为极端且致密时——同时具有极高偏心率和较小的半正焦弦——会出现显著差异。在此区域，并合时间强烈依赖于初始轨道相位，不同相位可能导致预测差几个数量级。我们还将结果与一个专门为层次三星系统发展的轨道平均并合时间公式进行比较，发现该公式与通用轨道平均及非轨道平均计算在高偏心区间均有较大差异。这些结果虽然表明对大多数系统轨道平均并合时间估计的稳健性，但也强调了在利用并合时间推断致密双星延迟时间分布和形成通道时，非轨道平均效应潜在的重要性。

English: Orbit-averaged equations are widely used to model the gravitational-wave-driven evolution of compact binaries. Their validity relies on the radiation-reaction timescale being much longer than the orbital period, an assumption that can break down for highly eccentric systems. In this work, we investigate the impact of orbit averaging on merger-time estimates by comparing orbit-averaged and non-orbit-averaged prescriptions. We apply our analysis to two different astrophysically motivated populations of black hole binaries in hierarchical triple systems, where von Zeipel-Lidov-Kozai oscillations can drive the inner binary eccentricities close to unity. We find that merger-time estimates obtained with orbit-averaged equations remain remarkably accurate across a wide range of eccentricities, even when the orbital timescale exceeds the radiation-reaction timescale. Significant discrepancies arise only for the most extreme and compact binaries, simultaneously characterized by very high eccentricities and small semi-latus recta. In this regime, the merger time becomes strongly dependent on the initial orbital phase, with different phases yielding predictions that can differ by several orders of magnitude. We also compare our results with an orbit-averaged merger-time prescription specifically developed for hierarchical triple systems and find substantial differences between this prescription and both the generic orbit-averaged and non-orbit-averaged calculations in the high-eccentricity regime. While these results demonstrate the robustness of orbit-averaged merger-time estimates for most systems, they also highlight the potential importance of non-orbit-averaged effects when merger times are used to infer compact-binary delay-time distributions and formation channels.

一句话导读：比较轨道平均与非轨道平均方法在层次三星黑洞双星并合时间估计中的精度，发现多数情况稳健，但极端高偏心紧致双星存在显著相位依赖偏差。

AthenaK 广义相对论磁流体力学的一种 HLLD 实现

An HLLD Implementation for General Relativistic Magnetohydrodynamics in AthenaK

Jacob Fields, George N. Wong, James M. Stone · astro-ph.HE, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们提出了在 AthenaK 天体物理代码中实现 HLLD 近似黎曼求解器，通过 tetrad 框架变换支持完整的广义相对论磁流体力学（GRMHD）。该实现利用 HLLD 迭代求解的初始猜测，消除了额外的守恒到原初变量反演的需要，大幅提升了性能而不影响精度。此外，通过与一阶通量修正耦合，该方法在磁化强度超过 10^4 时仍能可靠使用，并在 SANE 吸积盘中实现了这一结果。与 HLLE 相比，SANE 盘显示 HLLD 产生了更强磁化的漏斗和更精确的视界通量。我们还将新的 HLLD 实现应用于等质量双中子星并合。对于长寿命残骸，HLLD 增强了外层磁剪切应力，导致较弱的较差自转。然而，并合后期引力波辐射较弱，残骸持续表现出较低的致密度，同时产生更多动力学抛射物和更巨大的吸积盘。得益于改进的初始猜测，新求解器的成本相对适中：吸积盘测试仅比 HLLE 慢约 10-25%，而在使用微观物理状态方程的双中子星运行中，HLLD 比 HLLE 仅慢约 3%。

English: We present an implementation of an HLLD approximate Riemann solver for the AthenaK astrophysics code with support for full general relativistic magnetohydrodynamics via a tetrad frame transformation. Our implementation uses an initial guess for the HLLD iterative solve which eliminates the need for an additional conserved-to-primitive inversion, which greatly accelerates performance without affecting accuracy. Additionally, by coupling the method with a first-order flux correction, we are able to use the method reliably even when the magnetization exceeds $10^4$, which we achieve in a SANE accretion disk. Our SANE disk shows that HLLD leads to a more strongly magnetized funnel and more accurate horizon fluxes when compared with HLLE. We further apply the new HLLD implementation to an equal-mass binary neutron star merger. For our long-lived remnant, HLLD enhances the magnetic shear stresses in the outer layers and leads to weaker differential rotation. However, due to weaker gravitational wave emissions in the post-merger phase, the remnant is consistently less compact while producing more dynamical ejecta and a more massive disk. The cost of this new solver is relatively modest thanks to the improved initial guess: our accretion disk tests are only ${\sim}10-25\%$ slower than HLLE, and for our binary neutron star runs with a microphysical equation of state, we find that HLLD is only ${\sim}3\%$ slower than HLLE across all runs.

一句话导读：一项在 AthenaK 代码中实现 HLLD 求解器用于 GRMHD 模拟的方法论文，展示了在吸积盘和双中子星并合中的性能改进与物理差异。

相关度：low — 本文专注数值方法与天体物理模拟，不涉及全息原理或量子引力理论问题。

用第三代引力波探测器约束中子星状态方程

Constraining the Equation of State of Neutron Stars with third-generation Gravitational Wave detectors

Zhenyu Zhu, Richard O'Shaughnessy · astro-ph.HE, gr-qc, nucl-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们研究了双中子星并合事件数量和中子星质量分布对用第三代探测器约束中子星状态方程(EoS)、潮汐形变和半径以及核参数的影响。我们在给定事件数量和质量分布模型后生成模拟引力波信号，并计算每个事件的Fisher信息矩阵。通过将非EoS波形参数固定在其注入值并累积所有事件的Fisher矩阵，得到EoS参数的协方差。最后基于该协方差矩阵生成EoS、潮汐形变、半径和核参数的后验样本。我们发现更多事件会导致更紧的约束，这归因于观测事件增多和数据累积。同时，质量分布在约束EoS中起更重要作用。我们比较了现实的双峰高斯分布、均匀分布以及包含亚太阳质量中子星的均匀分布。结果表明，均匀分布比双峰模型给出更紧的约束，因为它包含更多低质量和高质量中子星。这也意味着接近1.4倍太阳质量的事件提供了部分冗余的EoS信息。此外，包含亚太阳质量中子星可通过显著降低亚饱和密度和内壳层的EoS不确定性进一步改善约束，凸显了亚饱和密度EoS的重要性。

English: We investigated the impact of the number of binary neutron star merger events and neutron star (NS) mass distributions on constraining the equation of state (EoS), tidal deformability and radius of NSs, as well as the nuclear parameters, using binary neutron star inspiral gravitational wave signals with third-generation detectors. We generate simulated gravitational wave signals and compute the Fisher information matrix for each event after the number of events and mass distribution models are given. The covariance of EoS parameters is obtained by holding the non-EoS waveform parameters fixed at their injected values and accumulating the Fisher matrix over all events. Finally, the posterior samples of EoS, tidal deformability, radius and nuclear parameters are generated based on this covariance matrix. We find that larger number of events lead to tighter constraints due to more observed events and data accumulation, as expected given the increased number of detections. Meanwhile, we note that the mass distribution plays a more important role in constraining the EoS. We compare a realistic bimodal Gaussian distribution, a uniform distribution and another uniform including sub-solar mass NSs. The results show that the uniform distribution yields tighter constraints than bimodal models because it includes more low-mass and massive NSs. This also implies that events near $1.4\,\Msun$ provide partly redundant information about the EoS. Additionally, including sub-solar mass NSs can further improve the constraints by significantly reducing the EoS uncertainties at sub-saturation densities and inner-crust, highlighting the importance of sub-saturation density EoS.

一句话导读：通过模拟第三代引力波探测器事件，研究事件数量和中子星质量分布对状态方程约束的影响。

相关度：high — 论文直接涉及经典引力/广义相对论支线，属于用户重点标注的经典引力研究范畴。

双中子星并合后引力波信号中体粘滞效应的可探测性

Detectability of bulk viscosity effects on post-merger gravitational wave signals from binary neutron star mergers

Zhenyu Zhu, Michail Chabanov, Richard O'Shaughnessy · astro-ph.HE, gr-qc, nucl-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们研究体粘滞（BV）对双中子星（BNS）并合后引力波（PMGW）信号的影响，及其在利用由近期引力波（GW）和X射线观测以及中子皮厚度测量所约束的现实状态方程（EoS）后验样本下的可探测性。使用基于近期含BV的BNS模拟的线性拟合公式来估计由BV效应引起的PMGW峰值频率偏移。随后，我们针对EoS后验样本集合评估该峰值频率偏移。我们使用Fisher信息矩阵以及ET和CE探测器网络在乐观情景下的可观测事件模拟数据集，来估计峰值频率的测量精度。我们发现，BV对PMGW的影响可能仅在具有较大对称能斜率（$L_{\rm sym}$）的EoS模型中可探测，而这正是中子皮厚度测量所倾向的。因此，对PMGW的BV效应的探测本身可以提供关于对称能及其斜率的丰富信息。然而，BV对PMGW的影响通常较弱，可能仅在一些极端和乐观的情况下被观测到。因此，对PMGW的观测及其分析可能无法通过BV效应提供非常精确的对称能测量。

English: We investigate bulk viscosity (BV) effects on post-merger gravitational wave (PMGW) signals from binary neutron star (BNS) mergers and their detectability using realistic equation of state (EoS) posteriors constrained by recent progress in gravitational-wave (GW) and X-ray observations, as well as neutron skin thickness measurements. A linear fitting formula based on recent BNS simulations with BV is used to estimate the peak frequency shift of the PMGWs caused by BV effects. Subsequently, we evaluate this peak frequency shift for our set of EoS posterior samples. We use the Fisher information matrix and the dataset of simulated observable events from the ET and CE detector network in an optimistic scenario to estimate the measurement accuracy of the peak frequency. We find that BV effects on PMGWs may be detectable only for EoS models with large values of the symmetry energy slope ($L_{\rm sym}$), as favored by neutron-skin thickness measurements. Thus, the detection of BV effects on PMGWs itself can provide abundant information about the symmetry energy and its slope. However, BV effects on PMGWs are generally weak and may be observed only in some extreme and optimistic cases. Therefore, observations of PMGWs and their analyses may not be able to provide very accurate measurements of the symmetry energy through BV effects.

一句话导读：Bulk viscosity effects on post-merger gravitational waves from binary neutron star mergers are weak and only detectable in optimistic cases with large symmetry energy slope, limiting their use for precise symmetry energy constraints.

相关度：high — Studies classical gravitational wave signals from binary neutron star mergers and equation-of-state effects, matching the classical gravity/GR focus tag.

桥接超大质量双黑洞的族群合成与引力波背景

Bridging the Population Synthesis of Supermassive Binary Black Holes and the Gravitational Wave Background

Kanyuni Iemoto, Boris Goncharov, Gabriela Sato-Polito, Xiaoming Bi · astro-ph.HE, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：脉冲星计时阵列(PTA)通过观测引力波背景(GWB)约束超大质量双黑洞(SMBHB)的族群性质。与内插族群合成库或仅考虑应变谱均值的方法不同，这里我们直接从半解析族群模型中捕捉其完整的应变概率密度。我们将新方法应用于半解析SMBHB族群模型，独立复现了NANOGrav合作组使用15年数据对该模型进行的参数估计。我们还展示了GWB中离散SMBHB贡献在多大程度上解除族群参数空间中的简并。最后，使用源计数强度作为我们计算的中间产物，我们以原理验证方式将PTA观测映射到基于数值流体动力学模拟“Illustris”的星系合并规则得出的SMBHB模型。我们发现星系合并后千秒差距到亚秒差距SMBHB间隔的延迟时间τ的影响，得到hc范围为(1-6)×10^-16，Nc范围为(0.3-7.1)×10^-3，τ最高可达8 Gyr。

English: Pulsar Timing Arrays (PTAs) constrain population properties of supermassive binary black holes (SMBHBs) through the observation of the gravitational wave background (GWB). Unlike other approaches that interpolate population-synthesis libraries or only consider the mean of the strain spectrum, here we capture its full strain probability density directly from semi-analytic population models. We apply our new method to the semi-analytic SMBHB population model, independently reproducing the parameter estimation for this model performed by the NANOGrav Collaboration with their 15-yr data. We also show the extent to which discrete SMBHB contributions to the GWB resolve degeneracies in the population parameter space. Finally, using the source-count intensity as the intermediate product in our calculation, we map PTA observations, as a proof of principle, to the SMBHB model based on the galaxy merger prescriptions from numerical hydrodynamical simulations "Illustris". We find the effect of delay times $\tau$ between kiloparsec and subparsec SMBHB separations following galaxy mergers, finding $h_{\rm c}$ spanning $(1$-$6)\times10^{-16}$ and $N_{\rm c}$ spanning $(0.3$-$7.1)\times10^{-3}$ for $\tau$ up to 8 Gyr.

一句话导读：本文提出直接从半解析族群模型捕获完整应变概率密度的方法，并应用于SMBHB模型以复现NANOGrav结果及约束合并延迟时间。

相关度：low — 用户重点关注全息引力与凝聚态类比，本论文是经典天体物理引力波背景研究，与全息无关，仅与经典引力应用相关，故相关度低。

利用GFH-v2管道搜寻来自SN2023ixf的长暂态引力波

Search for Long-Transient Gravitational Waves from Supernova SN2023ixf using GFH-v2 Pipeline

Sandhya Sajith Menon, Lorenzo Pierini, Pia Astone, Cristiano Palomba, Francesco Safai Tehrani, Lorenzo Silvestri, Ornella Juliana Piccinni, Simone Dall'Osso, Stefano Dal Pra, Sabrina D'Antonio, Sergio Frasca, Dafne Guetta, Paola Leaci, Michal Bejger, Alicia Calafat, Evan Goetz, Rafel Jaume Amengual, Ian Jones, David Keitel, Andrzej Krolak, Iuri La Rosa, Andrew Melatos, Joan-Rene Merou, Lorenzo Mirasola, Rosa Poggiani, Claudio Salvadore, Alicia M. Sintes, Karl Wette, Michele Zanolin · astro-ph.IM, astro-ph.HE, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们针对SN2023ixf（M101星系中一颗附近的II型核心坍缩超新星）可能的新生磁星残骸，展示了一次定向搜寻长暂态引力波。分析使用LIGO Hanford和Livingston在工程运行15期间的数据，并利用与超新星相关的在源时间窗内的重合数据。我们目标是快速旋转、非轴对称中子星发出的信号，其自转减慢由引力波发射主导，导致频率呈幂律下降，应变幅度相应减小。搜寻使用基于广义频率Hough变换的GFH-v2管道执行。没有候选体通过重合与后续分析。因此，我们给出了作为初频和椭率函数的最大可探测距离上限。对于最高椭率区间，90%上限在分析的大多数频带中达到约1-2.5 Mpc的距离。尽管这些上限低于M101的距离，但此次搜寻提供了GFH-v2对附近核心坍缩超新星的首次应用，并表征了其在真实探测器数据上的性能。

English: We present a directed search for long-transient gravitational waves from the possible newborn magnetar remnant of SN2023ixf, a nearby Type II core-collapse supernova in the M101 galaxy. The analysis uses LIGO Hanford and Livingston data from Engineering Run 15, using coincident data lying within the on-source window associated with the supernova. We target signals from a rapidly rotating, non-axisymmetric neutron star whose spin-down is dominated by gravitational-wave emission, producing a power-law decrease in frequency and a corresponding decrease in strain amplitude. The search is performed with the GFH-v2 pipeline, based on the Generalized Frequency Hough transform. No candidate survives the coincidence and follow-up analysis. We therefore set upper limits on the maximum detectable distance as a function of initial frequency and ellipticity. For the highest ellipticity interval, the 90% upper limits reach distances of about 1-2.5~Mpc across most of the analysed band. Although these limits are below the distance to M101, the search provides the first application of GFH-v2 to a nearby core-collapse supernova and characterizes its performance on real detector data.

一句话导读：利用GFH-v2管道对SN2023ixf可能磁星残骸的长暂态引力波进行定向搜索，未发现候选体并设置了距离上限。

相关度：low — 该研究是经典的引力波数据搜索，与全息理论或量子引力无关，仅涉及广义相对论框架下的天体物理信号分析。

近视界潮汐瓦解事件

Near-Horizon Tidal Disruption Events

Yang Yang, Xian Chen · astro-ph.HE, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：潮汐瓦解事件（TDEs）为研究超大质量黑洞周围的时空几何提供了独特的动力学探针。通常的TDEs发生在大约10^6-10^7太阳质量的黑洞周围，恒星在事件视界之外很远的地方被瓦解。然而，本文识别出一类特殊的TDEs，它们围绕质量大于约10^8太阳质量且快速旋转的超大质量黑洞发生，此时潮汐瓦解半径接近引力半径。我们将这些事件称为“近视界TDEs”，并通过计算Kerr时空中的测地线，研究视界的接近如何改变碎片演化及随后的回落过程。我们发现，对于沿抛物轨道遭遇黑洞的恒星，多达约90%-95%的恒星碎片要么直接坠入黑洞，要么逃离系统。相比之下，束缚轨道能保留显著更大比例的碎片。利用所得的碎片分布，我们计算了回落率，发现束缚轨道产生本质上更高的峰值回落率和更短的衰减时标，相对于抛物轨道约高/短10^3倍。然而，当恒星轨道角动量特别低时，峰值回落率可能因碎片损失到坠入轨道而大幅降低。这种快速回落与质量亏损的结合自然解释了如ASASSN-15lh等超亮TDEs，而标准TDE模型难以重现这些事件。我们的工作确立了近视界TDEs作为强场引力的新探针，并作为识别大质量、快速旋转的超大质量黑洞的有力工具。

English: Tidal disruption events (TDEs) offer a unique dynamical probe of the spacetime geometry around supermassive black holes (SMBHs). While conventional TDEs occur around SMBHs of $M_\bullet \sim 10^6 - 10^7 M_\odot$, where stars are disrupted far from the event horizon, here we identify a special class of TDEs around rapidly spinning SMBHs with masses $M_\bullet \gtrsim 10^8 M_\odot$, where the tidal-disruption radius approaches the gravitational radius. We term these events "near-horizon TDEs" and, by calculating geodesics in Kerr spacetime, investigate how the proximity of the horizon modifies the debris evolution and subsequent fallback. We find that for stars encountering the SMBH on parabolic orbits, up to $\sim (90 \%-95 \%)$ of the stellar debris either plunges directly into the SMBH or escapes the system. Bound orbits, by contrast, retain a substantially larger fraction of the debris. Using the resulting debris distribution, we calculate the fallback rates and find that bound orbits produce intrinsically higher peak fallback rates and shorter decay timescales, by factors of $\sim 10^3$ relative to parabolic orbits. However, when the stellar orbital angular momentum is particularly low, the peak fallback rate can be substantially suppressed by debris lost to plunge orbits. This combination of rapid fallback and a mass deficit naturally explains overluminous TDEs such as ASASSN-15lh, which standard TDE models have struggled to reproduce. Our work establishes near-horizon TDEs as a new probe of strong-field gravity and a promising tool for identifying massive, rapidly spinning SMBHs.

一句话导读：本文提出围绕快速旋转大质量黑洞的近视界潮汐瓦解事件，显示束缚轨道产生高回落率并可解释超亮TDE，为强场引力提供新探针。

相关度：high — 论文明确研究经典引力/广义相对论中的强场时空与测地线，属于用户重点标签之一。

动态引力坍缩中的光子面延伸

Photon surfaces extensions for dynamical gravitational collapse

Roberto Giamb\`o, Camilla Lucamarini · gr-qc · v4 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们从arXiv:gr-qc/0005050出发，在一般动态球对称背景下推导了光子面的方程。我们证明，一个类时超曲面成为光子面的条件可重构为一个非自治动力系统，其分析表明，当该曲面由类光径向测地线生成时，同一条件也成立。作为应用，我们考虑一个著名的球对称尘埃云引力坍缩模型。在边际束缚情形下，光子面条件约化为精确的平面动力系统；分析表明，外部光子面 r=3M 向云内的延伸是唯一的且为类光，当且仅当中心奇点被覆盖；否则存在一开集的类时延伸。我们将所得结果与arXiv:1910.13758中的结果比较，并解析地研究该类光延伸是否覆盖Lemaitre-Tolman-Bondi（LTB）模型中形成的奇点。

English: The equations for the photon surface in spherical symmetry are worked out, starting from arXiv:gr-qc/0005050, in the most general dynamical setting. We show that the condition for a timelike hypersurface to be a photon surface can be reformulated as a non-autonomous dynamical system, whose analysis reveals that the same condition also holds when the surface is generated by a null radial geodesic. As an application, we consider a well-known model of a spherical dust cloud undergoing gravitational collapse. In the marginally bound case the photon surface condition reduces to an exact planar dynamical system, whose analysis shows that the extension of the exterior photon surface $r=3M$ into the cloud is unique, and null, if and only if the central singularity is covered, an open set of timelike extensions being available otherwise. Comparing our findings with those in arXiv:1910.13758, we then investigate analytically whether the null extension covers the singularity developing in the Lemaitre-Tolman-Bondi (LTB) model.

一句话导读：证明球对称引力坍缩中光子面条件可化为动力系统，且尘埃云坍缩时外部 r=3M 光子面延伸的类光唯一性与中心奇点被覆盖等价。

相关度：high — 该论文直接属于用户标注的“经典引力/广义相对论”方向，研究引力坍缩中光子面延伸与中心奇点覆盖的关系，但不涉及全息对应或量子引力。

通过提高探测阈值减少引力波群体分析中的系统不确定性

Reducing systematic uncertainties in gravitational-wave population analyses by increasing the detection threshold

Noah E. Wolfe, Matthew Mould, Jack Heinzel, Salvatore Vitale · gr-qc, astro-ph.IM · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：从引力波瞬变目录中，可以约束其源的天体物理群体特性和合并致密双星的形成通道。然而，天体物理结论可能因群体似然函数的错误设定或错误估计而产生偏差。尽管对误报率（FAR）或信噪比（SNR）设置了探测阈值，当前目录仍可能被噪声瞬变污染。此外，随着目录的增长，群体似然函数的计算精度会下降。当前解决这些问题的方法通常随事件数量增多而缩放性变差，并且可能对未来目录不可行。在这里，我们评估了一个简单的补救措施：提高群体分析中包含事件的显著性阈值。为确定该方法的有效性，我们使用当前探测器灵敏度对来自黑洞合并的最多1600个引力波信号的模拟目录进行了完整贝叶斯参数估计。我们表明，当我们分析更少但SNR更高的事件时，关于黑洞群体的统计不确定性的增长取决于感兴趣的源参数。当SNR阈值从11提高到15——将目录规模减少三分之二——我们发现质量分布的统计不确定性只增长了几个百分之十，自旋分布的约束基本不变；同时，高红移宇宙合并速率的不确定性增加了一倍以上。与此同时，群体似然函数估计的数值不确定性减少了一半以上，使我们能够在没有额外计算开销的情况下确保无偏推理。我们的结果表明，关注更高显著性事件是利用不断增长的引力波目录促进稳健天体物理推断的有效方法。

English: From catalogs of gravitational-wave transients, the population-level properties of their sources and the formation channels of merging compact binaries can be constrained. However, astrophysical conclusions can be biased by misspecification or misestimation of the population likelihood. Despite detection thresholds on the false-alarm rate (FAR) or signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), the current catalog is likely contaminated by noise transients. Further, computing the population likelihood becomes less accurate as the catalog grows. Current methods to address these challenges often scale poorly with the number of events and potentially become infeasible for future catalogs. Here, we evaluate a simple remedy: increasing the significance threshold for including events in population analyses. To determine the efficacy of this approach, we analyze simulated catalogs of up to 1600 gravitational-wave signals from black-hole mergers using full Bayesian parameter estimation with current detector sensitivities. We show that the growth in statistical uncertainty about the black-hole population, as we analyze fewer events but with higher SNR, depends on the source parameters of interest. When the SNR threshold is raised from 11 to 15---reducing our catalog size by two--thirds---we find that statistical uncertainties on the mass distribution only grow by a few 10% and constraints on the spin distribution are essentially unchanged; meanwhile, uncertainties on the high-redshift cosmic merger rate more than double. Simultaneously, numerical uncertainty in the estimate of the population likelihood more than halves, allowing us to ensure unbiased inference without additional computational expense. Our results demonstrate that focusing on higher-significance events is an effective way to facilitate robust astrophysical inference with growing gravitational-wave catalogs.

一句话导读：通过提高信噪比阈值来降低引力波目录群体分析中的系统误差，并以模拟数据论证其有效性。

相关度：low — 论文聚焦于引力波观测数据分析中的统计学方法，不涉及全息原理或量子引力理论构造，仅属经典引力波天体物理范畴。

大质量自旋-2场对引力波传播的影响

Effects of massive spin-2 fields on gravitational wave propagation

Jose A. R. Cembranos, \'Alvaro Cendal, Hector Villarrubia-Rojo · gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：在广义相对论的标准无质量引力子之外，大质量自旋-2场自然出现于广义相对论的扩展理论中，例如大质量双引力或额外维度模型。本工作探索了这些场对引力波传播的观测特征。采用与此类理论一致的现象学框架，我们在极端相对论极限下推导了解析传递函数，并建立了可探测性界限。最后，我们利用当前和未来的引力波探测器，对可探测的参数空间给出了预测。

English: Massive spin-2 fields in addition to the standard massless graviton arise naturally in extensions of General Relativity, such as massive bigravity or models with extra dimensions. This work explores the observational signatures of these fields on the propagation of gravitational waves. Adopting a phenomenological framework consistent with such theories, we derive an analytical transfer function in the ultrarelativistic limit and establish detectability bounds. Finally, we provide forecasts for the accessible parameter space using current and future gravitational wave detectors.

一句话导读：研究大质量自旋-2场对引力波传播的观测效应，给出解析传递函数与可探测性预测。

相关度：low — 该论文集中于经典引力下的引力波传播与大质量自旋-2场效应，与用户关注的全息超流超导、QNM等支线没有直接关联。

不可分离旋转几何的耦合道谱理论：旋转 AdS-Teo 虫洞中的正规模式与双边界响应

Coupled-Channel Spectral Theory for a Non-Separable Rotating Geometry: Normal Modes and Two-Boundary Response in the Rotating AdS-Teo Wormhole

Ramesh Radhakrishnan, Gerald B. Cleaver, William Julius · gr-qc · v4 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们研究了具有受控不可分离角形变的旋转渐近反德西特（AdS）Teo 可穿越虫洞上的标量扰动。几何保持规则虫洞喉部和所需 AdS 渐近行为，显式四极形变在中间区域产生角向道耦合。推导了一个无能层参数区间的充分条件，并在该区间内进行谱分析。将几何导出的 Klein-Gordon 方程投影到球谐函数上，得到矩阵值 Sturm-Liouville 系统及相应的二次算子束。喉部正则性与可归一化 AdS 边界条件给出离散正规模谱的行列式量子化条件。二、四、六通道计算显示在增大角向基时低阶正规模频率具有系统数值收敛性。还在未施加近实选择判据的情况下考察了完整的有穷广义本征谱；在无能层参数范围和所研究的数值分辨率内未发现增长标量模。六通道转动延拓表现出有限内部能级排斥，并伴随各通道本征向量的集体重分配。同一耦合道谱框架形式地定义了矩阵值双边界响应，其极点由正规模匹配条件选取；文中响应图使用约化二通道有效模型说明该结构，而非对完整六通道响应的数值重建。

English: We investigate scalar perturbations of a rotating asymptotically anti-de Sitter (AdS)Teo traversable wormhole with a controlled nonseparable angular deformation. The geometry retains a regular wormhole throat and the required AdS asymptotics, while explicit quadrupolar deformation generates angular-channel coupling in the intermediate region. A sufficient condition is derived for an ergoregion free parameter regime in which the spectral analysis is performed. Projecting the geometry derived Klein Gordon equation onto spherical harmonics yields a matrix valued Sturm Liouville system and an associated quadratic operator pencil. Throat regularity together with normalizable AdS boundary conditions leads to a determinant quantization condition for the discrete normal mode spectrum. Two, four, and six channel calculations demonstrate systematic numerical convergence of the retained low lying normal mode frequencies under enlargement of the angular basis. The complete finite generalized eigenspectrum is also examined without imposing a near reality selection criterion, and no growing scalar mode is found within the ergoregion free parameter range and numerical resolutions studied. The six channel rotation continuation exhibits a finite interior level repulsion feature accompanied by collective redistribution of the multichannel eigenvectors. The same coupled spectral framework formally defines a matrix valued two boundary response whose poles are selected by the normal-mode matching condition, the response plots presented here illustrate this structure using the reduced two channel effective model rather than a numerical reconstruction of the full six channel response.

一句话导读：本文用耦合道谱方法研究旋转 AdS-Teo 虫洞中的标量微扰，计算离散正规模和双边界响应，并在无能层参数范围内未发现增长标量模。

相关度：high — 该论文主要研究旋转 AdS-Teo 虫洞背景上标量扰动的谱分析和线性稳定性，属于经典引力/广义相对论范畴，直接命中用户经典引力支线重点标签；摘要未涉及全息对偶或量子引力内容，故不判为理论全息。
