正则黑洞弦与单模引力中Maxwell场支持的(2+1)维黑洞

Regular Black Strings and $(2+1)$ Black Hole in Unimodular Gravity Supported by Maxwell Fields

G. Alencar, V. H. U. Borralho · gr-qc · v4 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：在这项工作中，我们在单模引力中获得具有动力学宇宙学函数的正则解。这种广义相对论的替代理论通过对时空体积元施加额外条件来修正度量体积密度为一个预设的非动力学量，该量在单模坐标中可表示为常数。在此过程中，宇宙学常数并不直接出现在作用量中，而是作为场方程的积分常数出现。通过利用能量-动量张量的非守恒，我们证明积分常数变为函数Λ(x)=Λ0+δΛ(x)，其中Λ0是真空宇宙学常数，δΛ(r)是由物质诱导的宇宙学贡献，由几何和电磁扇区决定。从几何函数H(r)的定义出发，我们验证了Maxwell电动力学作为这些解的源的有效性。我们还表明，与各个解相关的质量函数的线性组合也可以一致地被Maxwell场支持，这一性质通常不被非线性电动力学所共有。这一结果使我们能够构造更广泛的正则解族并研究其物理性质。特别地，我们分析了所生成解的能量条件、曲率不变量和热力学性质。

English: In this work, we obtain regular solutions with a dynamical cosmological function in unimodular gravity. This alternative theory to General Relativity imposes an additional condition on the spacetime volume element by fixing the metric volume density to a prescribed non-dynamical quantity, which can be represented by a constant in unimodular coordinates. In this procedure, the cosmological constant does not appear directly in the action, but rather as an integration constant of the field equations. By using the non-conservation of the energy-momentum tensor, we show that the integration constant becomes a function $\Lambda(x)=\Lambda_0+\delta\Lambda(x)$, where $\Lambda_0$ is the vacuum cosmological constant and $\delta\Lambda(r)$ is a matter-induced cosmological contribution determined by the geometry and the electromagnetic sector. From the definition of the geometric function $H(r)$, we verify the validity of Maxwell electrodynamics as a source for the solutions. We further show that linear combinations of the mass functions associated with the individual solutions can also be consistently supported by the Maxwell field, a property that is not generally shared by nonlinear electrodynamics. This result allows us to construct a broader family of regular solutions and investigate their physical properties. In particular, we analyze the energy conditions, curvature invariants, and thermodynamic properties of the generated solutions.

一句话导读：单模引力中由Maxwell场支持的正则黑洞弦和(2+1)维黑洞，其宇宙学常数作为物质诱导的动力学函数出现。

相关度：medium — 论文涉及经典引力中的正则黑洞解和热力学，但未涉及全息或量子引力，与重点研究支线无直接关联。

具有动态时序保护的度量仿射引力

Metric affine gravity with dynamical chronology protection

Moustafa Ismail, David Mattingly · gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：修改引力理论常常引入广义相对论之外的几何结构，以在不引入特设物质场的情况下解决引力领域中的未决问题。例如，模拟引力通过分离度规的共形模产生有效的宇宙学暗扇区，而 Horava-Lifshitz 引力通过赋予时空一个优选的动力学叶状结构实现幂次可重整化。尽管时序保护并非这两种理论的原始动机，但二者都能通过稳定因果性经典地实现它。这表明时序保护本身可以提升为构建修改引力理论的指导原则，特别是在其量子引力层面的实现会在有效作用量中留下红外印记的情形下。受此可能性驱动，我们引入一个玩具度量仿射引力模型，其时序保护扇区纯粹是几何性的。该模型破坏射影不变性，并选取一个类时的闭合非度量性一形式。在全局精确扇区中，这一形式定义了一个时间函数，通过仿射几何实现稳定因果性。在无物质、空间平坦的 FLRW 分支上，引力扇区包含 GR 的两个张量模和一个标量模。我们研究了这一纯粹几何的、扩展广义相对论的时序保护方案的经典宇宙学效应及标量模动力学。

English: Modified theories of gravity often introduce geometric structure beyond general relativity to address unresolved problems in the gravitational sector without invoking ad hoc matter fields. Mimetic gravity, for example, generates an effective cosmological dark sector by isolating the conformal mode of the metric, while Horava-Lifshitz gravity attains power-counting renormalizability by endowing spacetime with a preferred dynamical foliation. Although chronology protection was not the original motivation for either theory, both can realize it classically through stable causality. This suggests that chronology protection itself may be elevated from a derived property to a guiding principle for constructing modified gravitational theories, especially if its implementation at the quantum-gravitational level leaves infrared imprints in the effective action. Motivated by this possibility, we introduce a toy metric-affine gravity model whose chronology-protecting sector is purely geometric. The model breaks projective invariance and selects a timelike, closed non-metricity one-form. In the globally exact sector, this one-form defines a time function, realizing stable causality through affine geometry. On the matter-free, spatially flat FLRW branch, the gravitational sector contains the two tensor modes of GR and a single scalar mode. We investigate the classical cosmological effects and scalar-mode dynamics of this purely geometric chronology-protecting extension of general relativity.

一句话导读：提出一种度量仿射修改引力模型，纯几何地实现时序保护，并研究其宇宙学效应和标量模动力学。

相关度：low — 论文属于经典修改引力与宇宙学，与全息/AdS-CFT、黑洞量子引力等信息论方向无关，与用户重点全息研究支线不重合。

用分层贝叶斯模型测量干涉引力波探测器中的毛刺率

Measuring the rate of glitches in interferometric gravitational wave detectors with a hierarchical Bayesian model

Gregory Ashton, Colm Talbot, Andrew Lundgren, Ann-Kristin Malz, Joseph Areeda · gr-qc, astro-ph.IM · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：地基引力波探测器正在常规地探测暗宇宙，发现数百次致密天体碰撞。然而，地面非高斯噪声伪迹通常被称为毛刺，会降低对信号的灵敏度，并可能与信号重叠，产生有偏差的天体物理推断。我们引入了一个分层贝叶斯模型来测量毛刺率，相对于现有的触发器计数方法，该模型能够在不被高斯噪声背景污染的情况下测量低信噪比区域的毛刺率，前提是总体模型准确。该框架适用于任何毛刺模型，并根据所选模型测量毛刺率：这里我们使用anti毛刺模型，因此推断的速率是短时毛刺的速率，而不是所有毛刺的。该方法基于标准分层推断，但包含几个新特征：带分位数压缩的分层推断(HIQC)、通用近似循环超似然、以及通过拟合基函数估计时域速率。我们用模拟数据和注入毛刺验证了该方法，然后将其应用于第四次LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA运行数据，演示了24小时内毛刺率的时间分辨推断。推断的毛刺率与触发器计数估计一致，但不需要任意阈值，并提供了时间行为的更细粒度视图。最后，我们展示了如何将单个探测器的速率估计转换为符合毛刺概率，并据此提供证据表明被撤回的引力波候选体GW230630_070659很可能是两个符合的毛刺。

English: Ground-based gravitational wave detectors are now routinely surveying the dark Universe, finding hundreds of collisions between compact objects. However, terrestrial non-Gaussian noise artefacts, commonly known as glitches, reduce the sensitivity to signals and can overlap signals, producing biased astrophysical inferences. We introduce a hierarchical Bayesian model to measure the glitch rate, which improves upon existing trigger-counting methods in its capacity to measure the rate down into the low signal-to-noise regime without contamination from the Gaussian noise background, provided the population is accurately modelled. The framework accommodates any glitch model, and measures the rate with respect to the model chosen: here we use the antiglitch model, so the rate inferred is that of short-duration glitches rather than of all glitches. The methodology builds on standard hierarchical inference, but includes several novel features: hierarchical inference with quantile compression (HIQC), a generic approximation for the recycled hyperlikelihood, and a time-domain rate estimated by fitting basis functions. We validate the methodology using simulated data with injected glitches and then apply it to data from the fourth LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA observing run, demonstrating time-resolved inferences of the glitch rate over a 24 h period. The inferred glitch rate is consistent with estimates from trigger counts, but requires no arbitrary threshold and provides a more fine-grained view of the temporal behaviour. Finally, we demonstrate how our individual-detector rate estimates can be transformed into a coincident glitch probability and utilise this to provide evidence that the retracted gravitational-wave candidate GW230630_070659 is likely a pair of coincident glitches.

一句话导读：采用分层贝叶斯模型测量引力波探测器中的毛刺率，并用于分析LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA运行数据及识别候选事件为符合毛刺。

相关度：low — 论文关注引力波探测器噪声分析与统计方法，属于经典引力实验数据分析，与理论全息或量子引力无直接关联。

高功率调幅连续波月球激光测距作为 μHz 随机引力波背景探测器

High-Power AM-CW Lunar Laser Ranging as a $\mu$Hz SGWB Detector

Slava G. Turyshev · gr-qc, astro-ph.IM · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：高功率调幅连续波月球激光测距（AM-CW LLR）是一种随机引力波背景（SGWB）实验，通过测量引力波引起的地球-月球轨道漂移和扩散来进行探测。其可观测量为由单独分辨的空心角锥后向反射器（HCCR）返回的 GHz 调制包络的相干相位，该相位可转换为绝对距离，并通过其随时间变化的均值和协方差进行分析。响应出现在月球轨道谐波频率处；主导的低偏心率通道中心位于 f2=0.847245 μHz，五年宽带预报适用于在该共振附近、频率平滑尺度超过 T_obs^-1=6.34 nHz 的谱。1 kW、1064 nm 标量链路每秒可探测到 5.893×10^3 个光子，并在 100 秒内贡献约 88.8–124.3 μm 的光子噪声；预报采用了预计总距离精度 80 μm 和 50 μm。在协方差主导的弱信号区域，Ω_gw^95 ∝ σ_R^2 ν_Eff^-1 T_obs^-4。利用已发表的地球-月球响应归一化，五年 95% 阈值在 80 μm 精度、500 yr^-1 采样时为 1.86×10^-7 D_tot，在 50 μm 精度、1500 yr^-1 采样时为 2.42×10^-8 D_tot，其中 D_tot 为全分析阈值除以单位因子响应锚定预报。若端到端标定、采样和边际化给出 D_tot ≤ 1.15，则高性能方案在五年内对 Ω_gw=7.08×10^-8 达到标称源统计相等。这些可测量的性能和信息保持要求将高功率 AM-CW LLR 定义为在 0.85 μHz 地球-月球共振处的独立定量 SGWB 实验。

English: High-power amplitude-modulated continuous-wave lunar laser ranging (AM-CW LLR) is a stochastic-gravitational-wave-background (SGWB) experiment that measures GW-driven drift and diffusion of the Earth--Moon orbit. Its observable is the coherent phase of a GHz modulation envelope returned by an individually resolved single hollow corner-cube retroreflector (HCCR), converted to absolute range and analyzed through its time-dependent mean and covariance. The response occurs at lunar orbital harmonics; the dominant low-eccentricity channel is centered at $f_2=0.847245\,\mu{\rm Hz}$, and a five-year broadband forecast applies to spectra smooth over the resolution scale $T_{\rm obs}^{-1}=6.34\,$nHz about that resonance. A scalar 1-kW, 1064-nm link yields $5.893\times10^3$ detected photons s$^{-1}$ and an $88.8$--$124.3\,\mu$m photon contribution in 100 s, while the forecasts adopt projected total range precisions of $80$ and $50\,\mu$m. In the covariance-dominated weak-signal regime, $\Omega_{\rm gw}^{95}\propto\sigma_R^2\nuEff^{-1}T_{\rm obs}^{-4}$. Using the published Earth--Moon response normalization, the five-year 95\% thresholds are $1.86\times10^{-7}\Dtot$ for $80\,\mu$m and $500\,{\rm yr}^{-1}$ and $2.42\times10^{-8}\Dtot$ for $50\,\mu$m and $1500\,{\rm yr}^{-1}$, where $\Dtot$ is the full-analysis threshold divided by the unit-factor response-anchored forecast. If end-to-end calibration, sampling, and marginalization give $\Dtot\le1.15$, the higher-performance campaign reaches nominal source-statistic equality for $\Omega_{\rm gw}=7.08\times10^{-8}$ in five years. These measurable performance and information-retention requirements define high-power AM-CW LLR as a standalone quantitative SGWB experiment at the $0.85\,\mu{\rm Hz}$ Earth--Moon resonance.

一句话导读：提出用高功率调幅连续波月球激光测距作为 μHz 随机引力波背景探测器，给出五年观测的灵敏度阈值与实验要求。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/引力波实验探测，与全息/黑洞量子引力等理论支线无直接联系；在经典引力/广义相对论主题下有一定相关度。

新广义相对论的经典检验

Classical tests of New General Relativity

V. P. Vandeev, V. A. Parkhomenko, A. N. Semenova, M. A. Matveev · gr-qc · v3 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：在“新广义相对论”（NGR）中，静态球对称真空解由两个参数表征，而在广义相对论（GR）中，Schwarzschild 解只有一个参数。本工作利用相对论引力的五个经典检验（近日点进动、光线偏折、Shapiro 时间延迟、de Sitter 测地线进动效应以及引力红移），推导出了比原始文献 [1] 更严格的第二个参数约束。

English: In New General Relativity (NGR), the static spherically symmetric vacuum solution is characterized by two parameters, unlike General Relativity (GR), where the Schwarzschild solution has only one parameter. In this work, using the five classical tests of relativistic gravity (perihelion precession, light bending, Shapiro time delay and de Sitter geodetic precession effect, as well as gravitational redshift), a more stringent constraint on the value of the second parameter was derived compared to the original work [1].

一句话导读：利用五个经典引力测试对新广义相对论中的额外参数给出更严格约束。

相关度：high — 论文研究经典引力/广义相对论中的静态球对称解及经典检验，与用户明确关注的经典引力支线直接相关。

Schwarzschild 和 Kerr 黑洞自然多项式的性质

Properties of natural polynomials for Schwarzschild and Kerr black holes

Michelle Foucoin, Lionel London · gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：黑洞的准正规模式在引力波理论、信号建模和数据分析中扮演多种重要角色，但其数学性质仍存在开放问题。人们已提出经典多项式理论的若干方面作为研究准正规模式正交性与完备性的框架。我们最近提出了一类对准正规模式“自然”的多项式，它们受准正规模式边界条件约束，并精确三对角化 Teukolsky 径向方程。这些多项式可能有助于更好地理解该方程准正规模式解的向量空间性质。本文概述这些多项式的解析性质：三项递推关系、阶梯算符和主导微分方程。我们证明 Schwarzschild 和 Kerr 黑洞的自然多项式是具有复值参数的 Pollaczek-Jacobi 多项式。同时，我们观察到 Schwarzschild 特有的一个新性质：多项式的三项递推关系总是在物理泛音指标处达到峰值。该工作支持这些多项式的更广泛应用，以及将其推广到 Schwarzschild 和 Kerr 之外的黑洞时空。

English: The quasi-normal modes of black holes play various important roles in gravitational wave theory, signal modeling, and data analysis; however, there remain open questions about their mathematical properties. Aspects of classical polynomial theory have been proposed as a framework to investigate quasinormal mode orthogonality and completeness. We have recently presented a class of polynomials that are "natural" to quasi-normal modes in that they are restricted by the quasi-normal mode boundary conditions, and exactly tridiagonalize Teukolsky's radial equation. In turn, these polynomials may be useful for better understanding the vector space properties of quasi-normal mode solutions to that equation. Here, we provide an overview of these polynomials' analytic properties: their 3-term recurrence relation, ladder operators and governing differential equation. We demonstrate that the natural polynomials for Schwarzschild and Kerr black holes are Pollaczek-Jacobi polynomials with complex valued parameters. Along the way, we observe a novel property that is particular to Schwarzschild: the polynomials' 3-term recurrence relation always peaks at the physical overtone index. This work supports the broader application of these polynomials, as well as their extension to black hole spacetimes beyond Schwarzschild and Kerr.

一句话导读：研究 Schwarzschild 与 Kerr 黑洞准正规模式自然多项式的解析性质，证明其为复参数 Pollaczek-Jacobi 多项式。

相关度：high — 该工作研究经典 Schwarzschild/Kerr 黑洞的准正规模式数学性质，直接属于经典引力/广义相对论与 QNM 方向，与用户关注的经典引力支线高度相关。

引力平方反比定律的短程测试

Short-Range Tests of the Gravitational Inverse-Square Law

Jiro Murata, Takuhiro Fujiie, Sae Suzuki · gr-qc, hep-ex · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们提出了一种在短距离上对引力平方反比定律的实验约束，采用了一种在广泛长度尺度上一致的形式体系。我们基于先前的综述，纳入了过去十年新实验数据的全面更新——涵盖桌面力、卡西米尔测量、慢中子散射和对撞机搜寻。这项工作有助于将实验结果与扩展广义相对论的理论模型进行直接比较。此外，还引入了包括额外维模型在内的各种模型参数化之间的比较。最后，将桌面实验结果与高能对撞机实验结果在汤川势和幂律势下进行了比较。

English: Experimental constraints on the gravitational inverse-square law at short range are presented, employing a consistent formalism across a wide range of length scales. We provide comprehensive updates incorporating new experimental data from the past decade - spanning tabletop forces, Casimir measurements, slow neutron scattering, and collider searches - building upon our previous review. This work facilitates the direct comparison of experimental results with theoretical models that extend general relativity. Furthermore, a comparison between various model parametrizations, including extra-dimensional models, is introduced. Finally, results from tabletop experiments are compared with those from high-energy collider experiments for both Yukawa and power-law potentials.

一句话导读：短程引力平方反比定律实验约束的综述更新，涵盖实验与理论模型的比较。

相关度：low — 论文涉及经典引力实验测试，与全息或量子引力理论无直接关联，属于经典引力支线，仅在主题分类上相关。

引力波与物质的相互作用：超越四极扰动

Gravitational wave interactions with matter: beyond quadrupolar perturbations

Ulrich K. Beckering Vinckers, Nigel T. Bishop · gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：先前的工作利用 Bondi-Sachs 形式发展了线性化引力波与物质相互作用的理论，但扰动被限制为四极的，即角依赖是 ℓ=2 的球谐函数。本文将理论推广到闵可夫斯基背景上一般 ℓ 的引力波情形。对任意 ℓ≥2 得到了引力波阻尼与加热效应的公式。研究发现这些效应通常会增强，这表明在双中子星并合后信号或核坍缩超新星的引力波观测中不太可能看到更高的 ℓ 阶模。

English: Previous work has developed the theory of linearized gravitational wave (GW) interactions with matter using the Bondi-Sachs formalism, but with the perturbations restricted to be quadrupolar, i.e., the angular dependence is spherical harmonic with $\ell=2$. Here, the theory is extended to the case of GWs on a Minkowski background with general $\ell$. Formulas for the GW damping and heating effects are obtained for arbitrary $\ell\ge 2$. It is found that the effects are, generally, enhanced, and this suggests that it is unlikely that higher $\ell$-modes will be seen in GW observations of the post-merger signal of a binary neutron star merger, or of a core collapse supernova.

一句话导读：将线性化引力波与物质相互作用理论从四极推广到任意 ℓ 阶，给出改进的阻尼/加热公式，并指出高阶模在观测中不太可能被直接看到。

相关度：low — 论文专注于经典引力波与物质的相互作用，未涉及全息或量子引力，仅与经典引力/广义相对论支线相关，但与本用户侧重的全息与量子引力主题关系不大。

修正的黑反弹致密物体的热力学与辐射预测

Corrected thermodynamics and radiation predictions of modified black bounce compact objects

Shokhzod Jumaniyozov, Javlon Rayimbaev, Yassine Sekhmani, Satimbay Palvanov, Olmos Tursunboyev, Dilshod Karshiev · gr-qc · v3 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们研究了通过Simpson-Visser（SV）正则化应用于Schwarzschild改进引力（MOG）黑洞所获得的规则致密物体的热力学性质和辐射特性。所得SV-MOG时空的时移函数同时包含MOG耦合参数α和黑反弹参数l，随l的取值在规则黑洞、单向虫洞和可穿越虫洞之间光滑过渡。我们推导了黑洞分支的霍金温度和热容，识别出由热容变号表示的二阶相变；对于没有因果视界、因此不存在真正霍金辐射的无视界虫洞分支，我们改用不稳定光子球的Lyapunov指数构造了物理上定义良好的有效温度。量子引力修正通过由参数β1、β2参数化的对数项纳入熵中，我们讨论了它们在圈量子引力和弦理论中理论偏好的取值范围，并表明在小视界半径处对Bekenstein-Hawking面积律的偏离变得显著。线性标量扰动分析进一步表明，有效径向势在黑洞外部和虫洞喉部始终非负，表明对单极扰动的稳定性与热容所推断的热力学稳定性无关。

English: We study the thermodynamic properties and radiation characteristics of a regular compact object obtained by applying the Simpson-Visser (SV) regularisation to the Schwarzschild modified gravity black hole. The resulting SV-MOG spacetime, whose lapse function involves both the MOG coupling parameter alpha and the black-bounce parameter l, smoothly interpolates between a regular black hole, a one-way wormhole, and a traversable wormhole depending on the parameter l. We derive the Hawking temperature and heat capacity for the black hole branch, identifying second-order phase transitions signaled by sign changes in CV, and, for the horizonless wormhole branch where no causal horizon and hence no genuine Hawking radiation exists we instead construct a physically well-defined effective temperature from the Lyapunov exponent of the unstable photon sphere. Quantum gravitational corrections to the entropy are incorporated via logarithmic terms parameterized by coefficients beta1, beta2, whose theoretically preferred ranges in loop quantum gravity and string theory we discuss, and we show that deviations from the Bekenstein-Hawking area law become significant at small horizon radii. A linear scalar-perturbation analysis further shows that the effective radial potential remains non-negative throughout the black-hole exterior and across the wormhole throat, indicating stability against monopole perturbations independently of the thermodynamic stability inferred from the heat capacity.

一句话导读：对SV-MOG黑反弹致密物体的黑洞/虫洞分支统一给出热力学、有效温度、量子熵修正与稳定性分析。

相关度：medium — 论文涉及非全息的黑洞/虫洞热力学、修正引力和经典稳定性，与用户关注的全息/凝聚态主线无直接重叠，仅通过“经典引力/广义相对论”支线相关。

de Sitter广义相对论的一阶规范方法

A First-Order Gauge Approach to de Sitter General Relativity

P. Salgado · gr-qc · v3 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们提出了一种 de Sitter 相对论的一阶规范表述，其中伪半径 l(x) 被提升为时空场，局域地将 SO(4,1) 破缺为 SO(3,1)。这里 l(x) 是几何补偿场，dl(x) 是相应的序参量。这修改了强等效原理：局域真空随物质分布而变化，而非保持固定，但广义相对论的传播自由度不变。在固定 l(x) 时，作用量具有 SO(3,1) 规范不变性，但变化 l(x) 会破坏 SO(4,1) 完整平移部分的协变性。通过 Inonu-Wigner 收缩，抽象生成元成为显式时空变换，在宏观 l 趋于无穷和微观 l 趋于 0 的极限下给出非线性 Killing 矢量。广义 Noether 定理给出一个统一能量-动量流，将标准张量与正则共形物质流耦合。该流在无物质时恒为零，导致局域真空能量渐近消失并使背景衰变到 Minkowski 时空。独立变分得到耦合的 de Sitter 场方程，我们代数地证明了它们与坐标流形上的张量分裂等价。规范 Bianchi 恒等式进一步表明 Lambda(x) 是时空挠率的内禀来源，从而将其一致地识别为对称性破缺的局域序参量。因此宇宙常数问题被重新表述，而不是数值求解：从早期宇宙密度推导 Lambda(x) 衰减到其观测值仍是未来工作。

English: We propose a first-order gauge formulation of de Sitter relativity in which the pseudo-radius l(x) is promoted to a spacetime field, locally breaking SO(4,1) to SO(3,1). Here, l(x) is a geometric compensator and dl(x) the associated order parameter. This modifies the Strong Equivalence Principle: the local vacuum follows the matter distribution instead of remaining fixed, while the propagating degrees of freedom of General Relativity are unchanged. At fixed l(x), the action is SO(3,1)-gauge invariant, but varying l(x) destroys covariance under the full translational sector of SO(4,1). Through the Inonu-Wigner contraction, abstract generators become explicit spacetime transformations, yielding nonlinear Killing vectors in both the macroscopic l tends to infinity and microscopic l tends to 0 limits. The generalized Noether theorem gives a unified energy-momentum current coupling the standard tensor to the proper conformal matter current. It vanishes identically without matter, causing the asymptotic extinction of local vacuum energy and the decay of the background to Minkowski spacetime. Independent variations yield the coupled de Sitter field equations, and we algebraically prove their equivalence to the tensorial splitting on the coordinate manifold. Gauge Bianchi identities further show that Lambda(x) is an intrinsic source of spacetime torsion, consistently identifying it with the local order parameter of the symmetry breaking. The cosmological constant problem is therefore recast, not numerically solved: deriving the decay of Lambda(x) from early-universe densities to its observed value remains future work.

一句话导读：通过把 de Sitter 伪半径提升为补偿场并破缺 SO(4,1) 到 SO(3,1)，构造了经典引力的一阶规范理论，并将宇宙常数重新解释为挠率的局域序参量。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论的规范表述，与用户重点标签中的“经典引力/广义相对论”直接相关，但不涉及全息、超流超导或相变等其余支线。

动力学 Barbero--Immirzi 引力中的引力波传播与标准汽笛：作用量级分析

Gravitational-wave propagation and standard sirens in dynamical Barbero--Immirzi gravity: an action-level analysis

Zhi-Fu Gao, Hui Wang, Luiz Carlos Garcia de Andrade, Na Wang, Guo-Qiang Jin, Zhou-Jian Cao · gr-qc · v3 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们发展了一个作用量级框架，用于识别哪些动力学 Barbero--Immirzi 扇区能够在玻色型、双导数、曲率线性的 Einstein--Cartan 类中修正宇宙学张量传播。消去代数 Lorentz 联络表明，Holst 与 Palatini 之比贡献于标量动能度规，而横迹无迹张量归一化由宇称偶的 Hilbert--Palatini 系数控制。因此，在宇称偶归一化固定的情况下，动力学 Holst 系数本身不会产生异常的引力波摩擦：最小动力学 Holst 理论精确落在广义相对论传播面上。然后我们构造了一个领先的解析宇称不变非最小实现，展示了满足两个 Friedmann 方程的正则宇宙学背景，并验证了沿该基准的规范双导数动能条件。最后，我们重新表述已发表的 GWTC-3 和 GWTC-4.0 约束以及两个公开的 GWTC-4.0 超后验产物，以确定标准汽笛约束的是哪种作用量级组合。相应的目录约束仍然宽泛，并依赖于所采用的先验和总体假设；它约束的是非最小宇称偶曲率扇区的演化，而非最小 Barbero--Immirzi 挠率，从而为标准汽笛传播测试提供了作用量级的解释。

English: We develop an action-level framework for identifying which dynamical Barbero--Immirzi sectors can modify cosmological tensor propagation in a bosonic, two-derivative, curvature-linear Einstein--Cartan class. Eliminating the algebraic Lorentz connection shows that the Holst-to-Palatini ratio contributes to the scalar kinetic metric, whereas the transverse-traceless tensor normalization is controlled by the parity-even Hilbert--Palatini coefficient. Consequently, a dynamical Holst coefficient at fixed parity-even normalization does not by itself generate anomalous gravitational-wave friction: the minimal dynamical-Holst theory lies exactly on the general-relativistic propagation surface. We then construct a leading analytic parity-invariant nonminimal realization, exhibit a regular cosmological background satisfying both Friedmann equations, and verify the invariant two-derivative kinetic conditions along that benchmark. Finally, we recast published GWTC-3 and GWTC-4.0 constraints and the two public GWTC-4.0 hyperposterior products to determine which action-level combination present standard sirens constrain. The corresponding catalog constraints remain broad and depend on the adopted priors and population assumptions; itconstrains the evolution of a nonminimal parity-even curvature sector rather than minimal Barbero--Immirzi torsion, providing an action-level interpretation of standard-siren propagation tests.

一句话导读：从作用量层面分析动力学 Barbero--Immirzi 引力对引力波传播的影响，并表明标准汽笛约束的是非最小宇称偶曲率扇区而非最小挠率。

相关度：medium — 论文涉及经典引力理论与引力波传播，但并非全息或量子引力领域，仅与广义相对论的拓展相关。

引力波信号中波形系统效应与透镜特征之间的相互作用

On the interplay between waveform systematics and lensing signatures in gravitational-wave signals

Disha Hegde, Justin Janquart · gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：卓越的引力波事件GW231123表现出异常高的总质量和接近极端的自旋，同时其推断源性质在不同波形模型下显示出强烈差异。几项后续研究倾向于透镜解释，并声称波形系统效应减小，这引发了一个问题：明显的透镜证据是否可能源于波形建模的系统效应。使用类似GW231123的数值相对论注入，我们发现透镜自由度可以减少波形差异并产生不可忽略的透镜支持。然而，这种行为并非在所有注入中系统性地出现，也未发现所有波形和透镜模型都青睐透镜且同时减少波形差异的案例。

English: The exceptional gravitational-wave event GW231123 exhibited unusually high total mass and near-extremal spins, while revealing strong waveform-dependent variations in its inferred source properties. Several follow-up studies favored a lensed interpretation, with reduced waveform systematics, raising the question of whether apparent lensing evidence could arise from waveform modeling systematics. Using GW231123-like numerical-relativity injections, we find that lensing degrees of freedom can reduce waveform discrepancies and yield non-negligible lensing support. However, this behavior is not systematic across injections, and cases where all waveform and lens models favor lensing while reducing waveform discrepancies are not found.

一句话导读：该研究用数值相对论注入检验GW231123的透镜证据是否可能是波形建模系统效应所致，发现并非系统性。

相关度：medium — 论文主要研究引力波事件中的透镜建模与波形系统效应，属于经典引力和引力波天体物理，与全息或量子引力无直接关联，但与经典引力支线相关。

第三代探测器长持续时间引力波信号的快速贝叶斯推断

Fast Bayesian Inference for Long-Duration Gravitational-Wave Signals in 3G detectors

Anand S. Sengupta · gr-qc, astro-ph.CO, astro-ph.IM · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：第三代（3G）引力波探测器将观测近一天的双中子星旋近过程，因此地球自转成为信号的一部分而非可忽略的修正。这破坏了通常的内禀参数与外禀参数分离，并给贝叶斯推断造成重大计算瓶颈。我们证明精确的五谐波Jaranowski-Krolak-Schutz（JKS）分解能够将旋转天线幅度响应与计算成本高的内禀波形计算分离开来。通过在其相位曲率设定的频率网格上对内禀波形采样，再应用误差受控的相对分箱，21.3小时信号的对数似然评估被加速约10^4倍，使日长3G信号推断变得可行。

English: Third-generation (3G) gravitational-wave detectors will observe binary-neutron-star inspirals for nearly a day, so Earth's rotation becomes part of the signal rather than a negligible correction. This destroys the usual separation between intrinsic and extrinsic parameters and creates a major computational bottleneck for Bayesian inference. We show that the exact five-harmonic Jaranowski-Krolak-Schutz (JKS) decomposition restores the separation of the rotating antenna amplitude response from the computationally expensive intrinsic waveform calculations. By sampling the intrinsic waveform on a frequency grid set by its phase curvature and then applying error-controlled relative binning, likelihood evaluations for a 21.3-h signal are accelerated by O(10^4), making day-long 3G signal inference practical.

一句话导读：利用JKS分解与相对分箱，将3G探测器长达一天引力波信号的贝叶斯推断加速四个数量级。

相关度：low — 论文主题为经典引力波数据处理与计算方法，与全息理论或量子引力无关，仅与经典引力/广义相对论支线相关。

HMG与星系-星系弱透镜推断的平坦旋转速度的比较

Comparison of HMG and flat rotation velocities inferred from galaxy-galaxy weak lensing

Robert Monjo · astro-ph.CO, astro-ph.GA, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：尽管暗物质模型取得了成功，但尚未解决的问题促使人们探索诸如修正引力理论等替代方案。在此背景下，我们检验了超圆锥修正引力（HMG）与从弱透镜数据推断的星系旋转曲线的兼容性。研究解决了修正牛顿动力学（MOND）在跨不同星系尺度普遍适用性方面的现存局限。通过假设广义相对论（GR）的局部有效性，并分析来自星系-星系弱透镜的近期圆周速度数据，我们的发现将星系动力学异常解释为从宇宙膨胀继承而来的虚拟加速度，而无需引入暗物质。结果表明，HMG在1 Mpc尺度上比MOND略好地重现了平坦速度曲线。因此，这些观测支持HMG作为可行的引力模型，凸显了其解释星系及其它尺度动力学的潜力。需要更广泛数据集的进一步研究来证实这些初步见解。

English: Despite the success of dark-matter models, unresolved issues require exploring alternatives such as modified gravity theories. In this context, we examine the compatibility of the Hyperconical Modified Gravity (HMG) with galaxy rotation curves inferred from weak-lensing data. The research addresses the existing limitations of Modified Newtonian Dynamics (MOND), which often struggle with universal applicability across different galactic scales. By assuming local validity of General Relativity (GR) and analyzing recent data on circular velocities from galaxy-galaxy weak lensing, our findings interpret the galactic dynamics anomaly as a fictitious acceleration inherited from the cosmic expansion, without invoking dark matter. The results indicate that HMG successfully reproduces flat velocity curves on scales of 1 Mpc slightly better than MOND. Therefore, these observations support HMG as a viable gravitational model, highlighting its potential to account for dynamics on galaxies and other scales. Further research with extensive datasets is required to confirm these preliminary insights.

一句话导读：HMG修正引力模型无需暗物质即可在1 Mpc尺度上比MOND稍好地重现星系平坦旋转速度。

相关度：low — 论文研究修正引力与星系动力学，不涉及全息/量子引力或强关联系统，与用户关注方向无直接关联。

引力透镜中的频移与观测方向变化

Frequency shift and viewing direction variations in gravitational lensing

Miko{\l}aj Korzy\'nski, Mateusz Kulejewski · astro-ph.CO, astro-ph.GA, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：在引力透镜系统中，透镜、源和观测者之间的相对横向速度会导致观测到的辐射发生频移。尽管在大多数天体物理情形中这种频移通常可以忽略，但已有针对电磁波和引力波的探测策略提出。本文对该效应进行了严格的理论处理，推导了嵌入宇宙学时空的透镜系统中频移的一般表达式。该公式在任意距离和速度下均有效，包括高度相对论性区域，适用于任意 Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker 度规。在先前关于运动透镜工作的基础上，我们详细分析了相对论速度运动的透镜引起的频率效应。此外，我们通过推导源的观测方向变化的一个精确公式，扩展了标准透镜理论。这一结果对强各向异性辐射体（如发射引力波的致密双星系统）具有重要意义。最后，我们量化了高速度运动透镜系统中时间延迟效应引起的透镜与源的两个像之间的视在错位。

English: In a gravitational lensing system, the relative transverse velocities of the lens, source, and observer induce a frequency shift in the observed radiation. While this shift is typically negligible in most astrophysical contexts, strategies for its detection have been proposed for both electromagnetic and gravitational waves. This paper provides a rigorous theoretical treatment of the effect, deriving general expressions for the frequency shift within a lensing system embedded in a cosmological spacetime. Our formulation remains valid for arbitrary distances and velocities, including highly relativistic regimes, under any Friedmann-Lema\^{i}tre-Robertson-Walker metric. Expanding upon previous papers on moving lenses, we provide a detailed analysis of frequency effects induced by lenses moving at relativistic speeds. Furthermore, we extend standard lensing theory by deriving an exact formula for the variation in the source's viewing direction. This result is of interest for strongly anisotropic emitters, such as compact binary systems emitting gravitational waves. Finally, we quantify the apparent misalignment between the lens and the source's two images produced by time-delay effects in lens systems moving with high velocity.

一句话导读：严格推导了宇宙学时空中运动透镜系统的频移和视角变化公式，并分析了高速度下的时间延迟错位。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力与广义相对论中的引力透镜理论，不在全息或量子引力重点支线内，但符合用户关注的经典引力/广义相对论标签。

关于脉冲星计时阵列对引力波信号的角向定位

On the angular localization of gravitational-wave signals by pulsar timing arrays

Stephen R. Taylor · astro-ph.HE, astro-ph.IM, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们给出了利用脉冲星计时阵列进行引力波信号定位的完整研究。我们推导了Cramér-Rao天区定位精度的解析表达式，刻画了脉冲星与引力波源的角距离$\xi$以及脉冲星距离测量精度$\sigma_L$的影响。地球项与脉冲星项之间的干涉效应在天球坐标Fisher矩阵元中产生有助于定位的快速角振荡，并由变化更平缓的天线响应梯度信息加以补充。这些因素的相对重要性取决于脉冲星距离是精确已知（即$\sigma_L\leq\lambda_{\mathrm{GW}}/(1-\cos\xi)$）还是不精确。若脉冲星距离精确已知，则提高距离精度会使信号定位按$\Delta\Omega_\mathrm{sky}\propto(\sigma_L/L)^2$改善，直到地球-脉冲星系统达到衍射极限$\Delta\Omega_\mathrm{sky}\propto(\lambda_{\mathrm{GW}}/L)^2$。若脉冲星距离未知得不好，定位精度会降低，但靠近源的更多脉冲星是最佳改进手段。以$\alpha$标记脉冲星时，在小角度极限下对未边缘化Fisher矩阵有$\Delta\Omega_\mathrm{sky}\propto(\sum_\alpha \mathrm{SNR}_\alpha^2/\xi_\alpha^2)^{-1}$，我们还推导了该结果向任意脉冲星-源夹角的解析推广。最后，我们研究了将脉冲星项相位视为与双星或PTA性质无关的冗余参数的场景。这种相位解耦情形——目前所有PTA连续波搜索都如此进行——给出的定位性能与天线响应驱动情形相近，并且随着脉冲星距离精度提高没有显著改进。

English: We provide a complete study of gravitational-wave signal localization using pulsar timing arrays. We derive analytical expressions for the Cram\'er-Rao sky localization precision that delineate the impact of the angular proximity, $\xi$, between the pulsar and the gravitational wave source, and the precision, $\sigma_L$, with which pulsar distances are known. Interference between the Earth and pulsar terms creates rapid angular oscillations for sky-coordinate Fisher matrix elements that aid localization, which is complemented by more broadly varying antenna response gradient information. The relative importance of these factors depends on whether pulsar distances are known precisely [i.e., $\sigma_L\leq\lambda_\mathrm{GW}/(1-\cos\xi)$] or imprecisely. If pulsar distances are known precisely, tightening this distance precision improves signal localization according to $\Delta\Omega_\mathrm{sky}\propto(\sigma_L/L)^2$ until the Earth-pulsar system reaches its diffraction limit, $\Delta\Omega_\mathrm{sky}\propto(\lambda_\mathrm{GW}/L)^2$. If pulsar distances are not known well, localization precision is degraded, but more pulsars in close proximity to the source is the best means of improving. With $\alpha$ indexing pulsars, this scales as $\Delta\Omega_\mathrm{sky}~\propto~(\sum_\alpha \mathrm{SNR}_\alpha^2/\xi_\alpha^2)^{-1}$ in the small-angle limit of the unmarginalized Fisher matrix, and we derive the analytic generalization to any angle between a pulsar and the source. Finally, we study a scenario where pulsar-term phases are treated as nuisance variables that are unconnected to binary or PTA properties. This phase-decoupled scenario, which is how all PTA continuous wave searches are currently conducted, delivers localization performance similar to the antenna-response--driven case, and does not exhibit significant improvement as pulsar distance precisions are tightened.

一句话导读：通过解析Fisher矩阵系统研究了脉冲星计时阵列对引力波源的角向定位精度，揭示了脉冲星距离精度、脉冲星-源夹角以及地球-脉冲星干涉效应对定位的影响。

相关度：low — 该论文属于经典引力波天文学与脉冲星计时阵列数据分析，不涉及全息原理、量子引力或强耦合场论，与用户关注的超流、相变、纠缠等全息研究方向关联较弱。

畴壁湮灭在诱导引力波上留下的印记

Imprint of domain wall annihilation on induced gravitational waves

Rishav Roshan · hep-ph, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：畴壁湮灭可以在诱导引力波谱上留下独特的印记。湮灭过程中，畴壁的大部分能量转化为最初导致Z2对称性破缺从而形成畴壁的标量场，以及任何耦合的粒子。如果产生的标量足够长寿命，其延迟衰变会在畴壁湮灭之后驱动一个早期物质主导阶段，显著放大来自原初扰动的诱导引力波。随后向辐射主导的转变通过熵注入稀释畴壁贡献，同时保留增强的诱导信号。这形成了在互补频带内可探测的双峰引力波谱。我们探索了可观测的参数空间，并展示了多频带探测如何能够探测早期宇宙的对称性破缺。

English: Domain wall annihilation can leave a distinctive imprint on the induced gravitational wave spectrum. During annihilation, most of the domain wall energy transforms into the scalar field responsible for the initial $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry breaking that created the walls, along with any coupled species. If the produced scalar is sufficiently long-lived, its delayed decay drives an early matter-dominated phase following domain wall annihilation, significantly amplifying induced gravitational waves from primordial perturbations. The subsequent transition to radiation domination dilutes the domain wall contribution through entropy injection while preserving the enhanced induced signal. This creates a gravitational wave spectrum with two distinct peaks detectable across complementary frequency bands. We explore the observable parameter space and demonstrate how multi-band detection can probe early universe symmetry breaking.

一句话导读：提出畴壁湮灭通过长寿命标量延迟衰变驱动的早期物质主导放大诱导引力波，形成双峰谱，为多频带探测早期宇宙对称性破缺提供了途径。

相关度：low — 该工作属于早期宇宙引力波宇宙学，与用户列出的全息、量子引力、凝聚态或原子物理等研究支线没有直接关联。

用第一代和第二代黑洞对黑洞面积定律的群体级验证

Population-Level Verification of the Black-Hole Area Law with First- and Second-Generation Black Holes

Shao-Peng Tang, Yin-Jie Li, Yi-Zhong Fan · astro-ph.HE, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：霍金面积定理指出，经典黑洞的总事件视界面积永不减少。此前对该定律的引力波检验集中于单个响亮的并合事件，仅探测了双星参数空间的稀疏子集。本文首次进行了群体级检验。利用最新引力波目录中并合黑洞的分解，将其分为恒星坍缩起源的低自旋子群体和由前者经层级并合形成的高自旋子群体。若高自旋黑洞为并合残骸，面积定理要求其视界面积大于低自旋双星并合前的总面积。仅使用241个事件吸积阶段的参数估计（未引入并合-铃宕信息），发现第二代黑洞视界面积分布的两个峰均高于第一代相应峰。各峰位移显著（2.1–3.7σ），当将两个子群体用一个共同的位移参数联系时，面积减小被决定性地排除（≳5.0σ）。因此黑洞力学第二定律在当前目录主导的准圆、中等自旋并合中统计成立，这也支持了我们对恒星坍缩和层级并合黑洞分类的稳健性。

English: Hawking's area theorem states that the total event-horizon area of classical black holes can never decrease. Gravitational-wave tests of this law have so far focused on individual loud mergers, thus probing only a sparse subset of binary parameter space. Here we perform the \emph{first} population-level test. We exploit the decomposition of the coalescing black holes in the latest gravitational-wave catalogue into a low-spin subpopulation of stellar-collapse origin and a high-spin subpopulation assembled through hierarchical mergers of the former. If the high-spin black holes are merger remnants, the area theorem requires their horizon areas to exceed the total pre-merger areas of the low-spin binaries. Using parameter estimation restricted to the inspiral of 241 events, so that no merger-ringdown information enters the inference, we find that both peaks of the horizon-area distribution of second-generation black holes lie above their first-generation counterparts. The displacement is significant at each peak ($2.1$--$3.7\sigma$), and when we tie the two subpopulations with a single common shift, an area decrease is excluded decisively ($\gtrsim5.0\sigma$). The second law of black-hole mechanics thus holds statistically across the quasicircular, moderately spinning mergers that dominate current catalogues, which in turn underpins the robustness of our classification of stellar-collapse and hierarchical-merger black holes.

一句话导读：首次群体级验证霍金面积定理，基于引力波目录241个事件，发现第二代黑洞面积显著高于第一代，排除面积减小约5σ。

相关度：high — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论，直接检验黑洞面积定理，是经典引力支线的重点内容。

检验QGP星假说：oMEGACat BH-2系统作为色超导夸克-胶子等离子体星的候选体

Testing the QGP Star Hypothesis: The oMEGACat BH-2 System as a Candidate Color-Superconducting Quark-Gluon Plasma Star

Herman J. Mosquera Cuesta · astro-ph.HE, gr-qc, hep-ph, nucl-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：最近在球状星团ω Centauri中发现的长周期双星系统oMEGACat BH-2，为探测致密暗天体的真实本质提供了前所未有的机会。该系统中质量巨大的暗伴星被推断质量约为4.46_{-1.01}^{+1.22} 太阳质量，位于中子星与经典恒星质量黑洞之间的“质量间隙”。本文探索oMEGACat BH-2伴星不是经典黑洞，而是一种稳定的、自束缚的夸克-胶子等离子体（QGP）星的假说，该模型由近期考虑非线性电动力学（NLED）和量子色动力学（QCD）渐近自由的广义相对论模型描述。我们将推断的伴星质量与QGP星模型预言的新质量-半径（M-R）关系进行比较。我们发现，ω Centauri天体的推断质量恰好落入超大规模QGP星预言的宽质量谱（0到>7太阳质量）范围内。尽管这种一致性不足以声称证据，但表明oMEGACat BH-2可能成为首个观测到的QGP星候选体，代表一种稳定的、无奇点的恒星坍缩终态。我们认为，未来通过JWST以及FAST和SKA等射电望远镜的方位监测可进一步约束oMEGACat BH-2的轨道参数。同时，LIGO、VIRGO、KAGRA、LISA、ET和CE等台站对f和g模式的引力波后续观测可能对区分经典黑洞与模型的“引力永恒坍缩致密物体”状态至关重要。

English: The recent discovery of a long-period binary system in the globular cluster $\omega$ Centauri, oMEGACat BH-2 \citep{Whitaker2026}, provides an unprecedented opportunity to probe the true nature of compact dark objects. The system's massive, dark companion has been inferred to have a mass of $4.46_{-1.01}^{+1.22} M_\odot$, which places it in the ``mass gap'' between neutron stars and the canonical stellar-mass black holes. In this Letter, we explore the hypothesis that the oMEGACat BH-2 companion is not a classical black hole, but a stable, self-bound Quark-Gluon Plasma (QGP) star, as described by recent general relativistic models that incorporate Nonlinear Electrodynamics (NLED) and the asymptotic freedom of Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD) \citep{Mosquera2025}. We compare the inferred mass of the companion with the novel Mass-Radius ($M$-$R$) relation predicted by the QGP star model. We find that the inferred mass of the $\omega$ Centauri object lies squarely within the wide mass spectrum predicted for hypermassive QGP stars ($0$ to $>7$ $M_\odot$). Although this consistency is not enough for claiming evidence, it suggests that oMEGACat BH-2 may be the first observed candidate for a QGP star, representing a stable, non-singular end-state of stellar collapse. We argue that future astrometric monitoring with JWST and radio-telescopes like FAST and SKA can further constrain the oMEGACat BH-2 orbital parameters. Meanwhile, gravitational-wave follow-ups for the $f$ and $g$ modes by observatories like LIGO, VIRGO, KAGRA, LISA, ET and CE can be crucial for distinguishing a classical black hole from the ``gravitational eternally collapsing `kompact' object'' (\GECKO) state of our QGP star model.

一句话导读：提出球状星团中的oMEGACat BH-2伴星可能是QGP星而非黑洞，并建议通过引力波等观测进一步检验。

相关度：medium — 论文研究经典引力下的致密天体（QGP星）与黑洞质量间隙，属于经典引力/广义相对论支线，但未涉及全息或量子信息。

数据驱动的 AdS/QCD 模型中矢量介子性质

Vector-meson properties in a data-driven AdS/QCD model

Xun Chen, Floriana Giannuzzi, Stefano Nicotri · hep-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：我们在全息 AdS/QCD 框架内研究了 ρ、ψ 和 Υ 介子的性质，涵盖其真空现象学和介质内动力学。通过在一个最初为研究热力学函数而发展的模型中重构味特异规范动能函数，我们计算了零温下矢量介子质量谱、衰变常数以及强子真空极化对缪子反常磁矩的贡献。此外，我们评估了热谱函数，以分析热密介质中质量移动、共振展宽和矢量束缚态的熔化。最后，我们使用 Kubo 公式提取等离子体电导率随温度和化学势的变化。

English: We investigate the properties of $\rho$, $\psi$, and $\Upsilon$ mesons within a holographic AdS/QCD framework, capturing both their vacuum phenomenology and in-medium dynamics. By reconstructing flavor-specific gauge kinetic functions within a model originally developed to study thermodynamic functions, we compute the vector-meson mass spectrum, the decay constants and the hadronic vacuum polarization contributions to the anomalous magnetic moment of the muon at zero temperature. Furthermore, we evaluate the thermal spectral functions to analyze mass shifts, resonance broadening, and the melting of vector bound states in a hot and dense medium. Finally, using the Kubo formula, we extract the electrical conductivity of the plasma as a function of temperature and chemical potential.

一句话导读：在数据驱动 AdS/QCD 模型中计算了 ρ、ψ、Υ 介子的真空与有限温度密度性质，并用 Kubo 公式得到电导率。

相关度：high — 论文主要研究全息 AdS/QCD 中的矢量介子谱与输运性质，与全息 QCD/核物理及热化/输运主题高度相关。

对称能不确定性、非线性σ-δ耦合和暗物质在中子星宏观性质中的相互作用

The Interplay of Symmetry Energy Uncertainties, Nonlinear $\sigma-\delta$ Coupling, and Dark Matter in Neutron Star Macroscopic Properties

Zhaohui Feng, Xiaoxuan Zhai, Shuangxuan Chen, Defu Hou · nucl-th, hep-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-09-09

中文摘要：对称能的密度依赖性约束不佳，给致密核物质的状态方程（EOS）以及随后中子星性质带来了显著不确定性。我们在相对论平均场（RMF）框架内系统研究了饱和密度n0处对称能E_sym及其斜率L的不确定性如何影响中子星性质，包括非线性σ-δ耦合和可能的暗物质（DM）掺混的影响。对于固定的E_sym(n0)和L(n0)，我们发现当g_σδ=-0.004时，σ-δ耦合会引起EOS的异常软化，同时增加最大中子星质量并减小星体半径和潮汐形变度。在E_sym(n0)=36 MeV且暗物质费米动量k_F^DM=50 MeV时也观察到类似行为。在这种情况下，RMF EOS满足GW170817的潮汐形变约束。我们还发现，中子星的表面曲率与E_sym的刚度密切相关，对称能越软，表面曲率越大。我们的结果还表明，E_sym在n0附近的行为主要由同位旋标量参数而非同位旋矢量参数主导。

English: The poorly constrained density dependence of the nuclear symmetry energy introduces significant uncertainties in the equation of state (EOS) of dense nuclear matter and, consequently, in neutron-star properties. We systematically investigate how uncertainties in the symmetry energy $E_{\rm sym}$ and its slope $L$ at saturation density $n_0$ affect NS properties within the relativistic mean-field (RMF) framework, including the effects of nonlinear $\sigma$-$\delta$ coupling and possible admixture of dark matter(DM) . For fixed $E_{\rm sym}(n_0)$ and $L(n_0)$, we find that the $\sigma$-$\delta$ coupling with $g_{\sigma\delta}=-0.004$ induces an abnormal softening of the EOS, which simultaneously increasing the maximum NS mass and reducing the stellar radius and tidal deformability. A similar behavior is found in the presence of DM with Fermi momentum $k_F^{\rm DM}=50~\mathrm{MeV}$ at $E_{\rm sym}(n_0)=36~\mathrm{MeV}$. In this case, the RMF EOS satisfies the tidal-deformability constraint from GW170817. We further find that the surface curvature of NSs is strongly correlated with the stiffness of $E_{\rm sym}$, with softer symmetry energy corresponding to larger surface curvature. Our results also indicate that the behavior of $E_{\rm sym}$ around $n_0$ is mainly governed by isoscalar rather than isovector parameters.

一句话导读：该论文利用相对论平均场模型中子星宏观性质，发现非线性σ-δ耦合和暗物质会软化EOS，但同时增大最大质量、减小半径和潮汐形变。

相关度：medium — 论文虽以核物理和状态方程为主，但通过中子星宏观性质与广义相对论框架相联系，与经典引力/广义相对论支线有一定交集，与全息方向无关。

引力波记忆效应的波形模型：III. 无自旋双星系统的唯象频域模型

Waveform models for the gravitational-wave memory effect: III. Phenomenological frequency-domain model for nonspinning binaries

Arwa Elhashash, David A. Nichols · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE · v1 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：我们提出了一个用于无自旋双黑洞并合、近圆轨道引力波（GW）记忆信号的唯象频域模型。我们分别针对GW记忆信号的主导球谐模(2,0)建立了振幅和相位模型。振幅和相位模型由初等函数和超越函数的叠加构成，可以高效求值。模型的两个部分都基于质量比1至8范围内的数值相对论代理模型进行标定。通过使用第四次观测运行Advanced LIGO灵敏度曲线计算与数值相对论模型的失配来评估其精度。在LIGO覆盖的总质量参数空间内，失配约为10^-4至10^-3。所得频域模型比作者先前提出的相关时域模型在计算上更高效。时域和频域模型的开源实现已提供于Python包GWMemoryModel中，可用于无自旋双黑洞的相关分析。

English: We present a phenomenological frequency-domain model for the gravitational-wave (GW) memory signal from nonspinning binary-black-hole mergers on quasicircular orbits. We develop separate amplitude and phase models for the dominant $(l,m)=(2,0)$ spherical-harmonic mode of the GW memory signal. The amplitude and phase models are built from superpositions of elementary and transcendental functions, which can be evaluated efficiently. Both portions of the model are calibrated against a numerical-relativity surrogate model over mass ratios from one to eight. Their accuracy is assessed by computing their mismatch with the numerical-relativity models using the Advanced LIGO sensitivity curve from the fourth observing run. The mismatches are of the order $10^{-4}\unicode{x2013}10^{-3}$ over the parameter space of total mass covered by LIGO. The resulting frequency-domain model is a more computationally efficient waveform model for the GW memory signal than a related time-domain model earlier produced by the authors. An open-access implementation of both the time-domain and frequency-domain models is provided in the Python package GWMemoryModel, which can be used for relevant analyses of nonspinning binary black holes.

一句话导读：为无自旋双黑洞并合的引力波记忆信号构建了高效唯象频域波形模型。

相关度：low — 该论文属于经典引力与引力波波形建模，与理论全息或焦点标签无关联。

二次 f(R) 引力中的屏蔽标量毛与利用弱透镜区分黑洞和中子星

Screened Scalar Hair and the Weak-Lensing Separation of Black Holes from Neutron Stars in Quadratic $f(R)$ Gravity

Yashmitha Kumaran, Il\'idio Lopes · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：二次 $f(R)$ 引力携带一个有质量标量自由度——标量子（scalaron），其有限程 $\lambda$ 会屏蔽它对致密天体外部几何的影响。我们证明，这种屏蔽使得同质量黑洞与中子星的外部解彼此分离。史瓦西度规的修正呈 Yukawa 抑制，按照 $e^{-r/\lambda}$ 衰减而非耦合的幂律；因此任何对该耦合的幂级数展开都无法看到它；此外，外部解本质上各向同性，因此仅反转时间势能（如单势解所做）无法满足场方程。将此几何应用于 Gauss-Bonnet 定理，我们发现首阶偏转角恰好是广义相对论结果 $4GM/b$，标量子的贡献在弯曲光的组合中完全抵消。首个修正项带有不寻常的符号 $\lambda^{-1/2} b^{-3/2} e^{-b/\lambda}$，在标量子程长之外仍被屏蔽，并与直接数值求积对比，在 $b=4\lambda$ 处约 20% 一致，在 $b=12\lambda$ 处改进到 7%。存留下来的不是程度差异，而是种类差异。实现这一点的三个要素——耦合的非解析性、内禀各向同性规范以及压力加权的标量荷——首次在同一直洽推导中得到。静态黑洞没有标量毛，其透镜行为与 GR 完全一致；中子星则获得由抵抗坍缩的压力所加权的标量荷，因此与 GR 不同。因此弱透镜可以作为该理论的判别手段，而视界作为物质表面的对立面，原则上仍然是判据之一。观测优势不在于大 $b$ 处的偏转角，而在于光子球附近的强场成像，以及恒星内部（标量荷由物态方程决定）的成像。

English: Quadratic $f(R)$ gravity carries a massive scalar degree of freedom, the scalaron, whose finite range $\lambda$ screens its influence on the geometry outside a compact object. We show that this screening severs the exterior of a black hole from that of a neutron star of the same mass. The correction to the Schwarzschild metric is Yukawa-suppressed, falling as $e^{-r/\lambda}$ instead of polynomially in the coupling, and is thus invisible to any expansion in powers of that coupling; also, the exterior is intrinsically isotropic, so that inverting the temporal potential alone, as in single-potential solutions, fails to solve ]field equations. Applying the Gauss-Bonnet theorem to this geometry, we find the leading deflection angle to be exactly the general-relativistic $4GM/b$, the scalaron contributions cancelling identically in the combination that bends light. The first correction carries the unfamiliar signature $\lambda^{-1/2}b^{-3/2}e^{-b/\lambda}$, screened beyond the scalaron range and confirmed against direct quadrature to around 20% at $b=4\lambda$, improving to 7% at $b=12\lambda$. What survives is not a difference of degree but of kind. The three ingredients that deliver it, non-analyticity in the coupling, the intrinsically isotropic gauge and the pressure-weighted scalar charge, are here obtained within a single, self-consistent derivation for the first time. A static black hole carries no scalar hair, and lenses precisely as GR requires; a neutron star acquires a scalar charge weighted by the pressure supporting it against collapse, and does not. Weak lensing hence closes as a discriminant of the theory, whilst the horizon, as against a material surface, remains one in principle. The observational advantage lies not in bending angles at large $b$ but in the strong-field imaging of the photon sphere, and in the stellar interior, where the scalar charge is fixed by EoS.

一句话导读：二次 f(R) 引力中，黑洞没有标量毛而中子星有压力加权标量荷，其弱透镜修正被指数屏蔽，但强场成像可区分二者。

相关度：high — 论文直接研究二次 f(R) 修正引力中的黑洞/中子星弱透镜行为，属于用户重点标签中的“经典引力/广义相对论”支线。

空间曲率是否会在FLRW表观视界产生新的热力学临界性？

Does spatial curvature generate new thermodynamic criticality at the FLRW apparent horizon?

Samuel Lepe, Joel Saavedra · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：我们将[1]的表观视界热力学构造推广到具有非零空间曲率、无相互作用冷暗物质和全息型暗能量的FLRW宇宙。对于非零曲率，尺度因子是视界状态方程中的另一个几何变量。因此，通常的临界性条件在未对曲率部分提供闭合约定之前是欠定的。我们引入一个无量纲曲率变量，并将热力学变分限制为在该变量取常数的切片上进行。在每一这样的切片上，正的全息耦合和大于1的非线性幂次保证存在唯一的正临界点。临界比容、临界温度和临界压强会被空间曲率改变，而临界比和平均场临界指数保持不变。因此，曲率只是重标度临界量，并不会产生新的局域普适类。我们用物理热力学体积和与重标定后的视界温度共轭的熵构造了一个亥姆霍兹势。在二次全息模型中，由竞争分支的吉布斯自由能相等导出了与麦克斯韦构造等价的参数化共存曲线。相应的潜热在临界端点处消失，从而在固定曲率系综中产生全局的一级共存。物理FLRW轨迹是否会穿过临界或共存轨迹是一个独立的动力学问题，因为无量纲曲率变量通常会在宇宙膨胀过程中演化。

English: We generalize the apparent-horizon thermodynamic construction of [1] to a FLRW universe with non-zero spatial curvature, non-interacting cold dark matter and holographic-type dark energy. For nonzero curvature, the scale factor is an additional geometric variable in the horizon equation of state. The usual criticality conditions are thus not well defined until a closure prescription for the curvature sector is provided. We introduce a dimensionless curvature variable and restrict the thermodynamic variations to slices of constant value of this variable. For each such slice, a positive holographic coupling and nonlinear powers larger than one guaranty the existence of a unique positive critical point. The critical specific volume, temperature and pressure are shifted by the spatial curvature, while the critical ratio and the mean-field critical exponents remain unchanged. It thus rescales the critical quantities, without generating a new local universality class. We construct a Helmholtz potential out of the physical thermodynamic volume and the entropy conjugate to the rescaled horizon temperature. In the quadratic holographic model, the parametric coexistence curve which is equivalent to the Maxwell construction is derived from the equality of the Gibbs free energies of the competing branches. The associated latent heat disappears at the critical endpoint, resulting in a global first-order coexistence in the fixed-curvature ensemble. Whether a physical FLRW trajectory intersects the critical or coexistence locus is a separate dynamical question because the dimensionless curvature variable generally evolves during cosmological expansion.

一句话导读：将表观视界热力学推广到含空间曲率与全息型暗能量的FLRW宇宙，发现曲率只重标临界量而不改变普适类，并在固定曲率系综中得到一级共存与临界端点。

相关度：medium — 论文讨论FLRW表观视界的热力学临界性与一级相变共存曲线，与‘相变与气泡成核’及‘经典引力/广义相对论’标签相关，但并非全息强关联体系或全息超流/超导研究。

通过连续引力波探测致密天体的磁场

Probing magnetic fields of compact objects with continuous gravitational waves

Gopalkrishna Prabhu, Aditya Kumar Sharma, R. Prasad, David Keitel, Shasvath J. Kapadia · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：自转且形变的致密天体（如中子星）是连续引力波的典型源。这类天体可能带有能强烈影响其自转演化、进而影响其引力波可探测性的磁场。我们首次采用贝叶斯框架，利用LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA（LVK）第三次观测运行（O3）中未探测到连续引力波的观测结果，结合各LVK和Einstein@Home搜索给出的振幅上限，对银河系致密天体的诞生磁场分布施加星族层面的约束。为此，我们模拟了自转演化由引力波辐射和磁偶极辐射主导的致密天体星族，并探讨了多种椭圆度模型和磁场衰减时标，发现诞生磁场分布的超参数$B_0$下限被约束在 $10^{9.6}\mathrm{G} \lesssim B_0 \lesssim 10^{13.3}\mathrm{G}$ 区间内。此外，我们还把先前对银河系致密天体总数量的约束重新解释为具有星族平均磁场的致密天体数量上限，并给出其随椭圆度和引力波频率的变化关系。

English: Spinning, deformed compact objects such as neutron stars are canonical sources of continuous gravitational waves. These objects may be born with magnetic fields that can strongly influence their spin evolution and, consequently, their gravitational wave detectability. Employing a Bayesian framework, we use for the first time, the non detection of continuous gravitational waves in the LIGO Virgo KAGRA (LVK) third observing run (O3), using reported amplitude upper limits from various LVK and Einstein@Home searches, to place population level constraints on the birth magnetic field distribution of Galactic compact objects. To this end, we simulate compact object populations whose spin evolution is governed by gravitational wave emission and magnetic dipole radiation. We explore multiple ellipticity models and magnetic field decay timescales, and find that the lower limit on the birth magnetic field distribution hyperparameter $B_0$ is constrained to lie in the range $10^{9.6}\mathrm{G} \lesssim B_0\,\lesssim 10^{13.3}\mathrm{G}$. Furthermore, we reinterpret the number constraints on the total population of Galactic compact objects, reported previously by \citet{Prabhu_2024}, as upper limits on the number of compact objects with a population averaged magnetic field, presented as a function of ellipticity and gravitational wave frequency for all searches considered here.

一句话导读：利用连续引力波非探测结果对银河系致密天体（中子星）的诞生磁场分布进行贝叶斯星族推断。

相关度：low — 研究与全息/量子引力无关，属于经典致密天体连续引力波的天体物理与统计推断范畴，因此仅具有低相关性。

用强引力透镜双黑洞并合探测暗物质：近期前景

Probing Dark Matter with Strongly Lensed Binary Black Hole Mergers: Prospects in the Near Future

Koustav N. Maity, Souvik Jana, Ankur Barsode, Tejaswi Venumadhav, Parameswaran Ajith · gr-qc, astro-ph.CO · v1 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：引力波瞬变信号被 intervening 星系和星系团强引力透镜后，预计将构成地面探测器可探测事件的一小部分（约0.1%-0.5%）。一个强引力透镜双黑洞并合会产生多个在不同时间到达探测器的引力波信号副本。这些透镜事件的丰度和时间延迟分布对透镜星群的质量、密度轮廓和红移分布敏感，进而对暗物质的基本性质敏感。Jana等人（Phys.Rev.Lett. 135 (2025) 11, 111402）最近提出一种利用下一代引力波探测器可探测的强透镜事件来约束暖暗物质（WDM）粒子质量的方法。在本工作中，我们研究了利用升级后的LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA网络即将进行的观测来约束WDM粒子质量的前景。这需要对探测器选择效应进行仔细建模。即将到来的第五次观测运行（O5）预计只能给出适度的界限，因为在那早期阶段预计只有少数透镜对。到第六次观测运行（O6），我们预测WDM粒子质量界限为 m_wdm^{-1} ≲ 0.1-0.2 keV^{-1}（m_wdm ≳ 5-10 keV），与现有最严格的天体物理约束相当。O6之后的运行将进一步把约束收紧大约一个量级。因此，强透镜引力波提供了对暗物质的互补、有竞争力且独立的探针。

English: Gravitational-wave~(GW) transients, strongly lensed by intervening galaxies and clusters, are expected to constitute a small fraction ($\sim 0.1$-$0.5\%$) of the events detectable by ground-based detectors. A strongly lensed binary black hole (BBH) merger will produce multiple copies of the GW signal arriving at different times at the detector. The abundance and time-delay distribution of these lensed events are sensitive to the mass, density profile, and redshift distribution of the lens population, and in turn, to the underlying nature of dark matter. Jana et al. (Phys.Rev.Lett. 135 (2025) 11, 111402) recently proposed a method to constrain the masses of warm dark matter (WDM) particles making use of the strongly lensed events detectable by next-generation GW detectors. In this work, we investigate the prospects of constraining the mass of the WDM particle using upcoming observations of the upgraded LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA network. This requires a careful modeling of the detector selection effects. The upcoming fifth observing run (O5) is expected to yield only a modest bound, since only a few lensed pairs are expected at that early stage. By the sixth observing run (O6), we forecast a bound on the WDM particle mass, $m_{\rm wdm}^{-1} \lesssim 0.1$-$0.2\text{keV}^{-1}$ ($m_{\rm wdm} \gtrsim 5$-$10\text{keV}$), which is comparable to the tightest existing astrophysical constraints. Runs beyond O6 will tighten the constraint by roughly an order of magnitude. Strongly lensed GWs therefore offer a complementary, competitive, and independent probe of dark matter.

一句话导读：利用强引力透镜双黑洞并合事件预测暖暗物质质量约束，表明未来O6运行可达到与现有天体物理约束相当的水平。

相关度：low — 该研究属于引力波天体物理与暗物质探测，与给定的全息/黑洞信息/凝聚态等理论物理支线无明显关联。

利用引力波探测中子星状态方程普适性

Probing Neutron Star Equation of State Universality with Gravitational Waves

Praveer Tiwari, Ajit Kumar Mehta, K. G. Arun · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE, nucl-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：当前对中子星状态方程的推断假设所有中子星共享同一状态方程。然而，对致密物质物理理解不足可能产生不同的长寿命恒星亚群，它们遵循不同的质量-潮汐形变关系，从而使这一假设失效。在这封信中，我们证明下一代引力波天文台能够将中子星状态方程的普适性从假设转变为可检验的假说。通过将分层贝叶斯推断与中子星状态方程的分段多方参数化相结合，我们建立了一个利用下一代引力波天文台检验状态方程普适性的框架。我们发现，即使只有20%（10%）的种群表现出所考虑的代表性非普适参数化之一，约30（50）次网络信噪比高于100的明亮双中子星并合事件就足以提供令人信服的证据证明状态方程普适性的偏离。

English: Current neutron star equation-of-state inferences assume that a common equation of state describes all neutron stars. However, {poorly understood dense-matter physics may give rise to distinct long-lived stellar subpopulations following different mass--tidal-deformability relations, invalidating this assumption. In this Letter, we demonstrate that next-generation gravitational-wave observatories enable neutron star equation-of-state universality to be transformed from an assumption into a testable hypothesis. By combining hierarchical Bayesian inference with a piecewise-polytropic parametrization of the neutron star equation of state, we establish a framework for testing equation-of-state universality with next-generation gravitational-wave observatories. We find that about 30 (50) loud binary neutron star mergers with network SNRs above 100 are sufficient to provide compelling evidence for departures from equation-of-state universality, even if only 20% (10%) of the population exhibits one of the representative non-universality parametrizations considered here.

一句话导读：提出了利用下一代引力波观测检验中子星状态方程普适性的统计框架，并估算了所需事件数。

具有 L^s_loc Christoffel 符号的球对称弱零奇异性不可延展性

Spherically symmetric inextendibility of weak null singularities with Christoffel symbols in $L^s_{\text{loc}}$

Peter Cameron, Jan Sbierski · gr-qc, math.DG · v1 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：受广义相对论中强宇宙监督猜想的启发，我们证明了球对称弱零奇异性作为具有连续度规且 Christoffel 符号属于 L^s_loc（s>1）的球对称洛伦兹流形是不可延展的。该结果假设面积半径函数沿横向于弱零奇异性的导数满足适当的爆破条件，并结合了文献[1]中关于跨越零边界的连续球对称延拓的刚性结果。特别地，我们证明这些假设被 Reissner-Nordström-Vaidya 时空以及爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦-标量场系统下亚极值 Reissner-Nordström 的小而一般球对称扰动所产生的一类时空满足。

English: Motivated by the strong cosmic censorship conjecture in general relativity, we prove the inextendibility of spherically symmetric weak null singularities as spherically symmetric Lorentzian manifolds with a continuous metric and Christoffel symbols in $L^{s}_{\text{loc}}$ for $s>1$. The result assumes a suitable blow-up condition on the derivative of the area-radius function transverse to the weak null singularity in combination with a rigidity result on continuous spherically symmetric extensions across null boundaries proven in [1]. In particular we show that these assumptions are satisfied by the Reissner-Nordstr\"{o}m-Vaidya spacetime as well as by a class of spacetimes arising from small and generic spherically symmetric perturbations of subextremal Reissner-Nordstr\"{o}m under the Einstein-Maxwell-scalar field system.

一句话导读：证明在连续度规且 Christoffel 符号具有 L^s 正则性的条件下，球对称弱零奇异性不可延展，并验证 RN-Vaidya 与微扰 RN 时空满足相应条件。

3G探测器中长时标引力波信号的快速贝叶斯推断

Fast Bayesian Inference for Long-Duration Gravitational-Wave Signals in 3G detectors

Anand S. Sengupta · gr-qc, astro-ph.CO, astro-ph.IM · v1 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：第三代（3G）引力波探测器将观测持续近一天的双中子星并合前旋近信号，因此地球自旋成为信号的一部分，而非可忽略的修正。这破坏了通常的内在/外在参数分离，并为贝叶斯推断带来重大计算瓶颈。我们证明精确的五谐波Jaranowski-Krolak-Schutz（JKS）分解可以将旋转天线振幅响应与计算昂贵的内在波形计算分离。通过在其相位曲率设定的频率网格上对内在波形采样，再应用误差控制的相对分箱，一个21.3小时信号的似然评估被加速约10^4倍，使得全天3G信号推断变得可行。

English: Third-generation (3G) gravitational-wave detectors will observe binary-neutron-star inspirals for nearly a day, so Earth's rotation becomes part of the signal rather than a negligible correction. This destroys the usual separation between intrinsic and extrinsic parameters and creates a major computational bottleneck for Bayesian inference. We show that the exact five-harmonic Jaranowski-Krolak-Schutz (JKS) decomposition restores the separation of the rotating antenna amplitude response from the computationally expensive intrinsic waveform calculations. By sampling the intrinsic waveform on a frequency grid set by its phase curvature and then applying error-controlled relative binning, likelihood evaluations for a 21.3-h signal are accelerated by O(10^4), making day-long 3G signal inference practical.

完美流体暗物质对Yang-Mills修正带电黑洞阴影观测量的影响

Influence of Perfect Fluid Dark Matter on Shadow Observables of Yang-Mills modified charged black holes

Md Sabir Ali, Abhishek Negi, Sanjay Pant · gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：我们研究了完美流体暗物质（PFDM）对Yang-Mills启发带电黑洞的影响，特别关注由此产生的关键黑洞观测量的修正。通过将PFDM项嵌入时空几何中，我们考察了阴影形态、光子测地线以及相关能量发射谱的变化。我们的分析表明，PFDM导致阴影尺寸、形状和圆度出现显著偏差，并显著影响圆轨道的稳定性。此外，能量发射率对Yang-Mills电荷和暗物质分布都有强烈依赖。这些结果表明，暗物质引起的环境效应可以在黑洞阴影和辐射过程中留下可观测的特征，为约束暗物质模型以及利用即将到来的高精度观测（如事件视界望远镜和下一代干涉仪）探测非Kerr几何提供了潜在途径。

English: We investigate the influence of perfect fluid dark matter (PFDM) on Yang--Mills--inspired charged black holes, with a particular focus on the resulting modifications to key black hole observables. By embedding a PFDM term into the spacetime geometry, we examine the alterations in shadow morphology, photon geodesics, and the associated energy emission spectra. Our analysis reveals that PFDM induces notable deviations in the shadow size, shape, and circularity, and significantly impacts the stability of circular orbits. Furthermore, the energy emission rate exhibits a strong dependence on both the Yang--Mills charge and the dark matter distribution. These results indicate that environmental effects arising from dark matter can imprint observable signatures on black hole shadows and radiation processes, offering a potential pathway to constrain dark matter models and probe non-Kerr geometries with forthcoming high-precision observations such as those from the Event Horizon Telescope and next-generation interferometers.

大偏心率渐近和后牛顿偏心引力波傅里叶模式的快速解析近似

Large-Eccentricity Asymptotics and Fast Analytic Approximation for Fourier modes of Post-Newtonian Eccentric Waveforms

Xiaolin Liu, Zhoujian Cao · gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：本文发展了对准开普勒参数化下后牛顿双星系统在大偏心率区域引力波傅里叶模式进行解析渐近的方法。作者还推导了辐射尾部贡献中偏心率增强函数的大偏心率渐近展开。基于这些结果，他们构造了一种端点约束的解析近似，可显著加速大偏心率下傅里叶模式的计算。该解析近似的整体误差控制在10^{-3}以内，且对p≤200、e≤0.9的傅里叶模式均有效。这项工作为高偏心率双星系统的频域引力波建模提供了解析构件。

English: In this work, we develop analytic asymptotic methods for computing the Fourier modes of gravitational waves from post-Newtonian binary systems in the quasi-Keplerian parametrization in the high eccentricity regime. We also derive the large-eccentricity asymptotic expansion of the eccentricity enhancement function appearing in the tail contributions to the radiation. Furthermore, based on these results, we construct an endpoint-constrained analytic approximation that significantly accelerates the computation of the Fourier modes at large eccentricity. The overall error of this analytic approximation is controlled within $10^{-3}$, and it remains valid for Fourier modes with $p\le200$ and $e\le 0.9$. This approach provides analytic building blocks for modeling frequency-domain gravitational waves from highly eccentric binaries.

非对易薄壳引力真空星的阴影

Shadow of a Noncommutative Thin-Shell Gravastar

M. A. Anacleto, A. T. N. Silva, L. Casarini · gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：天文学的主要挑战之一是直接观测黑洞。然而，通过光子观测区分黑洞与无视界的超致密天体可能很困难，因为后者也可能拥有不稳定的圆光子轨道。超致密天体的一个例子是Gravastar（引力真空星），最初由Mazur和Mottola提出。为了明确概念，我们在非对易模型中构建了一个球对称薄壳gravastar模型，其中这些效应通过具有最小宽度√θ的洛伦兹能量密度分布来整合。该模型使用切割-粘贴技术构建，将非奇异的de Sitter内部连接到非对易Schwarzschild外部，并在界面超曲面Σ上满足Israel衔接条件。我们通过能量条件检验了模型的稳定性，强调了非对易参数对其行为的影响。还利用从线性化稳定性分析得到的参数η研究了模型稳定性。我们还表明，由于非对易性，光子轨迹和偏转角随着非对易参数的增加而变化。我们提出的具有外部非对易几何的gravastar模型，可以被视为在这种类型引力背景下带电黑洞的一个稳定且可行的替代方案。

English: One of the main challenges in astronomy is the direct observation of black holes. However, distinguishing black holes from ultra-compact horizonless objects through photon observations can be difficult, since the latter may also possess unstable circular photon orbits. An example of an Ultra-Compact Object is a Gravastar (gravitational vacuum star), initially proposed by Mazur and Mottola. For definition purposes, we construct a spherically symmetric thin-shell gravastar model within a noncommutative model, in which these effects are integrated by a Lorentzian distribution of the energy density with a minimum width $\sqrt{\theta}$. The model is constructed using the cut-and-paste technique to connect a nonsingular de Sitter interior to a noncommutative Schwarzschild exterior, satisfying the Israel junction conditions on the interface hypersurface $\Sigma$. We examine the stability of the model through its energy conditions, highlighting the influence of the noncommutative parameter on its behavior. {Model stability was also investigated using the parameter $\eta$, derived from the linearized stability analysis.} We also show that, owing to noncommutativity, the photon trajectories and deflection angles change as the noncommutative parameter increases. Our proposed gravastar model, with noncommutative geometry on its exterior, can be considered a stable and viable alternative to the charged black hole in the context of this type of gravity.

一句话导读：构建非对易薄壳引力真空星模型，研究其稳定性与光子阴影，作为黑洞的替代天体。

相关度：medium — 该论文属于经典引力/广义相对论中的致密天体与阴影研究，与用户指定的全息/引力研究方向关联较弱，但可作为经典引力支线的参考。

基于统一Heun函数框架的高效稳定计算一般Kerr轨道引力波通量

Efficient and stable computation of gravitational-wave fluxes from generic Kerr orbits via a unified Heun-function framework

Changkai Chen, Zhoujian Cao, Jiliang Jing · gr-qc · v4 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：极端质量比旋近的建模依赖于对一般Kerr轨道引力波通量的精确高效计算。传统频域方法常受限于昂贵的辅助参数搜索以及强场或高频区域的数值不稳定性。本文通过将角向和径向Teukolsky方程统一改写为合流Heun函数形式来解决这些挑战。采用混合解析延拓算法计算连接系数，消除了对辅助参数的依赖，直接得到全局收敛解和散射振幅。为处理一般轨道高度振荡的源被积函数，实现了自适应双幂映射求积方法。在标准双精度算术下的全面基准测试表明，对于总计168个低阶模式的总辐射通量，该方法相对误差约为10^{-11}，计算成本相比当前最优的GeneralizedSasakiNakamura.jl和pybhpt包通常降低3至13倍。尤其对于高度振荡的高阶模式，与专用振荡积分器如GeneralizedSasakiNakamura.jl相比，该框架可实现高达60倍的加速。这些精度与效率的提升表明该框架是强场微扰理论的稳健工具，为高阶自力和快速高精度波形生成提供了数值基础。

English: Modeling extreme-mass-ratio inspirals hinges on the accurate and efficient computation of gravitational-wave fluxes from generic Kerr orbits. Conventional frequency-domain techniques are often limited by costly auxiliary parameter searches and numerical instabilities in the strong-field or high-frequency regimes. We address these challenges by reformulating both the angular and radial Teukolsky equations in terms of confluent Heun functions. Employing a hybrid analytic continuation algorithm to compute the connection coefficients eliminates the dependence on auxiliary parameters, directly yielding globally convergent solutions and scattering amplitudes. To resolve the highly oscillatory source integrands for generic orbits, we implement an adaptive bi-power mapping quadrature. Comprehensive benchmarks under standard double-precision arithmetic demonstrate that, for the total radiative flux summed over 168 low-order modes, our method achieves relative errors of order $10^{-11}$, with computational costs typically reduced by factors of 3--13 compared to the state-of-the-art GeneralizedSasakiNakamura. jl and pybhpt packages. Notably, for highly oscillatory high-order modes, our framework achieves a speedup of up to 60 times compared to specialized oscillatory integrators like GeneralizedSasakiNakamura. jl. These demonstrated gains in precision and efficiency establish the framework as a robust tool for strong-field perturbation theory, providing the numerical foundation for high-order self-force calculations and rapid, high-precision waveform generation.

一句话导读：通过将Teukolsky方程重新表述为合流Heun函数并解析计算连接系数，显著提升Kerr轨道引力波通量计算的速度与精度。

相关度：high — 论文研究经典广义相对论中的强场黑洞微扰和引力波通量计算，直接属于用户重点标签中的“经典引力/广义相对论”支线，因此相关性高。

正则黑洞、超对称孤子与全息旋转等离子体盘上的边界-边界对偶

Boundary-Boundary Duality on Regular Black Holes, Supersymmetric Solitons and Holographic Spinning Plasma Disks

Andres Anabalon, Horatiu Nastase · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：我们构造了具有负宇宙学常数的四维爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦理论的一族新的精确旋转解，描述正则旋转 AdS 黑洞和平滑孤子。这些几何没有曲率奇异性，并允许参数空间中不存在闭合类时曲线的开区域，而超对称极限对应于因果正则的无视界组态。这些解的一个显著特征是存在至少两个余维一的共形边界。第一个是闵可夫斯基时空，其中能量-动量张量的期望值描述强耦合共形等离子体的有限旋转盘，其边缘以光速旋转。第二个边界是具有零能量-动量张量但非零 Cotton 张量的旋转黑洞几何，定义了一个全息理论，其中对偶引力子在边界处被固定。我们证明，在适当的解析延拓以及将全息能标与旋转流体的洛伦兹因子等同后，与对偶引力子相关的能量密度精确再现了等离子体的能量密度，从而为边界-边界对偶提供了证据。事实上，电磁对偶在支持引力子的边界和支持对偶引力子的边界之间交换电、磁流，从而在这些边界理论之间提供了广义的镜像对称。因此，洛伦兹因子被等同于重正化能标的电磁对偶。在适当极限下，这些解约化为平面 Reissner-Nordström-AdS 黑洞，而超对称孤子没有正则静态极限。

English: We construct a new family of exact rotating solutions of four-dimensional Einstein--Maxwell theory with negative cosmological constant describing regular spinning AdS black holes and smooth solitons. The geometries are free of curvature singularities and admit open regions of parameter space without closed timelike curves, while supersymmetric limits correspond to causally regular horizonless configurations. A remarkable feature of these solutions is the existence of at least two codimension-one conformal boundaries. The first is Minkowski spacetime, where the expectation value of the energy--momentum tensor describes a finite spinning disk of strongly coupled conformal plasma whose edge rotates at the speed of light. The second boundary is a rotating black-hole geometry with vanishing energy--momentum tensor but non-vanishing Cotton tensor, defining a holographic theory in which the dual graviton is fixed at the boundary. We show that the energy density associated with the dual graviton exactly reproduces that of the plasma after a suitable analytic continuation and the identification of the holographic energy scale with the Lorentz factor of the rotating fluid, providing evidence for a boundary-boundary duality. Indeed, electromagnetic duality exchanges the electric and magnetic currents between the boundary supporting the graviton and that supporting the dual graviton thus providing a generalized mirror symmetry between these boundary theories. Hence, the Lorentz factor is identified with the electromagnetic dual of the renormalization scale. In an appropriate limit, the solutions reduce to the planar Reissner--Nordstr\"om--AdS black hole, whereas the supersymmetric solitons have no regular static limit.

一句话导读：构造带负宇宙学常数的 4D Einstein-Maxwell 精确旋转正则黑洞/孤子，提出并给出边界-边界对偶与旋转共形等离子体盘的全息证据。

相关度：high — 论文构造了 Einstein-Maxwell 理论中的精确旋转正则黑洞和孤子，涉及经典时空几何、奇异性和类时曲线等经典引力关键问题，同时又将解嵌入 AdS/CFT 获得边界-边界对偶，因此对经典引力/广义相对论关注线高度相关。

1/2 BPS 三点函数的开-闭-开三重性

Open-Closed-Open Triality for 1/2 BPS Three-Point Functions

Keisuke Konosu, Kenta Suzuki · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：开-闭-开三重性通过一个公共的闭弦理论联系两种不同的开弦描述。本文针对四维 N=4 超 Yang-Mills 理论中受保护的 1/2 BPS 三点函数，发展了一种开-闭-开三重性。从表示巨引子的行列式插入出发，我们构造出 V 型开弦高斯三矩阵模型；通过色-味变换，推导出由三个双基本矩阵组成三角 quiver 的 F 型开弦模型。其闭弦 quiver 游走包含有向三次环，这是二部图两点情形所没有的特征。我们通过推导精确的有限 N 单巨引子多项式、复现已知的 F 型大 N 鞍点，并借助组合数据间的显式双射证明 V 型与 F 型特征展开的等价性，来检验该对应。在闭弦端，连通的三矩阵收缩决定三色整数 Strebel 曲面。我们论证相应的靶空间构造自然由重心细分 Belyi 映射或彩色 Hurwitz 星座描述。

English: Open-closed-open triality relates two distinct open string descriptions through a common closed string theory. In this paper, we develop an open-closed-open triality for protected half-BPS three-point functions in four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills theory. Starting from determinant insertions representing giant gravitons, we formulate a V-type open-string Gaussian three-matrix model and, through a color-flavor transformation, derive an F-type open-string model of three bifundamental matrices forming a triangular quiver. Its closed quiver walks include oriented cubic cycles, a feature absent from the bipartite two-point case. We test the correspondence by deriving exact finite-$N$ single-giant polynomials, recovering the known F-type large-$N$ saddles, and proving the equivalence of the V- and F-type character expansions via an explicit bijection of their combinatorial data. At the closed string corner, connected three-matrix contractions determine three-colored integer Strebel surfaces. We argue that the corresponding target-space construction is naturally described by a barycentrically subdivided Belyi map or a colored Hurwitz constellation.

一句话导读：在 N=4 SYM 中建立 1/2 BPS 三点函数的开-闭-开三重性，并将其与三角 quiver 矩阵模型、Strebel 曲面和 Belyi/Hurwitz 组合结构联系起来。

相关度：low — 该工作属于 AdS/CFT 基础字典和半 BPS 关联函数的矩阵模型/开闭弦构造，不涉及全息超流超导、p波、相变/气泡、QNM、经典引力等用户重点标签；因此与当前重点研究支线无明显直接交集，仅有背景参考价值。

迈向量子黑洞视界的无尽旅程

The endless journey towards the horizon of a quantum black hole

Victor Franken, Thomas G. Mertens, Bruno de S. L. Torres · hep-th, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：在量子引力中，量子效应是否会导致黑洞视界附近出现非平凡结构，以及黑洞内部是否为某个大N极限的涌现特性，是一个非常重要的问题。我们证明，黑洞的强混沌性质（由其谱的随机矩阵统计所刻画）将使一位经过适当引力修饰的落体观测者需要无限长的固有时才能到达视界。这使得黑洞内部对于任何外部观测者而言在操作上不可及，标志着量子引力中大N因果结构的显著破裂。为了模拟落体观测者的体验，我们研究了在二维量子黑洞背景中与物质场耦合的落体粒子探测器，发现当探测器进入接近黑洞视界的无限长非微扰旅程时，其激发概率出现峰值。

English: It is a problem of great importance in quantum gravity whether quantum effects lead to nontrivial structure near black hole horizons, and if their interiors are emergent features of a large $N$ limit. We show that the strongly chaotic nature of black holes, captured by random matrix statistics of their spectrum, makes it so that a suitably gravitationally dressed infalling observer takes an infinite amount of proper time to reach the horizon. This makes the black hole interior operationally inaccessible to any outside observer, marking a sharp breakdown of the large $N$ causal structure of black holes in quantum gravity. To model the experience of an infalling observer, we study the response of an infalling particle detector coupled to a matter field in a 2d quantum black hole background, finding a peak in the detector's excitation probability as it enters an infinitely long non-perturbative journey close to the black hole horizon.

一句话导读：展示黑洞的量子混沌与随机矩阵谱使落体观测者需无限固有时才能到达视界，从而在操作上无法进入黑洞内部。

相关度：low — 该研究聚焦于黑洞量子内部结构与落体探测者动力学，与用户指定的全息超流超导、p波序参量、畴壁/孤子/涡旋、QNM、D3-D7探针膜等具体研究支线不直接相关。

动态打开的耦合SYK Majorana结中的红外记忆与量子置乱

Infrared Memory and Scrambling in a Dynamically Opened Coupled-SYK Majorana Junction

Ali Vahedi · hep-th, cond-mat.str-el · v1 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：我们研究一个由两个耦合的Sachdev–Ye–Kitaev（SYK）系统构成的动态打开的Majorana结。与时间相关的双线性耦合把系统从几乎退耦的情形驱动到强混合区间，从而实现了相互作用的Majorana光闸。利用围绕共形q=4 SYK鞍点的大N Keldysh理论低频分析，我们发现这种打开机制保留很强的红外记忆，且相关反常响应对红外截断越来越敏感。两个N=8 SYK团簇的精确有限维模拟显示，打开后扇区间纠缠增强、宇称转移，并出现跨扇区算符置乱。这些结果将共形SYK动力学的红外结构与有限尺寸多体置乱联系起来，说明动态打开的相互作用Majorana系统在二次极限之外仍能保留长波记忆。

English: We study a dynamically opened Majorana junction formed by two coupled Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev (SYK) systems. A time-dependent bilinear coupling drives the system from a nearly decoupled to a strongly hybridized regime, providing an interacting realization of a Majorana shutter. Using a low-frequency analysis of the large-$N$ Keldysh theory around the conformal $q=4$ SYK saddle, we find that the opening retains a strong infrared memory, with the associated anomalous response becoming increasingly sensitive to the infrared cutoff. Exact finite-dimensional simulations of two $N=8$ SYK clusters show enhanced inter-sector entanglement, parity transfer, and cross-sector operator scrambling after the opening. These results connect the infrared structure of conformal SYK dynamics with finite-size many-body scrambling and show how a dynamically opened interacting Majorana system can retain long-wavelength memory beyond the quadratic limit.

一句话导读：动态打开的耦合SYK Majorana结在共形SYK鞍点附近保留显著红外记忆，并通过有限尺寸动力学增强扇区间纠缠与跨扇区置乱。

相关度：low — 该文主要研究SYK多体量子动力学、纠缠与置乱，未涉及用户标注的全息超流、相变/气泡成核、QNM、探针膜/驱动或经典引力等具体支线。

解紧致化与AdS$_5\times$ S$^5$主关联函数的平直空间极限

Decompactification and the Flat-Space Limit of AdS$_5\times$ S$^5$ Master Correlators

Burkhard Eden, Paul Heslop, Harshal Kulkarni, Arthur Lipstein, Romain Ruzziconi, Akshay Yelleshpur Srikant · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：我们研究AdS$_5$/CFT$_4$全息关联函数的平直空间极限，同时考虑内部S$^5$的解紧致化。我们证明该极限自然地在主传播子和主关联函数框架中表述，后者重求和了无穷多Kaluza-Klein模。对于边界关联函数，所得表达式可解释为运动学限制在5维的振幅。然后我们考虑外部插入保持在AdS$_5\times$S$^5$体内部后取极限的主关联函数，并论证它们恢复了10维闵氏空间中无运动学限制的平直空间振幅。最后，我们证明，若对球面进行解析延拓使体几何变为AdS$_5 \times$dS$_5$，则平直空间极限与边界极限可交换，所得公式建议一种在具有两个时间方向的平直空间中、满足两个同时零约束的10维运动学振幅的解释。我们讨论了这些构造对平直空间全息及其与对偶CFT的Carrollian极限关系的启示。

English: We study the flat-space limit of AdS$_5$/CFT$_4$ holographic correlators while accounting for the simultaneous decompactification of the internal S$^5$. We show that this limit is naturally formulated in terms of master propagators and correlators, which resum the infinite tower of Kaluza--Klein modes. For boundary correlators, the resulting expressions can be interpreted in terms of amplitudes with kinematics restricted to 5d. We then consider master correlators whose external insertions are kept in the AdS$_5\times$S$^5$ bulk before taking the limit, and argue that they recover flat-space amplitudes with unrestricted kinematics in 10d Minkowski space. Finally, we show that the flat-space limit commutes with the boundary limit if we analytically continue the sphere so that the bulk geometry becomes AdS$_5 \times$dS$_5$, and the resulting formulae suggest an interpretation in terms of amplitudes in flat space with two time directions and with 10d kinematics satisfying two simultaneous null constraints. We discuss the implications of these constructions for flat-space holography and its relation to the Carrollian limit of the dual CFT.

一句话导读：该论文研究AdS5/CFT4主关联函数在S5解紧致化下的平直空间极限，揭示其与10维平直空间振幅及Carrollian极限的联系。

相关度：low — 该论文属于AdS/CFT基础与平直时空全息的一般理论，与给定的具体重点标签（如全息超流超导、p波等）无直接关联。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

修正熵引力中的静态球对称解

Static Spherically Symmetric Solutions in Modified Entropic Gravity

A. Rostami, M. Rostampour, A. Yarahmadi, K. Rezazadeh · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：在Verlinde提出的熵引力范式中，假设位于全息屏幕上的微观自由度遵循能量均分定律。然而，统计力学意味着这种能量分配可能获得依赖于温度的修正。考虑这些修正会导致从热力学推导出的引力场方程发生变化。我们在静态球对称时空情形下求解得到的修正爱因斯坦方程，并确定度规分量的一般结构。我们的发现表明，如果温度修正函数呈现f(T)∝T^2的行为，相应的时空几何会退化为平直的闵可夫斯基度规。因此，要获得对平直时空的微小偏离，需要考虑对该纯二次温度依赖的轻微偏离。这种偏离可以被解释为编码了额外的引力效应，可能涉及并非完全由全息屏幕描述的物质贡献。作为这些效应的结果，有效引力势获得了一个对数修正，这可能引起对牛顿引力的偏离。这一方面在极弱引力场区域尤为重要，这些修正可能影响粒子运动，并对天体物理和宇宙学背景产生影响。

English: Within the entropic gravity paradigm introduced by Verlinde, one assumes that the microscopic degrees of freedom residing on the holographic screen obey the equipartition law of energy. Nevertheless, implications of statistical mechanics suggest that this energy sharing can acquire corrections that depend on temperature. Taking such modifications into account leads to altered gravitational field equations when derived from thermodynamic considerations. We solve the resulting modified Einstein equations in the case of a static, spherically symmetric spacetime and determine the general structure of the metric components. Our findings indicate that if the temperature correction function behaves as $f(T)\propto T^{2}$, the corresponding spacetime geometry reduces to the flat Minkowski metric. Therefore, to obtain small deviations from flatness, it is necessary to consider slight departures from this purely quadratic temperature dependence. Such deviations can be interpreted as encoding additional gravitational effects, potentially associated with matter contributions that are not entirely described by the holographic screen. As a result of these effects, the effective gravitational potential acquires a logarithmic correction, which can give rise to deviations from Newtonian gravity. This aspect is especially significant in the regime of very weak gravitational fields, where these corrections may affect particle motion and could carry implications for both astrophysical and cosmological contexts.

一句话导读：研究温度修正熵引力下的修正爱因斯坦方程，发现f(T)∝T^2时时空平直，偏离时引力势出现对数修正，影响弱引力场。

相关度：medium — 该论文属于修正引力理论，与经典引力/广义相对论标签相关，但不属于用户重点关注的全息或经典引力精确研究方向，因此相关度为中等。

KMOC 形式论中经典极限的平滑性

Smoothness of Classical Limit in KMOC Formalism

Pritish Sinha · hep-th · v5 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：我们重新审视了 Kosower、Maybee 和 O'Connell 发展的形式论中，包含可观测量经典极限的平滑性。在先前工作 [1-3] 的基础上，我们证明了散射角、辐射场和角冲量这三类包含可观测量的经典极限在微扰的所有阶上都是平滑的，不存在任何所谓的超经典发散。我们使用标量 QED 作为模型。我们的分析在一定程度上表明，KMOC 形式论可以通过简单地聚焦于所有按 ℏ^0 标度的项来计算经典辐射，因为所有按 ℏ 的负幂标度的项都会消失。通过这一分析，我们成功地分离出了对经典极限有贡献的项的类型，从而使物理可观测量能够被更直接、更高效地计算。

English: In this paper, we revisit the smoothness of the classical limit of inclusive observables in the formalism developed by Kosower, Maybee and O'Connell (KMOC). Building on the earlier work [1-3], we prove that the classical limit of three classes of inclusive observables, namely scattering angle, radiative field and angular impulse is smooth and does not suffer from any so-called superclassical divergences at all orders in perturbation. We use scalar QED as a model. Our analysis goes some way in showing that KMOC formalism can be used to compute classical radiation by simply focusing on all the terms that scale as $\hbar^{0}$, as all the terms that scale with inverse power of $\hbar$ vanish. Through this analysis, we have successfully isolated the classes of terms that contribute to the classical limit, thereby allowing direct and more efficient computations of physical observables.

一句话导读：证明 KMOC 形式论中三类包含可观测量（散射角、辐射场、角冲量）的经典极限在所有微扰阶平滑，无超经典发散，可仅通过提取 ℏ^0 项高效计算经典辐射。

相关度：medium — 该论文属于 KMOC 散射形式论中的经典极限分析，目的是高效计算经典辐射、散射角等可观测量，紧密服务于经典引力/广义相对论的计算方法，因此与用户经典引力支线相关，但未涉及全息/AdS 或量子引力内容。

共形场论的磁化界

Magnetised Bounds for Conformal Field Theories

Christopher P. Herzog, William H. Pannell, Biswajit Sahoo, Andreas Stergiou · hep-th, cond-mat.str-el · v2 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：我们研究了具有全局 U(1) 对称性的三维保宇称、电荷共轭不变的共形场论在背景磁场下的若干方面。基于磁场将理论驱动到带隙相的假设，构建了一个到四阶导数阶的局域有效作用量。该作用量在各种背景下求值，并用于获得守恒流和应力-能量张量的单点和两点函数。发展了色散论证，并证明它们对有效作用量的 Wilson 系数施加了很强的约束，从而得到大磁场下 CFT 响应的普适预言以及背景磁单极算符的标度维数。这些一般结果还通过自由复标量、自由 Dirac 费米子和一个全息 Einstein-Hilbert-Maxwell 模型的显式计算进行了检验。

English: Aspects of parity-preserving, charge-conjugation-invariant, three-dimensional conformal field theories (CFTs) with a global $U(1)$ symmetry in the presence of a background magnetic field are investigated. A local effective action is constructed to four-derivative order, based on an assumption that the magnetic field drives the theory into a gapped phase. This action is evaluated in a variety of backgrounds, and is used to obtain one- and two-point functions of the conserved current and stress-energy tensor. Dispersive arguments are developed and shown to impose powerful constraints on the Wilson coefficients of the effective action, leading to universal predictions for the CFT response at large magnetic field and the scaling dimensions of background monopole operators. These general results are further examined through explicit calculations in the free complex scalar, free Dirac fermion, and a holographic Einstein-Hilbert-Maxwell model.

一句话导读：通过有效场论和色散约束研究三维 CFT 在外磁场下的响应，并给出全息模型验证。

相关度：low — 论文主题为一般 CFT 在磁场下的有效场论约束和色散边界，与用户关注的全息超流超导、相变、非均匀态等具体凝聚态全息方向没有直接关联。
