一种统一杨-米尔斯场、引力与物质费米子的几何作用量原理

A Geometric Action Principle for Unification of Yang-Mills Fields, Gravity and Matter Fermions

Pedro D. Alvarez, Fernando Izaurieta, Cristian Quinzacara · hep-th, gr-qc, math-ph, math.MP · v5 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：单个几何作用量，作为受限的六维 Clifford 生成不变量族的一个线性组合，同时生成引力、杨-米尔斯和费米子扇区，并使它们的结构与相对系数相关联。所得参数空间包含已知的线性化无鬼二次引力模型，其约化玻色子扇区具有传播挠率。因此，我们的结果建立了一条经济的几何途径来实现规范-引力统一，既不要求额外维度，也不要求外部引入的对称性破缺场。特别地，Clifford 代数构造自然容纳这样的参数子空间：约化玻色子扇区重现已知的无鬼理论，而独立的 Klein-Gordon 型费米子动能项消失。

English: A single geometric action, constructed as a linear combination of a restricted six-dimensional family of Clifford-generated invariant terms, simultaneously generates the gravitational, Yang--Mills, and fermionic sectors and correlates their structure and relative coefficients. The resulting parameter space contains known linearized ghost-free quadratic gravitational models with propagating torsion in a reduced bosonic sector. Our results therefore establish an economical geometric route to gauge--gravity unification that requires neither extra dimensions nor an externally introduced symmetry-breaking field. In particular, the Clifford-algebraic construction naturally accommodates parameter subspaces in which the reduced bosonic sector reproduces known ghost-free theories and the independent Klein--Gordon-type fermionic kinetic term vanishes.

一句话导读：构建一个 Clifford 代数几何作用量，同时统一引力、杨-米尔斯与费米子场，且无需额外维度或外部对称性破缺场。

相关度：medium — 论文研究几何作用量统一引力、杨-米尔斯与费米子，主要属于经典引力/广义相对论方向，与用户重点标签中的经典引力/广义相对论相关；但与全息、黑洞量子引力等信息方向无关。

Sasaki-Einstein 五流形上的全息谱函数

Holographic spectral functions for Sasaki-Einstein 5-manifolds

Yu Nakayama, Tadashi Okazaki · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：我们研究了与四维超共形场论对偶的一般 Sasaki-Einstein 5 流形上的全息谱函数，包括超对称指标、超对称 zeta 函数和超对称行列式。超对称 zeta 函数的解析结构，特别是其留数和特殊值，使得能够计算 Sasaki-Einstein 流形的曲率平方积分和次领头全息反常。该谱框架的适用范围不限于环几何，也涵盖非环 Sasaki-Einstein 流形。对于环 Sasaki-Einstein 流形，我们发展了一种组合方法，可直接从环数据计算全息谱函数和全息几何不变量。

English: We investigate holographic spectral functions for general Sasaki-Einstein 5-manifolds dual to four-dimensional superconformal field theories, including supersymmetric indices, supersymmetric zeta functions, and supersymmetric determinants. The analytic structure of the supersymmetric zeta function, particularly its residue and special value, allows for the computation of the curvature-squared integral of the Sasaki-Einstein manifold and the subleading holographic anomaly. The reach of this spectral framework is not restricted to toric geometries and accommodates non-toric Sasaki-Einstein manifolds. For toric Sasaki-Einstein manifolds, we develop a combinatorial method to compute the holographic spectral functions and the holographic geometric invariants directly from the toric data.

一句话导读：通过 Sasaki-Einstein 5 流形上的超对称谱函数计算曲率平方积分与次领头全息反常，并用组合方法处理环情形。

相关度：low — 论文属于 AdS/CFT 谱与几何不变量方向，未涉及用户给出的具体全息超导、非平衡或经典引力等支线。

Kerr-MOG黑洞的单值性、隐藏共形对称性与熵积公式

Monodromy, Hidden Conformal Symmetry and Entropy Product Formula For Kerr-MOG Black Hole

Parthapratim Pradhan · hep-th, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：我们使用黑洞热力学和monodromy技术研究了四维Kerr-MOG（Kerr修正引力）黑洞的二维共形场论对偶。通过这些独立方法，我们推导出对偶CFT的左、右温度与中心荷的一致表达式。最后，我们利用这些方法推导了熵积。我们证明该乘积既不是普适的，也不是量子化的。我们还通过分析无质量标量场的近区动力学，探讨了非极值Kerr-MOG黑洞的隐藏共形对称性。所得到的Kerr-MOG/CFT对应将近区几何识别为二维CFT，其特征温度由式(\ref{temperatures})给出。此外，利用Cardy公式，我们重现了对偶CFT的微观熵，并与Kerr-MOG黑洞的Bekenstein-Hawking熵精确一致。我们还证明，近区Kerr-MOG几何中的低频标量吸收截面（灰体因子）与对偶二维CFT的有限温吸收截面精确匹配。此外，近区标量波动方程表现出隐藏的(SL(2,\mathbb{R})_L \times SL(2,\mathbb{R})_R)共形对称性。这些独立结果为Kerr-MOG黑洞存在Kerr/CFT型全息对偶提供了有力的证据，建立了四维Kerr-MOG时空与二维共形场论之间的一致对应关系。

English: We examine the two-dimensional conformal field theory (2D CFT) dual of the four-dimensional Kerr-MOG (Kerr-modified gravity) black hole using the black hole thermodynamics and monodromy techniques. From these independent procedures, we derive consistent expressions for the left- and right-moving temperatures and the central charge of the dual CFT. Finally, we derive the entropy product using these approaches. We show that the product is \emph{not} universal as well as not quantized. We further explore the hidden conformal symmetry of the non-extremal Kerr-MOG black hole by analyzing the near-region dynamics of a massless scalar field. The resulting Kerr-MOG/CFT correspondence identifies the near-region geometry with a two-dimensional CFT characterized by the temperatures ($T_{L}, T_{R}$) derived in~(\ref{temperatures}). Moreover, using the Cardy formula, we reproduce the microscopic entropy of the dual CFT and find exact agreement with the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy of the Kerr-MOG black hole. We also show that the low-frequency scalar absorption cross section~(greybody factor) in the near-region Kerr-MOG geometry precisely matches the finite-temperature absorption cross section of the dual 2D CFT. In addition, the near-region scalar wave equation exhibits a hidden $(SL(2,\mathbb{R})_L \times SL(2,\mathbb{R})_R)$ conformal symmetry. These independent results provide strong evidence for the existence of a Kerr/CFT-type holographic duality for the Kerr-MOG black hole, establishing a consistent correspondence between the four-dimensional Kerr-MOG spacetime and a two-dimensional conformal field theory. horizon.

一句话导读：通过热力学与monodromy方法推导Kerr-MOG黑洞的左右温度、中心荷及熵积，并验证隐藏共形对称性和吸收截面匹配，支持Kerr/CFT全息对应。

相关度：low — 论文研究Kerr-MOG黑洞的Kerr/CFT型全息对应，与用户指定的重点标签（全息超流超导、相变、QNM等）无直接交叉，因此关联度较低。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

黑洞的热力学拓扑与时空不变量

Thermodynamic topology of black holes and an invariant of spacetime

Cao H. Nam · gr-qc, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：我们提出了一种探索黑洞热力学拓扑的形式体系，无需引入以往研究中采用的辅助变量Θ∈[0,π]。该形式体系基于一种离壳巨势，适用于能与环境交换能量和物质的处于热平衡和化学平衡的热力学系统。我们将一个向量场定义为离壳巨势的梯度，其零点对应于平衡构型，从而对应黑洞解；随后利用其归一化分量构造一个守恒的拓扑张量。相关联的拓扑通量由某一固定系综参数超曲面内所有零点的局部拓扑指数之和给出。我们进一步将渐近拓扑通量定义为守恒拓扑通量在一族有限超曲面趋近系综参数空间渐近边界时的极限值。我们发现渐近拓扑通量由渐近/背景时空几何决定。这促使我们猜想：渐近拓扑通量是与渐近/背景时空几何相关联的不变量。在基于渐近拓扑通量的时空分类中，AdS的平直极限是非平凡的，为AdS距离猜想提供了一个可能的互补视角。

English: We present a formalism for exploring the thermodynamic topology of black holes, without introducing the auxiliary variable $\Theta\in[0,\pi]$ employed in previous studies. The formalism is based on an off-shell grand free energy appropriate to thermodynamic systems that can exchange both energy and matter with their environment, thereby allowing thermal and chemical equilibrium. We define a vector field as the gradient of the off-shell grand free energy, whose zeros correspond to equilibrium configurations and hence to black hole solutions, and then we construct a conserved topological tensor from its normalized components. The associated topological flux is given by the sum of local topological indices of all zeros contained within a hypersurface at fixed ensemble parameters. We further define the asymptotic topological flux as the limiting value of the conserved topological flux through a family of finite hypersurfaces as they approach the asymptotic boundary of the ensemble parameter space. We find that the asymptotic topological flux is determined by the asymptotic/background spacetime geometry. This motivates the conjecture that the asymptotic topological flux is an invariant associated with the asymptotic/background spacetime geometry. Within the spacetime classification based on the asymptotic topological flux, the flat-space limit of AdS is found to be nontrivial, providing a potentially complementary perspective on the AdS distance conjecture.

一句话导读：提出一种不依赖辅助变量Θ的黑洞热力学拓扑新形式，通过离壳巨势梯度的零点构造守恒拓扑通量，并指出渐近拓扑通量由背景时空几何决定，可能与AdS距离猜想相关。

相关度：medium — 主题涉及经典引力中的黑洞热力学拓扑，与用户关注的经典引力/广义相对论支线部分重叠，但未涉及其他重点标签。

Brown-York张量的熵路线：类光与类时超曲面的统一框架

Entropic route to Brown-York tensor: A unified framework for null and timelike hypersurfaces

Krishnakanta Bhattacharya, Bhera Ram, Bibhas Ranjan Majhi · gr-qc, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：基于Padmanabhan最初为推导Einstein方程并凸显引力涌现本质而引入的熵泛函，我们展示了其在更广泛背景下的稳健性。利用相同的熵密度，我们证明Brown-York张量与熵泛函的多动量（polymomentum）的切向投影相关，从而为类时和类光超曲面提供了统一的构造。这一视角将类光和类时两种情形置于相同的变分基础上，并澄清了类光Brown-York张量结构性差异（包括其通常的非对称性），无需在表述中采用单独或特设的假设。我们进一步将分析扩展到标量-张量理论，表明基于熵的表述能够复现预期的运动方程以及相应的BY张量。我们的结果为拟局部引力量提供了连贯的变分诠释，并揭示了连接体动力学与边界动量的共同底层结构。

English: Building on Padmanabhan's entropy functional, originally introduced to derive Einstein's equations and highlight the emergent nature of gravity, we demonstrate its robustness in a broader context. Using the same entropy density, we show that the Brown-York tensor is associated with a tangential projection of the entropy-functional polymomentum, thereby providing a common construction applicable to both timelike and null hypersurfaces. This perspective places the two (null and timelike) cases on the same variational footing and clarifies the structural differences of the null Brown-York tensor, including its generally non-symmetric character, without resorting to separate or ad hoc prescriptions in the formulation. We further extend the analysis to scalar-tensor theories, showing that the entropy-based formulation reproduces the expected equations of motion along with the corresponding BY tensor. Our results provide a coherent variational interpretation of quasi-local gravitational quantities and reveal a common underlying structure linking bulk dynamics and boundary momentum.

一句话导读：利用熵泛函统一导出类时与类光超曲面上的Brown-York张量，扩展至标量-张量理论。

相关度：high — 论文聚焦于经典引力与广义相对论中的拟局部量，属于用户重点关注的经典引力/广义相对论方向。

特征形式与特殊和乐

Eigenforms and special holonomy

Bobby Acharya, Luc\'ia Cabrera, Simone Corbo, Jason D. Lotay · math.DG, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-07

中文摘要：我们证明了完备非紧具有特殊和乐的 Ricci 平坦流形上 Hodge Laplacian 的 L² 特征形式的存在性与不存在性结果，特别关注引力瞬子。我们简要描述了这些结果在超弦理论和 M 理论中的物理解释。

English: We prove existence and non-existence results for $L^2$ eigenforms for the Hodge Laplacian on complete non-compact Ricci flat manifolds with special holonomy, with a particular focus on gravitational instantons. We briefly describe the physical interpretation of these results in superstring and M-theory.

相关度：medium — 论文研究的是引力瞬子（Ricci 平坦特殊和乐流形）上的谱几何问题，属于经典引力的数学物理方向，但并非直接涉及引力波、黑洞或全息，因此相关性中等。

Kerr-de Sitter 拟正规模的激发因子和例外点

Excitation factors and exceptional points of Kerr-de Sitter quasinormal modes

Dario Rossi, Naritaka Oshita, Leonardo Gualtieri, Emanuele Berti · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：我们采用两种不同技术计算 Kerr-de Sitter 黑洞拟正规模的激发因子：Heun 函数表示以及将解展开为超几何函数。Kerr-de Sitter 拟正规模谱的一个普遍特征是不同过模的复频率重合的例外点。在例外点附近，模式表现出先前在质量场扰动下的 Kerr 黑洞中发现的“滞后现象”，并且单个模式的激发因子绝对值显著增强，同时它们获得几乎相反的相位。我们将激发因子外推到小的宇宙学常数值，发现与先前 Kerr 极限下的计算结果吻合良好。

English: We compute the excitation factors of Kerr-de Sitter black hole quasinormal modes using two different techniques: a Heun function representation and an expansion of the solution in terms of hypergeometric functions. Exceptional points at which the complex frequencies of different overtones coincide are a generic feature of the Kerr-de Sitter quasinormal mode spectrum. Near exceptional points, the modes exhibit a "hysteresis phenomenon" that was previously found for Kerr black holes perturbed by massive fields and the absolute value of the excitation factors of the individual modes is significantly enhanced, while they acquire nearly opposite phases. We extrapolate the excitation factors to small values of the cosmological constant and find good agreement with previous calculations in the Kerr limit.

一句话导读：通过两种解析技术计算 Kerr-de Sitter 黑洞拟正规模的激发因子，研究例外点附近模式行为和 Kerr 极限一致性。

相关度：high — 论文研究经典引力中的 Kerr-de Sitter 黑洞拟正规模及其激发因子，与重点标签“经典引力/广义相对论”相匹配。

DANSur_HM：在基于深度学习的引力波代理模型中模块化地纳入高阶模

DANSur_HM: Modularly incorporating higher modes in a deep learning based gravitational-wave surrogate

Osvaldo Gramaxo Freitas, Anastasios Theodoropoulos, Nino Villanueva, Jos\'e A. Font, Antonio Onofre, Alejandro Torres-Forn\'e, Jos\'e D. Martin-Guerrero · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE, astro-ph.IM · v1 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：数值相对论（NR）模拟提供了双黑洞（BBH）系统并合过程中发射的引力波（GWs）的最忠实表示。在引力波天文学背景下，参数估计等任务可能需要在整个参数空间内每秒进行大量波形评估。由于每次评估都进行完整的NR模拟在计算上不可行，已开发出对现有NR波形进行插值的方法，即代理模型，并取得了显著成功。本文在我们先前工作的基础上，引入了基于神经网络训练快速代理模型的方法，以生成BBH并合波形，包括基本(2,2)模以及(3,3)、(2,1)、(4,4)、(3,2)、(4,3)和(5,5)等高阶模。先在近似数据上进行预训练，再在NR数据上微调，可以平滑参数空间；利用GPU的并行能力在训练期间将NR波形投影到倾角-相位(ι, φ)球面上，可以同时拟合所有探索的模。所开发的代理模型DANSur_HM实现了平均失配约10^-4，最差失配为2.5×10^-3，在NVIDIA V100 GPU上的吞吐量超过每秒6×10^5个波形。参数估计测试确认了包含高阶模的实用性。

English: Numerical relativity (NR) simulations provide the most faithful representation of the gravitational waves (GWs) emitted by binary black hole (BBH) systems during merger. In the context of GW astronomy, tasks such as parameter estimation can require vast numbers of waveform evaluations per second across the entire parameter space. Since performing full NR simulations for each evaluation is not computationally feasible, interpolating methods for existing NR waveforms, known as surrogate models, have been developed with marked success. In this paper, we build on our previous work to introduce methods to train a fast surrogate model based on neural networks in order to generate BBH merger waveforms, including the fundamental (2,2) mode, as well as the (3,3), (2,1), (4,4), (3,2), (4,3) and (5,5) higher-order modes. Applying a pretraining step on approximant data before fine-tuning on NR data allows us to smooth out the parameter space, and making use of the parallelization ability of GPUs to project the NR waveforms in the inclination-phase $(\iota, \phi)$ sphere during training allows the fitting of all the explored modes simultaneously. The developed surrogate model, \texttt{DANSur\_HM}, achieves average mismatches of the order of $10^{-4}$, with the worst mismatch at $2.5\times10^{-3}$, and achieves throughput above $6\times10^5$ waveforms/second on an NVIDIA V100 GPU. Parameter estimation tests confirm the usefulness of the inclusion of higher modes.

一句话导读：提出一种基于深度学习的引力波代理模型，可快速生成包含高阶模的双黑洞并合波形。

相关度：low — 该研究属于经典引力波天文学的数据驱动建模方法，与全息理论、量子引力或经典引力理论本身无直接关联。

标量拟正规模式：通过辅助参数展开的七维Kerr-AdS黑洞

Scalar quasinormal modes of Kerr--AdS$_{\bf 7}$ via accessory parameter expansions

Juli\'an Barrag\'an Amado · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：我们研究了具有三个独立转动参数的七维Kerr-AdS黑洞的标量扰动。在该几何中，Klein-Gordon方程分离为一个径向和两个角向二阶线性常微分方程，每个方程都有五个正则奇点。我们应用Hill行列式方法在两个不同参数区域中计算辅助参数的解析展开。所得辅助参数展开与四维N=2箭图规范理论的Nekrasov-Shatashvili函数的瞬子部分一致。这些展开随后被用于推导慢转极限下的角本征值和小黑洞极限下的拟正规模式频率的微扰表达式。

English: We study scalar perturbations of a seven-dimensional Kerr--AdS black hole with three independent rotation parameters. In this geometry, the Klein--Gordon equation separates into one radial and two angular second-order linear ordinary differential equations, each with five regular singular points. We apply the Hill determinant method to compute analytic expansions of the accessory parameters in two different parameter regimes. The resulting accessory parameter expansions coincide with those obtained from the instanton part of the Nekrasov--Shatashvili function of four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=2$ quiver gauge theories. These expansions are then used to derive perturbative expressions for the angular eigenvalues in the slowly rotating limit and for the quasinormal mode frequencies in the small black hole limit.

一句话导读：通过Hill行列式方法计算七维Kerr-AdS黑洞标量扰动的辅助参数展开，并推导慢转和小黑洞极限下的角本征值与QNM频率。

相关度：high — 论文聚焦于经典引力框架下的AdS黑洞标量扰动与拟正规模式，与用户重点标签“经典引力/广义相对论”及“QNM与非线性演化”直接相关。

致密星中的非广延熵印记

Non-extensive entropy signatures in compact star

B. N. Jayawiguna, M. H. Al Ghifari, A. Rohim, A. Sulaksono · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE · v1 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：将应用于黑洞视界的非广延熵模型通过Wald熵形式与广义爱因斯坦–希尔伯特作用量 f(R) 联系起来。我们证明，在修正引力框架下，由MIT袋模型描述的夸克星结构满足与HESS J1731−347和GW190814相关联的致密天体的观测上、下限。此外，我们评估了有效能量条件和相应的声速，以检验恒星结构的物理合理性和稳定性。所研究的全部非广延熵模型均满足这些条件。我们的结果表明，这类奇异致密天体为探索Barrow、Tsallis-Cirto和Rényi等非广延熵模型提供了一个令人信服且可信的框架，反之亦然。

English: The non-extensive entropy models applied to the black-hole horizon are connected to the generalized Einstein--Hilbert action, $f(R)$, via the Wald entropy formalism. We demonstrate that quark star configurations characterized by the MIT bag model under this modified gravity approach conform to the observational lower and upper bounds established for compact objects linked to HESS J1731$-$347 and GW190814. Furthermore, we assess the effective energy conditions and corresponding speed of sound to evaluate the physical plausibility and stability of the stellar configurations. All non-extensive entropy models studied satisfied these conditions. Our findings show that the exotic compact objects offer a compelling and credible framework for exploring aspects of non-extensive entropy models, such as the Barrow, Tsallis-Cirto, and R\'enyi formalisms, and vice versa.

一句话导读：将Barrow、Tsallis-Cirto和Rényi非广延熵模型通过Wald熵纳入f(R)修正引力，用于构造满足HESS J1731−347和GW190814观测约束的夸克星，并通过能量条件与声速验证其稳定性。

度规 f(R) 引力中无剪切宇宙学的约束闭合与引力波内容

Constraint closure and gravitational-wave content of shear-free cosmologies in metric f(R) gravity

Mudhahir Al Ajmi, Amare Abebe · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：我们推导了度规 f(R) 引力中 Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker 宇宙学线性无剪切微扰传播内容所满足的一致性条件。剪切自由约束的散度被证明等价于总动量守恒方程，因此并不意味着测地流；其投影时间导数则给出非平凡的积分条件。标量、矢量和张量扇区必须分别检验。剪切自由条件移除了独立的横迹无迹电-磁 Weyl 对，因此没有通常的 + 或 × 张量引力波存留。标量子并未被代数地移除，但其四变量谐和系统必须保持在由剪切自由约束及其各阶时间导数生成的时间依赖一致子空间中。对于空间平坦膨胀 de Sitter 区域中的测地无剪切汇，这排除了所有非零固定共动标量谐和模式，包括参数 α,Λ>0 的正常 R+αR^2−2Λ 模型。矢量模式满足一个依赖曲率的全局本征值条件；在正密度物质且 f'>0、1+w>0 时，其右侧为负，因此该分支上无非零矢量谐和模式存活。常见的 coasting R^3 例子之所以避开这一符号障碍，只是因为它位于 f'<0 分支。因此，在所施加的无剪切扇区内不存在张量辐射；标量辐射虽在运动学上未被排除，但仍是一个依赖于模型和背景的约束闭合问题。

English: We derive the consistency conditions governing the propagating content of linear shear-free perturbations of Friedmann-Lema\^{\i}tre-Robertson-Walker cosmologies in metric $f(R)$ gravity. The divergence of the shear-free constraint is shown to be the total momentum-conservation equation and therefore does not imply geodesic flow. Its projected time derivative instead supplies a nontrivial integrability condition. Scalar, vector, and tensor sectors must then be tested separately. Shear-freeness removes the independent transverse-traceless electric-magnetic Weyl pair, so no ordinary $+$ or $\times$ tensor gravitational wave survives. The scalaron is not removed algebraically, but its four-variable harmonic system must remain in the time-dependent consistent subspace generated by the shear-free constraint and all of its time derivatives. For a geodesic shear-free congruence in the spatially flat expanding de Sitter patch, this excludes every nonzero fixed comoving scalar harmonic, including the healthy $R+\alpha R^2-2\Lambda$ model with $\alpha,\Lambda>0$. Vector modes obey a curvature-dependent global eigenvalue condition whose right-hand side is negative for positive-density matter with $f'>0$ and $1+w>0$; hence no nonzero vector harmonic survives on that branch. The familiar coasting $R^3$ example evades this sign obstruction only because it lies on an $f'<0$ branch. Thus tensor radiation is absent in the imposed shear-free sector, whereas scalar radiation is not excluded kinematically but remains a model- and background-dependent constraint-closure question.

一句话导读：研究度规 f(R) 引力中 FLRW 无剪切微扰的约束闭合；无剪切条件排除张量引力波，矢量模式依赖背景符号，标量模式未在运动学上被排除。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典修正引力/宇宙学线性微扰与引力波传播研究，符合经典引力/广义相对论支线；但与全息、量子引力或凝聚态/核物理等用户主要支线不直接相关。

SINFONIA：用于轨道数值积分与加速的辛、slimpetic和Magnusian（神经）流

Introducing SINFONIA: Symplectic, slimplectic and Magnusian (Neural) Flows for Orbital Numerical Integration and Acceleration

Lidia J. Gomes Da Silva · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE, astro-ph.IM, cs.LG, physics.comp-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：长时间引力波建模必须同时解析快速轨道运动和缓慢耗散演化，同时防止小数值误差累积为长期相位漂移。这里我们探讨有限时间演化映射本身是否可以被学习为显式、可微、保结构的对象，并通过完整的旋进重复组合。我们构造了三种神经流架构：定义在Galley加倍相空间上的辛和slimpetic流[SINFONIA-J0]；Taylor锚定流[SINFONIA-J1]；以及在相互作用图像中学习有限时间耗散修正的Magnusian流[SINFONIA-J2]。应用于2.5PN中子星旋进时，三者都揭示了相同的控制机制：长期精度不只由逐点映射误差决定，还由其到单个由能量-角动量平衡固定的长期通道上的带符号投影决定。编码该结构使所学映射可在达到全轨道周期及以上的时间步长下，通过10^2–10^5次窗口组合保持准确直至并合，链式相位误差比基准slimpetic积分器低多个数量级且成本更低。同样的长期结构还可用于物理推断：当通道不约束时，累积相位保留足够信息以恢复未建模的动力学摩擦型力，既可作为参数形式也可作为分离度的学习函数。网络关闭对照将学习贡献与各映射中固有的解析结构分开。这些结果为保结构学习演化映射作为引力波源建模中快速长时积分和物理推断的工具建立了概念验证。

English: Long-duration gravitational-wave modelling must resolve fast orbital motion together with slow dissipative evolution while preventing small numerical errors from accumulating into secular phase drift. Here we ask whether the finite-time evolution map itself can be learned as an explicit, differentiable, structure-preserving object and then repeatedly composed through a complete inspiral. We construct three neural-flow architectures: a symplectic and slimplectic flow on Galley's doubled phase space, [SINFONIA-J0]; a Taylor-anchored flow, [SINFONIA-J1]; and a Magnusian flow that learns the finite-time dissipative correction in the interaction picture, [SINFONIA-J2]. Applied to a 2.5PN neutron-star inspiral, all three expose the same controlling mechanism: long-time accuracy is governed not by pointwise map error alone, but by its signed projection onto a single secular channel fixed by energy--angular-momentum balance. Encoding this structure allows the learned maps to remain accurate through $10^{2}$--$10^{5}$ window compositions to coalescence at timesteps of a full orbital period and beyond, reaching chained phase errors orders of magnitude below a benchmark slimplectic integrator at lower cost. The same secular structure can also be exploited for physics inference: when the channel is left unconstrained, the accumulated phase retains enough information to recover an un-modelled dynamical-friction-like force, both parametrically and as a learned function of separation. Network-off controls isolate the contribution of learning from the analytic structure already built into each map. These results establish a proof of concept for structure-preserving learned evolution maps as tools for fast long-duration integration and physics inference in gravitational-wave source modelling.

一句话导读：提出结构保持的神经流积分器用于引力波旋进长时间模拟，并利用经典长期通道实现高精度加速和物理推断。

相关度：low — 论文主要涉及引力波源建模中的数值积分和机器学习方法，不涉及全息、量子引力或经典引力理论本身，仅与经典引力应用间接相关。

从复杂度结构标量边界条件出发的广义相对论中子星解

Solutions for neutron stars in General Relativity from a complexity structure scalar boundary condition

Robert S. Bogadi, Megandhren Govender, Genly Leon, Andronikos Paliathanasis · gr-qc, math-ph, math.MP · v1 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：在球对称各向异性流体的边界上，引入了一个涉及复杂度因子（由Riemann张量正交分解产生的结构标量）的新条件。该条件无需对几何或物质变量施加任何额外限制即可生成一个模型。该模型在物理上是合理的，并表明在表面处评估的复杂度标量值存在一个非零的正最小约束。这表明零复杂度是一种全局化约束，不适用于边界，特别是在强引力区域中的致密天体。

English: A new condition involving the complexity factor, a structure scalar arising from the orthogonal splitting of the Riemann tensor, is generated at the boundary of a spherically symmetric anisotropic fluid. This condition leads to the generation of a model, without invoking any further constraints on geometry or matter variables. The model generated is physically reasonable and suggests that there is a non-zero, positive minimum constraint on the complexity scalar value evaluated at the surface. This suggests that vanishing complexity is a globalized constraint which does not apply at the boundary, specifically for compact objects in the strong-gravity regime.

一句话导读：利用复杂度因子作为边界条件生成广义相对论中子星模型，发现表面复杂度标量存在非零正最小值。

并非所有共振都是平等的：优先排序极端质量比旋近中的潮汐共振

Not All Resonances Are Created Equal: Prioritizing Tidal Resonances in EMRIs

B\'eatrice Bonga (Bart), Patrick Bourg (Bart), Bram ten Brink (Bart), H. A. (Bart), Peters · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：附近致密天体的潮汐扰动可以驱动极端质量比旋近（EMRI）中的共振“踢动”，在LISA波段引力波形中留下可能可探测的相位偏移。我们在弱潮汐、三体层次结构下对EMRI参数空间中的这些潮汐共振进行了系统调查，将扰动体视为在共振时标上静止。对于一般Kerr轨道，我们计算了(i) 共振轮廓在(p,e,x)中的位置，以确定每个(n,k,m)共振在何处被遇到，(ii) 相关的共振持续时间，以及(iii) 角动量L_z和Carter常数Q的跳跃幅度（在静止模型中，由于时间平移对称性，ΔE消失）。结合跳跃幅度和共振持续时间，我们构建了一个对波形建模最相关的共振的实用排序，同时强调即使是中等强度的共振也可能通过影响后续共振进入时的相位而仍然重要。所有轮廓和跳跃数据均公开可用。

English: Tidal perturbations from nearby compact objects can drive resonant "kicks" in extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs), imprinting potentially detectable phase shifts in LISA-band gravitational waveforms. We provide a systematic survey of these tidal resonances across EMRI parameter space in the weak-tide, three-body hierarchy, treating the perturber as stationary on the resonance timescale. For generic Kerr orbits we compute (i) resonance contours in $(p,e,x)$ that locate where each $(n,k,m)$ resonance is encountered, (ii) the associated resonance duration, and (iii) the jump amplitudes in the angular momentum $L_z$ and the Carter constant $Q$ (with $\Delta E$ vanishing by time-translation symmetry in the stationary model). Combining jump amplitudes with resonance durations, we construct a practical ranking of the resonances most relevant for waveform modeling, while emphasizing that even modest resonances may still be important through their influence on the phase at which subsequent resonances are entered. All contour and jump data are publicly available.

一句话导读：系统调查EMRI中的潮汐共振，计算共振位置、持续时间和守恒量跳跃，并给出对LISA波形建模最相关的共振排序。

相关度：high — 论文直接属于经典引力/广义相对论，研究EMRI中的潮汐共振及其对LISA引力波波形的影响，符合用户经典引力支线重点。

引力波信号中波形系统误差与透镜特征的相互作用

On the interplay between waveform systematics and lensing signatures in gravitational-wave signals

Disha Hegde, Justin Janquart · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：异常引力波事件GW231123显示出异常高的总质量和近极端自旋，同时其推断的源属性存在强烈的波形依赖性变化。几项后续研究倾向于透镜解释，且波形系统误差减小，这引发了外观透镜证据是否可能源自波形建模系统误差的问题。通过使用类似GW231123的数值相对论注入，我们发现透镜自由度可以减少波形差异并产生不可忽视的透镜支持。然而，这种行为在注入中并非系统性出现，并且未发现所有波形和透镜模型都支持透镜且同时减少波形差异的案例。

English: The exceptional gravitational-wave event GW231123 exhibited unusually high total mass and near-extremal spins, while revealing strong waveform-dependent variations in its inferred source properties. Several follow-up studies favored a lensed interpretation, with reduced waveform systematics, raising the question of whether apparent lensing evidence could arise from waveform modeling systematics. Using GW231123-like numerical-relativity injections, we find that lensing degrees of freedom can reduce waveform discrepancies and yield non-negligible lensing support. However, this behavior is not systematic across injections, and cases where all waveform and lens models favor lensing while reducing waveform discrepancies are not found.

一句话导读：通过数值相对论注入研究GW231123事件中波形系统误差与引力透镜特征之间的相互作用，发现透镜自由度有时可降低波形差异，但并非系统性。

来自统一场方程的引力波八种局域耦合

Eight Local Couplings of Gravitational Waves from Unified Field Equations

Hong-Bo Jin, Yue-Liang Wu · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE · v1 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：引力波探测器在假设额外偏振进入测地偏离的情况下，记录的是一系列局域耦合的线性混合。真空广义相对论只允许两个横迹无迹振幅。仍需确定该混合中可能存在的最大耦合集合，以及纯张量臂长结果是否唯一地选择广义相对论。零动量的 E(2) 小群将应变振幅 p=(p_{+},p_×,p_x,p_y,p_b,p_l) 分类，通常记为 h_P，它决定沿光线的电潮汐张量。因此，作为差分臂长记录的测地偏离只包含进入该张量的那些 p_P。在螺旋度 ±1 下的 Lorentz 混合提供了引力磁（GEM）场，该场不进入潮汐张量。若 GEM 场是静态的，则不会作为波传播。随时间变化的螺旋度±1 流会源生引力磁波，并以耦合的形式进入混合，需通过其测量量来隔离。对于仅依赖推迟时间的辐射区波，β g_⊥ = k × ∂t(p_x,p_y)，这八个耦合为 Pvec=(p,βg⊥)。这里采用关于 p 的统一场方程来阐明 Pvec 中每个分量的起源；每个耦合的测量量可在混合中隔离出相应的偏振，这有助于区分不同偏振并进行模型检验。

English: A gravitational-wave (GW) detector records a linear mixture of local couplings under the assumption that extra polarizations enter geodesic deviation. Vacuum general relativity (GR) admits two transverse-traceless (TT) amplitudes. It remains to determine the largest set of couplings that can sit in that mixture, and whether a tensor-only arm-length result selects GR uniquely. The little group \(E(2)\) of a null four-momentum classifies the strain amplitudes \(\mathbf{p}=(p_{+},p_{\times},p_{x},p_{y},p_{b},p_{\ell})\), commonly written \(h_{P}\), which determine the electric tidal tensor along a ray. Geodesic deviation, recorded as differential arm length, therefore contains only those \(p_{P}\) that enter that tensor. Lorentz mixing at helicity \(\pm1\) supplies a gravito-magnetic (GEM) field that does not enter the tidal tensor. if GEM field is static, that does not propagate as a wave. A time-varying helicity-\(\pm1\) current sources a GEM wave that enters the mixture as a coupling, to be isolated by its measured quantity. For a radiation-zone wave that depends only on retarded time, \(\betag_{\perp}=\hat{\mathbf{k}}\times\partial_{t}(p_{x},p_{y})\), and the eight couplings are \(\Pvec=(\mathbf{p},\betag_{\perp})\). Here we adopt unified field equations on \(\mathbf{p}\) to clarify the origin of each component of \(\Pvec\); the measured quantity of each coupling then isolates the polarizations in that mixture, which favors identification of distinct polarizations and model tests.

一句话导读：该文通过统一场方程和 E(2) 小群分类，提出引力波探测中除广义相对论的两个TT振幅外可能存在八个局域耦合，并探讨如何用测量量区分不同偏振与理论模型。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论中的引力波偏振分类研究，与用户关注的部分经典引力路线相符，但不涉及全息或其他高能理论，整体相关性中等。

替代引力理论中的黑洞

Black holes in alternative theories of gravity

Jose Luis Bl\'azquez-Salcedo, Fech Scen Khoo, Burkhard Kleihaus, Jutta Kunz · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：黑洞及其强引力场为超越广义相对论的引力理论提供了重要检验场所。在众多候选理论中，近期大量工作集中于标量-张量理论，其中标量场与高阶曲率项耦合。此类理论中出现的黑洞解可显著不同于广义相对论的史瓦西解和克尔解。这些黑洞的特征性质包括不稳定性、阴影和引力波谱，可用于约束底层理论的耦合常数。

English: Black holes, with their strong gravitational fields, provide an important testing ground for theories of gravity beyond General Relativity. Among the many proposed alternatives, considerable recent work has focused on scalar-tensor theories in which a scalar field couples to higher-curvature terms. The black hole solutions that arise in such theories can differ significantly from the Schwarzschild and Kerr solutions of General Relativity. Characteristic properties of these black holes include instabilities, shadows, and gravitational wave spectra, which can be used to constrain the couplings of the underlying theories.

一句话导读：综述了修改引力理论中的黑洞解及其不稳定性、阴影和引力波特征，这些可用于约束理论耦合。

相关度：high — 论文主题属于经典引力/广义相对论，完全匹配用户重点标签中的“经典引力/广义相对论”支线。

标准相对论效应对Gaia-like天体测量星表的影响

The influence of standard relativistic effects on Gaia-like astrometric catalogs

Gabriel Rodr\'iguez-Moris, Sergei A. Klioner · astro-ph.IM, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：随着Gaia等天体测量空间望远镜的观测精度提高，牛顿理论框架下的位置天文学已不再适用。为了建模在相应精度水平上所有相关效应，需要广义相对论框架下更精细的描述。目前已知各种相对论效应如何影响给定的天体测量观测，尤其是太阳系天体的最大光偏折可以针对给定观测者容易估计。然而，给定源的天体测量参数是由对该源的一系列观测计算得到的。根据相对论效应的不同，受该效应影响的观测比例在某一精度水平上可能变化并且可能非常小。我们的目标是研究未考虑的相对论效应对Gaia-like天体测量望远镜观测源参数的影响。利用Gaia的标准相对论框架来突出某些影响最显著的情况。然后，使用模拟工具AGISLab，我们将若干源的Gaia-like观测拟合到标准Gaia-like天体测量模型，但使用不同的不完整相对论模型。由此获得如果在相对论模型中删除不同元素时天体测量参数的误差。还提供并讨论了误差的简化解析描述。我们证明，由于每源多次观测的平均，天体测量参数的误差远小于单次观测中相应的相对论效应。这为未来旨在比Gaia更高精度的天体测量项目简化相对论模型开辟了道路。

English: With the observational accuracy of astrometric space telescopes such as Gaia, the Newtonian theoretical framework for positional astronomy is no longer appropriate. To model all effects relevant at the corresponding levels of accuracy, a more sophisticated description in the framework of General Relativity is required. It is generally known how various relativistic effects affect a given astrometric observation. In particular, the maximal light deflection of a given Solar System body can be easily estimated for a given observer. However, astrometric parameters for a given source are computed from a series of observations of that source. Depending on the relativistic effect, the fraction of observations affected, at some given level of accuracy, by that relativistic effect can vary and also be very small. Our goal is to investigate the impact of unaccounted relativistic effects on the astrometric parameters of a source observed by a Gaia-like astrometric telescope. The standard relativistic framework for Gaia is used to highlight such effects in some cases where they are most significant. Then, using the simulation tool AGISLab, we fit a set of Gaia-like observations of a given number of sources to the standard Gaia-like astrometric model for astrometric source parameters, but using different incomplete relativistic models. In this way we obtain the errors of the astrometric parameters if one were to drop different elements of the relativity model. A simplified analytical description of the errors is also provided and discussed. We demonstrate that, due to averaging over many observations per source, the errors in the astrometric parameters are drastically smaller than the corresponding relativistic effects in individual observations. This opens a way to potentially simplify the relativistic model for future astrometric projects aiming for higher accuracy than Gaia.

一句话导读：研究未考虑的相对论效应对Gaia-like天体测量星表参数的影响，发现多次观测平均使误差远小于单次观测效应。

相关度：low — 论文属于广义相对论在天体测量中的应用，不涉及全息或量子引力，因此与用户关注的全息/引力理论主题关联度低。

第四次LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA观测运行中的洞见目录

A catalogue of insights from the fourth LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA observing run

Christopher P L Berry, Daniel Williams · astro-ph.HE, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：在过去的十年中，引力波科学从一个单次探测即获诺贝尔奖的时代发展到了（几乎）日常发生的阶段。LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA（LVK）合作组织的旗舰科学成果是其探测目录，其中包含一套全面的分析结果，描述了源的天体物理性质及其对基础物理和宇宙学的影响。我们回顾了LVK网络第四次观测运行（O4）中的发现。最新的每个目录均包含约100个新探测。在这些新探测中，有引人注目的单个发现：迄今探测到的最强信号，以及前所未有的源。然而，该领域的重大进展来自群体层面的分析，利用不断增加的样本量来推断合并致密双星的天体物理分布、对广义相对论的潜在偏离以及宇宙膨胀速率的约束。对最新数据的分析揭示了多个源亚群的证据。这些观测为研究大质量恒星的演化和并合双星的形成提供了独特的探针。随着更多观测，我们对引力波信号及其源的理解将继续加深。O4的最终目录计划于2026年12月发布，并且计划进行具有更高探测器灵敏度的未来观测运行。这些将带来更大的目录和更多强信号，继续推进引力波科学。

English: Over the last decade, gravitational-wave science has developed from an era where a single detection was a Nobel-winning achievement to an (almost) everyday occurrence. The LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA (LVK) Collaboration's flagship scientific results are now their catalogues of detections, which include a comprehensive set of analysis results characterising the astrophysical properties of sources, and their implications for fundamental physics and cosmology. We review discoveries from the fourth observing run (O4) of the LVK network. The most recent catalogues each include $\sim100$ new detections. Among these new detections are remarkable single discoveries: the loudest signals detected so far, and sources unlike those seen before. However, significant advances in the field are coming from population-level analyses, leveraging the increasing sample size to infer constraints on the astrophysical distribution of merging compact binaries, potential deviations to general relativity, and the expansion rate of the Universe. Analyses of the latest data have revealed evidence for multiple subpopulations of sources. These observations provide a unique probe into the evolution of massive stars and the formation of coalescing binaries. Our understanding of gravitational-wave signals and their sources will continue to deepen with more observations. The final catalogue from O4}is due for release in December 2026, and future observing runs with even greater detector sensitivity are planned. These will bring even larger catalogues, and additional loud signals, continuing to advance gravitational-wave science.

一句话导读：综述LVK第四次观测运行中引力波探测目录的发现及其对天体物理、广义相对论和宇宙学的意义。

演化暗能量中的宇宙学与标准汽笛观测的一致性

Consistency between cosmological and standard siren observations in evolving dark energy

Macarena Lagos, William J. Wolf · astro-ph.CO, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：非最小耦合到引力的标量场可以驱动宇宙加速。这种非最小耦合（NMC）会产生非零的引力波摩擦函数 α_M(z)，从而修改从引力波源推断的光度距离相对于其电磁对应体的值。我们使用一个特定的 NMC 标量-张量模型（它与 DESI 数据所揭示的随时间变化的暗能量高度一致）来预言 α_M(z) 以及期望的 GW/EM 光度距离比 D_L^GW / D_L^EM，并将其映射到两种常用参数化——c_M 和 (Ξ_0, n) 模型。我们得到 c_M = -0.5 ± 0.2 以及 Ξ_0 = 0.88 ± 0.05、n = 3.2 ± 0.3，两者在 ≲1σ 水平上都与 GWTC-5 约束一致。这种一致性主要源于当前引力波数据的不确定性较大，使得测量结果既与 ΛCDM 一致，也与 NMC 模型一致。相反，在常用的参数化标量-张量模型（使用 α_M(z)=c_M Ω_Λ(z)/Ω_Λ0 和 CPL 参数化态方程 w_0w_a）下，由宇宙学数据分析导出的暗能量约束与 NMC 预言在 c_M（w_0w_a）参数上存在 2.2σ（3.6σ）的张力。我们确认，为了避免宇宙学不稳定性，这种参数化模型施加了很强的隐式先验，而当暗能量为动力学时，这些先验与物理上合理的标量-张量模型不相容。

English: A scalar field non-minimally coupled to gravity can be the driver of cosmic acceleration. Such a non-minimal coupling (NMC) can produce a non-zero gravitational-wave (GW) friction function $\alpha_M(z)$, which modifies the luminosity distance inferred from GW sources relative to its electromagnetic counterpart. We use a particular NMC scalar-tensor model, that explains time-varying dark energy in good alignment with DESI, to predict $\alpha_M(z)$ and the expected GW/EM luminosity-distance ratio $D_L^{\rm GW}/D_L^{\rm EM}$, and map it onto two common parametrizations---the $c_M$ and the $(\Xi_0,n)$ models. We find $c_M = -0.5\pm 0.2$ and $\Xi_0 = 0.88\pm 0.05$, $n=3.2\pm 0.3$, both consistent with GWTC-5 constraints at the $\lesssim 1\sigma$ level. This consistency is mostly driven by current large uncertainties in GW data, which lead to measurements consistent with both $\Lambda$CDM and the NMC model. By contrast, the dark energy constraints derived from analyzing cosmological data, under the common parametric scalar-tensor model using $\alpha_M(z)=c_M\Omega_\Lambda(z)/\Omega_{\Lambda 0}$ and the CPL parametrized equation of state $w_0w_a$, are in $2.2\sigma$ ($3.6\sigma$) tension with the NMC predictions for the $c_M$ ($w_0w_a$) parameter. We confirm that, in order to avoid cosmological instabilities, this parametrized model imposes strong implicit priors that are incompatible with physically-motivated scalar-tensor models when dark energy is dynamical.

一句话导读：检验演化暗能量的非最小耦合标量-张量模型如何通过引力波摩擦影响标准汽笛与宇宙学观测之间的一致性。

第二代时延干涉测量的解析灵敏度曲线

Analytical sensitivity curves of the second-generation time-delay interferometry

Chunyu Zhang · gr-qc, astro-ph.IM · v2 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：未来的空间引力波探测器将采用第二代时延干涉测量（TDI）来抑制激光频率噪声并达到所需的灵敏度。我们引入了逆光路算子 P_{i1i2...in}，可简洁表示第二代TDI组合并描述光传播。在瞬时等臂、等边三角形星座近似下，推导了天空和偏振平均响应函数、噪声功率谱密度（PSD）及TDI Michelson、Sagnac型（α,β,γ）、Monitor、Beacon、Relay和全对称Sagnac（ζ）组合及其正交A, E, T通道的灵敏度曲线的解析表达式和高精度近似公式。通过蒙特卡洛积分对10^4个随机采样天空位置和偏振角验证了解析结果的有效性。结果表明：(i) 第二代TDI与第一代对应组合灵敏度相同；(ii) A, E, T灵敏度和最优灵敏度与TDI代数和具体组合无关；(iii) A和E通道具有相同的平均响应、噪声PSD和灵敏度，而T通道在低频时响应和灵敏度弱得多；(iv) 除(α,β,γ)、ζ组合和T通道外，所有灵敏度曲线均呈现平坦段；(v) ζ的平均响应、噪声PSD和灵敏度与T通道的相应量成比例。这些解析和近似公式为未来空间引力波探测器的仪器优化和数据分析预研究提供了有用的等臂基准。

English: Forthcoming space-based gravitational-wave (GW) detectors will employ second-generation time-delay interferometry (TDI) to suppress laser frequency noise and achieve the sensitivity required for GW detection. We introduce an inverse light-path operator $\mathcal{P}_{i_{1}i_{2}i_{3}\ldots i_{n-1}i_{n}}$, which enables simple representation of second-generation TDI combinations and a concise description of light propagation. Under the instantaneous equal-arm, equilateral triangular-constellation approximation, analytical expressions and high-accuracy approximate formulas are derived for the sky- and polarization-averaged response functions, noise power spectral densities (PSDs), and sensitivity curves of TDI Michelson, Sagnac-type ($\alpha,\beta,\gamma$), Monitor, Beacon, Relay, and fully symmetric Sagnac ($\zeta$) combinations, as well as their orthogonal $A, E, T$ channels. The validity of the analytical results is confirmed via Monte Carlo integration with $10^{4}$ randomly sampled sky locations and polarization angles. Our results show that: (i) second-generation TDIs have the same sensitivities as their first-generation counterparts; (ii) the $A, E, T$ sensitivities and the optimal sensitivity are independent of the TDI generation and specific combination; (iii) the $A$ and $E$ channels have equal averaged responses, noise PSDs, and sensitivities, while the $T$ channel has much weaker response and sensitivity at low frequencies; (iv) except for the $(\alpha,\beta,\gamma)$ and $\zeta$ combinations and the $T$ channel, all sensitivity curves exhibit a flat section; (v) the averaged response, noise PSD, and sensitivity of $\zeta$ scale with those of the $T$ channel. These analytical and approximate formulations provide useful equal-arm benchmarks for instrument optimization and data-analysis preparatory studies for future space-based GW detectors.

一句话导读：提出第二代TDI组合的解析灵敏度曲线，证明其与第一代等效，并给出A/E/T等通道的普适性质。

相关度：medium — 论文涉及空间引力波探测器的经典引力波数据分析方法，属于经典引力/广义相对论范畴，但与全息或量子引力主题无直接关联。

引力脱耦的毛黑洞的回声

Echoes of a hairy black hole from gravitational decoupling

Yi Yang, Ali Ovgun, Gaetano Lambiase, Dong Liu, Zheng-Wen Long · gr-qc · v4 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：我们研究了在引力脱耦框架内构造的毛黑洞的轴向引力微扰，并探讨了该时空中类似回声的晚期信号的几何起源。我们推导了奇宇称主方程及相应的有效势，并用频域和时域方法计算了拟正规模谱。我们表明，轴向势会形成双峰结构，支持俘获腔并产生类似回声的波形。代表性的双峰结构还展现出不稳定-稳定-不稳定的光环结构，这为eikonal极限下的俘获腔提供了零测地线对应。因此，回声并非由人为施加的近地平条件产生，而是由有效势的几何结构动态涌现。我们的结果为通过引力波ringdown探测黑洞毛以及探索超越标准无毛范式的可能可观测偏离提供了有用的框架。

English: We study axial gravitational perturbations of a hairy black hole constructed within the framework of gravitational decoupling and investigate the geometric origin of echo-like late-time signals in this spacetime. We derive the odd-parity master equation and the corresponding effective potential, and compute the quasinormal mode spectrum using frequency-domain and time-domain methods. We show that the axial potential develops a double-peak structure that supports a trapping cavity and gives rise to echo-like waveforms. The representative double-peak configurations also exhibit an unstable--stable--unstable light-ring structure, which provides the null-geodesic counterpart of the trapping cavity in the eikonal limit. Therefore, the echoes are not produced by an artificially imposed near-horizon condition, but instead emerge dynamically from the geometric structure of the effective potential. Our results provide a useful framework for probing black hole hair through gravitational-wave ringdown and for exploring possible observational departures from the standard no-hair paradigm.

一句话导读：该文研究引力脱耦构造的毛黑洞的轴向扰动，发现双峰势阱自然产生回声信号，而非人为边界条件所致。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力与黑洞微扰论，非全息或量子引力范畴，但涉及黑洞拟正规模与回声等现象，与用户关注的经典引力和QNM研究方向相关。

修改引力弥合宇宙学张力

Modified gravity bridges the cosmological tensions

Gen Ye · astro-ph.CO, gr-qc · v3 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：标准宇宙学模型 ΛCDM 受到来自近期观测的各种张力的困扰，其中最紧迫的是哈勃张力，即观测到的宇宙膨胀速率比 ΛCDM 预言的大 5σ。最近，DESI 的结果还揭示了 ΛCDM 中重子声学振荡与宇宙微波背景（CMB）观测之间存在新的不一致。研究发现，这些主要的宇宙学张力可以追溯到一个共同的物理起源——引力与物质的非最小耦合，这与广义相对论（GR）的预期不同。贝叶斯模型比较显示，非最小耦合引力相对于 GR 和 ΛCDM 有强烈证据，贝叶斯因子 ln B=+12.1。该非最小耦合模型预言早期宇宙中引力在宇宙学尺度上应比现在强约 5%，这与当前观测一致，并将在未来的 CMB 和大爆炸核合成实验中得到检验。

English: The standard model of cosmology, $\Lambda$CDM, is plagued by various tensions with recent observations. The most pressing one is the Hubble tension, where the observed expansion rate of our Universe is $5\sigma$ larger than that predicted by $\Lambda$CDM. Recently, the result from DESI also reveals a new inconsistency between the baryon acoustic oscillation and cosmic microwave background (CMB) observations in $\Lambda$CDM. It is found that these major cosmological tensions can be traced back to a common physical origin - the non-minimal coupling between gravity and matter, different from the expectation of general relativity (GR). Bayesian model comparison reveals strong evidence of non-minimally coupled gravity over GR and $\Lambda$CDM, with a Bayes factor $\ln B=+12.1$. The non-minimal coupling model predicts that gravity in the early Universe should be $\sim5\%$ stronger on cosmological scales than it is today, which is consistent with current observation and will be tested by upcoming CMB and big bang nucleosynthesis experiments.

一句话导读：论文表明宇宙学哈勃张力等主要不一致可能源于引力与物质的非最小耦合，修改引力模型在贝叶斯比较中显著优于 ΛCDM。

相关度：medium — 论文聚焦于修改引力模型解释宇宙学观测张力，属于经典引力/修正引力和宇宙学交叉方向，但不涉及全息或量子引力。

基于时频过剩功率的 LISA 大质量黑洞双星快速并合前探测

Fast premerger detection of massive black-hole binaries in LISA based on time-frequency excess power

Francesco Nobili, Malvina Bellotti, Riccardo Buscicchio, Massimo Dotti, Alessandro Lupi · astro-ph.IM, astro-ph.HE, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：天基激光干涉仪LISA预期能观测来自宇宙时间长河中大质量黑洞双星的引力波。许多双星预计在并合前数小时至数周即可探测。我们提出一种用于这类双星并合前探测和初步表征的快速算法。该方法在短时傅里叶变换谱图中以啁啾时频形态搜索过剩功率。利用由四极频率演化定义的切片平铺时频平面，我们定义了相对于拟合的仪器噪声和银河系前景背景分布的信号显著性。单个搜索触发后跟一个相干性追踪器，该追踪器将随时间与同一物理信号一致的触发分组。因此，我们的分析提供啁啾质量和并合时间的逐步细化估计。我们基于SangriaHM LISA数据挑战数据集验证算法，成功探测了所有15个注入的大质量黑洞双星：其中14个在并合前数小时到数周被探测到，其中一个仅在并合后才被探测到。对于高信噪比源，算法给出的啁啾质量相对误差低于3%，并合时间不确定度可达数小时。处理10天数据段在单核上计算成本不到一秒，我们的方法适用于生成实时报警、触发保护性观测期以及为贝叶斯参数估计提供信息先验。

English: The Laser Interferometer Space Antenna is expected to observe gravitational waves from massive black hole binaries across cosmic time. Many are anticipated to be detectable hours to weeks before coalescence. We present a fast algorithm for the premerger detection and preliminary characterization of such binaries. The method performs a search for excess power with a chirping time-frequency morphology in short-time Fourier transform spectrograms. By tiling the time-frequency plane with slices defined by the quadrupole frequency evolution, we define a signal significance relative to a fitted background distribution of instrumental noise and Galactic foreground. Individual search triggers are followed by a coherence tracker that groups triggers consistent with the same physical signal over time. Doing so, our analysis provides progressively refined estimates of the chirp mass and coalescence time. We validate our algorithm on the SangriaHM LISA Data Challenge dataset, successfully detecting all 15 injected massive black-hole binaries: 14 of them hours to weeks before merger, while one is only detected after the binary coalescence. The algorithm yields chirp mass relative errors below $3\%$ for high-SNR sources and coalescence time uncertainties of up to a few hours. With a computational cost of less than a second to process a 10-day data segment on a single core, our approach is suitable for generating real-time alerts, triggering protected observational periods, and providing informative priors for Bayesian parameter estimation.

一句话导读：提出一种基于STFT时频过剩功率的快速算法，用于LISA对大质量黑洞双星并合前探测，可在数小时到数周前触发报警。

GWTC-4.0双黑洞并合的余留体反冲与宿主环境

Remnant recoil and host environments of GWTC-4.0 binary black-hole mergers

Joan Llobera-Querol, Eleanor Hamilton, Neha Singh, Marta Colleoni, Felip A. Ramis Vidal, Abbas Askar, Tomasz Bulik, Aleksandra Olejak, Sascha Husa, Yumeng Xu, Jorge Valencia · astro-ph.HE, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：确定双黑洞的天体物理起源以及并合余留体是否被保留在其诞生环境中，对于理解层级并合和中等质量黑洞的增长至关重要。我们识别了最可能与致密星团起源一致的引力波事件，并评估了它们的并合余留体是否保留在球状星团、核星团或星系势阱中。我们考虑了LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA探测器网络第四次观测运行前段（O4a）中84个与双黑洞并合一致的事件，以及第四段后半（O4b）中选出的3个事件。我们将参数估计后验与现场和星团双星系统的合成群体模型进行比较，使用贝叶斯因子，并考虑了这些形成通道的相对丰度。我们使用IMRPhenomXPNR波形模型计算了所有事件的反冲速度后验。我们识别出五个事件，其内在参数显示出对采用的致密星团模型的偏好，超过所考虑的现场双星群体，包括O4a中最大质量的事件GW231123_135430，而对高自旋的O4b事件GW241011_233834未发现对致密星团起源的稳健偏好。典型的反冲速度为每秒几百公里，具有延伸的高速尾。这些反冲表明并合余留体可能从典型的球状星团中喷射出来，而在核星团中的保留是可能的但不一定能保证。在所采用的模型内，典型球状星团中有效的层级增长可能具有挑战性，而核星团仍然是重复并合的可行环境。尽管结果依赖于所采用的群体模型，本分析强调了改进的群体模型和未来引力波探测器实现更高质量探测的重要性。

English: Determining the astrophysical origin of binary black holes and whether merger remnants are retained in their birth environments is essential for understanding hierarchical mergers and the growth of intermediate-mass black holes. We identified gravitational-wave events most consistent with dense-cluster origin and assessed whether their merger remnants are retained in globular clusters, nuclear star clusters, or galactic potentials. We considered 84 events consistent with binary-black-hole mergers from the first part of the fourth observing run (O4a) of the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA detector network, and 3 selected events from the second part (O4b). We compared parameter-estimation posteriors with synthetic population models for field and cluster binaries using Bayes factors, accounting for the relative abundances of these formation channels. We computed recoil-velocity posteriors for all events using the IMRPhenomXPNR waveform model. We identified five events whose intrinsic parameters show preference for the adopted dense-cluster models over the considered field-binary populations, including the most massive O4a event GW231123_135430, while finding no robust preference for a dense-cluster origin for the high-spinning O4b event GW241011_233834. Typical recoil velocities are a few hundred km/s, with extended high-velocity tails. These kicks suggest merger remnants are likely ejected from typical globular clusters, while retention in nuclear star clusters remains possible but not guaranteed. Within the adopted models, efficient hierarchical growth may be challenging in typical globular clusters, whereas nuclear star clusters remain viable environments for repeated mergers. Although results depend on the adopted population models, this analysis highlights the importance of improved population models and higher-quality detections enabled by future GW detectors.

一句话导读：分析GWTC-4.0引力波事件，评估双黑洞并合余留体是否被保留在球状星团或核星团等环境中，发现典型反冲速度可能导致从球状星团喷射。

中子星可观测对混合状态方程模型中转变密度的敏感性

Sensitivity of Neutron Star Observables to Transition Density in Hybrid Equation-of-State Models

N. K. Patra, Sk Md Adil Imam, Kai Zhou · nucl-th, astro-ph.HE, astro-ph.SR, gr-qc, hep-ph · v2 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：我们研究了转变密度ρ_tr如何影响中子星状态方程(EoS)的混合构造，其中低密度核子描述与基于声速参数化的模型无关高密度扩展进行匹配。使用四种代表性核子模型——泰勒展开、n/3展开、Skyrme和相对论平均场——它们由相同的核物质参数构建，我们隔离了低密度EoS与转变密度对中子星可观测量的影响。我们发现，在当前平滑匹配方案下，即使在采用相同高密度参数化时，对于常用的转变密度ρ_tr≈2ρ_0，中子星性质如半径和潮汐变形率仍对低密度EoS的选择保持显著敏感性。这种残余依赖性源于ρ_tr处匹配条件的差异，该差异会传播到高密度扩展中，因此不同的低密度输入会导致不同的有效高密度EoS。这些发现在两种不同的声速参数化下都是稳健的。定量上，在ρ_tr≈2ρ_0时，1.4 M⊙处半径和潮汐变形率的模型离散度分别超过当前观测不确定度约1.8倍和约1.4倍，而在ρ_tr=ρ_0时这些因子降至约1.05和0.4。因此，降低转变密度会系统地缩小模型间的离散度并带来更一致的预测。我们的结果表明，广泛使用的ρ_tr≈2ρ_0选择并不能保证混合EoS构造中的模型无关性，在从中子星观测推断致密物质性质时应将其视为系统不确定性的一个明确来源。

English: We investigate how the transition density \(\rho_{tr}\) affects hybrid constructions of the neutron-star equation of state (EoS) in which a nucleonic description at low densities is matched to a model-agnostic high-density extension based on a speed-of-sound parametrization. Using four representative nucleonic models--Taylor expansion, \(\frac{n}{3}\) expansion, Skyrme, and relativistic mean-field--built from identical nuclear matter parameters, we isolate the impact of the low-density EoS and the transition density on neutron star observables. We find that, within the present smooth-matching prescription, neutron star properties such as radii and tidal deformabilities retain significant sensitivity to the choice of low-density EoS for commonly adopted transition densities around \(\rho_{tr} \approx 2\rho_0\), even when the same high-density parametrization is employed. This residual dependence arises from differences in the matching conditions at \(\rho_{tr}\), which propagate into the high-density extension, so different low-density inputs lead to different effective high-density EoSs. These findings are robust across two distinct speed-of-sound parametrizations. Quantitatively, the model spread in radius and tidal deformability at $1.4\,M_\odot$ exceeds the current observational uncertainty by factors of $\sim 1.8$ and $\sim 1.4$ at $\rho_{\mathrm{tr}} \approx 2\rho_0$, whereas these factors reduce to $\sim 1.05$ and $\sim 0.4$ at $\rho_{\mathrm{tr}} = \rho_0$. Lowering the transition density, therefore, systematically diminishes the spread among models and leads to more consistent predictions. Our results demonstrate that the widely used choice \(\rho_{tr} \approx 2\rho_0\) does not guarantee model independence in hybrid EoS constructions, and should be treated as an explicit source of systematic uncertainty when inferring dense matter properties from neutron star observations.

一句话导读：研究表明中子星混合EoS中常用的转变密度2ρ_0会导致可观测量对低密度模型敏感，应视为系统性不确定来源。

相关度：medium — 论文涉及中子星状态方程及其可观测量，属于经典引力/致密天体领域，但与全息或量子引力无关；用户关注了经典引力支线，因此有一定相关。

快速自旋双黑洞上的磁化吸积：迷你盘热力学、磁输运与双喷流

Magnetized accretion onto rapidly spinning binary black holes: mini-disk thermodynamics, magnetic transport, and dual jets

Luciano Combi, Manuela Campanelli, Sean M. Ressler, Alexander J. Dittmann, Federico Cattorini · astro-ph.HE, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：嵌入富气体环境中的超大质量双黑洞是脉冲星计时阵列和未来天基引力波干涉仪很有前景的多信使源。它们的电磁辐射受双星周围非线性等离子体动力学支配，并预期携带与其轨道运动相关的变率。由于活动星系核本质上具有随机性，识别可靠的双星特征需要将大尺度环双星流与黑洞联系起来的预测模型。先前的相对论性吸积双星模拟大多聚焦于较小的间距和较低的自旋。我们对一个松弛的、磁化的环双星盘吸积到等质量双黑洞上进行了三维广义相对论磁流体动力学模拟，其中间距为30个引力半径，无量纲自旋χ=0.9。在这些间距下，每个黑洞周围的迷你盘是持续的物质库，但仍然表现出由偏心环双星盘和迷你盘之间晃动气体所决定的显著振幅调制。我们分析了磁通量如何从环双星盘输运到视界，并发射出强大的双喷流，能量提取效率可达约40%。贯穿视界的磁通量和喷流光度在两个黑洞之间交替，产生一种开-关式双喷流状态。宽阔的喷流漏斗在双星上方相互作用，形成一个有利于磁重联的持续电流片。我们探索了迷你盘热力学的影响，比较了腔内有效和低效冷却的情况。更热的迷你盘质量较小，表现出较弱的相干周期性，并且在贯穿视界磁通量相当的情况下发射出较不明亮的喷流。

English: Supermassive binary black holes embedded in gas-rich environments are promising multi-messenger sources for pulsar timing arrays and future space-borne gravitational-wave interferometers. Their electromagnetic emission is governed by nonlinear plasma dynamics around the binary and is expected to inherit variability associated with the orbital motion thereof. Because active galactic nuclei are intrinsically stochastic, identifying robust binary signatures requires predictive models that connect large-scale circumbinary flows to the black holes. Previous relativistic simulations of accreting binaries have mostly focused on smaller separations and lower spins. We perform three-dimensional general relativistic magnetohydrodynamic simulations of a relaxed, magnetized circumbinary disk accreting onto an equal-mass binary black hole with a separation of $30$ gravitational radii and dimensionless spin $\chi=0.9$. At these separations, the mini-disks around each black hole are persistent mass reservoirs but still show pronounced amplitude modulations governed by the eccentric circumbinary disk and the sloshing gas between the mini-disks. We analyze how the magnetic flux is transported from the circumbinary disk to the horizons, launching powerful dual jets with energy extraction efficiencies reaching $\approx 40\%$. The horizon-threading flux and jet luminosity alternate between the two black holes, producing an on-off dual-jet state. The wide jet funnels interact above the binary and form a persistent current sheet favorable to reconnection. We explore the influence of mini-disk thermodynamics, comparing efficient and inefficient cooling inside the cavity. Hotter mini-disks are less massive, exhibit weaker coherent periodicity, and launch less luminous jets despite comparable horizon-threading magnetic flux.

一句话导读：三维GRMHD模拟快速自旋等质量双黑洞吸积，揭示磁通输运驱动的交替双喷流，并比较迷你盘冷却效率对喷流光度的影响。

相关度：high — 该文直接研究经典引力/广义相对论框架下的双黑洞磁化吸积与喷流，属于用户经典引力支线，与预设重点标签一致。

具有来自玻色子暗物质的混合物态方程的超致密孪星

Ultra-compact twin stars with hybrid equations of state from bosonic dark matter

Ishfaq Ahmad Rather, Sarah Louisa Pitz, J\"urgen Schaffner-Bielich · astro-ph.HE, hep-ph, nucl-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：研究了从强一阶相变过渡到夸克物质并额外包含自相互作用玻色子暗物质流体的致密星性质。结果表明，与简单的单流体判据相比，加入暗物质会显著改变质量-半径构型的稳定性。对于质量相近而半径不同的所谓孪星构型，暗物质的存在消除了强子支与混合支之间的不稳定段，使得相变开始后的稳定质量-半径序列变为连续。我们还发现了由总致密度 C=M_tot/R_grav≥1/3 定义的稳定超致密天体(UCO)，并观察到两类UCO：暗物质晕类(f_DM≳0.9)和暗物质核类(f_DM≲0.02)。两类可通过正常物质的表面红移区分：暗物质核类达到z=0.73–0.77，暗物质晕类低于0.45。最后，还得到质量和可见半径相近但暗物质含量不同的“终极孪星”混合星解，它们具有不同潮汐形变率与表面红移。未来对半径和质量超出普通中子星范围的超致密中子星进行X射线与引力波测量，可以探测暗物质的存在及其性质，以及到夸克物质的一阶相变。

English: The properties of compact stars with a strong first-order phase transition to quark matter and with an additional fluid of self-interacting bosonic dark matter (DM) are studied. We find that the inclusion of DM changes considerably the stability of mass-radius configurations relative to the naive one-fluid criterion. For compact star configurations with similar masses and different radii, so-called twin stars, the presence of DM removes the unstable segment between the hadronic and the hybrid branch, so that the stable mass-radius sequence becomes continuous after the onset of the phase transition to quark matter. We furthermore find stable ultra-compact objects (UCOs), defined by a total compactness $C = M_\text{tot}/R_\text{grav} \ge 1/3$. We observe two distinct classes of UCOs: a DM-halo class with $f_\text{DM} \gtrsim 0.9$, and a DM-core class at $f_\text{DM} \lesssim 0.02$. The two classes can be separated by the surface redshift of the normal matter, which reaches $z=0.73$--$0.77$ for the DM-core class and stays below $0.45$ for the DM-halo class. Finally, we find hybrid star solutions of 'ultimate twins' with similar mass and visible radius, but different dark matter content, leading to different tidal deformabilities and surface redshifts. Future X-ray and gravitational measurements of ultra-compact neutron stars with radii and masses outside the allowed neutron star range can thereby probe the presence and the properties of DM in addition to a first-order phase transition to quark matter.

一句话导读：研究表明，暗物质可消除强子-混合孪星支间的不稳定段，并产生暗物质晕类和暗物质核类两类超致密天体，可能成为探测暗物质及夸克相变的窗口。

视在奇点的全息零点与极点

Holographic zeros and poles from apparent singularities

Edwan Pr\'eau · hep-th, cond-mat.str-el, math-ph, math.MP · v1 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：我们研究具有视在奇点的一般全息涨落方程及其关联的谱函数。我们考虑视在奇点逼近边界的区域，在此区域可以通过内外匹配类型的计算确定解的近边界行为。根据方程的性质，我们发现视在奇点与边界的合并可能为相应的谱函数产生零点或极点。我们的结果还表明，最近引入的全息乘积公式在视在奇点存在的情况下仍然适用。

English: We study general holographic fluctuation equations with apparent singularities, and the associated spectral functions. We consider the regime where an apparent singularity approaches the boundary, for which the near-boundary behavior of solutions can be determined via an inner/outer matching type of calculation. Depending on properties of the equation, we find that the merging of an apparent singularity with the boundary may generate either a zero or a pole for the corresponding spectral function. Our results also indicate that the recently introduced holographic product formula still applies in the presence of apparent singularities.

IIB型超引力的边值问题与全息

Boundary-Value Problem in Type IIB Supergravity and Holography

Soumya Adhikari, Junho Hong, Chanyoung Joung, Geum Lee, Sourav Roychowdhury · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：在精密全息中，IIB型超引力的欧氏在壳作用量往往无法再现对偶场论的大N自由能领头阶：例如，IIB型赝作用量在 EAdS_5×S^5 背景上恒等于零。我们通过从第一性原理重新审视欧氏 IIB 型赝作用量的边值问题来解决这一困难。通过变分原理对可容许边界条件进行分类，我们构造了一个推广的赝作用量，其边界项体现了进行全息比较时所选取的系综——固定势还是固定量子化 Page 荷。对于固定五形式荷系综，所得的边界项重现了最近提出的 IIB 型超引力 Pasti-Sorokin-Tonin 表述的拓扑修正，适用于感兴趣的全息背景。随后我们在两个互补背景上检验了这个推广的赝作用量：D1-D5 系统的 EAdS_3×S^3×M_4 近邻几何，以及对偶于五维 SCFT 的弯曲 EAdS_6×S^2×Σ 解。在这两种情形下，十维在壳作用量都与相应的低维超引力作用量精确一致，从而与对偶 SCFT 的大 N 自由能领头阶一致。我们的结果确立了边界条件——因而系综——的选择是直接在 IIB 型超引力中进行精密全息的一个基本要素，扩展了最近在十一维超引力中的分析。

English: In precision holography, the Euclidean on-shell action of type IIB supergravity often fails to reproduce the leading large-$N$ free energy of the dual field theory: the type IIB pseudo action, for instance, vanishes identically on the $\mathrm{EAdS}_5\times S^5$ background. We address this issue by revisiting the boundary-value problem of the Euclidean type IIB pseudo action from first principles. Classifying the admissible boundary conditions through the variational principle, we construct a generalized pseudo action whose boundary terms implement the choice of ensemble --- fixed potentials versus fixed quantized Page charges --- in which the holographic comparison is performed. For the fixed five-form-charge ensemble, the resulting boundary term reproduces the recently proposed topological correction to the Pasti--Sorokin--Tonin formulation of type IIB supergravity for holographic backgrounds of interest. We then test the generalized pseudo action on two complementary backgrounds: the $\mathrm{EAdS}_3\times S^3\times M_4$ near-horizon geometry of the D1-D5 system and the warped $\mathrm{EAdS}_6\times S^2\times\Sigma$ solutions dual to five-dimensional SCFTs. In both cases the ten-dimensional on-shell action agrees exactly with that of the corresponding lower-dimensional supergravity, and thereby with the leading large-$N$ free energy of the dual SCFT. Our results establish the choice of boundary conditions --- and hence of ensemble --- as an essential ingredient of precision holography directly in type IIB supergravity, extending recent analyses in eleven-dimensional supergravity.

一句话导读：通过构造推广的 IIB 型超引力赝作用量边界项，解决其在全息背景下在壳作用量为零的问题，并验证了与对偶大N自由能的一致性。

相关度：medium — 与重点标签中的“经典引力/广义相对论”相关，但其核心是精密全息字典与超引力边值问题，而非用户主要关注的凝聚态、探针膜或QNM支线。

来自BF理论的JT引力中的锥形缺陷：量子化、融合与加权模空间

Conical Defects in JT Gravity from BF Theory: Quantization, Fusion, and Weighted Moduli Spaces

Wei Gu · hep-th, math.AG · v1 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：我们从BF理论出发，构造了Jackiw–Teitelboim（JT）引力中锥形缺陷的规范不变BF表示，并通过重构引力变量固定其椭圆方向。在引力扇区中，该BF可观测量重现了度规缺陷插入及其分布型曲率源，并使绕缺陷的holonomy固定为椭圆共轭类。对于圆盘上的单缺陷，位置积分与剩余的PSL(2,R)商空间结合，留下一个固定的椭圆扇区。该扇区的精确边界量子化直接给出基本kernel和单缺陷圆盘振幅，无需使用矩阵模型。同一个局部BF算子既描述尖锐缺陷也描述钝缺陷；它们的差别仅在多个缺陷位置被积分时显现。BF源的合成使分数亏缺具有可加性；要求熔合源仍处于引力锥扇区给出缺陷碰撞的可容许条件，而每个分量上双曲面积的正性给出相应的稳定性条件。这些条件与Hassett加权曲线一致。将上述BF结果与已知Hassett紧化及锥形Weil–Petersson几何结合，可得到固定阶亏格零接触项，并恢复deformed JT引力的cluster展开。因此，尖锐与钝缺陷通过其相对位置的紧化几何区分，而非通过基本BF算子。

English: We construct a gauge-invariant BF representative of a conical defect in Jackiw--Teitelboim gravity, with its elliptic direction fixed by the reconstruction of the gravitational variables. In the gravitational sector, the BF observable reproduces the metric defect insertion and its distributional curvature source, and fixes the holonomy around the defect to an elliptic conjugacy class. For a single defect on the disk, the position integral combines with the residual \(\mathrm{PSL}(2,\mathbb R)\) quotient, leaving a fixed elliptic sector. The exact boundary quantization of this sector gives the elementary kernel and one-defect disk amplitude directly, without using a matrix model. The same local BF operator describes both sharp and blunt defects. Their difference appears only when several defect positions are integrated. BF source composition makes the fractional deficits additive. Requiring a fused source to remain in the gravitational cone sector gives the admissibility condition for defect collisions, while positivity of the hyperbolic area on each component gives the corresponding stability condition. These conditions coincide with those for Hassett weighted curves. Combining these BF results with the known Hassett compactification and conical Weil--Petersson geometry yields the fixed-order genus-zero contact terms and recovers the cluster expansion of deformed JT gravity. Thus sharp and blunt defects differ through the compactified geometry of their relative positions, rather than through the elementary BF operator.

一句话导读：用BF理论构造JT引力锥形缺陷的规范不变表示，结合Hassett加权模空间解释尖锐与钝缺陷的差异，并恢复deformed JT引力的cluster展开。

相关度：low — 论文属于JT引力/BF形式量子化及加权模空间研究，与用户列出的全息超流超导、涡旋/非均匀态、相变/气泡成核、经典相对论等具体方向无直接交集。

极端质量比旋进中的潮汐增强共振：通向混沌的三体路径

Tidally-enhanced resonances in extreme-mass-ratio inspirals: A tertiary path to chaos

Kyriakos Destounis, Takuya Katagiri, Sajal Mukherjee, Kostas D. Kokkotas · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE, hep-ph, hep-th, nlin.CD · v1 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：极端质量比旋进（EMRIs）为探测强场引力和复杂相对论动力学提供了独特实验室。我们研究了一个潮汐形变的EMRI，由一个绕超大质量（非）旋转黑洞旋转的恒星质量次级体组成，该黑洞嵌入外部绝热变化的潮汐环境中。这些系统提供了相对论三体问题的一个受限但天体物理动机明确的实现，预期出现在活动星系核中，具有清晰的质量层级和辐射反作用时间尺度。外部潮汐形变破坏了Kerr时空的轴对称性，使测地线动力学不可积并产生混沌运动。通过庞加莱映射和由束缚径向、极向和方位运动基本频率之比构建的旋转曲线，刻画了不可积性的特征。我们识别出两个显著的平台，其宽度随潮汐场振幅增加，标志着从弱混沌到强混沌的转变。然后我们展示了混沌动力学对轨道相对潮汐场方向的敏感性。我们进一步分析了作用-角变量的固有时演化，表明与主导共振相关的角组合被相位锁定，从而使相关潮汐贡献能够引起运动常数的长期变化，而平台外的角组合则循环。这些结果阐明了显著平台的动力学意义，并为EMRIs中的潮汐共振提供了新的相空间表征。最后，我们讨论了辐射反作用效应在驱动EMRIs穿越潮汐岛区方面的潜在作用，以及对未来探测器引力波推断的影响。

English: Extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) provide a unique laboratory for probing strong-field gravity and complex relativistic dynamics. We study a tidally deformed EMRI composed of a stellar-mass secondary orbiting a supermassive (non-)rotating black hole embedded in an external, adiabatically varying tidal environment. These systems provide a restricted, yet astrophysically motivated, realization of the relativistic three-body problem, expected to appear in active galactic nuclei, with a clear hierarchy of masses and radiation-reaction timescales. The external tidal deformation breaks the axisymmetry of the Kerr spacetime, rendering the geodesic dynamics non-integrable and giving rise to chaotic motion. The resulting signature of non-integrability is characterized through Poincar\'e maps and rotation curves constructed from the ratios of the fundamental frequencies of bound radial, polar, and azimuthal motion. We identify two prominent plateaus whose widths increase with the tidal field amplitude, signaling a transition from weak to strong chaos. We then demonstrate the sensitivity of the chaotic dynamics to the orientation of the orbit relative to the tidal field. We further analyze the proper-time evolution of the action-angle variables, showing that the angle combinations associated with the dominant commensurabilities are phase locked, thereby allowing the associated tidal contributions to induce secular changes in the constants of motion, whereas off-plateau angle combinations circulate. These results clarify the dynamical significance of the prominent plateaus and provide a novel phase-space characterization of tidal resonances in EMRIs. Finally, we discuss the potential role of radiation-reaction effects in driving EMRIs through tidal island crossings and the implications for gravitational-wave inference with future detectors.

一句话导读：研究外部潮汐环境导致EMRI的Kerr测地线运动不可积并产生混沌，揭示了潮汐增强共振的平台结构及其对引力波推断的意义。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论中的EMRI动力学与混沌研究，与用户标注的'经典引力/广义相对论'重点标签直接相关，但与全息或量子引力方向无关。

单场暴胀中的普适CMB相位相干性

Universal CMB Phase Coherence from Single-Field Inflation

Sim\'eon Vareilles, Thomas Colas, Julien Grain, Federico Piazza, Vincent Vennin · astro-ph.CO, gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：原初扰动的相位相干性塑造了CMB的声学峰，并被视为暴胀的关键特征。通常认为，不同能标因而不同持续时间的暴胀情形会留下不同程度的相干性。我们证明这一预期并不成立：由于曲率扰动在超视界尺度上守恒，重组时期的相干性继承了一个仅由观测到的原初谱确定的数值。这一结论与暴胀能标及随后的膨胀历史无关。

English: The phase coherence of primordial perturbations shapes the acoustic peaks of the CMB and is regarded as a key signature of inflation. Inflationary scenarios at different energy scales, thus with different durations, are commonly expected to leave different levels of coherence. We show this expectation fails: because the curvature perturbation is conserved on super-Hubble scales, the coherence at recombination inherits a value fixed by the observed primordial spectrum alone. This holds irrespective of the inflationary energy scale and the subsequent expansion history.

一句话导读：暴胀原初扰动的相位相干性在重组时期由观测到的原初谱单独固定，与暴胀能标和后续膨胀历史无关。

多边界多体量子隐形传态

Multi-Boundary Many-Body Quantum Teleportation

Tal Schwartzman, Antonio F. Rotundo, Raz Monsonego, Shira Chapman · quant-ph, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：与标准量子隐形传态不同，多体隐形传态利用量子扰乱来传输量子信息。在该协议中，初始局域化的信息扩展到许多自由度中，随后通过系统间简单的耦合以及进一步的多体演化在接收端重新聚焦。该协议源于全息中可穿越虫洞的模型，并且已成为探测量子扰乱的有力工具。特别是，当离时序关联函数失效时，它能区分真正的扰乱与退相干或噪声，并揭示不同扰乱机制的特征，包括全息系统中预期的独特行为。全息理论预言，相关协议可以在多边界虫洞几何的选定边界之间传输信息。受此情景启发，我们研究了三个量子比特系统之间的单量子比特多体隐形传态。初始态由分布在它们之间的EPR对组成，提供了无限温度三边界全息态的一个简单模拟。我们使用随机电路，通过解析和数值方法分析了一维与全对全动力学。我们发现，一旦传播的信息到达与第三系统纠缠的量子比特区域，第三个系统就会抑制隐形传态。在一维情形，成功隐形传态所需的最小耦合强度以及保真度都取决于注入点到该区域的距离，这一特征让人联想到全息因果阴影。在全对全动力学下，成功的隐形传态反而局限于早期时间，并且只限于与第三系统纠缠的量子比特数目足够少的场景。因此，第三个系统提供了双面协议所不具备的、对信息传播的空间或子系统分辨能力。我们的结果朝着多体隐形传态网络迈出了一步。

English: Unlike standard quantum teleportation, many-body teleportation uses scrambling to transmit quantum information. In this protocol, initially localized information spreads over many degrees of freedom and is later refocused at the receiver by a simple coupling between the systems, followed by further many-body evolution. The protocol was developed from models of traversable wormholes in holography and has become a useful probe of scrambling. In particular, it can distinguish genuine scrambling from decoherence or noise when out-of-time-order correlators fail, and can reveal signatures of different scrambling mechanisms, including the distinctive behavior expected in holographic systems. Holography predicts that related protocols can transmit information between selected boundaries of multi-boundary wormhole geometries. Motivated by this setting, we study single-qubit many-body teleportation among three systems of qubits. The initial state consists of EPR pairs distributed among them, providing a simple analogue of an infinite-temperature three-boundary holographic state. We analyze the protocol with one-dimensional and all-to-all dynamics, both analytically and numerically using random circuits. We find that the third system suppresses teleportation once the spreading message reaches the region of qubits that are entangled with it. In one dimension, both the minimum coupling required for successful teleportation and the fidelity depend on the distance from the injection site to this region, a feature reminiscent of holographic causal shadows. For all-to-all dynamics, successful teleportation is instead restricted to early times and to sufficiently few qubits entangled with the third system. A third system therefore provides spatial, or subsystem, resolution of information spreading that is absent from the two-sided protocol. Our results offer a step toward many-body teleportation networks.

一句话导读：该工作受多边界全息虫洞启发，研究了三系统量子比特网络中的多体隐形传态，并揭示了第三个系统如何对信息扰乱提供空间分辨的抑制效应。

相关度：low — 论文主要关注多体量子隐形传态与扰乱探针，与用户重点标签中的全息凝聚态、相变、QNM、探针膜等方向无直接对应。

熵引力中的引力波与物质约束

Gravitational Waves and Matter Constraints in Gravity from Entropy

David S. Pereira · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：我们证明熵引力（GfE）在一般FLRW背景上存在张量传播病态。在真空、继承对称的单多重态扇区以及代数完美流体本构嵌入下，严格主TT系统仅当 Ḣ=K/a² 时具有非零实特征；离开该轨迹后，时间主根变为复数，表明高频双曲性障碍。在上述各向同性假设下，秩定理进一步表明一个基础多重态无法提供消除该问题所需的各向同性双矢响应。

English: We show that Gravity from Entropy (GfE) exhibits a tensor-propagation pathology on generic FLRW backgrounds. In vacuum, in the symmetry-inheriting single-multiplet sector, and for the algebraic perfect-fluid constitutive embedding, the strict-principal TT system has a nonzero real characteristic only when \(\dot H=K/a^2\). Away from this locus the temporal principal roots become complex, signaling a high-frequency hyperbolicity obstruction. Under the stated isotropy assumptions, a rank theorem further shows that one foundational multiplet cannot provide the isotropic bivector response needed to remove this problem.

环绕暗能量的带电动磁荷旋转宇宙学黑洞

Dyonic rotating cosmological black hole surrounded by quintessence

M. D. de Oliveira, Alexandre G. M. Schmidt · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：本文构造了一个新的黑洞度规解，描述在暗能量（quintessence）存在下带有电和磁荷的旋转宇宙学黑洞，其中宇宙学常数和暗能量与外部物质相关联。从一种Schwarzschild型度规出发，在函数 f(r) 中考虑所有能量贡献，我们通过Newman–Janis算法引入旋转，使用独特而一般的复化规则以避免可能的歧义。假设该度规满足含物质的Einstein场方程，我们计算了事件视界条件、总应力-能量张量 T_{\mu\nu}，以及Kretschmann和Ricci标量。我们还分析了宇宙学常数 Λ 和暗能量参数 α 对事件视界和能层的影响。在极限 α → 0 下，标量曲率不同于真空结果，即 R ≠ -4Λ，而奇点区域保持不变。最后，我们研究了测试粒子的角速度和转动速度，以及通过该黑洞解存在所需的能量密度和压强所给出的能量条件。

English: In this work, we constructed a new metric describing a rotating cosmological black hole with electric and magnetic charges in the presence of quintessence dark energy, where the cosmological constant and quintessence are associated with external matter. Starting from a Schwarzschild-type metric and considering all energy contributions in the function $f(r)$, we introduced rotation through the Newman--Janis algorithm using a unique and general complexification rule, avoiding possible ambiguities. Assuming that the metric satisfies the Einstein field equations with matter, we calculate the event horizon condition, the total stress--energy tensor $T_{\mu\nu}$, and the Kretschmann and Ricci scalars. We also analyze the influence of the cosmological constant $\Lambda$ and quintessence parameter $\alpha$ on the event horizon and ergosphere. In the limit $\alpha \rightarrow 0$, the scalar curvature differs from the vacuum result, $R \neq -4\Lambda$, while the singularity region remains unchanged. Finally, we investigate the angular and rotational velocities of a test particle, as well as the energy conditions, through the energy density and pressures required for the existence of this black hole solution.

一句话导读：构造了一个由quintessence暗能量包围的带电动和磁荷的旋转宇宙学黑洞新解，分析了视界、能层、能量条件等经典引力性质。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论支线，研究旋转黑洞解的构造与性质，而非全息或量子引力，因此与用户主要关注点有一定关联但不紧密。

一种用于统一杨-米尔斯场、引力和物质费米子的几何作用原理

A Geometric Action Principle for Unification of Yang-Mills Fields, Gravity and Matter Fermions

Pedro D. Alvarez, Fernando Izaurieta, Cristian Quinzacara · hep-th, gr-qc, math-ph, math.MP · v4 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：一个由六维限制空间中的Clifford生成不变项的线性组合构建的单一几何作用，同时生成引力、杨-米尔斯和费米子扇区，并关联它们的结构和相对系数。其参数空间在缩减玻色子扇区中包含已知的线性化无鬼二次引力模型及传播挠率。因此，我们的结果为规范-引力统一提供了经济几何路径，既不需要额外维度，也不需要外部引入对称破缺场。特别地，Clifford代数构造自然容纳某些参数子空间，其中缩减玻色子扇区重现已知无鬼理论，且独立的Klein-Gordon型费米子动能项消失。

English: A single geometric action, constructed as a linear combination of a restricted six-dimensional family of Clifford-generated invariant terms, simultaneously generates the gravitational, Yang--Mills, and fermionic sectors and correlates their structure and relative coefficients. The resulting parameter space contains known linearized ghost-free quadratic gravitational models with propagating torsion in a reduced bosonic sector. Our results therefore establish an economical geometric route to gauge--gravity unification that requires neither extra dimensions nor an externally introduced symmetry-breaking field. In particular, the Clifford-algebraic construction naturally accommodates parameter subspaces in which the reduced bosonic sector reproduces known ghost-free theories and the independent Klein--Gordon-type fermionic kinetic term vanishes.

一句话导读：提出一个基于六维Clifford代数不变项构建的几何作用原理，可同时统一引力、杨-米尔斯和费米子扇区，实现无需额外维度的规范-引力统一。

c=1 矩阵模型中“类空奇点”的命运

Fate of "Space-like singularities" in $c=1$ Matrix Model

Sumit R. Das, Shaun D. Hampton, Sinong Liu, Gautam Mandal · hep-th, cond-mat.stat-mech, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：二维弦论中的一类含时背景导致世界面上的超光速Liouville墙。在双重缩放、双标度的c=1矩阵模型中，这些背景涉及本征值泄漏到无穷远，集体场涨落沿类空区域变得强耦合，看似像奇点。我们将这些背景实现为矩阵模型中的量子淬火结果，保留矩阵势中的非线性项，从而偏离双标度极限。在费米子图像和Thomas-Fermi近似下，我们证明：虽然早期相空间密度在势能最大值附近的行为与双标度理论一致，但在时间约(log N)时，红外墙的影响变得显著；到更晚时间，以特征卷绕时间约(log N)^2，费米面上的折叠不断增殖并最终致密地覆盖相空间中的允许区域。利用作用-角变量，我们发现相空间密度在晚期围绕一个与时间和角度无关的值快速振荡。角空间中的粗粒化密度按幂律弛豫到与时间无关的平衡值，该幂律指数在很大程度上与初态细节无关。因此，类空奇点的出现是严格双标度极限的一个假象。最后我们讨论了末态在弦论中的诠释。

English: A class of time dependent backgrounds in two dimensional String Theory leads to superluminal Liouville walls on the worldsheet. In the dual double scaled $c=1$ matrix model these backgrounds involve eigenvalues leaking out to infinity, and the collective field fluctuations become strongly coupled along space-like regions, resembling singularities. We realize these backgrounds as results of quantum quenches in the matrix model, retaining non-linear terms in the matrix potential, thus departing from a double scaling limit. Working in the fermion picture in a Thomas-Fermi approximation, we show that while the early time behavior of the phase space density near the maximum of the potential agrees with that obtained in the double scaled theory, at times of the order $(\log N)$ the effect of the IR wall becomes significant. At later times, with a characteristic winding time of order $(\log N)^2$, folds on the fermi surface proliferate and eventually cover the allowed region in phase space densely. Using action-angle variables, we show that the phase space density oscillates around a time independent and angle independent value rapidly at late times. A coarse-grained density in the angle space relaxes to a time independent equilibrium value as a power law with an exponent largely independent of the details of the initial state. Thus, the appearance of a space-like singularity is an artifact of the strict double scaling limit. We comment on the interpretation of the final state in String Theory.

一句话导读：c=1矩阵模型中出现的类空奇点仅是严格双标度极限的假象，在量子淬火并保留非线性势项后奇点被规整化，系统弛豫到平衡态。

相关度：low — 论文关注二维弦论/矩阵模型中类空奇点的命运与淬火后弛豫，属于矩阵模型量子引力研究，与用户主要关注的全息超流、p波序参量、相变气泡等支线无直接关联。

岛规则下互信息的新见解：Page曲线

New insights on mutual information in the island approach to the Page curve

Anirban Roy Chowdhury, Souvik Paul, Sunandan Gangopadhyay · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：本文对Page时间后两组子系统互信息的行为提出了一个重要观察，为柯西切片上几何关联的再分布提供了深刻见解。我们早期工作已展示B+与B-间互信息在扰乱时间饱和为零，从而得到正确Page曲线；这表明在扰乱时间B+与B-无关联。值得注意的是，此时I与R的互信息I(I:R)发散，表明I与R最大纠缠，体现了柯西切片上不同区域间几何关联的转移。本文还利用双体区域的结果，给出了计算三体互信息I(I:R+:R-)的方法，这是此前文献缺失的新结果。

English: In this article, we have presented one of the very important observations, regarding the behavior of mutual information of two different sets of subsystems in the after Page time scenario. This provides us with some deep insights about the redistribution of geometrical correlation between the two different sets of subsystems on a Cauchy slice. In our earlier works, we have shown how the saturation of mutual information between two specific subsystems plays a vital role in obtaining the correct Page curve for the eternal black hole. In those works, we have shown that the mutual information between $B_+$ and $B_-$, that is, $I(B_{+}: B_{-})$, vanishes at scrambling time, which leads to the correct Page curve. That means that at scrambling time, there is no correlation between $B_+$ and $B_-$. Remarkably, it is observed that at this particular value of observer's time, the mutual information between $\mathcal{I}$ and $R$, that is, $I(\mathcal{I}:R)$, becomes singular. This indicates that the regions $\mathcal{I}$ and $R$ become maximally entangled. This provides us with a notion of the transfer of geometric correlation between different regions on the Cauchy slice. In this work, we have also provided a way to calculate the tripartite mutual information of regions $\mathcal{I}$,~$R_+$ and $R_-$, that is, $I(\mathcal{I}:R_+:R_-)$ on the Cauchy slice using the earlier results involving the bipartite regions. This is a new result which was missing in the earlier literature.

一句话导读：发现Page时间后B+与B-互信息在扰乱时间消失，同时I与R互信息发散，并给出三体互信息的新计算方法。

相关度：low — 摘要属于黑洞信息/岛规则/Page曲线，不在用户重点标签覆盖的全息凝聚态、相变、QNM、经典引力等支线内。

平面N=4超杨-米尔斯理论中奇数共形自旋的次次领头阶BFKL特征值

The next-to-next-to-leading order BFKL eigenvalue at odd conformal spin in planar N=4 super Yang-Mills

Alexander Prygarin, Claudelle Capasia Madjuogang Sandeu · hep-th, hep-ph · v3 · 更新 2026-09-04

中文摘要：我们给出了闭式形式的平面N=4超杨-米尔斯在奇数共形自旋n下的色单态BFKL次次领头阶特征值。在较低的两个阶上，嵌套调和和只出现在两个共轭点z=(|n|-1)/2+iν和zbar上。在三圈阶，它们在一个从反射点-zbar到z的整数平移自变量的阶梯上求值，并再向该阶梯末端外延伸一点，该处系数的取值由其到阶梯两端的两个距离决定。有七个族是例外：它们的系数需要求和指标随求和索引移动的阶梯和。对共形自旋统一的规则生成了所有奇数n≥3的47个族；在定义中没有列出任意额外自旋的系数，而n=1边界块单独提供。这些规则是从已计算的自旋重构而来，在每个自旋上都精确，在n≤99范围内以及预留的自旋n=101上经过验证，但并未对任意奇数n证明。每个原子表系数是1、π²和ζ₃的有理组合，系数和原子共享权重五的超越度。在ν=0处，截距与量子谱曲线值在每个已计算的奇自旋直至n=91上匹配，这同时检验了被积函数和约化在每个自旋的一个点上（其中大多数是首次检验）。偏离该直线时，闭式给出了块在自旋上统一的共线行为，而仅靠截距无法确定该行为。

English: We give the next-to-next-to-leading order color-singlet BFKL eigenvalue of planar N=4 super Yang-Mills at odd conformal spin n in closed form. At the two lower orders the nested harmonic sums appear only at the two conjugate points z=(|n|-1)/2+i nu and zbar. At three loops they are evaluated on a ladder of integer-shifted arguments from the reflected point -zbar up to z, with one further point past it, the coefficient at a rung fixed by its two distances to the ladder ends. Seven families are the exception: their coefficients need a ladder sum whose summand shifts with the summation index. Rules uniform in the conformal spin produce all forty-seven families at every odd n>=3; no per-spin coefficient is tabulated in the definition, and the n=1 boundary block is supplied separately. Those rules are a reconstruction from the computed spins, exact at every one of them, verified over the range n<=99 and at the holdout spin n=101, and not proved at arbitrary odd n. Each atom-table coefficient is a rational combination of 1, pi^2 and zeta_3, coefficient and atom sharing the transcendental weight five. Along nu=0 the intercepts match the Quantum Spectral Curve values at each computed odd spin up to n=91, which tests the integrand and the reduction together at one point of each spin, and for most of them for the first time. Away from that line the closed form gives the collinear behavior of the block uniformly in the spin, which the intercepts alone do not fix.

一句话导读：该文给出了平面N=4超杨-米尔斯理论中奇数共形自旋的NNLO BFKL特征值闭式表达式，并用量子谱曲线进行了验证。

相关度：low — 该论文的核心是平面N=4超杨-米尔斯中的微扰BFKL特征值计算，属于高能Regge极限与可积性方向，与用户给定的全息超流、相变、QNM、经典引力等研究支线均无直接重叠。
