来自全息的高阶形式（准）流体力学：变形与对偶

Higher-form (quasi)hydrodynamics from holography: deformations and dualities

Andr\'e Oliveira Pinheiro · hep-th, cond-mat.str-el, gr-qc · v4 · 更新 2026-09-03

中文摘要：我们利用规范-引力对偶研究了具有精确和近似高阶形式对称性的系统的低能动力学。这些对称性通过 AlAdS 时空中的无质量/有质量 p-形式推广 Maxwell/Proca 理论在全息上实现。通过适当的边界条件考虑边界理论的双迹变形。我们计算各向同性黑膜背景下的热关联函数，以刻画对偶边界理论的近平衡区域。在零质量极限下，对于弱双迹变形（相对于温度设定的标度），理论表现出流体力学区域；对于强变形，则出现准流体力学区域。打开体质量则导致由质量和双迹耦合共同控制的三重准流体力学区域。总的来说，我们发现低能谱受到极点碰撞、涌现对称性和对偶关系约束，后者部分源自体中的 Hodge 型对偶。对于非零质量，还存在双迹耦合的强/弱对偶。我们还表明，在背景电荷的低密度极限下，稳定高维带电物体的扩散需要相关变形。

English: We study the low-energy dynamics of systems with exact and approximate higher-form symmetries using Gauge-gravity duality. These symmetries are realised holographically via generalised Maxwell/Proca theories for massless/massive $p$-forms in AlAdS spacetimes. Double-trace deformations of the boundary theory are considered via appropriate boundary conditions. We compute thermal correlation functions in isotropic black brane backgrounds to characterise the near equilibrium regimes of the dual boundary theories. In the vanishing-mass limit, the theory exhibits a hydrodynamic regime for weak double-trace deformations (relative to a scale set by the temperature) and a quasihydrodynamic regime for strong deformations. Turning on the bulk mass gives rise instead to a triad of quasihydrodynamic regimes controlled by both the mass and the double-trace coupling. In general, we find the low-energy spectra to be constrained by pole collisions, emergent symmetries and duality relations, the latter originating in part from Hodge-type dualities in the bulk. For nonzero mass, there is an additional strong/weak duality of the double-trace couplings. We further show, in the low-density limit of background charge, that relevant deformations are necessary for stable diffusion of sufficiently high-dimensional charged objects.

一句话导读：论文从 AdS/CFT 导出具有高阶形式对称性的系统的（准）流体动力学，系统分析双迹变形、质量与 Hodge 对偶对低能谱的影响。

相关度：high — 论文涉及全息双迹变形与（准）流体力学模式，与用户的 multi-trace 和 QNM 相关研究支线有直接联系。

带电黑洞的跑动Love数

Running Love Numbers of Charged Black Holes

Sergio Barbosa, Sylvain Fichet, Lucas de Souza · hep-th, astro-ph.CO, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-03

中文摘要：量子场论真空中的虚粒子圈使得黑洞具有潮汐形变。我们使用2501.18684中发展的微扰形式，计算任意半径下无自旋带电黑洞的静态潮汐响应。由于引力与电磁潮汐响应相互混合，我们将Love数的概念推广为Love矩阵。我们导出了纯电和纯磁背景周围度规与电磁涨落的耦合运动方程。对于由引力有效场论（EFT）描述的大带电黑洞，我们计算了由任意F^{2n}算符塔诱导的完整Love矩阵集合。我们发现，尽管量子修正破坏电磁对偶，但电背景与磁背景中的Love矩阵通过电-磁交换下的Z_2对称性关联。超越EFT，我们计算了小磁黑洞的Love矩阵。我们证明Love矩阵的跑动受U(1)规范耦合跑动支配，并推导了任意谐波下Love与U(1) beta函数之间的对应关系。总体图景是，QFT诱导的磁黑洞潮汐响应在强场区饱和。这些结果表明，在阿贝尔暗扇区下带电的近极端磁黑洞可通过引力波观测探及。

English: Loops of virtual particles from the vacuum of quantum field theory (QFT) render black holes tidally deformable. We compute the static tidal response of unspinning charged black holes at arbitrary radius, using the perturbative formalism developed in 2501.18684. Since the gravitational and electromagnetic tidal responses mix, we generalize the notion of Love numbers to Love matrices. We derive the coupled equations of motion for the metric and electromagnetic fluctuations around purely electric and magnetic backgrounds. For large charged black holes, which are described by the Effective Field Theory (EFT) of gravity, we compute the full set of Love matrices induced by an arbitrary tower of $F^{2n}$ operators. We find that, although quantum corrections break electromagnetic duality, the Love matrices in electric and magnetic backgrounds are related by a $Z_2$ symmetry under electric-magnetic exchange. Going beyond EFT, we compute the Love matrices of small magnetic black holes. We show that the running of the Love matrices is governed by the running of the $U(1)$ gauge coupling, and we derive the correspondence between Love and $U(1)$ beta functions for arbitrary harmonics. The overall picture that emerges is that the QFT-induced tidal response of magnetic black holes saturates in the strong-field regime. These results imply that nearly-extremal magnetic black holes charged under an Abelian dark sector could be probed by gravitational-wave observations.

一句话导读：计算带电黑洞的量子效应诱导Love矩阵，揭示其跑动与U(1) beta函数的对应，并预示近极端磁黑洞可被引力波探测。

相关度：high — 论文集中研究经典广义相对论中的黑洞潮汐响应与可观测效应，与用户重点标签'经典引力/广义相对论'直接匹配。

带电大D膜范式的流体对偶

A fluid dual to the charged large D membrane paradigm

Supratim Halder, Manu Kurian, Mangesh Mandlik · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-03

中文摘要：按照带电大D膜范式的表述，带有U(1)规范场的引力理论中的任意动态黑洞解对偶于非引力背景中膜的动力演化。该膜带有应力能量张量和电荷流，其守恒方程控制其动力学。本文证明，这些膜组态的动力演化（在1/D的最低非平凡阶）可以映射为相对论性带电流体，从而为渐近平坦黑洞与特定一类流体系统建立对应关系。与标准的AdS/流体动力学对应不同，该对偶流体并不位于渐近边界上，而是严格定域在非引力膜的世界体上。通过在Eckart和Landau参考系中评估该系统，我们系统提取了非平衡输运系数，发现流体受负等效热导率和负热容支配。该机制提供了对热力学稳定性的流体动力学解释，有效地将先前建立的大D Reissner-Nordström几何中的准正则模阻尼转化为流体动力学语言。

English: According to the formulation of the charged large $D$ membrane paradigm, an arbitrary dynamic black hole solution to a theory of gravity with a $U(1)$ gauge field is dual to the dynamics of a membrane in a non-gravitational background. This membrane is endowed with a stress-energy tensor and a charge current, whose conservation equations govern its dynamics. In this work, we demonstrate that the dynamics of these membrane configurations (at the leading nontrivial order in $1/D$) can be mapped to a relativistic charged fluid, establishing a correspondence for asymptotically flat black holes with a particular class of fluid systems. Unlike the standard AdS/Hydrodynamics correspondence, this dual fluid does not reside on an asymptotic boundary, but is localised strictly on the non-gravitational membrane worldvolume. By evaluating the system in both the Eckart and Landau frames, we systematically extract the out-of-equilibrium transport coefficients. We find that the fluid is governed by a negative effective thermal conductivity and a negative heat capacity. This mechanism provides a hydrodynamic interpretation of thermodynamic stability, effectively translating the previously established quasinormal mode damping of the large-$D$ Reissner-Nordstr\"om geometry into the language of fluid dynamics.

一句话导读：将带电大D膜范式映射为相对论性带电流体，提取出具有负热导率/负热容的非平衡输运系数，并用流体语言解释RN黑洞QNM阻尼。

相关度：medium — 论文研究大D膜范式下的引力/流体对偶，并提取输运系数、联系QNM阻尼，与用户关注的经典引力及QNM方向相关；但主要属于经典引力中的高维展开方法，与全息凝聚态、超导等支线无关。

经典引力/广义相对论QNM与非线性演化

全息体中的近似高阶对称性与有质量p-形式的对偶性

Approximate higher-form symmetries and dualities of massive p-forms in the holographic bulk

Andr\'e Oliveira Pinheiro · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-03

中文摘要：我们基于体路径积分、全息重整化和边界条件改变形变，为连续高阶对称性及其低能有效描述发展了一个全息框架。我们展示了如何通过有质量反对称张量场，在体中以参数小体质量或与Robin边界条件相关的强形变，在全息上实现与缺陷电流相关的近似高阶对称性。我们进一步研究了体中无质量和有质量Hodge对偶性的后果，推导出由不同量子化选择相关的边界理论之间的相应二重性。我们的结果为基于有质量p-形式的全息理论中的近似对称性、二重性和强/弱耦合关系提供了一个统一视角。在精确(self-dual)对称度为(d-3)/2的情形下，我们推导了存在双trace形变时全息流-流关联函数的广义约束。

English: We develop a holographic framework for continuous higher-form symmetries and their low-energy effective descriptions, based on bulk path integrals, holographic renormalisation and boundary-condition-changing deformations. We show how approximate higher-form symmetries associated with a defect current can be realised holographically through massive antisymmetric tensor fields, either via parametrically small bulk masses or strong deformations associated with Robin boundary conditions. We further study the consequences of massless and massive Hodge dualities in the bulk, deriving the corresponding dualities between boundary theories related by different quantisation choices. Our results provide a unified perspective on approximate symmetries, dualities and strong/weak-coupling relations in holographic theories based on massive $p$-forms. In the self-dual case of an exact symmetry of degree $(d-3)/2$, we derive generalised constraints on holographic current-current correlators in the presence of double-trace deformations.

一句话导读：发展了有质量p-形式全息描述高阶对称性的框架，推导了近似对称性与对偶性以及双trace形变下的约束。

相关度：medium — 研究全息高阶对称性和有质量p-形式，与AdS/CFT字典和multi-trace形变相关，但偏离用户强调的具体体系。

黑洞热力学分支结构中的拓扑变化与响应奇异性

Topological changes and response singularities in black hole thermodynamic branch structure

Di Wu · hep-th, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-03

中文摘要：本文研究不同诊断量如何刻画黑洞热力学分支结构。在三个带电 AdS 黑洞例子中，热力学拓扑转变点 τ_a 与沿固定电荷参数路径求得的所考虑的分支响应发散点 τ_b 彼此分离。利用零点曲线 τ(r_h)，作者识别了这种分离背后的局域机制：在 τ_a 处，具有非零绕数的零点从被计数域边界进入或离开正半径物理分支集合，改变总拓扑数 W 而不使相应正半径分支奇异；在 τ_b 处，零点曲线在有限半径处形成转向点，两个绕数相反的分支湮灭，分支响应发散。因此，在所分析的例子中，拓扑数的改变记录分支隶属关系的变化，而响应奇异性记录沿许可分支的局域可逆性丢失。两个诊断量探测的是同一零点分支几何的不同局域事件。

English: In this paper, we investigate how different diagnostics characterize the thermodynamic branch structure of black holes. In three charged AdS black hole examples, the thermodynamic topological transition point $\tau_a$, where the total topological number changes, is separated from the point $\tau_b$ where the branch response considered here, evaluated along a path with fixed charge parameters, becomes singular. Using the zero-point curves $\tau(r_h)$, we identify the local mechanisms behind this separation. At $\tau_a$, a zero point with nonzero winding enters or leaves the positive-radius physical branch set through the boundary of the counted domain, changing $W$ without making the corresponding positive-radius branch singular. At $\tau_b$, the zero-point curve develops a finite-radius turning point, where two branches with opposite winding numbers annihilate and the branch response diverges. Thus, in the analyzed examples, a change in the topological number records a change in branch membership, whereas a response singularity records a loss of local invertibility along an admitted branch. The two diagnostics therefore probe different local events of the same zero-point branch geometry.

一句话导读：本文通过零点曲线解析黑洞热力学拓扑转变与响应发散的不同局域机制：前者对应分支归属改变，后者对应局域可逆性丧失。

相关度：medium — 本文属于用户列出的经典引力/广义相对论支线，研究 AdS 黑洞热力学拓扑和分支响应奇异性，与重点标签“经典引力/广义相对论”和“相变与气泡成核”相关；但不涉及全息超流、全息 QCD、冷原子或探针膜等具体全息场论内容。

相变与气泡成核经典引力/广义相对论

复数 CFT：全息与界面

Complex CFTs: Holography and Interfaces

Luis Camargo-Carlos, Michael Gutperle · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-03

中文摘要：我们研究了复数共形场论以及这类理论之间的界面，使用全息和二维 CFT 方法。我们利用全息证明了复共轭 CFT 的 Im-flip 性质。我们构造了一个复数 Janus 解，并计算了一些全息观测量。复数 RG 流界面通过数值方法得到。在 CFT 侧，我们为带和不带背景电荷的自由玻色子 CFT 构造了复数界面。在两种情况下都构造了边界态，并计算了透射和反射系数。

English: We investigate complex conformal field theories and interfaces between such theories, using methods of holography and two dimensional CFT. We use holography to prove the Im-flip property of complex conjugate CFTs. We construct a complex Janus solution and calculate some holographic observables. Complex RG-flow interfaces are obtained numerically. On the CFT side, complex interfaces are constructed for free boson CFTs with and without background charge. Boundary states are constructed, and transmission and reflection coefficients are calculated in both cases.

一句话导读：利用全息方法和二维 CFT 研究了复数 CFT 及其界面，构造了复数 Janus 解并计算了透射反射系数。

相关度：medium — 论文研究全息界面与复数 CFT，虽与凝聚态全息超导不直接相关，但界面/Janus 解与畴壁/孤子等非均匀结构方向有关，且属于 AdS/CFT 字典。

能量-动量平方引力中的引力重子产生

Gravitational Baryogenesis in Energy-Momentum Squared~Gravity

David S. Pereira, Francisco S. N. Lobo, Jos\'e Pedro Mimoso · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-03

中文摘要：我们证明物质扇区本身可以在能量-动量平方引力 $f(R,\mathcal T^2)$（其中 $\mathcal T^2\equiv T_{\mu\nu}T^{\mu\nu}$）中驱动重子产生。高密度物质修正通过与 $\mathcal T^2$ 以及完整组合 $f(R,\mathcal T^2)$ 的导数耦合，为重子不对称性提供了新的含时源。值得注意的是，退耦温度不视为自由参数；相反，它由 Weinberg $B-L$ 破坏算子的冻结过程固定，从而将不对称性与轻中微子质量标度和修正的膨胀历史直接联系起来。通过分析代表性幂次 $n=1/4$, $n=1/2$, $n=5/8$ 和 $n=1$，我们发现熵演化能清晰地区分这些模型。$n=1/4$ 分支不能作为自洽的辐射时期模型，$n=1/2$ 分支熵安全但过弱无法复现观测到的不对称性，$n=1$ 分支仅在熵稀释后存活。值得注意的是，$n=5/8$ 分支提供了最干净的可行实现，在受控的有效场论层级下产生观测到的重子-熵比。

English: We demonstrate that the matter sector itself can drive baryogenesis in energy-momentum squared gravity, $f(R,\mathcal T^2)$ with $\mathcal T^2\equiv T_{\mu\nu}T^{\mu\nu}$. High-density matter corrections provide new time-dependent sources for the baryon asymmetry through derivative couplings to $\mathcal T^2$ and to the full combination $f(R,\mathcal T^2)$. Notably, the decoupling temperature is not treated as a free parameter; instead, it is fixed by the freeze-out of the Weinberg $B-L$-violating operator, directly linking the asymmetry to the light-neutrino mass scale and to the modified expansion history. Analyzing representative powers $n=1/4$, $n=1/2$, $n=5/8$, and $n=1$, we find that entropy evolution sharply distinguishes the models. The $n=1/4$ branch cannot serve as a self-contained radiation-era model, the $n=1/2$ branch is entropy-safe but too weak to reproduce the observed asymmetry, and the $n=1$ branch survives only after entropy dilution. Remarkably, the $n=5/8$ branch provides the cleanest viable realization, generating the observed baryon-to-entropy ratio with controlled effective-field-theory hierarchies.

一句话导读：在能量-动量平方修改引力中，物质修正可在高密度下通过导数耦合产生重子不对称，其中 n=5/8 分支给出最干净的可行实现。

相关度：low — 论文研究修改引力中的宇宙学重子产生，与用户关注的全息/经典引力具体支线（如全息超流、QNM等）无关。

带主标量毛发的旋转黑洞的现象学扩展：超越 Horndeski 引力中的阴影特征

Phenomenological Rotating Extension of Black Holes with Primary Scalar Hair: Shadow Signatures in Beyond Horndeski Gravity

Kourosh Nozari, Milad Hajebrahimi, Sara Saghafi, G. Mustafa, Emmanuel N. Saridakis · gr-qc, astro-ph.CO, hep-th · v3 · 更新 2026-09-03

中文摘要：事件视界望远镜（EHT）对M87*的成像提供了强场引力的直接检验，测得其角阴影直径θd=42±3 μas，圆度偏差ΔC≤0.1。这类观测允许对Kerr范式以及对无毛定理可能偏离的情形进行定量检验。在引力的标量-张量扩展中，黑洞可能拥有主标量毛发，从而引入质量与自旋之外的额外独立参数。本文基于超越Horndeski引力中带主标量毛发的黑洞解，构造了一个转动构型，并分析了其光子区和阴影形成。我们发现，标量毛发参数Q会引起阴影的特征性修改：负Q使阴影增大并降低扁率，正Q则减小阴影并增强变形。以M87*作为代表性案例并施加EHT对θd和ΔC的约束，我们确定了可行的(a,Q)参数空间。我们发现，当前观测并不排除带主标量毛发的转动黑洞，尽管Q>0时允许区域受到显著限制。最后，标量毛发诱发的偏离约为微角秒量级，接近当前仪器的灵敏度极限，并处于下一代视界尺度成像的可及范围。

English: The Event Horizon Telescope (EHT) image of M87* provides a direct test of strong-field gravity, measuring an angular shadow diameter $\theta_{d}=42\pm 3~\mu\mathrm{as}$ and a circularity deviation $\Delta C\leq 0.1$. Such observations allow quantitative tests of the Kerr paradigm and of possible deviations from the no-hair theorem. In scalar-tensor extensions of gravity, black holes may possess primary scalar hair, introducing an additional independent parameter beyond mass and spin. In this work, we construct a rotating configuration inspired by black hole solutions with primary scalar hair in beyond Horndeski gravity and analyze their photon regions and shadow formation. We show that the scalar hair parameter $Q$ induces characteristic modifications of the shadow, and in particular negative $Q$ enlarges the shadow and reduces its oblateness, while positive $Q$ shrinks and enhances its distortion. Adopting M87* as a representative case within this framework and imposing the EHT bounds on $\theta_{d}$ and $\Delta C$, we identify the viable $(a,Q)$ parameter space. We find that current observations do not exclude rotating black holes with primary scalar hair, although the allowed region is significantly restricted for $Q>0$. Finally, the scalar-hair-induced deviations are of order $\mathcal{O}(\mu\mathrm{as})$, placing them near the sensitivity threshold of present instruments and within reach of next-generation horizon-scale imaging.

一句话导读：用M87*的EHT观测约束带主标量毛发的旋转黑洞，发现负（正）毛发参数会增大（减小）阴影并改变形状，现有观测仍允许这类黑洞。

相关度：medium — 该论文属于经典引力的黑洞阴影研究，只命中用户重点标签中的'经典引力/广义相对论'，未涉及任何全息或具体支线。

反超局域性与暴涨的高原势模型

Anti-Ultralocality and Plateau Models of Inflation

Joshua Shterenberg, David Garfinkle, Anna I. Rosenzweig, David Shlivko, Paul J. Steinhardt · gr-qc, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-03

中文摘要：反超局域性指的是在耦合的爱因斯坦-标量场方程中，空间梯度项相对于速度项的增长。它是暴涨开始之前减速膨胀的一个特征。先前的数值相对论研究表明，在具有幂律暴胀势的模型中，反超局域性会阻止暴涨的开始。在本文中，我们表明，具有高原形暴胀势的模型——这被认为是产生低于当前观测上限的张标比的最简单方法——尤其容易受到反超局域性效应的影响。其原因在于高原的平直性以及普朗克密度与高原势能之间约10个数量级的能量密度差距。为研究此问题，我们开发了一个评估一般暴胀模型可行性的协议，并将其应用于高原势，使用先前已验证的数值相对论代码。我们发现，从一般初始条件出发，爱因斯坦方程中梯度项相对于非梯度项的增长要么使暴涨无法持续足够的e-折数，要么触发量子失控阶段。我们还表明，为避免这些问题所需的初始条件精细调节随着暴涨能标的降低而变得更加严重，这不利于降低张标比的常见方法。

English: Anti-ultralocality refers to the growth of spatial gradient terms relative to velocity terms in the coupled Einstein--scalar field equations. It is a characteristic feature of decelerated expansion before the onset of inflation. Previous numerical relativity studies have shown that anti-ultralocality prevents the onset of inflation in models with power-law inflaton potentials. In this paper, we show that models with plateau-shaped inflaton potentials, which are considered to be the simplest way to generate a tensor-to-scalar ratio below current observational upper limits, are especially vulnerable to anti-ultralocality effects. The reasons are the flatness of the plateau and the energy density gap of $\sim 10$ orders of magnitude between the Planck density and the plateau potential energy. To study the problem, we develop a protocol for assessing the viability of inflationary models in general, and we apply it to a plateau potential using a previously validated numerical relativity code. We find that, starting from generic initial conditions, the growth of gradient terms in the Einstein equations relative to non-gradient terms either prevents inflation from lasting for enough $e$-folds or triggers a phase of quantum runaway. We show that the fine-tuning of initial conditions necessary to avoid these issues becomes more severe as the energy scale of inflation is made smaller, disfavoring common approaches for reducing the tensor-to-scalar ratio.

一句话导读：高原势暴胀模型因势的平坦性和巨大能量差，对反超局域性特别敏感，更容易导致e-折不足或量子失控，且低能标需要更精细的初始条件。

相关度：low — 摘要内容为经典引力框架下的宇宙学暴胀动力学，不涉及全息、量子引力、纠缠或凝聚态等用户关注方向，与用户研究支线关联很弱。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

强磁场及反常磁矩对超子中子星物质状态方程和质量-半径结构的影响

Effects of Strong Magnetic Fields on the Equation of State and Mass-Radius Structure of Hyperonic Neutron-Star Matter with Anomalous Magnetic Moments

W. German, T. Mello, J. P. W. Diener, G. C. Hillhouse · nucl-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-03

中文摘要：我们在相对论平均场（RMF）框架下研究强磁场对冷、电荷中性、β平衡的超子中子星物质的状态方程（EoS）和恒星结构的影响。物质部分包含完整重子八重态和轻子，带电粒子被朗道量子化，所有重子通过其反常磁矩（AMM）与磁场耦合。计算采用RMF FSU2H超子参数化，并对零场、恒定磁场和密度相关磁场轮廓进行了比较。我们发现强磁场通过朗道量子化和AMM诱导的自旋劈裂的竞争效应改变超子组成。朗道量子化使磁化超子状态方程变软。AMM的加入为超子物质提供了额外的磁硬化机制。有无AMM耦合的结果都与2.00-2.02太阳质量中子星和小半径的观测相容。因此，当前工作为评估AMM对磁化超子中子星物质的组成、磁化、状态方程和质量-半径结构的影响提供了一个基准。

English: We investigate the effects of strong magnetic fields on the equation of state (EoS) and stellar structure of cold, charge-neutral, $\beta$-equilibrated hyperonic neutron-star matter within a relativistic mean-field (RMF) framework. The matter sector contains the full baryon octet and leptons, while charged particles are Landau quantized and all baryons are coupled to the magnetic field through their anomalous magnetic moments (AMM). The calculation is performed with the RMF FSU2H hyperonic parameterization and compared for zero field, constant magnetic fields, and density-dependent magnetic-field profiles. We find that strong magnetic fields modify the hyperonic composition through the competing effects of Landau quantization and AMM-induced spin splitting. Landau quantization softens the magnetized hyperonic equation of state. The inclusion of AMM provides an additional magnetic stiffening mechanism in hyperonic matter. The results for the inclusion of the AMM coupling and not are still consistent with observations of $2.00$-$2.02\,M_{\odot}$ neutron stars and small radii. The present work therefore provides a benchmark for assessing the influence of AMM on the composition, magnetization, equation of state, and mass-radius structure of magnetized hyperonic neutron-star matter.

一句话导读：通过相对论平均场模型研究强磁场下含超子中子星物质中朗道量子化与反常磁矩对状态方程和质量-半径关系的竞争效应。

相关度：medium — 论文属于经典引力/广义相对论范畴，研究中子星物态方程与结构，与用户重点关注的全息/量子引力主题无关，但契合经典引力支线。

有限密度等离子体中矢量介子的自旋排列

Spin alignment of vector mesons in a plasma at finite density

Fei Wang, Zi-qiang Zhang · hep-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：我们在软墙全息模型中研究了有限密度下 J/psi 和 phi 介子的谱函数与自旋排列。该模型提供了夸克-胶子等离子体背景，其中矢量介子由体矢量场表示。我们推导了不同自旋道产生率与介质中对应谱函数之间的关系，并考察了它们对化学势、介子动量和温度的依赖。我们分析了矢量介子相对于热浴运动所诱导的自旋排列。在 T = 0.15 GeV 时，J/psi 谱函数呈现清晰共振峰，表明 c-cbar 对仍可形成准稳定束缚态；随着化学势增大，该峰变低、变宽，标志着介质中解离增强。对于相同温度下的 phi 介子，没有观察到显著峰，表明其发生显著熔化；因此其谱函数更适合描述热环境中不稳定 s-sbar 对的分布。在 T = 0.15 GeV，螺旋度框架下的自旋排列参数 rho_00 对 J/psi 呈现相对于 1/3 的正偏差，对 phi 呈现负偏差。有限化学势会改变上述偏差：对 J/psi 减小其大小，对 phi 增大其大小。

English: We study the spectral functions and spin alignment of $J/\psi$ and $\phi$ mesons at finite density within a soft wall holographic model that provides the quark-gluon plasma background, with vector mesons represented by bulk vector fields. We derive the relation between the production rates in distinct spin channels and the corresponding in-medium spectral functions, and examine their dependence on the chemical potential, meson momentum, and temperature. We analyze the spin alignment induced by the motion of the vector meson relative to the thermal bath. At $T=0.15~\mathrm{GeV}$, the $J/\psi$ spectral function exhibits a clear resonance peak, indicating that the $c\bar{c}$ pair can still form a quasistable bound state. As the chemical potential increases, this peak becomes lower and broader, signaling enhanced dissociation in the medium. For the $\phi$ meson at the same temperature, no pronounced peak is observed, indicating substantial melting; its spectral function thus better characterizes the distribution of unstable $s\bar{s}$ pairs in the thermal environment. At $T=0.15~\mathrm{GeV}$, the helicity-frame spin alignment parameter $\rho_{00}$ shows a positive deviation from $1/3$ for the $J/\psi$ and a negative one for the $\phi$ meson. A finite chemical potential alters these deviations, reducing the magnitude for the $J/\psi$ while increasing it for the $\phi$ meson.

一句话导读：用软墙全息模型研究有限密度等离子体中 J/psi 与 phi 介子的谱函数和自旋排列，发现化学势会增强 J/psi 解离并显著改变 rho_00 的偏差。

相关度：medium — 论文属于全息 QCD/核物理方向，聚焦有限密度下的矢量介子谱函数与自旋排列，未落入用户列出的具体子支线标签，但与该方向主题相通。

旋转等离子体中的全息轻夸克能量损失

Holographic light-quark energy loss in a spinning plasma

Yan-Qing Zhao, Zhou-Run Zhu · hep-ph · v2 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：我们研究了由大黑洞极限下的Myers-Perry几何得到的旋转黑膜背景所描述的强耦合等离子体中的轻夸克能量损失。参数a表征该全息设置中双流体的推进/旋转，并与相应极限中的角速度相关。我们采用两种互补探针——下落弦和射击弦描述——来计算分别平行于旋转轴和垂直于旋转轴运动的轻夸克的停止距离与瞬时能量损失。结果表明，轻夸克在热环境中更容易热化。对于平行传播，逆流构型促进热化，而同向传播构型抑制热化。对于横向传播，增大旋转运动的幅度会促进热化。此外，瞬时能量损失沿着终点的轨迹增加。

English: In this work, we investigate light-quark energy loss in a strongly coupled plasma described by a spinning black-brane background obtained from the large-black-hole limit of the Myers--Perry geometry. The parameter $a$ characterizes the boost/rotation of the dual fluid in this holographic setup and is related to the angular velocity in the corresponding limit. We employ two complementary probes, the falling-string and shooting-string descriptions, to compute the stopping distance and the instantaneous energy loss of a light quark moving either transverse or parallel to the rotation axis. The results show that light quarks thermalize more readily in a hot environment. For parallel propagation, a counter-propagating configuration facilitates thermalization, whereas a co-propagating configuration suppresses it. For transverse propagation, increasing the magnitude of the rotational motion promotes thermalization. In addition, the instantaneous energy loss increases along the trajectory of the endpoint.

一句话导读：通过全息模型研究旋转等离子体对轻夸克能量损失的影响，发现平行旋转轴运动时逆流促进热化，横向运动时旋转增强热化。

相关度：low — 论文研究全息QCD中的轻夸克能量损失，与用户重点标注的全息超导、非均匀凝聚、相变等方向不匹配，因此相关性较低。

不相交子系统的量子复杂性动力学

Quantum Complexity Dynamics for Disjoint Subsystems

Yale Fan, Nicholas Hunter-Jones, Andreas Karch, Minju Kum, Shivan Mittal · hep-th, cond-mat.str-el, quant-ph · v1 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：量子复杂性已成为在局域可观测量达到平衡很久之后，探测混沌、热化以及黑洞内部的时间尺度上的自然探针。然而，其时间演化几乎仅在局限于单一连通区域的子系统中被研究。我们利用全息方法和随机量子电路的互补工具表明，由多个不相交区域组成的非连通子系统相比连通情形会引发定性的新物理。首先，在有限温度下，占据总系统不到一半的子系统在晚期可以携带高复杂性，即使其更大的补集的复杂性仍然较低。这种通常层级关系的反转本质上是热效应：它在无限温度极限下消失，而该极限正是随机量子电路所模拟的机制。其次，如果子系统的复杂性在早期达到平衡，那么将其分割为m个不相交分量可进一步将该时间尺度缩小因子m，这一现象在全息和随机量子电路中都有体现。这些结果不仅深化了复杂性之几何与计算概念间的对应，也激励人们在量子动力学中寻找新的复杂性现象。

English: Quantum complexity has emerged as a natural probe of chaos, thermalization, and the black hole interior on timescales long after local observables have equilibrated. However, its time evolution has been studied almost exclusively in subsystems confined to a single connected region. We show, using complementary tools from holography and random quantum circuits, that noncontiguous subsystems composed of multiple disjoint regions give rise to qualitatively new physics compared to the contiguous case. First, at finite temperature, a subsystem occupying less than half of the total system can carry high complexity at late times, even as the complexity of its larger complement remains low. This inversion of the usual hierarchy is intrinsically thermal: it vanishes in the infinite-temperature limit, which is the regime modeled by random quantum circuits. Second, if a subsystem's complexity equilibrates at an early time, then fragmenting it into $m$ disjoint components can further reduce this timescale by a factor of $m$, a phenomenon we exhibit in both holography and random quantum circuits. These results not only sharpen the correspondence between geometric and computational notions of complexity, but also motivate the search for novel complexity phenomena in quantum dynamics.

一句话导读：利用全息与随机量子电路，揭示不相交子系统的复杂性动力学出现新的热化与层级反转现象。

相关度：low — 用户重点标签主要针对全息凝聚态现象、相变、QNM等特定方向，而本文聚焦全息复杂性的一般动力学，不涉及这些具体模型或现象。

D1D5 中一个 1/4-BPS 手征代数：从电荷集中到 BPS 混沌

A 1/4-BPS chiral algebra in D1D5: from charge concentration to BPS chaos

Alexandre Belin, Suzanne Bintanja, Alessandra Gnecchi, Damian R. Musk, Kyriakos Papadodimas · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：D1D5 CFT 在 BPS 态上表现出电荷集中疑难，该疑难类似于（但比 N=4 SYM 更简单）。我们考虑了 D1D5 CFT 中由超荷上同调产生的 1/4-BPS 算符的手征代数，以及由此得到的有理关联函数。利用交叉对称性和该手征代数，我们对于一般耦合和有限中心荷，非微扰地证明了在电荷集中点之外存在一族 1/4-BPS 态。我们进一步讨论了其对 D1D5 CFT 中 BPS 混沌的可能影响，并在 orbifold 点上计算了晚期 BPS 平台。

English: The D1D5 CFT exhibits a charge concentration puzzle for BPS states, similar to, but simpler than, that of $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM. We consider the chiral algebra of 1/4-BPS operators in the D1D5 CFT arising from a supercharge cohomology, and the resulting rational correlators. Using crossing symmetry and this chiral algebra, we establish a non-perturbative proof of the existence of a family of 1/4-BPS states away from the charge concentration locus for general coupling and finite central charge. We further discuss possible implications for BPS chaos in the D1D5 CFT and compute a late-time BPS plateau at the orbifold point.

一句话导读：在 D1D5 CFT 中利用 1/4-BPS 手征代数与交叉对称性，非微扰证明电荷集中点之外存在一族 BPS 态，并讨论 BPS 混沌及 orbifold 点晚期平台。

相关度：low — 该文关注 D1D5 CFT 中的 1/4-BPS 手征代数、电荷集中与 BPS 混沌，与用户重点标签（如全息超流超导、经典引力、冷原子/凝聚态实验等）无直接重合。

Viel-dreibein 引力的质谱

On the mass spectrum of Viel-dreibein gravity

Eloy Ay\'on-Beato, Elizabeth Rodr\'{\i}guez Querts · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：我们研究Viel-dreibein引力的AdS波构型——这是一个由N个相互作用的自旋2场组成的三维理论——以此作为刻画这一多重量力理论质量谱的切入点。我们成功实现了将该系统重新写为单一dreibein的高阶导数理论的已知策略，从而导出了所讨论AdS波剖面所遵循的精确2N阶运动方程。我们引入的第二个新意是：利用最近关于任何N次多项式的根可以用日益著名的$\mathscr{A}$-超几何函数表示的结果，将该方程因式分解为N个Klein-Gordon算子的乘积。值得注意的是，这让我们能够独立于所涉及引力子数来确定整个理论的质量谱，这对于显式构造AdS波解至关重要。这使得我们得以探索该理论的参数空间，并找到其所有临界点（不仅仅是Breitenlohner-Freedman界），在这些点上质量二次方会出现远更广泛的简并性。在这些点，解允许大量新的对数模式，导致多种$\log^{\chi-1}$渐近行为（$\chi\le N$），这标志了推测存在对偶的对数共形场论。

English: We study the AdS-wave configurations of Viel-dreibein gravity, a three-dimensional theory of $N$ interacting spin-2 fields, as a pretext to characterize the mass spectrum of this multigravity theory. We manage to implement the known strategy of rewriting the system as a higher-derivative theory for a single dreibein, thereby deriving the precise $2N$th-order equation governing the dynamics of the AdS-wave profile in question. The second novelty we introduce is a factorization of such an equation as the product of $N$ Klein-Gordon operators, exploiting the recent representation of the roots of any $N$th-degree polynomial in terms of the increasingly celebrated $\mathscr{A}$-hypergeometric functions. Remarkably, this allows us to determine the mass spectrum of the full theory independently of the number of involved gravities, which is essential for an explicit construction of the AdS-wave solutions. This enables us to explore the parameter space of the theory and find all its critical points (not only the Breitenlohner-Freedman bounds) where a much wider range of degeneracies in the squared masses arises. At those points, the solutions allow a zoo of new logarithmic modes leading to several $\log^{\chi-1}$ asymptotic behaviors, with $\chi\le N$, a signature of the existence of allegedly dual logarithmic conformal field theories.

一句话导读：通过AdS波分析，将Viel-dreibein多重量力系统化为高阶导数方程并因式分解为N个Klein-Gordon算子，从而解析确定质量谱，并在临界点发现类似log CFT的对数新模式。

相关度：medium — 该文研究三维多重引力的经典AdS波解与质量谱，与用户标注的经典引力/广义相对论支线直接相关，但未涉及全息凝聚态、QNM、涡旋等用户其他重点支线，因此相关度为中。

等$\mu/T$全息熵及其对强耦合量子流体的吸引子

Iso-$\mu/T$ holographic entropy and its attractor for a strongly coupled quantum fluid

W. Barreto · hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：数值证据表明，在远离平衡阶段，一段消失的熵产生时期会诱发强耦合等离子体中主导能量条件的后续违反。这种行为似乎也适用于Bjorken膨胀的热而密的强耦合量子流体。在此背景下，已经确定介质中化学势-温度比($\mu/T$)随着初始电荷密度$\rho_0$的增大和/或初始能量密度$\varepsilon_0$的减小而增加。这里，我们提出一种数值方法，通过变化$(\varepsilon_0,\rho_0)$来演化Bjorken R电荷等离子体，从而生成保持恒定$\mu/T$的曲线。这使我们能够讨论所有Bekenstein-Hawking熵密度演化到具有相同$\mu/T$的构型的情况。这种方法能够与$\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM等离子体情况($\mu/T=0$)进行更直接的比较，从而为熵产生与主导能量条件违反之间的关联提供更清晰的证据。此外，这一过程为热而密的强耦合量子流体提供了iso$-\mu/T$熵密度流体动力学吸引子的表征（尽管是数值且局部的）。

English: Numerical evidence has shown that a period of vanishing entropy production during far-from-equilibrium stages induces subsequent violations of the dominant energy condition in strongly coupled plasmas. This behavior also appears to hold for a Bjorken-expanding, hot and dense strongly coupled quantum fluid. In this context, it has been established that the chemical potential-to-temperature ratio ($\mu/T$) in the medium increases with higher initial charge density, $\rho_0$, and/or lower initial energy density, $\varepsilon_0$. Here, we present a numerical method to evolve Bjorken R-charged plasmas by varying $(\varepsilon_0,\rho_0)$ in order to generate a curve that preserves a constant $\mu/T$. This allows us to address the case in which all Bekenstein-Hawking entropy densities evolve towards configurations with the same $\mu/T$. This approach enables a more direct comparison with the $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM plasma case ($\mu/T=0$) and, consequently, provides clearer evidence of the correlation between entropy production and violations of the dominant energy condition. Moreover, this procedure offers a characterization (albeit numerical and partial) of the iso$-\mu/T$ entropy density hydrodynamic attractor for hot and dense strongly coupled quantum fluids.

一句话导读：通过数值演化Bjorken R电荷等离子体生成等$\mu/T$曲线，研究熵产生与主导能量条件违反的关联，并刻画iso-$\mu/T$熵密度吸引子。

相关度：low — 该研究属于全息强耦合等离子体非平衡流体动力学，与用户重点关注的全息超流超导、p波、畴壁、相变、QNM等方向没有直接重叠，相关性较低。

Fuchsian共振与极点跳跃的视界到边界词典

Fuchsian Resonance and a Horizon-to-Boundary Dictionary for Pole-Skipping

Yoon-Seok Choun · hep-th, gr-qc, math-ph, math.MP · v1 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：极点跳跃发生在黑洞或黑膜背景的延迟格林函数在特殊复频率和动量处未被唯一定义的情况。局域近视界机制是已知的：在共振处，Frobenius递推矩阵失去秩，使得光滑视界解空间扩大。我们处理两个边界归一化解是如何进入这一共振视界解空间的全局问题。对一般非极端背景上的一般二阶标量径向方程，在共振阶处的Frobenius递推产生一个可解性条件。我们证明该条件的消失等价于视界递推矩阵的奇异性、对数Frobenius项的缺失以及两个独立光滑视界解的存在性。远离共振，源零点等价于响应归一化解的视界光滑性，而响应零点等价于源归一化解的视界光滑性。我们进一步分析依赖参数的共振逼近过程中非共振内行解的简单极点。移除该奇点后，其共振极限正比于大根Frobenius解，比例因子正是同一可解性函数。继续延拓到边界表明，同一条件等价于两个正则化边界连接系数的同时消失。这建立起局域Fuchsian共振与极点跳跃的边界源-响应0/0结构之间的视界到边界词典。最后，不同的趋近方向可以选择不同的共振解，这种选择由同一可解性函数的一阶参数变分所控制。

English: Pole-skipping occurs at special complex frequencies and momenta where the retarded Green function of a black-hole or black-brane background is not uniquely defined. The local near-horizon mechanism is well known: at resonance, a Frobenius recurrence matrix loses rank and the space of smooth horizon solutions enlarges. We address the global question of how two boundary-normalized solutions enter this resonant horizon solution space. For a general second-order scalar radial equation on a nonextremal background analytic near the horizon, the Frobenius recurrence at the resonant order yields a solvability condition. We prove that its vanishing is equivalent to singularity of the horizon recurrence matrix, absence of the logarithmic Frobenius term, and existence of two independent smooth horizon solutions. Away from resonance, the source zero is equivalent to horizon smoothness of the response-normalized solution, while the response zero is equivalent to horizon smoothness of the source-normalized solution. We then analyze the parameter-dependent simple pole of the nonresonant ingoing solution as resonance is approached. After removing this singularity, its resonant limit is proportional to the larger-root Frobenius solution, with proportionality factor given by the same solvability function. Continuing to the boundary shows that the same condition is equivalent to simultaneous vanishing of the two regularized boundary connection coefficients. This establishes a horizon-to-boundary dictionary between local Fuchsian resonance and the boundary source-response 0/0 structure of pole-skipping. Finally, different directions of approach can select different resonant solutions, with the selection governed by the first parameter variation of the same solvability function.

一句话导读：严格证明极点跳跃的Fuchsian共振条件等价于边界连接系数的整体消失，建立了从视界Frobenius结构到边界Green函数零点/极点的完整词典。

相关度：medium — 论文研究极点跳跃的数学对应，与准正则模式(QNM)的零点/极点结构相关，但未涉及用户重点标签中的具体物态或凝聚态全息模型，关联间接。

AdS4 Einstein-Scalar 引力中平面黑洞上的微扰双标量毛发

Perturbative Double-Scalar Hair on Planar Black Holes in AdS$_{4}$ Einstein-Scalar Gravity

Sangheon Yun · hep-th, gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：我们在四维 Einstein 引力耦合两个标量的系统中构造了双标量毛发的平面黑洞，其势函数源自 ABJM 一致性截断的 11 维超引力。从已知的单标量毛发黑洞膜出发，我们以振幅 ε 微扰地开启第二个标量 φ_m，并将耦合的 Einstein-标量系统解到 ε² 阶。线性化的 φ_m 轮廓是精确的超几何函数，与 π 毛发无关；其视界正则支总是附带源，因此不存在自发的 φ_m 毛发。由于对偶算符 O_- 是无关的 (Δ=4)，二阶响应会发展出对数跑动凝聚，其闭式、与方案无关的斜率为 √21/70 C_s²。重整化自由能是方案依赖的，但在固定熵下几何温度移动 δT/T₀ ≈ -0.345 ε² 不是。完整的度规和 π 反作用是闭式得到的，可对角化成两个 Pöschl-Teller 问题，且有 δπ = 6/√7 δH。对偶流体的三个输运系数在该阶仍精确共形，而声速与自由能一样是方案依赖的。

English: We construct a two-scalar hairy planar black hole in four-dimensional Einstein gravity coupled to two scalars whose potential descends from the ABJM consistent truncation of eleven-dimensional supergravity. Starting from the known single-scalar hairy black brane, we switch on the second scalar $\phim$ perturbatively in its amplitude $\eps$ and solve the coupled Einstein-scalar system to order $\eps^{2}$. The linearized $\phim$ profile is an exact hypergeometric function, independent of the $\pp$ hair, whose horizon-regular branch always carries a source, so there is no spontaneous $\phim$ hair. Because the dual operator $\mathcal{O}_{-}$ is irrelevant ($\Delta=4$), the second-order response develops a logarithmically running condensate with a closed-form, scheme-independent slope $\tfrac{\sqrt{21}}{70}C_{s}^{2}$. The renormalized free energy is scheme dependent, but the geometric temperature shift $\delta T/T_{0}\approx-0.345\,\eps^{2}$ at fixed entropy is not. The full metric and $\pp$ back-reaction is obtained in closed form, diagonalizing into two P\"oschl-Teller problems with $\delta\pp=\tfrac{6}{\sqrt7}\,\delta H$. Three transport coefficients of the dual fluid remain exactly conformal to this order, while the sound speed, like the free energy, is scheme dependent.

一句话导读：在 ABJM 截断启发的 AdS4 Einstein-scalar 理论中，从单标量毛平面黑洞微扰开启第二个标量；线性支恒带源故无自发毛发，二阶效应给出对数跑动凝聚，同时揭示了自由能与声速的方案依赖性和温度移动的方案无关性。

相关度：medium — 该工作基于 ABJM 截断构造 AdS4 平面黑洞的双标量微扰解，核心属于 AdS/CFT 字典与经典黑洞引力方向；与用户列出的全息超流、p 波、探针膜、相变与气泡等具体主线没有直接交叉，故列为中等相关。

来自类时奇点的正能量

Positive energy from timelike singularities

Fernando Ruiz Ruiz · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：计算了具有时间平移不变性和类时奇点的若干渐近AdS与渐近平直Einstein解的引力能量，其中包括具有Kasner类时奇点的时空和负质量黑洞。所使用的引力能量定义是Hawking与Horowitz三十年前提出的物理经典作用量的壳上Hamiltonian，它与Abbott-Deser及ADM表达式一致。为处理奇点，引入一个围绕奇点并与良定义等时切片叶状结构相容的类时正则化截断边界，计算在有限的截断参数值上进行，最终的能量表达式再令其趋于零。在所有考察的例子中，奇点对能量的贡献都有限且为正；其数值取决于奇点附近的几何，并使总引力能量为正。在回顾的解中，只有无奇点的AdS孤子具有相对于AdS空间的负引力能量。带负质量的AdS与Schwarzschild黑洞则分别与AdS空间和Minkowski空间具有相同的能量。

English: The energy of various asymptotically AdS and asymptotically flat solutions to the Einstein equations with time-translational invariance and a timelike singularity is computed, spaces with Kasner timelike singularities and black holes with negative masses among them. The definition used for gravitational energy is the on-shell Hamiltonian of the physical classical action proposed by Hawking and Horowitz thirty years ago and which agrees with the Abbott-Deser and ADM expressions. To deal with the singularity, a timelike regulating cut-off boundary surrounding the singularity compatible with a well-defined foliation in constant-time slices is introduced, so calculations are performed at a finite value of the regulating parameter, which is set to zero in the final expression for the energy. In all instances considered, the energy contribution from the singularity is finite and positive, its value depends on the geometry around the singularity and is such that the total gravitational energy is positive. Of the solutions reviewed, only the AdS soliton (which has no singularity) has negative gravitational energy relative to AdS space. The AdS and the Schwarzschild black holes with negative masses have respectively the same energies as AdS and Minkowski spaces.

一句话导读：采用Hawking-Horowitz壳上Hamiltonian计算含类时奇点的渐近AdS/平直时空的引力能量，发现奇点能量贡献有限且为正，负质量黑洞与相应真空同能量。

相关度：medium — 该文属于纯经典引力计算，研究类时奇点和负质量黑洞的引力能量，与用户经典引力/广义相对论支线相关；但不涉及全息字典、量子信息或用户的凝聚态/全息主线，总体参考意义中等。

一般GLPV理论中具有静态标量毛的黑洞的微扰与稳定性

Perturbations and stability of black holes with static scalar hair in general GLPV theories

Petarpa Boonserm, Antonio De Felice, Ratchaphat Nakarachinda, Shinji Tsujikawa, Pitayuth Wongjun · gr-qc, hep-ph, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：我们在一般的quartic-quintic GLPV理论（包括Horndeski）中推导了具有径向标量毛的静态球对称黑洞的背景方程和完整的奇、偶宇称二次作用量。在规则的、非退化分支上，该形式适用于越过Killing视界，并给出局部无鬼条件以及径向和角向eikonal特征。两个宇称部分的张量模式具有相同的平方径向速度。当径向坐标为类时，一般的反对称混合会导致偶宇称角向分支出现非标准的大多极标度。要求两个分支具有有限非零的eikonal相位速度，会选择quartic和quintic beyond-Horndeski函数之间的Horndeski相关兼容条件。该条件使协变退化方向对齐，并在规则且可逆时允许局部的disformal映射到Horndeski理论。对于视界处非零且有限的标量动能项，精确二次GLPV黑洞的每个非平凡分支在简单外视界附近局部不稳定或具有退化张量锥。除了已识别的例外，这一阻碍扩展到一般的移位和反射对称二次GLPV理论以及quartic-quintic类的正则Horndeski相关分支。最后，对于视界标量动能消失的标量-高斯-博内黑洞，我们构造了解析幂律quartic beyond-Horndeski形变，其关联的quintic函数由同一条件固定。在足够小的耦合下，所有局部无鬼和高频梯度稳定性条件在外域都成立。对于线性高斯-博内耦合，我们估计了一个内部尺度，低于该尺度背景和稳定性展开将失去微扰控制；这并不意味着物理不稳定性。

English: We derive the background equations and complete odd- and even-parity quadratic actions for static, spherically symmetric black holes with radial scalar hair in general quartic-quintic Gleyzes-Langlois-Piazza-Vernizzi (GLPV) theories, including Horndeski. On regular, nondegenerate branches, the formulation applies across Killing horizons and yields local no-ghost conditions and radial and angular eikonal characteristics. Tensor modes in both parity sectors share the same squared radial speed. When the radial coordinate is timelike, generic antisymmetric mixing induces nonstandard large-multipole scaling for both even-parity angular branches. Requiring both branches to have finite, nonzero eikonal phase speeds selects the Horndeski-related compatibility condition between the quartic and quintic beyond-Horndeski functions. This condition aligns the covariant degeneracy directions and permits a local disformal map to Horndeski when regular and invertible. For a finite, nonzero scalar kinetic term at the horizon, every nontrivial branch of the exact quadratic GLPV black hole is locally unstable or has a degenerate tensor cone near a simple outer horizon. Apart from identified exceptions, this obstruction extends to general shift- and reflection-symmetric quadratic GLPV theories and the regular Horndeski-related branch of the quartic-quintic class. Finally, for scalar-Gauss-Bonnet black holes with a vanishing horizon scalar kinetic term, we construct analytic power-law quartic beyond-Horndeski deformations whose associated quintic function is fixed by the same condition. At sufficiently small coupling, all local no-ghost and high-frequency gradient-stability conditions hold throughout the exterior. For a linear Gauss-Bonnet coupling, we estimate an interior scale below which the background and stability expansions lose perturbative control; this does not imply a physical instability.

一句话导读：推导GLPV理论中静态标量毛黑洞的二次摄动作用量，证明其局部不稳定性/退化张量锥，并在标量-高斯-博内情形构建稳定形变。

相关度：medium — 属于经典引力/广义相对论方向的修改引力与黑洞稳定性研究，与全息或其他凝聚态主线无直接关联。经典引力标签下相关，但对用户重点研究支线（如全息超流、畴壁、QNM等）相关性一般。

在Bertotti-Robinson-Bonnor-Melvin电磁场中的Reissner-Nordstrom黑洞

Reissner-Nordstrom in the Bertotti-Robinson-Bonnor-Melvin electromagnetic field

Marco Astorino · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：将磁Reissner-Nordstrom黑洞嵌入Bertotti-Robinson磁场中。然后以此为种子，通过Ernst方程的Harrison变换，生成在Bertotti-Robinson-Bonnor-Melvin磁场中的Reissner-Nordstrom度规。讨论了真空带毛I型Schwarzschild解的推广特例。通过显式微分同胚展示了Schwarzschild-Bertotti-Robinson各种形式之间的局部等价性。

English: The magnetic Reissner-Nordstrom black hole embedded into the Bertotti-Robinson magnetic field is presented. Then it is used, as a seed, to generate, through the Harrison transformation of the Ersnt's equations, the Reissner-Nordstrom metric in the Bertotti-Robinson-Bonnor-Melvin magnetic field. Special cases generalising, the vacuum hairy type I Schwarzschild solution is discussed. The local equivalence between various forms of the Schwarzschild-Bertotti-Robinson is shown by exhibiting the explicit diffeomorphism.

一句话导读：构造嵌入BRBM电磁场的RN黑洞解，并展示相关度规等价性。

相关度：high — 论文直接研究经典广义相对论中的带电黑洞嵌入外部电磁背景的解构造与变换，属于经典引力/广义相对论支线。

arXiv 摘要 · PDF

用广义 Kerr-Schild 变换为高维时空充电

Charging higher-dimensional spacetimes with a generalized Kerr-Schild transformation

Aravindhan Srinivasan, Marcello Ortaggio · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：我们探讨了如何通过沿测地类光矢量场 k 的广义 Kerr-Schild 变换，从真空种子构建高维 Einstein-Maxwell(-Chern-Simons) 解。假设矢量势 A 与 k 对齐，且 k 是满足“光学约束”的 Weyl 对齐类光方向，我们得到三个不同的解分支。若 k 是膨胀且扭曲的，则其剪切必须为零——该分支包括某些带荷 Taub-NUT 度量。若 k 是膨胀且无扭曲的，则有两个子族：剪切为零时对应具有非类光 Maxwell 场的 Robinson-Trautman 电真空解，或者具有类光场的剪切解。最后，当 k 不膨胀时，解约化为已知 Kundt 解的子集。在所有情形中，Chern-Simons 项在壳上恒为零。顺带地，通过放宽对 A 的对齐假设，我们还获得了六维带荷 Taub-NUT 度量的推广，其中场强的磁部分是两个不同 Kähler 2-形式的线性组合，这与先前多于四维的例子不同。

English: We explore the construction of higher-dimensional Einstein-Maxwell(-Chern-Simons) solutions from vacuum seeds by means of a generalized Kerr-Schild transformation along a geodesic null vector field $\mathbf{k}$. Assuming the vector potential $\mathbf{A}$ to be aligned with $\mathbf{k}$, and $\mathbf{k}$ to be a Weyl aligned null direction satisfying the ``optical constraint'', we arrive at three distinct branches of solutions. If $\mathbf{k}$ is expanding and twisting, then its shear must vanish -- this branch includes certain charged Taub-NUT metrics. If $\mathbf{k}$ is expanding and twistfree, one finds two subfamilies: Robinson-Trautman electrovac solutions with a non-null Maxwell field if the shear is zero, or shearing solutions with a null field. Finally, the case when $\mathbf{k}$ is non-expanding reduces to a subset of the known Kundt solutions. In all cases, the Chern-Simons term turns out to be identically zero on-shell. In passing, by relaxing the alignment assumption on $\mathbf{A}$, we also obtain a six-dimensional extension of a charged Taub-NUT metric for which the magnetic part of the field strength is a linear combination of two distinct K\"ahler 2-forms, as opposed to the previously known examples in more than four dimensions.

一句话导读：利用广义 Kerr-Schild 变换系统性构造高维 Einstein-Maxwell(-Chern-Simons) 解，并给出带荷 Taub-NUT、Robinson-Trautman 等分支

相关度：medium — 论文主要涉及经典引力中的解析解构造，与全息/量子引力主线不直接相关，但属于用户标注的经典引力支线，具有一定参考价值。

诱导引力波的角功率谱：打破PTA观测中的模型与参数简并

Angular power spectrum of induced gravitational waves: breaking model and parameter degeneracies in PTA observations

Peng-Yu Wu, Di Wu, Jing-Zhi Zhou · astro-ph.CO, gr-qc, hep-ph, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：小尺度上的大原初曲率和张量扰动可以通过标量-标量、张量-标量和张量-张量源项产生二阶张量-标量诱导引力波（TSIGWs）。TSIGW被认为是随机引力波背景（SGWB）观测的主要贡献者之一。我们研究了在存在非高斯原初曲率扰动的情况下二阶TSIGW的能量密度谱。此外，我们首次计算了与二阶TSIGW相关的各向异性角功率谱，这为将它们与其它SGWB源区分开提供了一种有前途的工具。

English: Large primordial curvature and tensor perturbations on small scales can generate second-order tensor-scalar induced gravitational waves (TSIGWs) through scalar-scalar, tensor-scalar, and tensor-tensor source terms. TSIGWs are recognized as one of the principal contributors to the stochastic gravitational wave background (SGWB) observations. We explore the energy density spectrum of second-order TSIGWs in the presence of non-Gaussian primordial curvature perturbations. Furthermore, we present the first calculation of the anisotropic angular power spectrum associated with second-order TSIGWs, which provides a promising tool to discriminate them from other SGWB sources.

一句话导读：通过计算诱导引力波的各向异性角功率谱，为PTA观测中区分不同随机引力波背景源提供新手段。

相关度：low — 论文研究宇宙学诱导引力波的角功率谱，属于经典引力与宇宙学交叉方向，与用户的重点研究领域（全息、量子引力、凝聚态全息等）无明显关联。

暗物质晕内嵌黑洞的标量波散射

Scalar wave scattering by black holes embedded in dark matter halos

Shi-Qian Hu, Federico Piazza · gr-qc, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：我们研究嵌入暗物质晕中的静态黑洞对无质量标量波的散射。暗物质晕采用Hernquist轮廓建模，该轮廓允许爱因斯坦方程存在精确解。我们利用零测地线、光环近似、分波展开和复角动量（CAM）方法分析散射。几何分析表明，暗物质晕的存在对光环半径影响较弱，但会移动临界碰撞参数和光环碰撞参数。波动分析显示，特征性的黑洞干涉图样得以保持，其中暗物质晕的紧致性起主要修正作用，晕的标度半径起次要作用。对于足够紧致的晕，环境还会引起显著的引力红移。干涉极值的大部分位移可由适当选择 ωM_BH 的真空Schwarzschild解重现。然而，非零残差揭示了无法被这种有效频移吸收的真正晕贡献。最后，我们进行了散射截面的CAM重构，发现仅需少数Regge极点即可在大角度下实现高精度。

English: We study the scattering of massless scalar waves by static black holes embedded in dark matter halos. The halo is modelled by means of a Hernquist profile, for which the Einstein equations admit an exact solution. We analyse the scattering using null geodesics, the glory approximation, the partial wave expansion, and the complex angular momentum (CAM) method. The geometric analysis shows that the presence of the halo changes the light ring radius weakly but shifts the critical and glory impact parameters. The wave analysis shows that the characteristic black hole interference pattern is preserved, with halo compactness providing the leading correction and the halo scale radius playing a secondary role. For sufficiently compact halos, the environment also induces a sizeable gravitational redshift. Most of the displacement of the interference extrema can be reproduced by a vacuum Schwarzschild solution with an appropriately chosen $\omega M_{\rm BH}$. A non-vanishing residual, however, reveals a genuine halo contribution that cannot be absorbed into such an effective frequency shift. Finally, we perform the CAM reconstruction of the scattering cross section and find that only a few Regge poles are required to achieve high accuracy at large angles.

一句话导读：利用零测地线、分波展开和复角动量方法研究暗物质晕中黑洞的标量波散射，发现干涉图样基本保持但存在由晕紧致性主导的环境修正。

相关度：high — 论文主要研究暗物质晕背景下黑洞的经典引力散射行为，属于经典引力/广义相对论支线，与用户重点标签中的该项直接对应。

超越瞬时热化的再加热阶段引力波产生

Gravitational Waves from Reheating beyond Instantaneous Thermalization

Kyohei Mukaida, Tenta Tsuji · hep-ph, astro-ph.CO, hep-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：我们研究了在不对暴胀子衰变产物做瞬时热化假设的情况下，扰动性再加热过程中的引力波产生。注入的高能粒子通过受 Landau-Pomeranchuk-Migdal 效应制约的介质内近共线劈裂级联实现热化，并最终在热尺度附近通过弹性散射各向同性化。在完全热化之前存在的非热硬粒子群通过硬-软散射产生额外的引力波成分，在注入尺度转折以下满足 Ω_GW ∝ f^{1/2}。相同的各向同性化抹去了真空 1/k 轫致辐射软极点背后的方向性信息，使深红外谱从 Ω_GW ∝ f 变为 Ω_GW ∝ f^3。

English: We study gravitational-wave production during perturbative reheating without assuming instantaneous thermalization of the inflaton decay products. The injected energetic particles thermalize through an in-medium cascade of nearly collinear splittings subject to the Landau-Pomeranchuk-Migdal effect and are ultimately isotropized by elastic scatterings near the thermal scale. The non-thermal hard population present before complete thermalization produces an additional gravitational-wave component through hard-soft scatterings, with $\Omega_{\mathrm{GW}}\propto f^{1/2}$ below the injection-scale turnover. The same isotropization erases the directional information underlying the vacuum $1/k$ bremsstrahlung soft pole, changing the deep-infrared spectrum from $\Omega_{\mathrm{GW}}\propto f$ to $\Omega_{\mathrm{GW}}\propto f^3$.

一句话导读：通过考虑暴胀子衰变产物未瞬时热化的扰动再加热过程，发现非热硬粒子群的硬-软散射会产生额外引力波成分，并使深红外谱指数由 f 变为 f^3。

相关度：low — 该文研究宇宙再加热时期的引力波产生，核心是粒子热化与散射物理，不涉及全息、量子引力或黑洞信息，仅在弱场引力波层面与经典引力相关，因此与用户主线相关性低。

黑洞视界附近发射的散射辐射

Scattering Radiation Emitted Near a Black Hole Horizon

Peng Cheng · hep-th, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：我们研究一族在Schwarzschild黑洞视界外侧任意近处制备的外向电磁辐射态，其在局域源参考系中频率保持一致有界。随着制备点靠近视界，引力红移使该辐射趋于零Killing频率，并在任意固定外部散射半径处进入可控软区。这里考虑的辐射可视为霍金辐射谱的一部分。在计及穿过Schwarzschild势垒的透射之后，我们得到了弱曲率局域区域中的领头软响应，并构造了一个红外安全的包含性散射概率。通过红外三角，该响应与未来类光无穷远处的电磁记忆相关联。受条件作用的记忆记录探测透射辐射的零频分量的角投影，及其与伴随硬散射结果的关联。因此，它可以提供额外的渐近约束和辐射源的部分指纹。

English: We study a family of outgoing electromagnetic radiation states prepared outside but arbitrarily close to the horizon of a Schwarzschild black hole, with frequencies that remain uniformly bounded in the local source frame. As the preparation point approaches the horizon, gravitational redshift drives this radiation toward zero Killing frequency and into a controlled soft regime at any fixed exterior scattering radius. The radiation considered here may be viewed as part of the Hawking radiation spectrum. After including transmission through the Schwarzschild potential, we obtain the leading soft response in a localized region of weak curvature and formulate an inclusive scattering probability that is infrared safe. Through the infrared triangle, this response is related to electromagnetic memory at future null infinity. The conditioned memory record probes angular projections of the zero-frequency component of the transmitted radiation, together with their correlations with the accompanying hard scattering outcome. It can therefore provide an additional asymptotic constraint and a partial fingerprint of the radiation source.

一句话导读：研究黑洞视界附近电磁辐射的散射与记忆效应，通过红外三角将软响应与未来类光无穷远的电磁记忆关联，可提供辐射源的渐近探针。

相关度：medium — 论文属于广义相对论中黑洞时空电磁辐射与记忆效应的研究，符合用户经典引力/广义相对论支线；与全息超导、QCD等分支无直接关联。

SdS$_3$中的全息单点函数与测地线

Holographic One-Point Function and Geodesics in SdS$_3$

Arundhati Goldar, Nirmalya Kajuri, Rhitaparna Pal · hep-th, gr-qc · v3 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：Grinberg和Maldacena指出，AdS/CFT中重的热单点函数可以编码到达黑洞奇点的复测地线。我们在三维Schwarzschild--de Sitter空间中研究其de Sitter类似物，并限制在有限循环商空间$\mathrm{dS}_3/\mathbb Z_q$上。我们通过微分字典定义单点函数，并证明对于Bunch--Davies制备的积分有类似结果成立。利用精确的光场Green函数，体积分精确约化为一个收敛的一维变换，其大质量极限由近缺陷区域控制，并由一个复鞍点支配。其作用量再现了从未来无穷远到锥形缺陷的复测地线长度，其实部是重整化后的边界到视界固有时，虚部是视界到缺陷的距离。虚部出现在Lorentzian源基中归一化的单点系数中；在Euclidean基中，同一鞍点只贡献实部，两者通过一个固定的连接因子联系起来。

English: Grinberg and Maldacena showed that heavy thermal one-point functions in AdS/CFT can encode complex geodesics reaching a black hole singularity. We study the de Sitter analogue in three-dimensional Schwarzschild--de Sitter space, restricting to the finite cyclic quotients $\mathrm{dS}_3/\mathbb Z_q$. We define a one-point function through a differentiate dictionary and show that an analogous result holds for the Bunch--Davies-prepared integral. Using the exact light field Green function, the bulk integral reduces exactly to a convergent one-dimensional transform, whose large-mass limit is controlled by the near-defect region and governed by a complex saddle. Its action reproduces a complex geodesic length from future infinity to the conical defect, whose real part is the renormalized boundary-to-horizon proper time and whose imaginary part is the horizon-to-defect distance. The imaginary part appears in the one-point coefficient normalized in the Lorentzian source basis; in the Euclidean basis the same saddle contributes only the real part, the two being related by a fixed connection factor.

一句话导读：在三维Schwarzschild-de Sitter空间中，建立了de Sitter全息单点函数与复测地线长度之间的对应，其大质量极限由近缺陷区的复鞍点控制。

相关度：low — 该论文主要属于de Sitter全息与测地线对应，不在给定的用户重点标签范围内（如全息超流、QNM、相变等）。

平面 AdS 黑膜中的黑洞内部算子与标度对称性

Black Hole Interior Operators and Dilatation Symmetry in Planar Black Branes

Nirmalya Kajuri · hep-th, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：平面 AdS 黑膜具有一种标度对称性，可将某一温度下的膜解映射到另一温度下的解。自然地预期，体场模式的边界表示应继承该对称性，即它们的关联函数应在边界标度变换下协变地变换。我们推导出平面 AdS 黑膜中内部模式的任何边界表示都应满足的协变条件，然后证明 Papadodimas-Raju 镜像算子满足该条件。因此，Papadodimas-Raju 对体内部的态依赖重构继承了平面 AdS 黑洞的标度对称性。

English: Planar AdS black branes have a scaling symmetry that maps a brane solution at one temperature to a solution at another. It is natural to expect that boundary representations of bulk field modes should inherit this symmetry i.e. their correlators should transform covariantly under boundary dilatations. We derive a covariance condition that any boundary representation of interior modes in a planar AdS black brane should satisfy. We then show that Papadodimas-Raju mirror operators satisfy this condition. Thus the Papadodimas-Raju reconstruction of the bulk interior, although state-dependent, inherits the scaling symmetry of planar AdS black holes.

一句话导读：通过推导内部模式的边界表示协变条件，证明 Papadodimas-Raju 镜像算子虽然具有态依赖性，但仍继承平面 AdS 黑膜的标度对称性。

相关度：medium — 该文关注 AdS/CFT 中黑膜内部算子边界表示的标度/膨胀对称性，属于全息体态重构与黑洞量子引力信息方向，但未涉及用户标注的凝聚态、相变或经典引力等具体研究支线。

CGHS模型中的渐近代数与信息全息

Asymptotic Algebras and Holography of Information in CGHS Model

Waheed A. Dar, Nirmalya Kajuri, Rinkesh Panigrahi · hep-th · v3 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：信息全息（HoI）和岛公式是近年来处理黑洞信息丢失悖论的两种方法，它们得到不同的Page曲线。有观点认为Page曲线的差异源于每种方法所关注的代数。本文研究CGHS模型的候选代数。采用HoI文献中的通常假设，通过在将来零无穷构造辐射相空间和渐近代数，我们建立了与无质量标量场耦合的CGHS模型的HoI。我们证明右行模的量子态可以从右将来零无穷的任意小邻域恢复。然后我们论证岛重建与左右辐射代数的完全交换性不相容。我们讨论HoI与岛方法在CGHS模型中可能的区别。

English: Holography of Information (HoI) and the island formula are two recent approaches to the black hole information loss paradox that yield different Page curves. The difference between the Page curves has been argued to be rooted in the algebra that each approach focuses on. In this paper, we study the candidate algebras for the CGHS model. Making the usual assumptions that are made in HoI literature, we establish HoI for the CGHS model coupled to a massless scalar by constructing the radiative phase space and asymptotic algebras at future null infinity. We prove that the quantum state of the right-moving modes can be recovered from an arbitrarily small neighbourhood of the right future null infinity. We then argue that island reconstruction is incompatible with exact commutativity of the left and right radiative algebras. We discuss the possible distinction between the HoI and island approaches for the CGHS model.

一句话导读：该文在CGHS二维引力模型中建立信息全息，证明了右行模量子态可由右将来零无穷的任意小领域恢复，并指出岛重建与辐射代数交换性存在张力。

双荷 Kerr-Sen 黑洞的熵量子化与准正则模

Entropy Quantization and Quasi-normal Modes of Dyonic Kerr-Sen Black Holes

Muhammad Fitrah Alfian Rangga Sakti, Piyabut Burikham · hep-th, gr-qc · v2 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：我们研究了双荷 Kerr-Sen 黑洞（DKSBH）的内外视界热力学性质。研究发现其熵（或面积）乘积是普适的，仅依赖于黑洞角动量。随后，利用热力学关系研究 Kerr/CFT 对应中的对偶共形场论（CFT），并从二维 CFT 计算中心荷，发现中心荷也是普适的，只依赖于角动量。通过与 Kerr-Newman 黑洞比较，发现本质差异位于 CFT 的右行扇区。有趣的是，热力学方法还能显式给出其静态解——双荷伸缩子黑洞——的非零中心荷。此外，从 CFT 关系到多视界热力学，我们得到以黑洞参数表示的准正则模（QNM）谱的解析表达式；该 QNM 计算支持 Bekenstein-Hod 关于黑洞熵量子化的猜想。

English: We explore the properties of inner and outer horizon thermodynamics of dyonic Kerr-Sen black hole (DKSBH). It is observed that the entropy (or area) product is universal, depending only on the angular momentum of the BH. We then proceed to study the dual conformal field theory~(CFT) in the Kerr/CFT correspondence using thermodynamic relations and compute the central charges from 2D CFT. The central charges are found to be universal with only angular momentum dependence. By comparing to Kerr-Newman BH, it is found that the essential difference is in the right-moving sector of the CFT. Interestingly, we can then explicitly produce the non-vanishing central charges related to its static solution, the dyonic dilaton BH, using the thermodynamic method. Moreover, from the CFT relations to multi-horizon thermodynamics, we find the analytical expression of the quasi-normal modes~(QNM) spectra in terms of BH parameters. This QNM computation supports the Bekenstein-Hod conjecture on the BH's entropy quantization.

一句话导读：对双荷 Kerr-Sen 黑洞，利用内外视界热力学和 Kerr/CFT 对应得到普适中心荷及解析准正则模谱，从而支持 Bekenstein-Hod 熵量子化猜想。

相关度：medium — 该文包含 QNM 谱的解析计算，可与 QNM 研究主线发生交集，但其核心为黑洞熵量子化/Kerr-CFT 热力学，不涉及全息凝聚态、探针膜或非线性演化，因此相关度为中等偏间接。

将 HKLL 共形映射到平直时空

Conformally mapping HKLL to flat space

Nirmalya Kajuri, Rhitaparna Pal · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：我们研究了 AdS3 中共形耦合标量的 HKLL 重建问题的两个共形像。第一个映射是爱因斯坦静态宇宙一半上的 Dirichlet 问题。进一步的共形变换将这个半-ESU 问题映射到一个由均匀加速的圆形 Dirichlet 镜面所界定的 (2+1) 维闵可夫斯基空间区域中的无质量标量问题。然而，对于全局 HKLL 问题，得到的平直空间问题并不是具有任意边界数据的一般局部镜面问题；相反，镜面响应数据必须属于从可归一化的全局 AdS 模式继承而来的受限谱类。我们在平直空间中内禀地构造并求解了这个受限镜面问题。在此过程中，我们找到了一个合法的 smearing 函数的例子，而该函数本身并不满足运动方程。我们进一步考虑了 AdS-Rindler 楔形重建：通过共形映射到平直空间中的适当坐标系，我们可以简洁地看到 smearing 函数的发散行为。

English: We study two conformal images of the HKLL reconstruction problem for a conformally coupled scalar on AdS$_3$. The first mapping is to a Dirichlet problem on one half of the Einstein static universe. A further conformal transformation maps this half-ESU problem to a massless scalar in a region of $(2+1)$-dimensional Minkowski space bounded by an uniformly accelerated circular Dirichlet mirror. However, the global HKLL problem, the resulting flat-space problem is not a generic local mirror problem with arbitrary boundary data. Rather, the mirror response data must belong to a restricted spectral class inherited from normalizable global AdS modes. We formulate and solve this restricted mirror problem intrinsically in flat space. In the process, we find an example of a valid smearing function which itself does not satisfy the equations of motion. We further consider AdS-Rindler wedge reconstruction. By conformal mapping to an appropriate coordinate system in flat space, we find a simple way to see the blow-up of the smearing function.

一句话导读：通过共形映射研究 AdS3 上 HKLL 体态重建在爱因斯坦静态宇宙与平直空间中的像，并构造受限镜面问题的 smearing 函数。

相关度：low — 该论文属于 AdS/CFT 字典中的 HKLL 体态重建基础研究，与所列全息超流、相变、QNM 或经典引力等具体支线无直接重叠，因此相关性较低。

双标度SYK模型晚期斜坡：来自仙人掌图的大n尾部

The Late-time Ramp of the Double-Scaled SYK Model from the large-$n$ tail of Cactus Diagram

Yao Li · hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：我们直接从仙人掌图推导了双标度SYK模型中有限温度谱形因子的晚期斜坡，仙人掌图在文献[Berkooz:2020fvm]中被引入为弦图的多迹推广。尽管单迹观测量具有精确的弦图描述，多迹求和受到弦交叉权重q_{IJ}的阻碍，这些权重无法独立地平均为q。我们的关键思想是，产生斜坡的非解析贡献由仙人掌图展开的大阶尾部控制，并且对其低阶解析项的有限改变不敏感。我们将具有n次交叉迹配对的仙人掌图贡献分解为两个萌芽B_n和一个核K_n，确定了它们在固定0≤q<1下的大n渐近行为，并对所得尾部进行重求和。对于β_L=β+it和β_R=β-it，我们得到：Z_s^{sing}(β+it,β-it)=s_p c_N |t|/(2π) ∫_{E_min}^{E_max} e^{-2βE} dE + O(1)，其中s_p=2（若4整除p），1（其他情况）。这一结果再现了随机矩阵理论预言的线性斜坡，包括其温度依赖性和对称因子，并与半经典预言一致。它为一般q变形量子代数中斜坡的直接微观起源提供了依据。而平台则超出了本文分析和计算的范围。

English: We derive the late-time ramp of the finite-temperature spectral form factor in the double-scaled SYK model directly from cactus diagrams, which is introduced as a multi-trace generalization of chord diagram in Ref \cite{Berkooz:2020fvm}. Although single-trace observables admit an exact chord-diagram description, multi-trace sums are obstructed by the chord-intersection weights $q_{IJ}$, which cannot be averaged independently to $q$. Our key idea is that the non-analytic contribution responsible for the ramp is controlled by the large-order tail of the Cactus-diagram expansion and is insensitive to finite changes in its low-order analytic terms. Decomposing the contribution with $n$-cross-trace-pairings Cactus diagram into two buds $B_n$ and a kernel $\mathcal K_n$, we determine their large-$n$ asymptotics with fixed $0\leq q<1$ and resum the resulting tail. For $\beta_L=\beta+it$ and $\beta_R=\beta-it$, we obtain \begin{equation*} Z_s^{\mathrm{sing}}(\beta+it,\beta-it) =s_p c_N \frac{|t|}{2\pi} \int_{E_{min}}^{E_{max}} e^{-2\beta E} dE+O(1), \qquad s_p=\begin{cases}2,&4\mid p,\\1,&4\nmid p.\end{cases} \end{equation*} This result reproduces the linear ramp predicted by random matrix theory, including its temperature dependence and symmetry factor, and agrees with the semiclassical predictions. It provides a direct microscopic origin of the ramp within the general $q$-deformed quantum algebra. While the plateau lies beyond the scope of the present analysis and calculation.

一句话导读：通过仙人掌图大阶尾部的重求和，直接从双标度SYK模型导出有限温度谱形因子的线性斜坡。

相关度：low — 论文主要处理双标度SYK模型与随机矩阵理论中的谱形因子斜坡问题，与用户关注的全息超流、相变、经典引力等支线的直接关联较弱；仅multi-trace技术与个别用户标签重叠。

高频原初引力波 II：功率谱中指数截断的出现

Primary gravitational waves at high frequencies II: Emergence of the exponential cut-off in the power spectrum

Alipriyo Hoory, Jerome Martin, Arnab Paul, L. Sriramkumar · astro-ph.CO, gr-qc, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：（节选）在慢滚暴胀中，源自量子真空的原初引力波功率谱在从未离开哈勃半径的波数k上呈k^2上升。事实上，在这些小尺度上，功率谱在暴胀后的任意时刻都表现出类似行为。我们曾论证必须对原初引力波功率谱进行正则化，以截断大波数处非物理的二次上升。在本文中，我们利用Born近似，研究了当量子真空涨落经历从暴胀到辐射和物质主导时期越来越平滑的过渡时，正则化功率谱在小尺度上的行为。我们证明，当过渡被进一步平滑，小尺度或高频处的抑制会越来越尖锐。通过实例我们还表明，对于由无穷可微“有效势”描述的过渡，原初引力波的正则化功率谱在小尺度上呈现指数压制。我们论证，观测到这种指数下降有助于确定暴胀结束的能量尺度与时间。

English: [Abridged] In slow roll inflation, the power spectrum (PS) of primary gravitational waves (PGWs) generated from the quantum vacuum rises as $k^2$ over wave numbers $k$ which never leave the Hubble radius. In fact, over such small scales, the PS exhibits a similar behavior at any time after inflation. In a recent work, we had argued that the PS of PGWs has to be regularized to truncate the unphysical quadratic rise at large wave numbers. Assuming instantaneous transitions from inflation to the epochs of radiation and matter domination, we had shown that the regularized PS oscillates with a constant amplitude about a vanishing mean over small scales during these epochs. We had also smoothed the transition (actually, the `effective potential' governing the equation of motion of GWs) from inflation to radiation domination using a linear function and evaluated the regularized PS of PGWs post inflation. In such a case, we had shown that, over small scales, while the regularized PS continues to oscillate about zero, its amplitude decreases as $k^{-1}$. In this work, using the Born approximation, we examine the behavior of the regularized PS of PGWs over small scales when they are evolved through smoother and smoother transitions from inflation to the epochs of radiation and matter domination. We illustrate that, at small scales or high frequencies, the suppression in the regularized PS of PGWs occurs more and more sharply as the transition is smoothed further and further. With the help of examples, we also show that, in the case of transitions described by infinitely differentiable `effective potentials', the regularized PS of PGWs exhibits an exponential suppression on small scales. We argue that the observation of the exponential drop in the PS of PGWs can help us determine the energy scale and the time of the end of inflation. We clarify related issues and discuss the wider implications.

一句话导读：通过平滑从暴胀到辐射/物质主导时期的过渡，证明原初引力波功率谱在小尺度上出现指数截断，并可用于测定暴胀结束的能量尺度与时间。

相关度：medium — 论文聚焦于经典引力框架下的宇宙学原初引力波功率谱，与用户关注的经典引力/广义相对论支线直接相关，但与全息、凝聚态等主线无关。

SYK 模型的自由能与谱边

Free energy and spectral edge of the SYK model

Yukun He · math-ph, hep-th, math.MP, math.OA, math.PR · v3 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：我们引入一个微观框架，首次对 Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev 模型的 Schwinger-Dyson 热力学给出严格推导。对于固定偶数相互作用阶数 q≥4，我们证明退火与淬火归一化压强在任意固定逆温度 β>0 下收敛到 Schwinger-Dyson 压强 p_{SD,q}(β)。证明基于有限 N 的 Gibbs 态，包含三个新要素：保持体 Gibbs 态完整的单点空腔展开、给出标签一致条件因子化的有限维局域性估计，以及主图的精确 Majorana 热库表示。我们进一步确定了最大特征值的渐近位置，回答了 Feng-Tian-Wei 提出的问题。因此，样本自由能密度沿任意 β≡β_N→∞ 的序列几乎必然收敛到零温值。

English: We introduce a microscopic framework that gives the first rigorous derivation of the Schwinger--Dyson thermodynamics of the Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev model. For fixed, even interaction order $q\geq 4$, we prove that the annealed and quenched normalized pressures converge to the Schwinger--Dyson pressure $p_{{\rm SD},q}(\beta)$, at any fixed inverse temperature $\beta>0$. The proof is derived from the finite-$N$ Gibbs state, and it contains three new ingredients: a single-site cavity expansion that keeps the bulk Gibbs state intact, a finite-dimensional locality estimate yielding label-uniform conditional factorization of the Euclidean cavity fields, and an exact Majorana-bath representation of the leading diagrams. We further determine the asymptotic location of the largest eigenvalue, which answers a question posed by Feng--Tian--Wei. Consequently, the sample free-energy density converges almost surely to the zero-temperature value along every sequence $\beta\equiv \beta_N\to\infty$.

一句话导读：严格证明了 SYK 模型的退火/淬火压强收敛到 Schwinger-Dyson 压强，并确定谱边与自由能密度的零温收敛。

相关度：low — 论文研究 SYK 模型严格热力学与谱性质，未涉及用户重点标签中的全息超流超导、p 波序参量、畴壁/孤子/涡旋、相变成核、QNM、探针膜或经典引力等方向。

走向作用依赖的Palatini引力的联络表述

Towards a Connection Formulation of Action-Dependent Palatini Gravity

Callum Bell, David Sloan · gr-qc, hep-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：先前工作表明，一阶Palatini引力可以表述为非保守的Herglotz拉格朗日场论，该理论从其动力学变量空间中排除了共形模式。在本文中，我们分析了SO(1,3)规范联络在此作用依赖理论构建中的作用。联络可代数分解为互补的扇区，我们将其诠释为“形状”和“标度”分量。在Herglotz理论中仅保留前者，而后者并入作用依赖扇区。结果表明，联络分解的选择空间可以被平滑参数化。这一观察引出了“摩擦场论模空间”的存在，其中每个点对应联络在形状与标度部分之间的不同代数分割。模空间内的运动在不同Herglotz拉格朗日之间转换，它们的在壳动力学均复现一阶Palatini引力。其区别在于原始理论标度动力学的复现方式如何被划分到作用依赖与动力学挠率之间。

English: Previous work has shown that first-order Palatini gravity may be cast as a non-conservative Herglotz Lagrangian field theory, which excludes the conformal mode from its space of dynamical variables. In the present work, we analyse the role of the $SO(1,3)$ gauge connection in the construction of this action-dependent theory. The connection may be algebraically decomposed into complementary sectors which we interpret as `shape' and `scale' components. Only the former is retained within the Herglotz theory, while the latter is incorporated into the action-dependent sector. It is shown that the choice of connection decomposition is a space that can be smoothly parameterised. Such an observation leads to the finding that there exists a `moduli space of frictional field theories', in which each point corresponds to a distinct algebraic splitting of the connection into shape and scale pieces. Movement within the moduli space transitions between Herglotz Lagrangians whose on-shell dynamics all reproduce first-order Palatini gravity. The distinction lies in how the reproduction of the scale dynamics of the original theory is partitioned between action dependence and dynamical torsion.

一句话导读：本文研究作用依赖Palatini引力中SO(1,3)联络的分解，发现存在一个“摩擦场论模空间”，其中不同分割方式均复现一阶Palatini引力的动力学。

相关度：medium — 该论文属于经典引力理论（作用依赖的Palatini引力）的形式研究，不涉及全息对偶或量子引力，但与用户关注的经典引力/广义相对论研究线直接相关。

NICER 视角下的 PSR J1614−2230：一颗大质量且致密的毫秒脉冲星

A NICER view of PSR J1614$-$2230: a massive and compact millisecond pulsar

Lucien Mauviard, Sebastien Guillot, Lami Suleiman, Yves Kini, Denis Gonz\'alez-Caniulef, Christine Kazantsev, Pierre Stammler, Devarshi Choudhury, Bas Dorsman, Mariska Hoogkamer, Daniela Huppenkothen, Tuomo Salmi, Anna L. Watts, J\'er\^ome Novak, Natalie A. Webb, Jean-Francois Olive, Matthew Kerr, Lucas Guillemot, Isma\"el Cognard, Gilles Theureau · astro-ph.HE, nucl-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：利用脉冲轮廓建模，我们使用中子星内部成分探测器（NICER）、XMM-Newton 和钱德拉 X 射线天文台的数据，获得了毫秒脉冲星 PSR J1614−2230 的质量-半径测量结果。所有数据集均可由两个圆形热斑组成的简单模型很好地描述。最终结果给出赤道半径 R_eq=10.06^{+1.25}_{-0.87} km，引力质量 M=1.937^{+0.012}_{-0.013} M⊙（68% 等尾可信区间）。尽管此前有报道称在更高能段存在非热成分，但我们在相位平均和相位分辨光谱分析中均未发现其迹象。利用将紧致度与扁率或表面引力联系起来并针对该源自转频率定制的新关系，我们推断出一个热斑靠近极区、另一个靠近赤道的构型。质量后验主要由射电计时约束，半径约束因 X 射线脉冲显著性较低（8.5σ）而不太紧；然而在所有测试的几何结构和大气模型中，半径后验均倾向于低值（第 90 百分位 ≤12.08 km）。

English: Using pulse profile modeling, we obtain the mass-radius measurement of a millisecond pulsar (MSP) with data from the Neutron Star Interior Composition ExploreR, XMM-Newton and the Chandra X-ray Observatory. We report here the radius of PSR J1614$-$2230, the second most massive MSP confirmed by radio timing. All of the data sets are well described by a simple model composed of two circular hot spots. The final result yields an equatorial radius of $R_{\rm eq}=10.06 ^{+1.25}_{-0.87}\,$km, and a gravitational mass of $M=1.937^{+0.012}_{-0.013}\,M_{\odot}$ (equally tailed 68% credible intervals). Although a non-thermal component was previously reported at higher energies, we find no sign of it in either our phase-averaged or phase-resolved spectral analyses. Using new relations linking the compactness to oblateness or surface gravity, and tailored to the spin frequency of PSR J1614$-$2230, we infer a configuration with one hot spot near the pole, and another near the equator. The tight mass posterior is essentially informed by radio timing, while the radius constraint is not as tight due to the low source signal (8.5$\sigma$ X-ray pulse significance). However, over all geometries and atmosphere models tested, the radius posterior tends toward low values ($\lesssim12.08\,$km, 90th percentile in all cases).

一句话导读：通过对 NICER 等望远镜的脉冲轮廓建模，给出毫秒脉冲星 PSR J1614−2230 的质量和半径测量，其半径偏小，表明致密且质量大。

相关度：low — 论文专注于中子星观测和经典引力下的质量-半径约束，与理论全息、AdS/CFT 或量子引力无关，仅与经典引力/广义相对论中的致密星体相关。

状态方程与中子星性质：来自PSR J1614-2230、PSR J2124-3358和47 Tuc X7的新质量-半径约束

Equation of state and neutron star properties with new mass-radius constraints from PSR~J1614--2230, PSR~J2124--3358, and 47~Tuc~X7

Melissa Mendes, Isak Svensson, Hannah Gottling, Kai Hebeler, Achim Schwenk, Nathan Rutherford, Lucien Mauviard, Christine Kazantsev, Yves Kini, Denis Gonzalez-Caniulef, Sebastien Guillot, Anna Watts · astro-ph.HE, nucl-ex, nucl-th · v1 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：我们研究了来自PSR J1614-2230、PSR J2124-3358和47 Tuc X7的新质量-半径信息对联合状态方程推断的影响，该推断基于核密度下的手征有效场论约束，并采用不同的高密度扩展，包括微扰QCD约束。最大的影响来自重质量PSR J1614-2230星，它使重中子星的半径减小约0.4公里。此外，结合NICER、LIGO/Virgo和X射线的天体物理信息，使半径后验分布更趋于数据驱动，并对高密度扩展的敏感性降低。对于状态方程而言，新的质量-半径信息显著收紧了压强和声速的后验分布，尤其是在约三倍饱和密度处。最后，我们基于联合状态方程分析和其它天体物理源，对约束较差的PSR J2124-3358和47 Tuc X7的质量进行了预测。

English: We study the impact of new mass-radius information from PSR J1614-2230, PSR J2124-3358, and 47 Tuc X7 in a combined equation of state inference based on chiral effective field theory constraints at nuclear densities and using different high-density extensions, including perturbative QCD constraints. The largest impact stems from the heavy-mass PSR J1614-2230 star, which shifts heavy neutron stars to smaller radii by around 0.4 km. Moreover, the combined astrophysical NICER, LIGO/Virgo, and X-ray information drives the radius posterior to a more data-driven distribution, which is less sensitive to the high-density extension. For the equation of state, the new mass-radius information significantly tightens the pressure and speed-of-sound posterior distributions, especially around three times saturation density. Finally, we make predictions for the poorly constrained masses of PSR J2124-3358 and 47 Tuc X7 based on the combined equation of state analysis and the other astrophysical sources.

一句话导读：利用来自脉冲星的新质量-半径观测与核物理约束，得到更紧致的中子星状态方程推论。

相关度：medium — 该论文主要研究中子星物态方程与质量-半径关系，属于经典引力背景下致密天体物理应用，而非广义相对论基本理论或全息研究；与重点标签中的经典引力有一定交集。

磁型Love数区分夸克星与中子星

Magnetic-type Love number differentiating quark stars from neutron stars

Kenji Fukushima, Josuke Minamiguchi, Tomoya Uji · astro-ph.HE, nucl-th · v2 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：夸克星（QS）是一种假设且尚未发现的恒星天体，其存在将标志着核物质和夸克物质研究的范式转变。尽管紧致度是区分两类QS与中子星（NS）的已知特征，但一些QS在半径-质量平面上可能与NS重叠。为揭示其明显差异，我们研究了QS和NS的潮汐性质。我们发现磁型Love数是区分QS和NS的稳健指标，而当QS和NS具有相似质量和半径时，电型Love数则不够充分。最后，我们表明来自双星并合的引力波原则上可以对QS与NS之间的差异敏感。

English: The quark star (QS) is a hypothetical and yet undiscovered stellar object, and its existence would mark a paradigm shift in research on nuclear and quark matter. Although compactness is a well-known signature for distinguishing between two branches of QSs and neutron stars (NSs), some QSs can overlap with NSs in the radius-mass plane. To manifest their evident differences, we investigate the tidal properties of QSs and NSs. We then find that the magnetic-type Love number is a robust indicator for differentiating between QSs and NSs, whereas the electric-type one is insufficient when QSs and NSs have similar masses and radii. Finally, we show that gravitational waves from binary star mergers can, in principle, be sensitive to differences between QSs and NSs.

一句话导读：研究表明磁型Love数能稳健区分夸克星与中子星，而电型Love数在质量和半径相似时不足以区分，且引力波原则上可探测其差异。

相关度：high — 研究属于经典引力/广义相对论中致密天体潮汐变形与引力波相关主题，且用户明确关注经典引力/广义相对论支线。

旋转超大规模恒星核心坍缩的引力波：三维数值相对论计算

Gravitational waves from core collapse of rotating very-massive stars: 3D numerical relativity computation

Alan Tsz-Lok Lam, Masaru Shibata, Sho Fujibayashi · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE · v1 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：我们利用轴对称和三维（3D）数值相对论方法，数值研究了质量约为200、300、500和1100倍太阳质量的旋转超大规模恒星核心坍缩成黑洞的过程。结果表明，当所得黑洞的无量纲自旋超过0.8时，周围会持续形成一个巨大盘。这些巨大致密的盘，携带超过黑洞质量约10%的物质，容易发生非轴对称不稳定性，触发频率约为10-50 Hz的引力波暴。即使在几Gpc外，这类波也能被爱因斯坦望远镜和宇宙探索者探测到。我们还总结了轴对称坍缩产生的引力波信号，这些信号往往具有比非轴对称不稳定性引起的引力波更低的振幅和更高的频率。

English: We numerically study the collapse of rotating, very-massive stellar cores with masses of $\approx 200$, $300$, $500$, and $1100M_\odot$ into black holes using both axisymmetric and three-dimensional (3D) numerical relativity. Our results indicate that when the dimensionless spin of the resulting black hole exceeds 0.8, a massive disk consistently forms around it. These massive, compact disks, carrying more than about 10\% of the black hole's mass, are prone to non-axisymmetric deformations that trigger gravitational-wave bursts with frequencies around 10-50 Hz. Such waves could be detected by the Einstein Telescope and Cosmic Explorer, even from sources a few Gpc away. We also summarize the gravitational-wave signals from axisymmetric collapse, which tend to have lower amplitudes and higher frequencies than those caused by non-axisymmetric instabilities.

一句话导读：通过三维数值相对论模拟揭示了旋转超大规模恒星坍缩成黑洞时，形成大质量吸积盘并产生10-50 Hz引力波暴。

相关度：low — 该研究与全息/量子引力无关，属于经典数值相对论与引力波天体物理。

用于双黑洞探测的快速可扩展Transformer流水线

A Fast and Scalable Transformer Pipeline for Binary Black Hole Detection

Chayan Chatterjee, Abigail Petulante, Haowei Fu, Yang Hu, Roy Lau, Karan Jani · gr-qc, astro-ph.GA · v1 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：随着下一年代致密双星并合探测率的预期增长，迫切需要开发快速、稳健且可扩展的匹配滤波替代方案用于引力波搜索。Transformer模型已经彻底改变了自然语言和音频处理，但在引力波天文学中的应用基本尚未探索。在这项工作中，我们介绍了CASTRO，一个基于Transformer的符合搜索流水线，用于从Advanced LIGO探测器的双黑洞引力波信号。我们模型的主要特点之一是可以通过时间平移低成本估计误报率，而无需重复评估神经网络。我们在机器学习引力波搜索挑战（MLGWSC-1）数据集以及约五个月的O3b真实观测应变上评估了CASTRO。在基准数据集上测试时，CASTRO在大多数敏感的机器学习流水线中位居前列，并成功恢复了GWTC-3星表中其训练范围内的多数可靠事件。我们还将CASTRO与另一个Transformer架构GW-Whisper进行了基准比较，后者是OpenAI音频基础模型的领域适配。我们发现CASTRO在灵敏度上大幅优于这种改造后的音频模型，并将背景估计的计算成本降低了20倍。我们的结果展示了一种高度实用、可扩展的引力波深度学习搜索及经验背景估计方法，适用于未来的观测运行。

English: With the projected increase in the detection rate of compact-binary coalescences in the coming decade, there is critical need to develop fast, robust, and scalable alternatives to matched filtering for gravitational-wave searches. Transformer models have revolutionized natural language and audio processing but their application to gravitational-wave astronomy is still largely unexplored. In this work, we introduce \castor, a transformer-based coincident search pipeline for detecting binary black hole gravitational-wave signals from Advanced LIGO detectors. One of the major features of our model is that it allows the false-alarm rate to be estimated via time slides cheaply without requiring repeated evaluations of the neural network. We evaluate \castor\ on datasets from the Machine-Learning Gravitational-Wave Search Challenge (MLGWSC-1) and on approximately five months of real O3b observing strain. When tested on benchmark datasets, \castor\ ranks among the most sensitive machine-learning pipelines and successfully recovers the majority of confident events from the GWTC-3 catalog that lie within its training range. We also benchmark \castor\ against another transformer architecture, GW-Whisper, a domain-adaptation of OpenAI's audio foundation model. We find that \castor\ substantially outperforms the repurposed audio model in sensitivity and also reduces the computational cost of background estimation by a factor of 20. Our results demonstrate a highly practical, scalable approach for deep-learning gravitational wave searches and empirical background estimation for future observing runs.

一句话导读：提出CASTRO，一种基于Transformer的双黑洞引力波探测流水线，利用时间滑动高效估计误报率，在基准和真实数据上性能优异。

相关度：low — 该论文关注引力波信号的深度学习检测与背景估计，属于经典引力实验数据分析，与全息理论或经典引力理论本身无直接关联。

空间引力波探测中通过时间延迟干涉通道线性重组实现方向响应优化

Directional Response Optimization through Linear Recombination of Time-Delay Interferometry Channels in Space-based Gravitational Wave Detection

Heng-Sen Jiao, Jing-Rui Zhang, Hong-Bo Jin, Yun-Long Zhang · gr-qc · v1 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：LISA、Taiji 与 TianQin 等空间引力波探测器使用时间延迟干涉(TDI)来消除不等臂星座中的激光频率噪声。不同 TDI 观测量具有不同的天区响应，因此候选通道的线性组合可以增强某个天区的平均响应并抑制另一个天区。本文构造了 TDI 组合的频域响应矩阵，在目标天区和被抑制天区上取平均，并通过一个最大化这两个区域响应之比的广义本征值问题得到最优权重。在毫赫兹频率下，使用 A、E、T 通道、Sagnac 组合 α、β、γ 以及 16 链路 TDI 的示例表明，在所选频率附近可以实现天区零点和大区域对比；本征值 ρ 对于小基组约为 O(10)，对于大基组约为 O(10^2)。因此，该方法预期很适合近单色源，例如毫赫兹波段中可分辨的银河系双白矮星。目标对抑制比(TSR)在设计频率处达到峰值并随远离频率而快速下降，所以优化后的权重向量本质上是窄带的，适合在选定频率和天空方向附近进行定向搜索。

English: Space-based gravitational-wave detectors such as LISA, Taiji, and TianQin employ time-delay interferometry (TDI) to cancel laser-frequency noise for unequal-arm constellations. Since different TDI observables exhibit distinct sky responses, a linear combination of candidate channels can enhance the average response over one sky region while suppressing that of another. We construct a frequency-domain response matrix for TDI combinations, average it across target and suppressed sky regions, and derive the optimal weights via a generalized eigenvalue problem that maximizes the ratio between these two regional responses. At millihertz frequencies, examples with the $A$, $E$, and $T$ channels, the Sagnac combinations $\alpha$, $\beta$, and $\gamma$, and 16-links TDI show that a sky-region null and a large regional contrast are possible near the chosen frequency, with eigenvalues $\rho$ ranging from $\mathcal{O}(10)$ for small bases to $\mathcal{O}(10^2)$ for the larger set. The method is therefore expected to be well suited to nearly monochromatic sources such as the resolved Galactic double white dwarf binaries in the millihertz band. The Target-to-Suppression Ratio (TSR) peaks near the design frequency and falls quickly away from it, so the optimized weight vector is inherently narrowband and suited to targeted searches around a chosen frequency and sky direction.

一句话导读：通过线性组合TDI通道并求解广义本征值问题，可增强空间引力波探测器对目标天区的响应、压制其他区域，形成适合毫赫兹单色源的窄带定向探测。

相关度：low — 该文属于空间引力波探测的数据分析与探测器响应优化方法，虽可纳入引力波应用方向，但核心内容不涉及全息、量子引力或强耦合理论，与用户主要关注的理论研究支线关联较弱。

薄吸积盘照明下中子星的偏振图像：与黑洞的比较

Polarization Images of Neutron Stars Illuminated by a Thin Accretion Disk: A Comparison with Black Holes

Dan-Dan Feng, Guo-Ping Li, Chen-Yu Yang, Hong Lei · gr-qc, astro-ph.HE · v1 · 更新 2026-09-02

中文摘要：我们研究了由几何薄且光学薄的吸积盘照明的静态、球对称中子星的线偏振图像。利用多方物态方程构建中子星平衡位形，并在几何光学近似下求解零测地线方程和线偏振矢量的平行输运方程。数值结果表明，总偏振强度通常与总强度呈正相关，并在中子星表面附近达到最大。随着观测者倾角增大，偏振图像的对称性逐渐被打破。此外，磁场位型主要影响偏振矢量的方向，而对整体偏振强度分布的影响相对有限。为进一步刻画偏振方向的空间结构，我们引入了净电矢量位置角χ_net和二阶方位角傅里叶模角β_2。与Schwarzschild黑洞偏振图像的比较显示，两类致密天体在强偏振区域位置和中心无偏振信号区域大小上存在明显差异。这些结果表明，线偏振图像可提供超出总强度图像的额外信息，用于区分中子星与黑洞。

English: We investigate linear polarization images of static, spherically symmetric neutron stars illuminated by a geometrically and optically thin accretion disk. Neutron star equilibrium configurations are constructed with a polytropic equation of state, and the null geodesic equations and the parallel transport equation for the linear polarization vector are solved in the geometric optics approximation. The numerical results show that the total polarized intensity is generally positively correlated with the total intensity and reaches its maximum near the neutron star surface. As the observer inclination increases, the symmetry of the polarization images is progressively broken. In addition, the magnetic field configuration mainly affects the direction of the polarization vectors, while its influence on the overall polarized intensity distribution is comparatively limited. To further characterize the spatial structure of the polarization direction, we introduce the net electric vector position angle $\chi_{\mathrm{net}}$ and the second azimuthal Fourier mode $\angle\beta_2$. A comparison with polarization images of a Schwarzschild black hole reveals clear differences between the two types of compact objects in the locations of strongly polarized regions and the size of the central region without a polarization signal. These results show that linear polarization images provide information beyond total intensity images for distinguishing neutron stars from black holes.

一句话导读：对薄吸积盘照明的静态球对称中子星做线偏振成像计算，并与Schwarzschild黑洞对比，发现偏振信号的空间分布可作为区分两类致密天体的新探针。

相关度：high — 论文研究的是经典广义相对论下中子星与黑洞的吸积盘偏振成像，属于用户关注的‘经典引力/广义相对论’支线。
